### [1] [VTRL-Med: Progressive Visual Refinement with Topology-Guided Regularization for Medical Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32884)
*Guangyu Meng,Pengfei Gu,Peixian Liang,John P. Lalor,Erin Wolf Chambers,Danny Z. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTRL-Med：利用 SAM 结构特征与持续同调拓扑正则的渐进式医学图像深度聚类框架，在 13 个基准上平均准确率提升 4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度聚类方法存在两点不足：忽视捕捉目标边界与区域的类无关结构先验；未显式编码连通性、孔洞等拓扑模式。二者角色互补——视觉-结构特征提供主要判别表示，拓扑特征为残余视觉歧义提供全局几何线索。

Method: (1) 适配 SAM 提取保留掩码监督预训练结构线索的图像特征；(2) 两步过滤生成可靠伪标签：基于原型的筛选 + k 近邻一致性验证；(3) 渐进优化视觉分支，同时由基于持续同调（PH）描述子构建的辅助拓扑分支在训练中提供几何正则。

Result: 在 13 个医学图像基准上取得 SOTA 聚类性能，平均准确率较现有方法提升 4.48%；在 3 个代表性数据集上，拓扑引导正则相较仅视觉精炼再提升 1.3–4.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉-结构表示与拓扑正则相互补充，可有效提升无标签医学图像聚类的判别性与稳定性。

Abstract: Deep clustering offers a promising approach for analyzing large unlabeled medical image datasets, yet prevailing methods still face two limitations: they may neglect class-agnostic structural priors that capture object boundaries and regions, and they do not explicitly encode topological patterns (e.g., connectivity and holes). These sources of information play distinct and complementary roles: visual-structural features provide the primary discriminative representation for clustering, while topological features provide additional global geometric cues for residual visual ambiguities. We introduce VTRL-Med, a progressive visual-structural learning framework with topology-guided regularization for medical image clustering. VTRL-Med first adapts the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to extract image features that retain structural cues inherited from mask-supervised pretraining. It then generates reliable pseudo-labels through a two-step filtering process that combines prototype-based selection with k-nearest neighbor consistency validation. Finally, it progressively refines the visual branch while an auxiliary topology branch, constructed from persistent homology (PH) descriptors, provides additional geometric regularization during training. Across 13 medical image benchmarks, VTRL-Med achieves state-of-the-art clustering performance, improving average accuracy by 4.48% over existing methods. On three representative datasets, topology-guided regularization further improves accuracy by 1.3--4.3% over visual refinement alone.

</details>


### [2] [OpenStripe supports open-set identification and spatial stripe profiling in wild Amur tigers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32848)
*Yichun Peng,Yansong Li,Jin Hou,Qi Song,Xi Guo,Ning Zhang,Degang Wei,Huang Yang,Yu Guan,Jianping Ge,Lei Bao,Tianming Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenStripe：多智能体框架，重建虎体侧2D规范视图并编码四类可解释局部条纹结构，实现濒危野生虎的无监督开集个体分组，精度0.90、ARI 0.85。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱对濒危野生动物的非侵入式监测需要开集个体识别（无预定义类别、种群规模未知），而姿态形变与杂乱背景使图像比对困难；现有方法缺乏可解释性与对野外场景的适应性。

Method: 提出多智能体（multi-agent）框架OpenStripe：将野外图像中的虎躯干重建为规范的2D展开图案（消除姿态形变），并提取编码四种可解释的局部条纹结构——断裂点（breakpoints）、分叉（bifurcations）、不规则结构、圆形结构；四类结构互补融合后用于自动身份分组。

Result: 在东北虎豹国家公园28只野生东北虎的100条相机陷阱记录（约占该地已记录种群的三分之一）上评估：四类结构融合后匹配精度0.90、调整兰德指数ARI 0.85，在同后端条件下优于49种基线配置（其最高仅0.43精度、0.14 ARI）。条纹相似度随地理距离下降，分形维数与条纹间距等性状与地理邻近性相关。

Conclusion: OpenStripe提供了一种可解释的表示，将自动个体分组与野生种群的表型空间画像相衔接，兼具监测价值与生态学分析潜力。

Abstract: Non-invasive camera-trap monitoring of endangered wildlife requires open-set individual identification---grouping field images into identities without predefined classes or a known population size---yet pose deformation and cluttered backgrounds complicate image comparison. Here, we present OpenStripe, a multi-agent framework that reconstructs canonical two-dimensional (2D) tiger torso patterns and encodes four interpretable local stripe structures: breakpoints, bifurcations, irregular structures, and round structures. We evaluated OpenStripe on 100 camera-trap records from 28 wild Amur tigers (Panthera tigris altaica), representing nearly one-third of the documented population in the Northeast China Tiger and Leopard National Park. Combining all four complementary structures enabled automatic identity grouping with 0.90 matched accuracy and a 0.85 adjusted Rand index (ARI), outperforming 49 baseline configurations under the same backend (up to 0.43 accuracy and 0.14 ARI). In addition, results from OpenStripe indicate that stripe similarity in Amur tigers declines with geographic distance and suggest that traits such as fractal dimension and inter-stripe spacing are associated with geographic proximity. OpenStripe thus provides an interpretable representation that connects automated individual grouping with spatial phenotypic profiling in wild populations.

</details>


### [3] [CompMVR: Compression-Aware Multi-View Restoration Using Diffusion Models for Geometrically Consistent 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32800)
*Dong-hwi Kim,Chaewon Moon,Hojun Song,Dongbeom Kim,Junyeong Jang,Aro Kim,Gahyeon Kim,Heejung Choi,Jehee Kim,Gianella Cravioto,Sohyun Lee,Gyeongjin Choi,EunHye Jeong,Soo Ye Kim,Jaehyup Lee,Sang-hyo Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出压缩感知的多视角扩散修复框架 CompMVR，联合修复压缩退化的多视角图像并保持跨视角一致性，从而提升真实 3D 重建流程在损失压缩下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实 3D 重建流程依赖多视角图像，而这些图像常被有损压缩退化。现有工作主要研究单图或视频修复中的压缩伪影，其对多视角几何的影响被严重忽视。作者识别出"压缩导致的跨视角几何不一致"这一关键失效模式：视角相关的伪影会破坏特征匹配、稠密对应、相机参数估计及下游任务（如新视角合成）。

Method: 提出 CompMVR 框架：以多视角扩散修复为基础，联合精炼压缩视角，同时利用学习到的压缩先验与跨视角对应关系，在恢复视觉细节的同时提升跨视角几何可靠性；并构建了压缩多视角数据集以支持系统评测。与传统逐图独立优化 2D 感知质量的修复方法不同，该方法显式建模跨视角一致性。

Result: 在多数据集、多编解码器、多压缩等级上的实验表明，修复质量提升，且下游 3D 任务（新视角合成、相机位姿估计、视角匹配）性能均有增益。

Conclusion: 将"压缩多视角修复"确立为一个独立问题，并说明其对有损压缩条件下 3D 重建的重要性；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Real-world 3D reconstruction pipelines often rely on multi-view images degraded by lossy compression. While compression artifacts have been extensively studied in single-image or video restoration, their impact on multi-view geometry remains largely overlooked. In this work, we identify compression-induced cross-view geometric inconsistency as a critical failure mode, where view-dependent artifacts disrupt feature matching, dense correspondence, camera parameter estimation, and downstream 3D reconstruction tasks such as novel view synthesis. To address this problem, we propose CompMVR, a compression-aware multi-view diffusion restoration framework that jointly refines compressed views while preserving cross-view consistency, and construct compressed datasets for systematic evaluation. Unlike conventional restoration methods that optimize each image independently for 2D perceptual quality, CompMVR leverages learned compression priors and cross-view correspondence to recover visual details while improving geometric reliability across views. Experiments across diverse datasets, codecs, and compression levels demonstrate gains in restoration quality and downstream 3D tasks, including novel-view synthesis, camera pose estimation, and view matching. These results establish compressed multi-view restoration as a distinct problem and highlight its importance for 3D reconstruction under lossy compression. Code and data for this paper are available at https://mchaewon.github.io/CompMVR-project-page/.

</details>


### [4] [Video-MemComp: Taylor-Guided Sensitivity-Aware Memory Management for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32784)
*Yonghui Niu,Yuhao Ji,Chang Nie,Jiao Zhang,Yilong Li,Junlan Feng,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、敏感度感知的上下文管理框架Video-MemComp，通过一阶泰勒展开将压缩误差分解为正交的局部近似误差与层级结构敏感度两部分，分别用pre-RoPE安全区内的token聚合与动态KV预算分配来最小化，在长视频流式理解中实现超90%的KV显存节省且性能不降反升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频数据快速增长，需要能处理长时连续视觉输入的高效视频理解系统；MLLM面临严重显存瓶颈。现有压缩方法忽视近似误差的爆炸式累积问题。

Method: 基于一阶泰勒展开理论，将压缩误差分解为正交的局部近似误差与层级结构敏感度：(1) 在pre-RoPE安全区内进行token聚合，并引入注意力保持的logit偏置，缓解RoPE旋转导致的位置几何失真，以降低局部近似误差；(2) 结合离线梯度先验与在线上下文熵动态分配KV cache预算，优先保障高敏感层。整体免训练。

Result: 在MLVU、VideoMME、EgoSchema等基准上优于或媲美全缓存与SOTA压缩方法；Qwen2.5-VL-3B整体准确率由62.3%提升至64.1%，同时将KV cache压缩至每层等效6000 token，节省90%以上KV显存。

Conclusion: 通过对压缩误差的正交分解与分而治之的误差控制，Video-MemComp在免训练条件下实现了显存高效且性能不劣化的流式长视频理解，说明敏感度感知的误差建模是KV压缩设计的关键方向。

Abstract: The rapid growth of video data has created an urgent need for efficient video understanding systems that can handle long and continuous visual inputs, posing significant challenges for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to severe memory bottlenecks. Moreover, existing compression methods often overlook the explosive accumulation of approximation errors. To address this issue, we propose Video-MemComp, a training-free and sensitivity-aware context management framework for memory-constrained streaming video understanding. Our method theoretically decomposes compression error into orthogonal local approximation error and layer-wise structural sensitivity via a first-order Taylor expansion. To minimize local approximation error, we perform token aggregation in a pre-RoPE safe zone with an attention-conserving logit bias, mitigating geometric distortions caused by RoPE rotations. To manage layer-wise structural sensitivity, we dynamically allocate the KV cache budget based on offline gradient priors and online context entropy, prioritizing high-sensitivity layers. Video-MemComp outperforms or achieves performance comparable to full-cache and state-of-the-art compression methods on benchmarks including MLVU, VideoMME, and EgoSchema. Notably, it improves the overall accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-3B from 62.3% to 64.1% while reducing the KV cache to an equivalent of 6,000 tokens per layer, saving over 90% of KV memory.

</details>


### [5] [ImageRSI: Verification-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32120)
*Shuo Han,Zhilin Wang,Peng Ling,Wenjia Gong,Yang Liu,Guanjun Jiang,Yukun Cao,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImageRSI：冻结图像生成器的可复用"验证-修复-经验积累"框架，跨任务共享经验库并离线迭代更新评判器，在MindBench与自建ImageReq-Bench上取得CSA 52.6%与65.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂文生图请求难以稳定满足；已有"验证—修复"方法只能改善单次输出，修复经验无法迁移到其他请求，缺乏跨任务的可复用性与持续改进机制。

Method: 生成器保持冻结，三组件构成：任务内五维验证与修复；跨任务共享的经验库（候选经验须通过成对A/B验证方可入库）；评判器离线更新（在累积的图像-判断记录上做SFT与GRPO，复用前先评测）。同时构建ImageReq-Bench：961条围绕真实场景难例的请求，每条配评估清单。

Result: 以Nano Banana Pro为生成器时MindBench上Checklist Strict Accuracy达52.6%；ImageReq-Bench上CSA达65.66%；并对比了不同评判器训练配置下的系统效果。

Conclusion: 将验证修复、经验沉淀与评判器离线迭代解耦于冻结生成器之上，可实现跨请求的经验复用与系统级持续提升；经验入库的A/B校验与评判器复用前的评测是保证可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Complex text-to-image requests remain difficult to satisfy reliably. Verifying and repairing an image can fix an individual output, but a successful repair does not necessarily carry over to other requests. We present ImageRSI, a framework that keeps the image generator frozen and combines three components: five-dimensional verification and repair within each task, an experience library shared across tasks, and offline updating of the judge. Candidate experiences enter the library only after paired A/B validation, and the judge is updated with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on accumulated image-judgment records and evaluated before reuse. We also introduce ImageReq-Bench, a benchmark of 961 requests built around hard cases in real-world image-generation requests, each paired with an evaluation checklist. ImageRSI achieves a Checklist Strict Accuracy (CSA) of 52.6% on MindBench (with Nano Banana Pro as the generator) and 65.66% on ImageReq-Bench, and we compare system outcomes across judge-training configurations.

</details>


### [6] [Guided Zero-Shot Anomaly Explanation for Anonymized Crowd Video Monitoring: Context, Categorization, and Spatial Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32073)
*Mulugeta Weldezgina Asres,Lei Jiao,Christian Walter Omlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出XAVAD，一个面向匿名化视频异常检测的统一描述性可解释框架，融合零样本匿名化、异常类型分类、视觉定位与场景上下文描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习VAD虽进展显著，但可解释性仍不足；且在隐私保护（匿名化）VAD系统中，异常可解释性研究严重缺乏，而透明与信任对监控类系统落地至关重要。

Method: 采用多模态CLIP视频嵌入，结合改进的LLM辅助提示与pool-scoring机制进行异常类型识别；在Grad-ECLIP基础上引入提示引导的patch autofocus机制增强空间异常热图定位并提升计算效率；用VLM与精心设计的提示工程生成异常场景上下文与推理描述；并集成灵活零样本全身匿名化。

Result: 在多个公开CCTV及视频数据集上评估精度与计算开销，XAVAD相较SOTA基准取得显著提升，并给出XAI中隐私保护权衡的洞见；消融实验与多配置/建模替代方案对比验证了各模块有效性。

Conclusion: XAVAD实现了隐私保护与可解释VAD的统一，兼顾匿名化、类型识别、定位与语言化解释，为可信监控视觉系统提供可行路径。

Abstract: Despite the promising development of deep learning for video anomaly detection (VAD), explainability (XAI) of these models remains a challenge. Explainable and privacy-protecting VAD builds transparency and trust that facilitates adoption of computer vision systems, including safety and security monitoring. However, anomaly explainability in privacy-preserving VAD systems is underexplored. In this study, we present the XAVAD approach, a unified descriptive anomaly XAI for an anonymized VAD, that integrates flexible zero-shot anonymization, anomaly type categorization, visual localization, and scene context description. We provide empirical and qualitative assessments of multifaceted explanations integrated with full-body visual anonymization for privacy protection. We adopt a multimodal CLIP embedding for videos, using improved LLM-assisted prompting and pool-scoring mechanisms for anomaly-type identification. We enhance Grad-ECLIP for spatial anomaly heatmap localization, leveraging computation and accuracy through our novel prompt-guided patching autofocus mechanism. We employ VLMs to generate context and reasoning descriptions of detected anomalies through curated prompt engineering. We have evaluated the accuracy and computational cost of XAVAD on several publicly available CCTV and video datasets. Our experiments demonstrate substantial gains of our XAVAD over state-of-the-art benchmark approaches and provide insights into privacy protection trade-offs in XAI for VAD. We further present ablation studies and assessments across diverse configurations and modeling alternatives to demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed methods. Code: https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD}{https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD .

</details>


### [7] [NUViT: Vision Transformers for Fourier Space Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32041)
*Jeffrey Gu,Robert C. Heeter,Ellen D. Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NUViT，通过重新设计图像分块、数据表示与位置编码，使ViT适配傅里叶空间数据，并在多个频域任务上优于标准架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT在自然图像上表现优异，但其在笛卡尔网格上的方形patch tokenization无法适配傅里叶空间数据的频率与相位结构，限制了ViT向医学成像、显微成像等频域模态的迁移。

Method: 提出Non-Uniform ViT（NUViT），一种简单、通用的视觉Transformer：重新设计image tokenization、数据表示和positional encoding，以显式尊重傅里叶数据的频率—相位结构（即以非均匀方式对频域成分进行分块与编码）。

Result: 在傅里叶域图像分类、二维晶格识别、MRI标注、冷冻电镜蛋白异质性分析等任务上，NUViT一致地比标准架构更好地捕捉傅里叶数据结构。

Conclusion: 针对频域数据的结构特性改造ViT的tokenization与编码方式是有效且通用的，可作为傅里叶空间视觉任务的通用骨干。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated strong performance in many computer vision tasks but do not naturally extend beyond the natural image domain. One important example is Fourier-space data, which is used to represent a rich and diverse set of modalities, including medical imaging and microscopy. Standard ViT tokenization partitions an image into square patches on a Cartesian grid, which poorly fit the frequency and phase structure of Fourier-space data. We introduce Non-Uniform ViT, a simple, general-purpose vision transformer that redesigns image tokenization, data representation, and positional encoding to respect this structure. Experiments on Fourier-domain image classification, 2D crystal lattice identification, MRI annotation, and cryo-EM protein heterogeneity analysis show that NUViT consistently captures the structure of Fourier data better than standard architectures.

</details>


### [8] [Interpreting Physics in Video Encoders using Multi-Object Interaction Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31983)
*Ayushi Mehrotra,Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在冻结的自监督视频编码器（V-JEPA2、VideoMAE）潜空间中探测双体场景下的关系性物理属性（弹性/非弹性碰撞、动量守恒违背、动能、摩擦系数），并检验其跨环境迁移性，发现能力有限且不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码器日益被用作物理AI的世界模型，智能体在潜空间中滚动推演来规划，因此潜空间所承载的物理决定了可规划的范围。而既有可解释性工作多探测单物体量，诸如动量传递、恢复系数、摩擦等"关系性"物理量定义于物体之间，尚未被考察。

Method: 在两个冻结的自监督视频编码器（V-JEPA2与VideoMAE）上，于双体交互设定中探测关系性物理属性，并通过改变环境（V-JEPA2另测试其自带预测器）检验这些读出是否可迁移。

Result: 结果好坏参半：两个编码器都能可靠区分弹性与非弹性碰撞；V-JEPA2的预测器相对更难预测产生动能的碰撞（相比损失动能的碰撞）；动量违背检测仅在两个编码器之一中出现；动能读出仅在V-JEPA2上跨环境保持、VideoMAE不保持；两者均未形成可跨几何变化的摩擦系数表征。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型的潜空间对关系性物理的编码是部分且脆弱的，跨环境泛化不一致，尚不足以支撑依赖动量、恢复系数与摩擦等交互量的规划，提示需要针对关系性物理改进表征学习与评测。

Abstract: Video encoder models are increasingly used as world models for physical AI, where a policy plans by rolling out the latent, so what physics the latent holds bounds what the agent can plan over. Moreover, existing interpretability work probes quantities belonging to a single object; the quantities that make physics relational, such as momentum transfer, restitution, and friction, are defined between bodies and have not been examined. We probe relational physics attributes in two frozen self-supervised video encoders (V-JEPA2 and VideoMAE) under two-body settings and test if they transfer across environments. Our early findings are mixed. Both encoders separate elastic from inelastic collisions reliably. V-JEPA2's own predictor is less likely to predict a collision that creates kinetic energy compared to one that loses it. Momentum-violation detection appears in only one of the two encoders, a kinetic-energy readout survives a change of environment for V-JEPA2 but not for VideoMAE, and neither model holds a friction coefficient that survives a change of geometry.

</details>


### [9] [Spectra: Multi-Scale Implicit Decomposition for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31979)
*Dhananjaya Jayasundara,Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spectra，利用隐式神经表示（INR）揭示图像的重建动态，并与语义视觉特征双向融合，实现更具泛化性的深度伪造检测，并可扩展至人脸活体攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器多依赖RGB外观、频域伪影或其组合，这些线索常与特定生成器绑定，在未见过的操纵手法、压缩级别和数据集偏移下泛化性差，且随生成模型演进易失效。因此需要从"图像如何被重建"这一互补视角获取更本质的真伪差异。

Method: 为每张图像拟合一个图像特定的INR，提取多层级重建热图作为伪影感知线索；同时提取语义视觉表示，并通过双向融合机制让语义与重建伪影直接交互，联合建模。

Result: 在多个深度伪造基准上实验，鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化能力均有提升，稳定优于仅基于RGB外观或语义表示的检测器；该表示还可迁移至人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）。

Conclusion: INR重建动态提供了对特定生成流程依赖更低的取证线索，可作为面向人脸攻击检测的统一框架。

Abstract: Deepfakes increasingly undermine trust in digital media, yet existing detectors often struggle to generalize across unseen manipulation techniques, compression levels, and dataset shifts. Most approaches rely on RGB appearance, frequency-domain artifacts, or their combination, learning cues present in the final synthesized image. While effective in-distribution, such cues are frequently generator-specific and can diminish as synthesis models evolve. We argue that a complementary perspective can be obtained through implicit neural representations (INRs), which expose how an image is reconstructed rather than merely how it appears. By fitting an INR to an image and analyzing its internal activations, one can reveal reconstruction dynamics that are difficult to observe in the image domain alone and may capture more intrinsic differences between genuine and synthetic content. To this end, we propose Spectra, a deepfake detection framework that combines semantic visual representations with artifact-aware cues derived from INR reconstruction. Spectra extracts multi-level reconstruction heatmaps from image-specific INRs and jointly models them with semantic features through a bidirectional fusion mechanism. This enables direct interaction between image semantics and reconstruction artifacts, allowing the model to identify forgery cues that are less dependent on specific generation pipelines. Extensive experiments on multiple deepfake benchmarks demonstrate improved robustness and cross-dataset generalization, consistently outperforming detectors based solely on RGB appearance or semantic representations. Furthermore, we show that the proposed representation extends beyond deepfake detection to face presentation attack detection, highlighting its potential as a unified framework for facial attack detection.

</details>


### [10] [PixGR: Pixel-Space Generative Decoding for Fast Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31908)
*Ranran Huang,Wenjing Bian,Weixun Luo,Ye Mao,Krystian Mikolajczyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixGR将轻量级像素空间生成建模集成到前馈3D Gaussian重建中，用像素空间flow matching对含噪目标视图做生成式解码，产生共享3D Gaussian表示，规避VAE潜在压缩与两阶段误差传播，在稀疏视图下优于回归方法并保持亚秒级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D高斯重建在稀疏视图（视角间距大、外推）下存在场景不确定性建模不足的问题；而既有两阶段生成流水线依赖VAE潜在压缩，会带来误差传播与效率损失。

Method: 上下文观测仅编码一次为场景潜在表示，作为条件驱动从含噪目标视图的生成式解码，输出共享3D高斯表示；以像素空间flow matching建模该过程，通过可微渲染实现像素级生成监督下的端到端3D重建优化；为保持前馈效率，缓存场景表示并用轻量DiT解码器迭代推理。

Result: 在挑战性稀疏视图设置（尤其观测间距大与视角外推）下优于回归式方法；保持亚秒级推理速度，并与参数量显著更大的预训练生成模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 像素空间生成监督结合前馈高斯重建是建模场景不确定性的高效路径，可在不引入VAE与两阶段误差传播的前提下兼顾重建质量与推理效率。

Abstract: We present PixGR, an efficient framework that integrates lightweight pixel-space generative modeling into feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction to model scene uncertainty. Context observations are encoded once into a scene latent representation, which conditions generative decoding from noisy target views to produce a shared 3D Gaussian representation. We formulate this process using pixel-space flow matching, enabling end-to-end optimization of 3D reconstruction with direct pixel-space generative supervision through differentiable rendering. This avoids VAE-based latent compression and error propagation inherent in previous two-stage pipelines. To preserve feed-forward efficiency, PixGR caches the scene representation and performs iterative inference using a lightweight DiT decoder. Experiments demonstrate that PixGR outperforms regression-based approaches in challenging sparse-view settings, particularly with widely spaced observations and view extrapolation. Meanwhile, it maintains sub-second inference and remains competitive with substantially heavier pretrained generative models. Our project page is available at https://ranrhuang.github.io/pixgr/.

</details>


### [11] [StateBridge: Phase- and Track-Grounded Reasoning for Synthetic-to-Real Traffic Video Captioning and VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31898)
*Blessing Agyei Kyem,Richard Dzinyela,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Eugene Denteh,Andrews Danyo,Solomon Owusu-Ansah,Dickson Gyimah Asiedu,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StateBridge交通视频-语言框架，仅在合成数据上训练，通过分阶段片段构建、行人/车辆轨迹摘要与结构化提示，结合事实锁定式描述融合与证据多样化VQA共识，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2上取得官方S2分数55.4679。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通视频安全分析需要理解行人与车辆的交互，但"合成到真实"（synthetic-to-real）设定要求模型仅用仿真场景训练并泛化到真实交通场景，现有方法易依赖表观特征而非稳定的交通证据，泛化困难。

Method: 1) 分阶段（phase-aware）视频片段构建；2) 行人、车辆轨迹摘要；3) 结构化提示将视觉-语言模型锚定于稳定交通证据；4) 在合成描述生成与视觉问答样本上微调公开VLM；5) 事实锁定式描述融合（fact-locked caption fusion）与证据多样化VQA共识（evidence-diverse VQA consensus）整合输出。

Result: 在仅使用合成训练数据的条件下，于AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2获得官方S2分数55.4679。

Conclusion: 实验表明结构化证据迁移对交通安全描述生成与视觉问答任务有效，为合成到真实的交通视频-语言理解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Traffic safety videos provide rich evidence for understanding interactions between pedestrians and vehicles, but synthetic-to-real evaluation makes this setting challenging because models must learn from simulated scenes and generalize to real-world traffic. In this work, we present StateBridge, a traffic video-language framework designed to operate under this synthetic-only training protocol. Our approach uses phase-aware clip construction, pedestrian and vehicle track summaries, and structured prompts to ground vision-language models in stable traffic evidence rather than appearance alone. We fine-tune public vision-language models on synthetic captioning and visual question answering examples, then combine their outputs through fact-locked caption fusion and evidence-diverse VQA consensus to improve consistency across actor roles, behavioral phases, and question types. StateBridge achieves an official S2 score of 55.4679 on AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2 using only synthetic training data. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structured evidence transfer for traffic safety captioning and visual question answering.

</details>


### [12] [Reuse, Don't Retrain: Efficient, Modality-Versatile Geospatial Foundation Models through Feature Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32566)
*Mario Hamon,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究如何以特征蒸馏高效复用已有地理空间基础模型（GeoFM）表征，并进一步提出非配对多专家蒸馏方法TEDI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeoFM预训练成本高昂，而各模型族在骨干尺度与传感模态上重复预训练，存在算力浪费；需要更高效的既有表征复用途径。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1) 通过patch级特征匹配，将冻结的CROMA与TerraMind教师蒸馏到更小的ViT中；(2) 提出TEDI，用独立patch embedding将非配对的多光谱（TerraMind）与高分辨率RGB（DINOv3）输入路由至共享Transformer主干，无需共配准数据。

Result: 在九个PANGAEA任务上，蒸馏版TerraMind Tiny/Small/Base分别比同尺度官方模型高2.76/3.02/1.87 mean IoU，且均达到或超过更大一档的官方模型；蒸馏CROMA-Base提升2.05点；TerraMind-Base仅需43 A6000 GPU小时，约为独立预训练成本的0.5%。TEDI-Large取得单模型最高62.51 mIoU，总体优于两位教师并保留各自模态优势；TEDI-Base以少72%参数达到61.75 mIoU。

Conclusion: 特征蒸馏可显著改善GeoFM的精度—算力前沿，并能将互补专家整合为单一通用骨干，是一种低成本、可扩展的复用范式。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) are costly to pretrain, yet model families often repeat this process across backbone scales and sensing modalities. We investigate feature distillation as a more efficient way to reuse existing representations. First, we distill frozen CROMA and TerraMind teachers into smaller Vision Transformers through patch-level feature matching. Across nine PANGAEA tasks, distilled TerraMind Tiny, Small, and Base outperform their scale-matched released counterparts by 2.76, 3.02, and 1.87 mean IoU points, respectively; each also matches or exceeds the next-larger released model. Distilled CROMA-Base gains 2.05 points. TerraMind-Base required only 43 A6000 GPU-hours, approximately 0.5\% of the reported cost of independent pretraining. Second, we introduce TEDI, an unpaired multi-expert approach combining TerraMind for multispectral imagery and DINOv3 for high-resolution RGB. Separate patch embeddings route unpaired inputs into a shared Transformer trunk, removing the need for co-registered data. TEDI-Large achieves the highest single-model mean in our PANGAEA-9 comparison (62.51 mIoU), outperforming both teachers overall while retaining their modality-specific strengths. TEDI-Base reaches 61.75 mIoU with 72\% fewer parameters. These results show that distillation improves the GeoFM accuracy-compute frontier while consolidating complementary experts into a single versatile backbone.

</details>


### [13] [Efficient Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection via Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32486)
*Khaled Waleed Dawoud,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAAF框架，通过双信号自适应采样、阈值门控级联架构与信号感知加权融合，实现高效零样本视频异常检测，在多个基准上达到免训练零样本最优并显著提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型用于视频异常检测受限于其语义推理所需算力与真实视频流规模不匹配。目标是在无训练数据条件下兼顾检测覆盖度与计算效率。

Method: 三点创新：(1) 语义嵌入空间中的双信号自适应采样器——delta信号用帧间余弦距离捕捉突变事件，drift信号用相对滚动时序上下文的偏差检测静态与渐变异常，二者OR门控并结合自适应EMA阈值；(2) 阈值门控级联架构，轻量帧级打分先行，仅在置信度足够时触发昂贵的视频级时序分析；(3) 信号感知加权融合，依据触发帧选择的信号动态调整分数组合权重。

Result: 在四个基准上以更低成本取得有竞争力精度。免训练零样本方法中于XD-Violence(92.07%)、MSAD(86.70%)、ShanghaiTech(80.54%) AUC-ROC刷新SOTA，其中XD-Violence与MSAD亦超过监督基线。吞吐达44.95/48.61/41.06 FPS（XD-Violence/UCF-Crime/MSAD），较此前最佳零样本方法分别提升2.43×、2.63×、2.22×。

Conclusion: SAAF证明通过信号驱动的自适应采样与级联门控，可在零样本免训练条件下同时获得高精度与高吞吐，为VLM视频异常检测的实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models for video anomaly detection confronts a fundamental constraint. Their semantic reasoning demands computational budgets incompatible with the scale of real-world video streams. We present Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF), a framework that achieves efficient zero-shot anomaly detection through three key innovations. First, we introduce a dual-signal adaptive sampler operating in semantic embedding space: a delta signal captures sudden events via frame-to-frame cosine distance, while a drift signal detects static and gradual anomalies through deviation from rolling temporal context. Their OR-gated combination with adaptive EMA-based thresholding provides comprehensive anomaly coverage without training data. Second, we propose a threshold-gated cascaded architecture where lightweight frame-level scoring precedes expensive video-level temporal analysis, invoking it only when confidence warrants additional context. Third, we design a signal-aware weighted fusion mechanism that dynamically adapts score combination based on which signal triggered frame selection. Across four benchmarks, SAAF delivers competitive accuracy at substantially lower cost. Among training-free zero-shot methods, SAAF sets a new state of the art on XD-Violence (92.07%), MSAD (86.70%), and ShanghaiTech (80.54%) AUC-ROC, with XD-Violence and MSAD also surpassing supervised baselines. SAAF sustains 44.95, 48.61, and 41.06 FPS on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and MSAD, a 2.43$\times$, 2.63$\times$, and 2.22$\times$ throughput improvement over the prior best zero-shot method. Code: https://github.com/kdawoud91/SAAF

</details>


### [14] [TIE: Training-free CLIP Embedding Guidance for Compositional Text-to-Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32407)
*Sujung Hong,Tae Eun Choi,Youngjun Jun,Chanyong Yoon,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练、推理时的文本嵌入更新方法TIE，通过熵感知奇异值放大缓解概念丢失、基于CLIP注意力分数的插值-外推绑定解决概念混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I扩散模型仍难以满足提示条件，出现属性错配与物体缺失，可归为概念丢失（物体/属性缺失）与概念混淆（属性绑定到错误物体或多物体融合）。作者认为根源在于CLIP文本编码器捕捉细粒度语义能力有限，而现有方法在多物体、多属性提示下仍无法解决这两类问题。

Method: 进行嵌入层面的分析，得到两点发现：(1) 靠后提及的概念具有更高嵌入熵，表示不够集中，更常与概念丢失相关；(2) 第一个CLIP注意力层捕捉每个物体与其属性之间的绑定强度。据此设计TIE：通过一次文本嵌入更新提升语义对齐——用熵感知的奇异值放大处理概念丢失，用基于CLIP注意力分数的插值-外推绑定处理概念混淆，全程免训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，TIE在多概念场景下提升了语义保真度，且采样开销极小。

Conclusion: 嵌入层面的熵与注意力绑定分析可有效指导文本嵌入修正；单次嵌入更新的免训练方法即可同时缓解概念丢失与概念混淆，并在多种基于CLIP的T2I模型中适用，兼顾效果与效率。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have advanced notably, but still fail to satisfy prompt conditions, resulting in attribute mismatches and missing objects. These errors normally fall into two categories: concept loss, when an object or attribute (i.e., concept) goes missing, and concept confusion, when an attribute is assigned to the wrong object or multiple objects blend together. Recent studies suggest that these challenges are caused by the limited capacity of CLIP text encoder to capture fine-grained semantic details. Although several methods have been proposed, concept loss and concept confusion persist in recent T2I models when handling multi-object, multi-attribute prompts. In this paper, we conduct an embedding-level analysis to develop a training-free, inference-time solution for concept loss and concept confusion across CLIP-based T2I models. We find that (1) concepts mentioned later exhibit higher embedding entropy, reflecting less concentrated representations that are more often associated with concept loss, and (2) the first CLIP attention layer captures the strength of binding between each object and its attribute. Guided by our findings, we introduce TIE, a method that improves semantic alignment through a single text-embedding update. TIE addresses concept loss via entropy-aware singular value amplification and resolves concept confusion through interpolation-extrapolation binding based on CLIP attention scores, all in a training-free manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIE enhances semantic fidelity in multi-concept scenarios with minimal sampling overhead. The project page is available at https://hong-sujung.github.io/TIE/.

</details>


### [15] [Past as State, Present as Attention: Persistent-State Blockwise Flow Matching for Long-Horizon Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32373)
*Yi Liu,Xiangyue Zhang,Jianfang Li,Jia Ma,Yisheng He,Jianqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PASPA提出"过去为状态、当前为注意力"的原则，用块间持久状态压缩历史风格、块内双向自注意力建模当前局部动态，在块状流匹配框架下实现长时程共语动作生成，取得SOTA的FGD并保持长序列风格一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程共语动作生成需兼顾时间连贯性与风格一致性。现有方法按段生成：要么仅以近期少量帧为种子条件，导致风格逐渐漂移；要么保留不断增长的显式历史，维护代价高且在小数据下难以学习。作者将问题归因于"过去—现在"的不对称性：过去动作承载缓慢变化的风格，应被紧凑总结；当前片段包含快速变化的局部动态，需细粒度建模。

Method: 提出PASPA：以块间持久状态（inter-block persistent state）汇总历史，以块内双向自注意力（intra-block bidirectional self-attention）建模当前段。实现上采用块状流匹配（blockwise flow matching）框架，配合多块监督以适配可变长度历史、prefill-decode状态缓存实现高效自回归展开，以及混合式无分类器引导（hybrid CFG）增强历史与语音条件。

Result: 在多个公开基准上实验显示，PASPA达到SOTA的FGD指标，并在长序列生成中保持动作风格一致。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分历史的"状态化"与当前段的"注意力化"，PASPA以低维护成本实现了长时程共语动作的连贯生成与风格保持，为长序列条件生成提供了可推广的建模范式。

Abstract: Co-speech motion generation remains challenging over long horizons due to the need for temporal coherence and consistent style. Existing methods typically generate long motions segment by segment, either conditioning on a short seed of recent frames, which gradually drifts, or retaining ever-longer explicit histories, which are costly to maintain and hard to learn from limited data. We trace these limitations to a key asymmetry between past and present: past motion carries slowly varying style that should be compactly summarized, whereas the current segment exhibits fast-changing local dynamics that require fine-grained modeling. This asymmetry motivates a simple Past-As-State, Present-as-Attention principle for long-horizon co-speech motion generation. Following this principle, we propose PASPA, which summarizes past history with an inter-block persistent state and models the current segment with intra-block bidirectional self-attention. We instantiate PASPA within a blockwise flow matching framework, with multi-block supervision for effective training over variable histories, prefill-decode state caching for efficient rollout, and hybrid classifier-free guidance for enhanced history and speech conditioning. Extensive public-benchmark experiments show that PASPA achieves state-of-the-art FGD performance and preserves motion style across long-sequence generation.

</details>


### [16] [ReLEAP: Upcycling Vision Datasets through Cross-Task Unification and Semi-Supervised Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32289)
*Julia Simon,Daniel Steininger,Andreas Trondl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReLEAP：一个可扩展的 Python 框架，通过数据源抽象、跨任务标注复用与标签到共享层级分类体系的映射来统一异构图像数据集；在精准农业中整合 15 个数据集，并利用统一数据与基础模型进行无监督/弱监督/半监督的标注"上循环"（annotation upcycling）实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉任务需要大量标注数据，而现有数据集在标注格式、标签定义与目标任务上存在差异，阻碍数据复用与整合，尤其在数据稀缺、异质的领域。

Method: 构建 ReLEAP 框架：数据源抽象、标注在多个学习任务间的系统性复用、标签映射到共享层级分类体系。在精准农业上整合 15 个覆盖多物种与多标注类型的数据集，并基于统一数据训练领域专用模型与基础模型，在无监督、弱监督、半监督设置下自动生成高层级标注；辅以跨数据集与微调实验。

Result: 统一的数据访问与标注上循环显著扩大了可复用训练数据与标注池，同时提升了数据透明度；实验还揭示了数据集特性对自动标注效果的影响。

Conclusion: ReLEAP 支持更可持续、资源高效的既有数据集利用；框架与训练模型面向学术用途开源，以促进数据稀缺与异质领域中的可及性与协作研究。

Abstract: Developing robust vision models requires large amounts of annotated data, especially for tasks involving fine-grained class distinctions under variable environmental conditions. Even in domains with numerous existing datasets, differences in annotation formats, label definitions and targeted learning tasks hinder data reuse and integration. To address this challenge, we introduce ReLEAP, a scalable Python framework for unifying heterogeneous image datasets through data-source abstraction, systematic reuse of annotations across learning tasks, and mapping of labels to a shared hierarchical taxonomy. We demonstrate its applicability in precision agriculture by integrating fifteen diverse datasets covering a broad range of plant species and annotation types. Beyond unification, ReLEAP enables systematic recycling of existing data and provides a robust foundation for learning-based annotation upcycling. Leveraging this foundation, we conduct extensive cross-dataset and fine-tuning experiments to investigate how domain-specific models trained on unified data and foundation models can be used to automatically generate higher-level annotations under unsupervised, weakly supervised and semi-supervised settings, and how dataset characteristics influence their effectiveness. Our results show that unified data access and annotation upcycling substantially expand the pool of reusable training data and annotations while improving data transparency, enabling more sustainable and resource-efficient use of existing datasets. We release the framework and trained models for academic use to support accessible and collaborative research, particularly in data-scarce and heterogeneous domains.

</details>


### [17] [RootDown: Deploying and Benchmarking Models for Beet-Root Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32275)
*Gerardus Croonen,Andreas Trondl,Julia Simon,Daniel Steininger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套端到端图像流水线，用于在装载传送带上检测部分遮挡的甜菜，作为收获/储藏后自动质量检测的第一步：构建专用基准数据集，并提出基于 SAM 3 基础模型的自动标注策略生成边界框；对比人工标注与自动标注训练模型，并评测多种推理配置（含边缘设备）在精度、推理时间与功耗上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甜菜收获与储藏后的质量检测对保持含糖量和加工效率至关重要；在甜菜转运至田间堆垛或卡车的装载传送带上可实现对单个甜菜的自动视觉检测。但甜菜数量大、相互部分重叠与遮挡，使标注与检测均十分困难，且人工标注成本高。

Method: 提出端到端图像检测流水线用于装载场景下部分遮挡甜菜检测；构建专用基准数据集；提出利用 SAM 3 基础模型自动生成边界框的自动标注策略；分别在人工标注与自动生成标签上训练模型并对比；针对实际部署约束，基准测试多种推理配置，包括在边缘设备（Raspberry Pi 5 + Hailo 加速器）上的执行，评估精度、推理时间与功耗。

Result: 尽管存在显著遮挡与固有的标注歧义，基于基础模型的标签可替代相当比例的人工标注，以适度精度损失换取数据规模与标注工作量的平衡；配备 Hailo 加速器的 Raspberry Pi 5 可实现高性能、低功耗推理，适用于移动户外部署。

Conclusion: 基础模型自动标注是缓解遮挡场景标注瓶颈的可行途径，权衡为"以少量精度换数据量"；边缘设备方案在精度、时延与功耗上满足实际装载现场部署需求，为甜菜自动质量检测奠定基础。

Abstract: Quality inspection of sugar beets after harvest and storage is critical to preserve sugar content and ensure optimal processing efficiency. The transfer of harvested beets to field clamps or trucks provides an opportunity to visually inspect individual beets automatically on loader conveyors. However, this is a challenging task as the large number of beets causes partial overlap and occlusion, complicating both annotation and detection. In this work, we propose an end-to-end image-based pipeline for detecting partially occluded sugar beets in loading scenarios as a first step toward automated quality inspection. We introduce a dedicated benchmark and present an automatic annotation strategy that leverages the SAM 3 foundation model to generate bounding boxes. We compare models trained on manually annotated and automatically generated labels. Additionally, we investigate deployment considerations by benchmarking multiple inference configurations, including execution on an edge device, under realistic operational constraints regarding precision, inference time and power consumption. Our results show that, despite substantial occlusion and inherent annotation ambiguity, foundation-model-based labels can substitute for a substantial share of manual annotation, trading labeling effort for data volume at a modest accuracy cost. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a Raspberry Pi 5 equipped with a Hailo accelerator enables high-performance, low-power inference, making it a practical solution for mobile outdoor deployment.

</details>


### [18] [ACWM-RM: A Reference-Free Reward Model from Complementary Mismatch Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32222)
*Shibo Hong,Siyu Ren,FengJiao Chen,Zhongyuan Peng,Wei Shi,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVM-Eval基准(67.2k对)揭示IDM奖励在生成视频上评估动作跟随保真度(AF)的不可靠性，并提出ACWM-RM——一个无需真值视频即可联合评估视觉质量(VQ)与AF的奖励模型，在RL实验中优于现有奖励基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习优化动作条件世界模型(ACWM)需要能同时对VQ和AF进行可靠排序的奖励信号。现有奖励只覆盖单一维度：基于度量的奖励评估VQ但依赖真值视频且不直接评估AF；IDM奖励无参考评估AF但不保证VQ，且IDM通常在真实视频上训练，对含视觉伪影和物理不一致的生成视频奖励不可靠。

Method: (1) 构建Action and Video Mismatch Benchmark (AVM-Eval)，含67.2k对，评估IDM在真实与生成视频上的AF评估可靠性；(2) 提出ACWM-RM奖励模型，在奖励计算时无需真值视频即可联合评估VQ与AF；(3) 构建AVM-Pref偏好数据集(506.3k对)，动作不匹配对监督AF，视频不匹配对监督VQ并引入生成视频；(4) 先在真实视频上预训练动作条件预测器学习前向动力学，再与patch加权分数头在AVM-Pref上联合训练，将动力学迁移至生成视频的AF评估。

Result: AVM-Eval评估显示IDM在生成视频上可靠性有限；下游RL实验表明ACWM-RM在VQ和AF两项标准上均优于奖励基线。

Conclusion: 联合建模VQ与AF且无需真值参考的奖励模型ACWM-RM可为ACWM的强化学习优化提供更可靠的奖励信号，弥补现有度量奖励与IDM奖励的单维局限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has gained attention for improving action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), as it enables reward-driven optimization of visual quality (VQ) and action-following fidelity (AF) beyond flow-matching optimization. Realizing this potential requires reward signals that reliably rank videos by both VQ and AF. However, existing rewards capture only one dimension: metric-based rewards assess VQ but require ground-truth videos and do not directly assess AF, while inverse dynamics model (IDM) rewards assess AF without references but do not guarantee VQ. Moreover, IDMs, typically trained on real videos, may provide unreliable rewards for generated videos containing visual artifacts and physical inconsistencies. In this work, we introduce the Action and Video Mismatch Benchmark (AVM-Eval), comprising 67.2k pairs that evaluate how reliably IDMs assess AF on real and generated videos. We then propose ACWM-RM, a reward model that jointly assesses VQ and AF without ground-truth videos during reward computation. To train it, we first construct the Action and Video Mismatch Preference Dataset (AVM-Pref) with 506.3k pairs providing complementary supervision: action-mismatch pairs supervise AF, while video-mismatch pairs supervise VQ and expose the model to generated videos. We then pretrain an action-conditioned predictor on real videos to learn forward dynamics before jointly training it with a patch-weighted score head on AVM-Pref to transfer these dynamics to AF assessment on generated videos. Evaluation on AVM-Eval reveals the limited reliability of IDMs on generated videos, while downstream RL experiments show that ACWM-RM outperforms reward baselines in both criteria.

</details>


### [19] [Benchmarking Data-Free Continual Model Merging for Whole-Slide Image Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32207)
*Vu Minh Tran,Doanh C. Bui,Maï K. Nguyen,Khang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个无数据持续模型合并的端到端基准，系统比较六种合并策略在全切片图像生存分析中的表现，发现没有单一策略能在所有指标上占优，且删失感知指标会显著改变方法排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI生存分析的持续学习需要在不重访旧队列的前提下整合新知识；模型合并提供无数据方案，但不同合并策略在该任务上的相对表现缺乏系统理解。

Method: 建立统一基准流程，涵盖任务特定微调、顺序合并与推理，覆盖TASK-IL与CLASS-IL两种设定；在四个TCGA癌症队列、三种任务顺序下，用C-index、IPCW C-index、Forgetting和Backward Transfer评估六种代表性合并策略。

Result: 没有任何合并策略在全部评价标准上持续占优；采用删失感知（IPCW）评估会显著改变各方法的相对排名。

Conclusion: 该基准为面向WSI生存分析开发与评测更有效的持续模型合并方法提供了统一基础，并提示评估指标选择对结论具有实质影响。

Abstract: Continual survival analysis on whole-slide images (WSIs) requires models to integrate knowledge from sequentially arriving cancer cohorts while preserving previously learned information. Model merging offers a data-free alternative by combining independently fine-tuned task experts without retaining previous training data. However, the relative behavior of different merging strategies for WSI-based survival prediction remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we establish an end-to-end benchmark for data-free continual model merging, covering task-specific fine-tuning, sequential merging, and inference under both TASK-IL and CLASS-IL settings. We systematically evaluate six representative merging strategies across four TCGA cancer cohorts and three task orders using C-index, IPCW C-index, Forgetting, and Backward Transfer under a controlled protocol. Results show that no merging strategy consistently dominates across all evaluation criteria, while censoring-aware evaluation can substantially alter the relative ranking of the compared methods. This benchmark provides a unified foundation for developing and evaluating more effective continual model merging methods for WSI-based survival analysis.

</details>


### [20] [Real-Time Localisation of Dorsal Anatomical Regions on Dairy Cows in Commercial Rotary Parlours from RGB-D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32174)
*Vineet Prasad,Shiron Melvin,Sheikh Azid,Hai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单次前向的RGB-D感知前端，在商业转盘挤奶厅中同时定位目标奶牛、10个骨性关键点与4个背部解剖区域（鬐甲、背、臀、尾根），PCK@10达0.905、区域IoU 0.70–0.91，单帧26.4 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奶牛自动化监测多依赖人工指定身体部位（如测温用臀部、测姿态用背部），且已有方法要么整牛打分、要么在受控通道内检测稀疏关键点，均无法在拥挤的商业转盘平台上输出测温等任务所需的命名区域掩膜。

Method: 双编码器（颜色与深度）晚期融合，共享一个解码器与三个头，在整幅RGB-D帧上单次前向输出目标牛、10个关键点与4个命名背侧区域；自建转盘厅解剖区域数据集（538帧标注、牛只不重叠的测试集）并用稠密区域监督训练。

Result: 关键点PCK@10 0.905，区域IoU 0.70–0.91，T4 GPU上端到端26.4 ms/帧，快于11 fps相机；去掉区域头使PCK@10下降0.15，说明稠密区域监督是关键点精度的来源；双头一致性可在无标签下标记不可靠帧；在自有设备上仅用10帧标注即迁移（PCK@10 0.84），并将区域经深度投影到辐射热像仪，实现逐区域、逐牛的重复性温度测量。

Conclusion: 将关键点与命名区域联合稠密预测、并以一致性做无监督质量筛选，可支撑商业转盘挤奶厅的实时、可迁移区域级表型测量；区域作为中间表征兼具精度贡献与下游测温价值。

Abstract: Automated monitoring of dairy cows increasingly measures one part of the body, such as the rump for temperature or the back for posture, yet the region itself is usually placed by hand. Existing systems either score the cow as a whole or detect sparse keypoints, often in controlled walkways, and neither yields the named region masks that such measurements need on a crowded commercial rotary platform. We present a perception front end that finds the target cow, ten bony landmarks and four named dorsal regions (withers, back, rump and tail-head) in a single forward pass over a whole RGB-D frame from a commercial rotary parlour. Two encoders, one for colour and one for depth, are fused late and share one decoder with three heads. On an anatomical region dataset that we built on public rotary-parlour RGB-D images (538 annotated frames, cow-disjoint test set), the network reaches PCK@10 0.905 for landmarks and region IoU 0.70 to 0.91, and runs end to end in 26.4 ms per frame on a T4 GPU, against an 11 fps camera. Dense region supervision is what makes the landmarks accurate: removing the region head costs 0.15 PCK@10. Agreement between the two heads flags unreliable frames without labels. On our own rig at the largest dairy farm in Western Australia, the model transfers with ten labelled frames (PCK@10 0.84), and its regions are carried through depth into a radiometric thermal camera, giving one repeatable temperature per region and cow.

</details>


### [21] [Forging Emotions: a deep learning experiment on emotions and art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32163)
*Amalia Foka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过"Forging Emotions"实验探索用GAN进行视觉情感生成，指出当前科学界构建情感图像数据集的方法不足，主张借助心理学的情感联想网络，并需理解GAN及组合式GAN的结构以生成能传达或触发情感的新颖构图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算方法多被应用于交互媒体艺术，但现有研究集中于情感检测（affect detection），而忽视情感生成（affect generation）；生成式方法（尤其GAN）为情感生成提供了新的计算创造性可能。

Method: 开展名为"Forging Emotions"的实验，考察视觉情感数据集的使用以及GAN的工作过程，用于视觉情感生成，即生成能够传达或触发特定情感的图像。

Result: 实验得出两个结论：其一，计算机科学研究者目前用于构建描述情感这类高层概念的图像数据集的方法并不充分；其二，仅依赖基础心理学结论（如明亮或暗淡的色彩）来生成视觉情感似乎不足。

Conclusion: 建议依据心理学研究的情感联想网络来构建数据集；同时研究方向应聚焦于理解训练后的GAN与组合式GAN（compositional GANs）的结构，以生成通过画面主题真正新颖地传达或触发情感的构图。

Abstract: Affective computing is an interdisciplinary field that studies computational methods that relate to or influence emotion. These methods have been applied to interactive media artworks, but they have focused on affect detection rather than affect generation. For affect generation, computationally creative methods need to be explored that have recently been driven by the use of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), a deep learning method. The experiment presented in this paper, Forging Emotions, explores the use of visual emotion datasets and the working processes of GANs for visual affect generation, that is, for generating images that can convey or trigger specified emotions. This experiment concludes that the methodology used so far by computer science researchers to build image datasets for describing high-level concepts such as emotions is insufficient and proposes utilizing emotional networks of associations according to psychology research. Forging Emotions also concludes that to generate affect visually, merely corresponding to basic psychology findings, such as bright or dark colours, does not seem adequate. Therefore, research efforts should aim to understand the structure of trained GANs and compositional GANs in order to produce genuinely novel compositions that can convey or trigger emotions through the subject matter of generated images.

</details>


### [22] [Consommé: Conditioned Token Merging for Split Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32794)
*Man Ngo,Jan S. Rellermeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Consommé利用CLS token的双重角色（全局摘要信号+patch保留门控信号），在冻结骨干下实现单步任意预算的token缩减分割推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割推理中传输中间token通信成本高，且为每个端点部署微调骨干代价昂贵；在冻结骨干、仅传输小适配器的设定下，现有token缩减方法（如BPM）受限于逐层上限与浅层切分不可用。

Method: 观察到CLS token兼具双重作用：既是接收端分类所需的全局摘要，又是决定保留哪些patch的信号。据此设计信息瓶颈将CLS压缩为紧凑隐表示以替代完整CLS传输，并用其条件化一个可学习的patch门控，通过软分配将patch合并为恰好k个token，一步即达任意预算。

Result: 在DeiT-S/T上，于激进token预算下领先所有方法；在BPM无法工作的浅层切分处仍可运行；在温和预算下以远低于微调方法的部署成本达到其水平。

Conclusion: Consommé通过复用CLS token的双重功能，在冻结骨干设定下突破BPM的逐层预算限制，兼顾通信效率与部署经济性，扩展了分割推理的可用切分范围。

Abstract: Split inference partitions a Vision Transformer between a resource-constrained edge device and a server. However, transmitting intermediate tokens across the communication channel is costly. Deploying fine-tuned backbones to every endpoint becomes expensive as well. We study token reduction for split inference under a frozen backbone, where only a small adapter is shipped. We observe that the CLS token plays a dual role: it acts as the global summary the receiver needs for classification, and it serves as a signal on which patches to keep. We propose Consommé, a method utilizing both characteristics. There, an information bottleneck compresses the CLS token into a compact latent which is transmitted in place of the full CLS token and conditions a learned patch gate merging patches into exactly $k$ tokens via soft assignment. It reaches any budget in a single step, thus escaping the per-layer cap of bipartite matching (BPM). On DeiT-S/T, it leads all methods at aggressive token budgets and remains operable at shallow splits where BPM cannot operate, while matching fine-tuned methods at gentle budgets at a fraction of their deployment cost.

</details>


### [23] [PnP*: A certifiable solver for Perspective-n-Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31797)
*Bang-Shien Chen,Caitrin E. Eaton,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PnP*，首个实用的、可验证全局最优的PnP最大似然估计求解器，兼具高效性与可认证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从3D-2D对应关系估计相机位姿（PnP）是计算机视觉的基础问题。现有方法要么求解最小问题，要么用更简单的目标替代原目标；令人意外的是，针对真实MLE问题尚无可扩展的可认证求解器。

Method: 通过凸松弛求解MLE，并证明在适度噪声下该松弛是精确的（给出稳定性保证）；利用弦稀疏模式与商环约简缩小松弛规模，使问题可处理且可扩展。

Result: 数值实验验证了稳定性与可扩展性，PnP*以超过10 Hz的速度返回可证明正确的相机位姿。

Conclusion: PnP*是首个实用的、能保证高效计算并返回可认证全局最优解的PnP MLE求解器，且首次为PnP的MLE松弛提供稳定性保证。

Abstract: Estimating a camera pose from 3D-2D correspondences - Perspective-n-Point - is one of the most fundamental problems in computer vision. Existing methods either solve a minimal problem or replace the objective with an easier one. Surprisingly, no scalable certifiable solver for the actual Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) exists. We propose PnP*, the first practical solver that guarantees efficient computation and returns a certifiable globally optimal solution. We prove that the underlying convex relaxation is exact under moderate noise, which to our knowledge is the first stability guarantee for any relaxation for the MLE of PnP. Furthermore, we reduce the relaxation by exploiting chordal sparsity pattern and quotient ring reduction such that the relaxation is tractable and scalable. The stability and scalability results are verified numerically, as PnP* returns a provably correct camera pose at over 10 Hz.

</details>


### [24] [AudioLift: Audio-Driven Single Image to Full-Head Gaussian Avatar via Multi-view Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31747)
*Zhuoyuan Jiang,Weijie Lyu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Tiancheng Shen,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AudioLift 提出两阶段框架，以多视角视频为中间表示，无需参数化人脸模型或逐实例优化即可生成音频驱动 3D 说话头像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 说话头像方法依赖 3DMM/FLAME 等参数化人脸模型，其紧凑的运动表示限制了极端表情合成与头饰等配件的建模能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段进行同步多视角视频生成；第二阶段采用对不一致性鲁棒的高斯重建，从单张肖像图像恢复连贯的全头几何。

Result: 实验表明生成的 3D 说话头像能捕捉比现有方法更极端的表情，几何完整性更佳，且在完整 360° 视角范围内具有强多视角一致性。

Conclusion: 以多视角视频作为中间表示、摆脱参数化模型与逐实例优化，是提升表情表现力与全头几何完整性的有效途径。

Abstract: Existing 3D talking avatar methods mainly rely on parametric face models including 3DMM or FLAME, whose compact motion representations limit their ability to synthesize extreme expressions and to model accessories such as headwear. We introduce AudioLift, a two-stage framework that uses generated multi-view videos as the intermediate representation for audio-driven avatar generation without parametric face models or per-instance optimization. Our framework combines a synchronized multi-view video generation stage with an inconsistency-robust Gaussian reconstruction stage to recover coherent full-head geometry from a single portrait image. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AudioLift produces 3D talking avatars capable of capturing more extreme facial expressions than existing methods, with improved geometric completeness and strong multi-view consistency across the full $360^\circ$ viewing range.

</details>


### [25] [Cross-Architectural Feature Fusion for Coronary Artery Stenosis Detection in X-ray Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32730)
*Haobo Zhu,Mojtaba Lashgari,Jason Chai,Giovanni Luigi De Maria,Vicente Grau,Abhirup Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出融合YOLOv11-L检测器与预训练UNet++的混合框架Fusion-YOLOv11-L，通过通道注意力门控模块融合语义与血管结构特征，以提升XCA图像中狭窄检测性能，并结合测试时自适应/增强缓解跨域退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入式X射线冠状动脉造影（XCA）中狭窄检测对诊断至关重要，但人工判读耗时、主观且一致性差，尤其在低对比度与血管重叠情况下。现有深度学习方法通常仅依赖检测或分割，性能受限。

Method: 将YOLOv11-L检测器与在ARCADE血管分割数据集上预训练的UNet++通过通道注意力门控模块结合，融合语义特征与血管感知的结构特征；在重标注的ARCADE*数据集上评估，并在OxAMI数据集上进行跨域评估，采用测试时自适应与测试时增强缓解域偏移。

Result: 在ARCADE*上优于YOLOv11-L，AP@50为0.585，F1为0.596。跨域评估（OxAMI）显示域偏移导致性能下降，测试时增强将AP@50从0.434提升至0.480。

Conclusion: 跨架构特征融合与轻量级自适应可提升XCA自动狭窄检测的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Coronary artery disease remains a leading cause of mortality, with stenosis detection from invasive X-ray Coronary Angiography (XCA) critical for diagnosis. Manual interpretation is time-consuming, subjective, and variable, especially under low contrast and vessel overlap. Existing deep learning methods often rely solely on detection or segmentation, limiting performance.We propose a hybrid framework, Fusion-YOLOv11-L, combining a YOLOv11-L detector with a pretrained UNet++ via a channel attention gating module. This enables fusion of semantic and vessel-aware structural features, improving localisation of stenotic regions. The UNet++ is pretrained on ARCADE vessel segmentation dataset to provide anatomically informed representations. Experiments on the relabeled ARCADE (ARCADE*) dataset show improved performance over YOLOv11-L, achieving AP@50 of 0.585 and F1-score of 0.596. Cross-domain evaluation on a dataset from the Oxford Acute Myocardial Infarction (OxAMI) Study reveals performance degradation due to domain shift, mitigated using test-time adaptation and augmentation. Test-time augmentation improves AP@50 from 0.434 to 0.480, indicating better generalisability. These results show that cross-architecture feature fusion and lightweight adaptation improve robustness and generalisability of automated stenosis detection in XCA.

</details>


### [26] [DirCast: Live Esports Directing and Commentary with a Single Streaming Video-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31734)
*Zixian Gao,Lixin Xiu,Penghao Luo,Xiangcheng Cao,Ziqi Cai,Zhen Li,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DirCast，首个可同时完成直播导播与解说的单一流式视频语言模型，并在27场职业Dota 2比赛数据上验证其导播与解说均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频语言模型只能对已由人类导播剪辑好的直播画面进行解说，无法处理原始多路摄像机输入下的实时导播决策，而真实直播需要模型仅依赖过去信息同时完成“选机位”与“解说”两项任务。

Method: DirCast每秒以低分辨率扫描所有玩家摄像机，通过“保持或切换”（hold-or-switch）导播决策判断是否切镜及切向何处；随后对被选中的摄像机以高分辨率读取并在发言前进行理解。其上下文仅保留已播出的画面与已说出的解说，其他视角随时间过期，从而使任意长度流的计算成本有界。作者构建了包含27场职业Dota 2比赛的数据集，含每个英雄的独立摄像机、人类导播选择与解说员解说。

Result: DirCast的导播效果优于规则式与零样本导播（包括GPT-5）；在Proact-VL评测协议下，其解说在与GPT-4o的对比中以29.2%的胜率胜出，而十一个观看人类导播画面的流式解说模型中最强者仅为19.4%。

Conclusion: 将导播与解说统一到单一流式模型中，并利用原始多路摄像机输入与有界上下文设计，可显著超越依赖人类导播画面的解说方案，为端到端直播制作提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A live esports broadcast needs a director who chooses which camera goes on air and a caster who talks over that choice, every second and without knowing what comes next. Streaming video language models have learned to commentate, but only over a broadcast that a human director has already produced. We study live broadcast production, in which a model receives the raw camera of every player and must both direct and commentate from the past alone. We present DirCast, a single streaming video language model that does both. Every second it scans all cameras at low resolution, decides with a hold-or-switch director whether and where to cut, and reads the chosen camera at high resolution before it speaks. Its context keeps what went on air and what was said and lets the other views expire, which bounds the cost of a stream of any length. We build a dataset of 27 professional Dota 2 matches with a camera for every hero, the human director's choices and the casters' commentary. DirCast directs better than rule-based and zero-shot directors including GPT-5, and its commentary wins 29.2 percent of comparisons against GPT-4o under the Proact-VL protocol, against 19.4 percent for the strongest of eleven streaming commentators that watch the human broadcast.

</details>


### [27] [RADIANT: Object-Centric Feed-Forward Global Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32075)
*Sarah Zhang,Zhiyang Dou,Minghao Guo,Benjamin Tod Jones,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RADIANT 提出一种以物体为中心的前馈神经渲染架构，用每个物体 32 个表面块 token 表示场景，将注意力序列长度与多边形数量解耦，并结合不变形状因子注意力与基于深度测试点溅射的相机光线交叉注意力，实现无需逐场景优化的全局光照渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 transformer 的渲染器按单个三角面片分词，自注意力随网格分辨率呈二次增长。然而宏观辐射传输由底层几何与物体排布决定，而非特定网格离散化，细化网格只会膨胀 token 数量而不改变传输物理，因此需要离散化无关、以物体为中心的表示。

Method: 1）以每个物体固定 32 个表面块 token 表示，解耦注意力长度与多边形数；2）单一网络中，物体间场景 transformer 建模 3D 辐射交换，视角解码器在目标位姿渲染图像；3）不变形状因子注意力（invariant form-factor attention）将几何距离与表面朝向嵌入 query-key 点积，作为 SE(3) 不变内积并以学习的面积与材质增益加权，避免显式构造 O(N²) 偏置矩阵；4）无网格连接时，相机光线借助共享球面框架中经深度测试的点溅射对场景 token 做交叉注意力以处理主可见性。

Result: 在 103 万场景基准、同等模型容量下，RADIANT 以少 5 倍的训练步数达到 25 dB 色调映射 PSNR；600k 步时达 32.2 dB（较 RenderFormer 提升 3.3 dB，较 RenderFormer-V2 提升 7.6 dB）。512×512 分辨率、500 物体场景的前向传播仅需 0.83 s，计算成本随物体数而非多边形数扩展。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心、离散化无关的 token 表示可有效支撑前馈全局光照渲染，在精度、训练效率与可扩展性上均优于逐三角面分的 transformer 渲染器，且计算开销随物体数量而非网格复杂度增长。

Abstract: Feed-forward neural renderers synthesize global illumination across novel 3D scenes without per-scene optimization or runtime ray tracing. Existing transformer-based renderers tokenize individual triangles, so self-attention scales quadratically with mesh resolution. Yet macroscopic radiative transport is governed by the underlying surface geometry and object arrangements rather than a particular mesh discretization; finer tessellation can thus inflate token counts without changing the underlying transport physics, motivating a discretization-agnostic and object-centric representation. We introduce RADIANT (RADiance-based Inter-Actor Neural Transformer), an object-centric architecture for feed-forward global illumination from point inputs. By representing each object with a compact budget of 32 surface-patch tokens, RADIANT decouples attention sequence length from polygon counts. In a single network, an inter-object scene transformer models 3D radiative exchange and a view decoder renders images at target camera poses. To capture light transport without materializing O(N^2) bias matrices, invariant form-factor attention embeds geometric distances and surface orientations directly into query-key dot products as SE(3)-invariant inner products weighted by learned area and material gains. For primary visibility without mesh connectivity, camera rays cross-attend scene tokens guided by depth-tested point splats in a shared spherical frame. Across a 1.03M-scene benchmark under matched model capacity, RADIANT reaches 25 dB tone-mapped PSNR in 5x fewer training steps than RenderFormer, and achieves 32.2 dB at 600k steps (+3.3 dB over RenderFormer and +7.6 dB over RenderFormer-V2). We further demonstrate the scalability of RADIANT, which completes a 512x512 forward pass of a 500-object scene in 0.83 s, with computational cost scaling with the number of objects rather than polygons.

</details>


### [28] [STEP: Surgical Point Tracking from Sparse Endpoints and Pseudo-trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31951)
*Bing Fan,Anwesa Choudhuri,Zhongpai Gao,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Terrence Chen,Heng Fan,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STEP：两阶段框架，利用手术视频与稀疏物理端点学习外科点跟踪，通过时间可逆性/立体一致性筛选稠密监督，并以端点损失与往返损失约束，显著降低端点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科点跟踪需在组织形变与遮挡下维持持久对应，但稠密轨迹标注成本高昂，难以获取。

Method: 两阶段设计：第一阶段由冻结的离线教师模型生成候选轨迹，用时间可逆性与立体一致性筛选出稠密监督供因果学生模型学习；第二阶段引入鲁棒物理端点损失锚定终点对应，往返损失约束时间一致性；采用无梯度前缀暴露累积漂移，最终可微窗口限制激活内存；推理时以固定权重处理单一RGB流。

Result: STIR→VL-SurgPT迁移中，时间-立体联合筛选相较未筛选轨迹使端点误差降低7.0%；在29个未纳入端点训练清单的VL-SurgPT片段诊断中，相较稠密标签微调，端点误差由31.52像素降至16.80像素。

Conclusion: 将精选轨迹与物理观测相结合可有效训练外科点跟踪器。

Abstract: Surgical point tracking requires persistent correspondence through tissue deformation and occlusion, yet dense trajectory annotations are costly to obtain. We present STEP, a two-stage framework that learns from surgical video and sparse physical endpoints. First, a frozen offline teacher generates candidate trajectories, and temporal reversibility and stereo consistency select dense supervision for a causal student. Second, a robust physical endpoint loss anchors terminal correspondence, while a round-trip loss constrains temporal consistency. A gradient-free prefix exposes training to accumulated drift, while a final differentiable window bounds activation memory. The trained tracker processes a single RGB stream with fixed weights. In STIR-to-VL-SurgPT transfer, temporal-stereo union selection reduces endpoint error by 7.0% relative to unfiltered trajectories. On a post-hoc diagnostic of 29 VL-SurgPT clips excluded from the endpoint-training manifest, STEP reduces endpoint error from 31.52 to 16.80 pixels relative to dense-label fine-tuning. These results support combining curated trajectories with physical observations to train surgical point trackers.

</details>


### [29] [Synthetic-to-Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search via Multi-Model Retrieval Fusion and Cross-Encoder Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31930)
*Eugene Denteh,Blessing Agyei Kyem,Andrews Danyo,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Richard Dzinyela,Solomon Owusu-Ansah,Dickson G. Asiedu,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模型检索与重排序框架提升文本行人异常检索的合成到真实迁移，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4测试集取得94.7815% mAP与90.8493% R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务训练依赖合成图文对而评测使用真实监控图像，存在域差异、近重复帧与大量视觉相似干扰项，导致合成到真实的迁移困难。

Method: 先以双向对比学习、显式负样本采样、假阴性掩码和域随机化将Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B适配到合成行人描述；再以四视图图库编码融合六个互补Qwen3-VL检查点与冻结SigLIP2的归一化分数；对融合得分最高的候选，用Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B与查询联合评估，并将交叉编码器分数与检索分数混合得到最终排序；同时构建纯合成数据的干扰项匹配验证协议以调参。

Result: 官方测试设置下94.7815% mAP、90.8493% R@1，公开排行榜第八名。

Conclusion: 模型集成、跨编码器重排序与合成域无泄漏验证协议的组合可有效缓解合成到真实的域偏移，取得有竞争力的检索性能。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires a model to retrieve individuals from a large image gallery using natural-language descriptions of both appearance and behavior. This task is challenging because model training relies on synthetic image-text pairs, whereas evaluation is performed on real surveillance images containing domain differences, near-duplicate frames, and many visually similar distractors. We present a multi-model retrieval and reranking framework designed to improve synthetic-to-real transfer. The framework first adapts Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to synthetic person descriptions using bidirectional contrastive learning, explicit negative sampling, false-negative masking, and domain randomization. It then combines the normalized scores of six complementary Qwen3-VL checkpoints with those of a frozen SigLIP2 model using four-view gallery encoding. The fused retrieval scores identify the strongest candidates, which are jointly evaluated with the query using Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B. The cross-encoder and retrieval scores are subsequently blended to produce the final ranking. We also construct a distractor-matched validation protocol entirely from synthetic data to select checkpoint weights, fusion coefficients, candidate depths, and reranking parameters without accessing the official test images. The final system achieves 94.7815% mAP and 90.8493% R@1 under the official AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 test setting, with a reported eighth-place finish on the public leaderboard.

</details>


### [30] [Sam-Correction: Fully Adaptive Label Noise Reduction for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32377)
*Takuro Shimaya,Masahiro Saiko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAM-Correction，利用SAM基础模型在无需人工精修GT的情况下全自适应地纠正含噪GT分割掩码，通过另一并行训练的分割网络提供提示并渐进式去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师标签质量严重影响机器学习结果，医学图像分割因标注一致性难保证而常含噪GT；已有纠正方法依赖多名专家提供的干净GT，实践中不易获得。

Method: 以SAM为基础模型纠正噪声GT，提示由另一个同时训练的分割网络生成，二者联合、渐进式迭代降低标签噪声，实现全自适应而无需预先准备精修掩码。

Result: 在源自真实人工标注的多种类型标签噪声上，SAM-C能精确降噪，并训练出高性能分割模型。

Conclusion: 即使完全缺乏干净标签也可实现鲁棒模型训练，为噪声标签下的医学图像分割开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The quality of the teacher label has a dramatic impact on the outcome of the machine learning process. Medical image segmentation often suffers from a noisy ground truth (GT) mask due to difficulties in consistent annotation. Although a recent study has proposed a promising method to curate the noisy GT mask, this requires additional well-curated GT masks that are not always accessible without the aid of multiple medical experts. As an alternative, we propose SAM-Correction, which utilizes a foundation model to correct the noisy GT mask in a fully adaptive manner instead of manually preparing well-curated masks. In our approach, we progressively mitigate label noise via the segment anything model (SAM), where prompts are given from another segmentation network that we train concurrently. Experiments with various types of label noise derived from actual human annotations demonstrate that SAM-C achieves precise noise reduction and high-performing segmentation models trained thereby. Our method paves the way for robust model training even when clean labels are entirely unavailable.

</details>


### [31] [EigenGradCAM with AugSmooth: Enhancing Explainability and Precision in Multi-Stage Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32374)
*Arsham Azam,Sagar Ashraf,Tayyaba Farhat,Rasikh Ali,Sheeraz Akram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的深度学习框架用于四分类阿尔茨海默病分期：结合特征向量Grad-CAM与梯度平滑提升显著图定位稳定性，并用WGAN-GP生成合成样本缓解类别不平衡，测试准确率达97.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MRI的深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，限制其临床落地；同时数据存在类别不平衡（中度痴呆样本少）。

Method: 使用2D轴位MRI图像；将特征向量式Grad-CAM与梯度平滑结合，对梯度加权卷积层做PCA，并在显著权重分解前加入高斯噪声以提升热图稳定性；用带梯度惩罚的Wasserstein GAN（WGAN-GP）生成合成样本平衡四阶段训练分布；在Best Alzheimer MRI数据集上训练自定义CNN。

Result: 测试准确率97.2%，平衡准确率97.0%，MCC为0.944；各类别AUC在0.96–0.99之间；热图高亮海马体与内嗅皮层等AD生物标志物相关区域。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持强分类性能的同时提升了模型可解释性，但仍需进一步的临床验证。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a progressive neurological disorder associated with memory loss, cognitive decline, and behavioral disturbances. Early and accurate diagnosis is essential, yet MRI-based deep learning models often lack interpretability, limiting their clinical use. This study presents an explainable deep learning approach for four-class AD staging using 2D axial MRI images. The method combines eigenvector-based Grad-CAM with gradient smoothing to improve saliency localization and heatmap stability. Principal component analysis is applied to gradient-weighted convolutional layers, while Gaussian noise is added to saliency weights before decomposition. To address class imbalance in the Moderate Dementia category, Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) generates synthetic samples to balance the four-stage training distribution. Custom convolutional neural networks are trained on the Best Alzheimer MRI Dataset, achieving 97.2% test accuracy, 97.0% balanced accuracy, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.944. The area under the ROC curve ranges from 0.96 to 0.99 across classes. Generated heatmaps highlight the hippocampus and entorhinal cortex, regions associated with Alzheimer's disease biomarkers. These findings suggest that the proposed approach combines strong classification performance with improved model interpretability, although further clinical validation is needed.

</details>


### [32] [TileShare: Training-Free Acceleration of Overlapping Tiled ViT Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32233)
*Xiangzhong Ye,Zhongshen Li,Shen You,Ziyang Meng,Liang Xu,Junkai Ji,Qiuzhen Lin,Xiangtao Li,Ka-Chun Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TileShare 让固定窗口 ViT 解码器不再为每个重叠瓦片重复计算全部层：每个瓦片只保留少量层的注意力，其余层每 token 只算一次，并通过释放权重把各瓦片的计算结果混合成共享状态。无需训练即可获 1.38–1.96x 加速并与原始解码器质量持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定窗口 ViT 解码器在部署分辨率下以重叠瓦片方式运行，重叠带内每个 token 被多个完整解码器实例处理，造成约一半 per-token 计算重复（如 2.4B 参数的 MiniMax-H3 视频解码器）；但直接去掉瓦片不可行，因为注意力、全局 token 与坐标都与瓦片绑定。

Method: TileShare 保留每个被释放瓦片的注意力，其他所有操作对每个 token 只计算一次；被释放的计算只在一种步骤上发生变化——用释放权重把重叠 token 的各瓦片结果混合为单一共享状态。通过干预实验发现该混合在注意力读取共享状态处损害质量，且主要集中在最初几层，因此这些层按瓦片保留。每模型只需测量一次保留层数（解码器无需标签）。可选：两 GPU 小时自蒸馏使 MiniMax-H3 全层共享；另有一个在网络冻结时训练的坐标场，使完全共享的 CLIP 开放词表分割与滑窗持平。

Result: 两个 GPU 型号上比批量+编译瓦片法快 1.38–1.51x（FP8 达 1.96x）；DAVIS 上 PSNR/LPIPS/DISTS 与原始解码器等效。预冻结设置下在未见 UVG 视频上 PSNR、DISTS 持平、LPIPS 更优。可迁移至另外两个解码器，MAGI-1 上 PSNR/DISTS 等效；ViTok-v2 图像自编码器在 2K–4K 上 PSNR 持平、LPIPS/DISTS 更优，加速 1.5–1.8x。共报告 14 个解码器/编码器的保留层数；全共享设置下 CLIP 开放词表分割在七个基准上与滑窗持平。

Conclusion: 通过"少量层留瓦片、其余层共享 + 释放权重混合"的设计，TileShare 在无需训练的前提下大幅消除重叠瓦片冗余计算，同时保持甚至改善感知质量，并具备跨模型、跨模态（视频/图像解码、分割）的通用性。

Abstract: Fixed-window Vision Transformer (ViT) decoders run at deployment resolution as overlapping tiles: every token in an overlap band is processed by several full decoder instances. In the 2.4B-parameter MiniMax-H3 video decoder, about half of all per-token computation is duplicated. Removing the tiles fails because attention, global tokens and coordinates are bound to them. TileShare keeps each released tile's attention and computes every other operation once per token. The released computation changes at one kind of step only: an overlap token's per-tile results are blended into one shared state with the released weights. Interventions show that this blend costs quality where attention reads the shared states, mainly in the first few layers, which TileShare therefore keeps per tile. Without training, keeping six layers per tile decodes 1.38-1.51x faster than batched and compiled tiling on two GPU types (up to 1.96x with FP8) and is equivalent to the released decoder in PSNR, LPIPS and DISTS on DAVIS videos. On unseen UVG videos with the setting frozen beforehand, it matches PSNR and DISTS and improves LPIPS. TileShare carries over to two further decoders and is again confirmed on unseen data. It is equivalent in PSNR and DISTS on MAGI-1. On the ViTok-v2 image autoencoder at 2K-4K, it matches overlapping tiling in PSNR, improves LPIPS and DISTS and decodes 1.5-1.8x faster. How many layers stay per tile is measured once per model (for decoders, without labels); we report it for 14 decoders and encoders. Optionally, two GPU-hours of self-distillation let MiniMax-H3 share every layer without a loss in PSNR, and a coordinate field trained while the network stays frozen brings fully shared CLIP-based open-vocabulary segmentation to parity with sliding windows on seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [33] [Towards Effective and Transferable Physical Camouflage against Multi-View BEV-based 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32230)
*Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Daojing He,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BEVCA，一种面向多视角BEV 3D感知的对抗性伪装生成框架，通过融合基于BEV特征的对抗损失与多视角神经渲染模块，实现跨模型、跨任务可迁移的伪装攻击，在数字与物理场景下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自动驾驶广泛采用多视角BEV 3D感知模型，但其对抗伪装攻击的鲁棒性缺乏研究；现有伪装攻击多针对单视角2D检测器，在多视角3D感知下效果有限。

Method: 提出BEVCA框架，将基于BEV特征的对抗损失与多视角神经渲染模块相结合，生成可迁移的对抗伪装纹理。

Result: 在数字与物理设置下的大量实验表明，BEVCA优于现有最先进基线，并在真实世界条件下表现强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BEVCA填补了多视角BEV 3D感知对抗伪装研究的空白，证明该攻击具备有效性与可迁移性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving systems widely adopt multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) based 3D perception models due to their superior performance. Despite their success, the robustness of these models against adversarial camouflage attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing camouflage attacks primarily target single-view 2D detectors, limiting their effectiveness in multi-view 3D perception settings. To address this gap, we propose BEVCA, a novel adversarial camouflage generation framework tailored for multi-view BEV-based 3D perception. Our framework integrates a BEV-feature-based adversarial loss with a multi-view neural rendering module, enabling effective and transferable camouflage attacks across different models and tasks. Extensive experiments in both digital and physical settings demonstrate that BEVCA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and exhibits strong robustness under real-world conditions. Our code is available at https://github.com/WAVE-AT-IDEA/BEVCA.

</details>


### [34] [Neural Disjunctive Normal Forms for Learning Exact Rank Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32203)
*Diego Marcondes,Wonder A. L. Alves,Ronaldo F. Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对排序滤波器（rank filter）对结构元（structuring element）导数局部稀疏、难以梯度学习的问题，提出基于阈值分解与平滑阈值方程隐式微分的平滑替代梯度方法，通过直通（straight-through）过程实现前向精确评估、反向平滑求导，从而学习任意排序滤波器的实值结构元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态学算子（排序滤波器）关于结构元的导数具有局部稀疏性，导致基于梯度的学习效果受限，亟需一种既能保持算子精确评估又能将梯度分布到整个窗口的学习方法。

Method: 1) 利用阈值分解构造神经析取范式（neural DNF）表示，并给出整数图像与结构元下表示精确性的充分条件；2) 由平滑阈值方程的唯一根导出平滑替代函数；3) 通过隐式微分得到带归一化局部权重的闭式导数，其分布由平滑参数控制；4) 采用直通过程：前向计算精确排序滤波器，反向使用替代梯度；固定秩时可学习包括中值滤波在内的任意排序滤波器的实值结构元，并可推广至排序滤波器的复合、下确界与上确界。

Result: 在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST 图像去噪与视网膜血管分割实验中，从通用随机初始化出发，该方法在所有设置下均稳定优于使用精确次梯度训练；去噪任务中最佳网络在所有设置下优于平滑形态学网络；血管分割中学习到的腐蚀算子恢复出贴合血管的结构元（符合理论预期），而多数用精确次梯度训练的模型结构元仍接近初始化。

Conclusion: 通过将排序滤波器的导数经阈值分解与平滑替代梯度分散到整个窗口，并配合直通训练策略，可有效解决形态学算子梯度学习中的稀疏性问题，实现结构元的可靠学习与性能提升，且理论分析与实验结果一致。

Abstract: Gradient-based learning of morphological operators is hindered by locally sparse derivatives with respect to the structuring elements. We develop a method for learning rank filters that retains exact operator evaluation while distributing the derivatives across the window. Using threshold decomposition, we construct a neural disjunctive normal form representation and establish a sufficient condition for exactness with integer-valued images and structuring elements. We then derive a smooth surrogate from the unique root of a smooth threshold equation. Implicit differentiation gives closed-form derivatives with normalised local weights, whose distribution is controlled by a smoothing parameter. These derivatives are used in a straight-through procedure: the forward pass evaluates the exact rank filter, while the backward pass uses the surrogate. For fixed ranks, the procedure learns real-valued structuring elements of any rank filter, including the median, and extends to compositions, infima, and suprema of rank filters. Experiments on image denoising using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST and retinal vessel segmentation show that, starting from a generic random initialisation, the proposed approach consistently improves performance over training with exact subgradients in every setting. In image denoising, our best network also outperforms smooth morphological networks in all settings. On vessel segmentation, the learned erosions recover structuring elements that fit the vessels, as theoretically expected, whereas most of those trained with exact subgradients remain close to their initialisation.

</details>


### [35] [Depth-Dependent Safety Geometry in Vision-Language Models: Layer-Wise Localization and Bidirectional Refusal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32182)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Syed Talal Hassan,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级推理时框架，在冻结的VLM中定位并操控安全敏感表征；发现在Qwen3-VL-4B中安全几何随深度非均匀分布，早期视觉层可通过沿学习到的安全方向做激活引导实现强双向拒答控制（不安全拒绝率6%–96%），而深层虽沿安全方向可分却难以因果操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM广泛部署，但其安全行为的内部机制尚不清楚，缺乏对安全敏感表征的定位与操控手段。

Method: 无需重训练、不改参数：对冻结VLM做逐层激活差异分析以刻画安全几何；采用因果干预（沿学习到的安全方向做激活引导、激活幅值缩放）检验各层对拒答行为的因果影响；并在InternVL2.5-4B上做对比验证。

Result: 早期视觉层具强双向拒答控制能力，不安全拒绝率跨越6%–96%；较深层对幅值缩放更敏感且存在明显的安全—选择性权衡；更深层沿安全方向分离良好但对引导基本不敏感，表明表征可分性与因果可控性可解耦。InternVL2.5-4B呈定性相似的深度依赖激活几何，仅层位置与幅值不同。

Conclusion: 揭示了VLM视觉编码器中白盒、表征层面的拒答绕过攻击面，并呼吁在推理时对安全敏感表征进行稳定化处理。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are widely deployed, yet the internal mechanisms behind their safety behavior remain poorly understood. We present a lightweight inference-time framework for localizing and manipulating safety-sensitive representations in frozen VLMs, without retraining or modifying parameters. On Qwen3-VL-4B, layer-wise activation divergence analysis reveals non-uniform safety geometry across depth. Causal interventions show that early vision layers support strong bidirectional refusal control through activation steering along a learned safety direction, with unsafe rejection spanning 6%-96%, whereas later layers respond more to activation magnitude scaling and exhibit a substantial safety-selectivity trade-off. Deeper layers remain well separated along the safety direction yet are largely insensitive to steering, distinguishing representational separability from causal controllability. InternVL2.5-4B shows qualitatively similar depth-dependent activation geometry despite differences in layer location and magnitude. Our results expose a white-box, representation-level refusal-bypass attack surface in VLM vision encoders and motivate inference-time stabilization of safety-sensitive representations.

</details>


### [36] [DriveOSS: Dataset and Benchmark for 3D Anomaly Segmentation and Open-set Semantic Segmentation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32612)
*Zhiran Yan,Savankumar Bhanderi,Sourabh Lolge,Rui Song,Girdharbhai Paneliya,Shiva Agrawal,Gordon Elger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriveOSS，首个面向真实世界3D异常分割与开放集语义分割的多模态数据集与基准，含12,661帧同步标注数据，覆盖多种天气与光照条件，并划分为ID/OOD子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义分割方法多在闭集假设与标准驾驶条件下开发评测，所有类别预先已知；而真实驾驶中不可避免会遇到未见过的物体，闭集感知不足以支撑安全部署。现有数据缺乏面向开放集/异常分割的真实多模态基准。

Method: 构建DriveOSS数据集：采用6路环视RGB相机、128线LiDAR与GPS/IMU采集，共12,661帧同步且标注的帧，包含52个真实驾驶场景，覆盖晴天、夜间、多云、雨天、雪天条件；数据组织为ID子集（仅含已知类，用于训练）与OOD子集（额外包含标注为outlier类的未知物体，用于验证与测试）；并提供基准协议与代码。

Result: 大量实验表明，未见物体仍是当前3D感知系统的主要挑战，现有方法在开放集/异常分割场景下表现不佳。

Conclusion: DriveOSS为3D异常分割与开放集语义分割提供首个真实世界多模态数据集与统一评测基准，数据集、基准协议与代码已开源，推动开放世界3D感知研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D semantic segmentation have significantly improved scene understanding in autonomous driving. However, existing methods are typically developed under a closed-set assumption and evaluated under standard driving conditions, where all categories are known in advance. In real-world driving, autonomous vehicles inevitably encounter previously unseen objects, making closed-set perception insufficient for safe deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce DriveOSS, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first real-world multimodal dataset and benchmark specifically designed for 3D anomaly segmentation and open-set semantic segmentation. It contains 12,661 synchronized and annotated frames captured using six surround-view RGB cameras, a 128-beam LiDAR, and a GPS/IMU unit. The dataset comprises 52 real-world driving scenes collected under clear, nighttime, cloudy, rainy, and snowy conditions and is organized into ID (in-distribution) and OOD (out-of-distribution) subsets. The ID scenes contain only known classes and are used for training, whereas the OOD scenes additionally contain unknown objects annotated as the outlier class and are used for validation and testing. Extensive experiments show that unseen objects remain a major challenge for current 3D perception systems.
