The dataset, benchmark protocols, and code are available at https://github.com/chrisyan/DriveOSS.

</details>


### [37] [Fast and Useful: Adapting Distilled Generators for Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32512)
*Patryk Bartkowiak,Dominik Michels,Sören Pirk,Wojciech Pałubicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过给学生模型首步预测拟合低秩更新，使其短暂到达无引导基模型的中间状态，再改回原学生继续生成，生成图像可训练出显著更好的分类器，且保持快速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏文生图模型生成快，但用其图像训练的分类器性能明显劣于用慢速基模型图像训练的分类器。作者希望改造已发布的蒸馏生成器以产出更优训练数据，同时保留快速生成能力。

Method: 对学生的第一次预测拟合一个低秩更新，使其到达无引导基模型产生的中间状态；随后禁用该更新，由未改动的学生完成剩余生成。基模型仅提供训练目标，推理时不参与。消融对比了引导目标与更短跳跃（即监督所用状态的选择）。在三个生成器家族、ImageNet-1k 全规模（类名模板提示）、ResNet-50 上评估，并测试固定适配器在更多类别、细粒度数据集及不同学习器架构与训练配方上的泛化。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1k 全规模下，ResNet-50 top-1 从 8.4% 提升到 34.2%（FLUX.2-klein，每图两次去噪评估），从 12.7% 提升到 32.1%（SD-Turbo，每图三次去噪评估）。引导目标和更短跳跃的消融使分类器精度更低，说明用于监督的状态很重要。模板式适配优于更丰富提示的替代方案，而适配与 LLM 生成提示结合的效果取决于生成器。

Conclusion: 仅适配单个生成步骤即可让快速生成器对训练视觉模型显著更有用；固定适配器在额外类别、细粒度数据集及其他学习器架构与训练配方上同样有效。

Abstract: Distilled text-to-image models generate images quickly, but classifiers trained on those images can perform substantially worse than classifiers trained on images from their slower base models. We adapt released distilled generators to produce better training data while retaining fast generation. We fit a low-rank update to the student's first prediction so that it reaches an intermediate state produced by its unguided base, then disable the update and let the unchanged student finish generation. The base supplies training targets and is absent at inference. Guided targets and shorter jumps give classifiers lower accuracy in our ablations, showing why the state used for supervision matters. Across three generator families, the adapted images train better classifiers than images from the released students. With class-name templates at full ImageNet-1k scale, ResNet-50 top-1 rises from 8.4% to 34.2% for FLUX.2-klein and from 12.7% to 32.1% for SD-Turbo, using two and three denoiser evaluations per image, respectively. Fixed adapters also improve results on additional classes, fine-grained datasets, and other learner architectures and training recipes. Template-based adaptation remains useful against richer-prompt alternatives, while the benefit of combining adaptation with LLM generation prompts depends on the generator. Adapting a single generation step makes fast generators substantially more useful for training visual models.

</details>


### [38] [MDMR-Bench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multi-Reference Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32459)
*Bowen Zheng,Jintao Lin,Changheng Yi,Haoran Yang,Binxiao Huang,Chenyang Lei,Rui Liu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MDMR-Bench，一个面向多参考图像生成的多维度基准，涵盖实体融合、属性迁移、单图多元素提取、信息图操作与视觉推理密集型生成四类能力，并引入基于检查清单的细粒度评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参考图像生成基准评测维度有限，难以覆盖该任务多样化的挑战，无法系统刻画模型在不同能力维度上的表现与失败模式。

Method: 构建MDMR-Bench，按代表性能力设定组织多个评测子集，覆盖（1）常见实体融合与属性迁移、（2）单图多元素提取、（3）信息图操作、（4）视觉推理密集型生成；并提出基于检查清单的评测协议，将生成质量分解为全局级与原子级查询的细粒度评测项，替代单一整体打分。

Result: 对开源与闭源模型的基准测试表明，MDMR-Bench 能在不同维度上提供细粒度的性能洞察，并揭示模型的能力差异与失败模式。

Conclusion: MDMR-Bench 通过多维度任务设计与检查清单式评测协议，实现了更具诊断性和可解释性的多参考图像生成评估；数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Image generation models have rapidly advanced, with many models now supporting generation conditioned on multiple reference images and textual instructions. Such models enable a wide range of applications, including entity fusion and attribute transfer across images. However, existing benchmarks for multi-reference image generation rely on limited evaluation dimensions and fail to capture the diverse challenges of the task. To address this limitation, we propose MDMR-Bench, a Multi-Dimensional benchmark for Multi-Reference image generation. MDMR-Bench is organized around representative capability settings and consists of multiple evaluation subsets spanning diverse dimensions of multi-reference image generation, including (1) common entity fusion and attribute transfer, (2) multi-element extraction from a single image, (3) infographic manipulation, and (4) visual reasoning-intensive generation. This design enables systematic analysis of model capabilities and failure modes across different challenge dimensions. Furthermore, to achieve more reliable and interpretable evaluation, we introduce a checklist-based evaluation protocol that decomposes generation quality into fine-grained evaluation aspects. Instead of relying solely on holistic scoring, our framework uses structured checklists composed of both global-level and atomic-level queries, enabling more diagnostic assessment of model performance. By benchmarking both open-source and proprietary models, we demonstrate that MDMR-Bench provides fine-grained insights into model performance across different dimensions. The dataset and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [39] [Cycle-of-Thought: Self-Evaluative Iterative Reasoning for Table Visual Question Answering in Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32423)
*Somraj Gautam,Gaurav Harit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Self-RefineVQA，一个免训练、推理时自校正框架，让单个 VLM 循环扮演生成者、批判者与修正者，在无需标注数据和模型更新的条件下提升表格 VQA 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在表格等结构化内容上的推理不稳定，常出现空间错位与逻辑幻觉；现有改进方法依赖架构修改或昂贵的监督微调，需要大规模专家标注数据和大量算力，门槛高。

Method: 受人类修改行为启发，构建设计为训练无关、推理阶段的自反馈闭环：同一 VLM 通过提示引导迭代地充当生成器、批判者和修正者，对常见推理错误进行再评估与纠正；不引入额外标注数据或参数更新。

Result: 在表格 VQA 基准上，单语与跨语言理解均取得一致的准确率提升；对小模型增益尤为显著，2B–7B 轻量模型可媲美更大模型；复杂度对比显示推理成本线性增长，但数据效率更高、累计成本更低，避免了微调所需的标注瓶颈。

Conclusion: 基于提示的自改进是一种可扩展、易获取的范式，可为 VLM 提供可靠的表格推理能力；Self-RefineVQA 在无监督标注与无模型更新的前提下实现了性能与成本的较好平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved Visual Question Answering (VQA) performance, yet their reasoning over structured content such as tables remains inconsistent, often plagued by spatial misalignment and logical hallucinations. Existing approaches for improving table VQA typically rely on architectural modifications or expensive supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which impose high barriers to entry due to the need for large-scale, expert-annotated datasets and significant computational resources. In this work, we propose Self-RefineVQA, a training-free, inference-time self-correction framework that improves table VQA performance without additional labeled data or model updates. Inspired by human revision behavior, our method enables a single VLM to iteratively act as a generator, critic, and refiner, forming a closed self-feedback loop that corrects common reasoning errors through prompt-guided re-evaluation. Experiments on tabular VQA benchmarks demonstrate that Self-RefineVQA yields consistent accuracy gains in monolingual and cross-lingual understanding, with a particularly significant boost for small models, enabling lightweight models (e.g., 2B-7B parameters) to rival the performance of significantly larger counterparts. Furthermore, we provide a comparative complexity analysis demonstrating that while inference cost increases linearly, our framework offers superior data efficiency and a lower cumulative cost by eliminating the prohibitive annotation bottleneck of human labeling required for fine-tuning. Our results highlight the potential of prompt-based self-improvement as a scalable, accessible paradigm for reliable table reasoning in VLMs.

</details>


### [40] [Interactive image diagnosis using test-time backpropagation from the auxiliary task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32396)
*Takuro Shimaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务梯度辅助（MGA）方法，无需额外训练即可为多任务CAD模型引入交互性，放射科医师可对辅助任务预测给出反馈并获取额外诊断建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务CAD通过辅助任务预测可解释特征以增强可信度，但当辅助任务预测与放射科医师预期不符时，其诊断意见往往被弃用，CAD知识未被充分利用。

Method: MGA基于梯度信息，将放射科医师对辅助任务的反馈转化为对诊断预测的调整，无需重新训练即可与多任务模型结合；相较仅用前向传播的已有方法，MGA利用梯度实现更稳健的交互。

Result: 在乳腺钼靶与CT两个数据集上，MGA在多种辅助任务反馈下均成功提升诊断准确率；相比仅前向传播的方法更稳健，且可与之组合进一步提升精度。

Conclusion: MGA为将人工智能集成到影像诊断中提供了有前景的途径，实现了人机交互式多任务CAD。

Abstract: Multi-task deep learning has been developed to ensure explainability in computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) systems. These systems simultaneously predict interpretable characteristics of findings in auxiliary tasks to help radiologists assess the reliability of the diagnostic prediction. However, the CAD's opinion would not be referred to when the auxiliary task prediction deviates from the radiologist's expectation. To further leverage CAD's knowledge in the above situation, we proposed a multi-task gradient assist (MGA) method that brings interactivity to multi-task models without additional training. MGA allows radiologists to give feedback to the CAD and ask for additional diagnostic suggestions. Using two datasets of mammography and CT, we show that MGA successfully improves diagnostic accuracy upon feedback on various auxiliary tasks. Compared to an existing method using forward propagation only, MGA performs more robustly and can be combined to further improve accuracy. Our method paves a promising way for integrating artificial intelligence into image diagnosis.

</details>


### [41] [PASTA: Prompt-Aware Annealed Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32390)
*Ren Ajiki,Ryugo Morita,Stanislav Frolov,Brian Bernhard Moser,Ko Watanabe,Riku Takahashi,Andreas Dengel,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PASTA：一种免训练的扩散采样框架，通过在共享潜变量轨迹上按掩码选择性施加语义引导并采用退火调度，严格保持预留区域为空且维持整体视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实视觉设计需为标题、logo、UI等元素刻意保留干净区域。现有布局感知文生图方法难以严格保持预留区域：或通过操纵注意力位移内容，或通过噪声水平干预抑制条件，均牺牲视觉连贯性，导致平坦区域或预留与非预留区风格不一致。

Method: 提出Prompt-Aware Shared-Latent Training-Free Annealed Diffusion Sampling（PASTA）。免训练框架，在采样过程中仅对允许放置物体的区域施加语义引导；基于共享潜变量轨迹，将全提示与上下文提示的引导通过掩码引导采样进行融合；并用退火调度在早期去噪阶段抑制语义泄漏、后期逐步恢复视觉一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，PASTA在提升预留区域保真度的同时，保持了整体图像真实感与感知一致性。

Conclusion: PASTA以无需训练的方式实现严格预留区域约束，克服了注意力操纵与噪声干预导致的一致性缺陷，适用于实际视觉设计场景。

Abstract: While Diffusion models produce high-quality images, real-world visual design requires intentionally preserving clean regions for headlines, logos, or UI elements. Existing layout-aware text-to-image methods struggle to strictly preserve reserved regions, often introducing unwanted content that complicates downstream editing. Existing approaches either manipulate attention to displace content or suppress conditioning via noise-level interventions. However, these methods sacrifice visual coherence, producing flat regions or noticeable style inconsistencies between reserved and non-reserved areas. To address this limitation, we propose a Prompt-Aware Shared-Latent Training-Free Annealed Diffusion Sampling (PASTA), a training-free framework that enforces reserved-region constraints by selectively applying semantic guidance to object-allowed regions during sampling. PASTA operates on a shared latent trajectory, where full-prompt and context-prompt guidance are fused through mask-guided sampling. An annealed schedule suppresses semantic leakage during early denoising stages while gradually restoring visual consistency in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PASTA improves reserved-region fidelity while preserving overall image realism and perceptual consistency.

</details>


### [42] [Are Textual Residuals Truly Complementary? Learning Visual Complements under Semantic Superposition for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32709)
*Xiaoyang Li,Mingming Lu,Wei Zhou,Hao Li,Canhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Superposition-Aware Residual Calibration (SARC)，将稠密文本模态差异转化为稀疏、可选择且视觉对齐的补偿残差，用于文本增强可见光-红外行人重识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本增强 VI-ReID 方法默认"文本模态差异即视觉互补信息"，但作者通过语义叠加分析发现稠密文本残差会把有用外观变化与冗余信息纠缠在一起，直接残差融合不可靠。

Method: SARC 包含两部分：(1) Low-Interference Sparse Residual Learning：将模态导向的文本嵌入表示为共享字典上的稀疏激活强度，只保留正强度差，并抑制字典层重叠与激活加权干扰，重构紧凑可控残差；(2) Visual Complement and Counterfactual Calibration：以身份相关的 RGB-IR 视觉差异为参照对残差进行反事实校准，保留有意义的补偿语义并维持残差结构。

Result: 在 Tri-SYSU-MM01、Tri-RegDB、Tri-LLCM 三个数据集上，SARC 稳定提升 VI-ReID 主干性能，与文本辅助方法相比具竞争力；机理分析显示其文本变化更集中、字典级特征干扰更低、多数样本的残差-视觉互补对齐更好。

Conclusion: 有效的跨模态补偿要求文本残差被选择性表示并显式校准，而非直接当作视觉互补信息使用。

Abstract: Text-enhanced visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) exploits textual descriptions to compensate for appearance cues that are weak or unavailable under infrared imaging. Existing methods construct compensation from differences between modality-oriented textual representations, implicitly assuming that textual differences are visually complementary. We revisit this assumption through semantic superposition and show that dense textual residuals may entangle useful appearance variations with redundant, making direct residual fusion unreliable. We propose Superposition-Aware Residual Calibration, which transforms dense textual differences into selective and visually grounded complements. Low-Interference Sparse Residual Learning represents modality-oriented text embeddings as sparse activation strengths over a shared dictionary, selects positive strength differences, and suppresses both dictionary-level overlap and activation-weighted interference to reconstruct a compact, selectively controlled residual. Visual Complement and Counterfactual Calibration further calibrates the residual against identity-specific RGB-IR visual discrepancies, encouraging retained semantics to provide meaningful compensation while preserving the residual structure. Experiments on Tri-SYSU-MM01, Tri-RegDB, and Tri-LLCM show that SARC consistently improves the VI-ReID backbone and remains competitive with text-assisted methods. Mechanism analyses show that SARC produces more concentrated textual variations, reduces dictionary-level feature interference, and improves residual-visual-complement alignment for most samples. These results demonstrate that effective cross-modal compensation requires textual residuals to be selectively represented and explicitly calibrated, rather than directly treated as visual complements. Code and implementation details will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [43] [The Answer Knows Where to Look: Zooming before MLLMs Answer under Visual-Token Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32664)
*Zhensheng Hu,Guanglong Xu,Guanqiang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现MLLM的答案token在中层起即能定位证据区域，但答题前可用的注意力只能在上层、弥散地定位，且各模型深度不同；据此提出一个"look token"，在单次prefill中以后见之明学习模仿正确答案的注意力，从而在作答前产生集中的指针，并据此自适应选择无缩放、裁剪、方窗、指针自适应窗或foveated warp等观看方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效部署下每图视觉token预算很小，文档、图表、信息图中的细粒度文字变得不可读；放大（zoom in）可恢复证据，但区域必须在模型作答之前选好。作者希望解决"作答前如何知道该看哪里"这一时序问题。

Method: 先做诊断分析：对比答案token的定位能力与作答前注意力的定位能力。发现答案token自中间层起即可定位证据，而作答前注意力仅在上层、深度因模型而异且分布弥散；直接监督作答前注意力反而损害阅读。据此提出方法：引入专用look token，仅在单次prefill中处理、不进入作答pass，以hindsight方式学习模仿模型自身正确回答时的注意力，将知识蒸馏为作答前即存在的集中指针；其隐状态用于选择观看策略（无缩放、crop、方窗、指针自适应窗、foveated warp），并以每次观看的反事实结果作为监督信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL三个规模、五个文本密集基准上，每视图98个视觉token时超过最强基线4.8–9.1个点，超过注意力引导缩放11.1–17.0个点，且运行速度快于注意力引导式与基于grounding的缩放方法。方法无需框标注、无需逐模型层搜索、无外部定位器、作答前无需生成。

Conclusion: MLLM内部已隐含证据位置信息，但需以合适的"后见"监督方式提取为作答前可用的指针；让模型"看它答案会看的地方"可显著提升小视觉token预算下的文本密集理解，且兼顾效率与免标注。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) read images through visual tokens, and under the small per-image budgets that efficient deployment requires, the fine text of documents, charts and infographics becomes illegible. Zooming in recovers the evidence, but the region must be chosen before the model answers. We find that MLLMs know where the evidence is, but not in a form that can be read before they answer: the answer tokens localize it from the middle layers on, whereas the attention available before answering does so only in the top layers, at a depth that varies across models, and diffusely. Supervising the pre-answer attention to point degrades reading, and the benefit of zooming is governed by each image's information gap. Building on these findings, we introduce ours, which lets an MLLM look where its answer would. A dedicated look token, processed in a single prefill and kept out of the answering pass, learns in hindsight to attend as the model's own correct answers do, extracting this knowledge into a concentrated pointer before any answer exists. Its hidden state then selects how to look, from no zoom to crops, square windows, windows that adapt to the pointer and foveated warps, supervised by the counterfactual outcome of every view. ours needs no box annotations, per-model layer search, external localizers or generation before the answer. On Qwen3-VL at three scales and five text-rich benchmarks, it surpasses the strongest baseline by 4.8-9.1 points at 98 visual tokens per view and attention-guided zooming by 11.1-17.0 points, while running faster than attention-guided and grounding-based zooming.

</details>


### [44] [SemGeo-Gen: Unsupervised Generation of Approximate Cross-Instance Semantic-Geometric Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32663)
*Roy Amoyal,Shira Ifergane,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督方法SemGeo-Gen，从弱对齐3D物体集合中自动生成近似的跨实例语义-几何对应监督，避免昂贵的逐点人工标注，并可提升真实图像语义对应模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨实例的精确逐点对应往往存在歧义，且大规模人工标注代价高昂、不切实际，因此需要一种可扩展的自动监督来源。

Method: 仅给定粗粒度类别级旋转对齐，将多视角DINOv2特征提升至3D，用连续分段仿射配准对齐实例，并通过语义一致性剪枝候选匹配；所得3D对应可投影到渲染视图，生成3D-3D、3D-2D、2D-2D监督。

Result: 对KeypointNet稀疏人工标注验证，取得93% PCK@0.10与97% PCK@0.15；用作合成预训练后，在SPair-71k上微调可使当前先进语义对应模型提升最多5个PCK@0.10点。

Conclusion: 从3D资产生成的近似语义-几何监督能有效改善真实图像对应学习，是可替代昂贵人工标注的可扩展方案。

Abstract: We introduce SemGeo-Gen, an unsupervised method for generating approximate cross-instance semantic-geometric supervision from weakly aligned 3D object collections. Our goal is not to recover exact point-level correspondences, which are often ambiguous across different instances and would require expensive, impractical manual supervision at scale. Instead, we automatically produce approximate correspondences that are semantically meaningful, geometrically consistent, and diverse enough to train modern correspondence models. Given only coarse category-level rotation alignment, SemGeo-Gen lifts multi-view DINOv2 features to 3D, aligns object instances using continuous piecewise-affine registration, and prunes candidate matches using semantic consistency. The resulting 3D correspondences can be projected into rendered views, yielding scalable 3D-3D, 3D-2D, and 2D-2D supervision without manual annotation. We validate the generated correspondences against sparse human annotations from KeypointNet, obtaining 93% PCK@0.10 and 97% PCK@0.15. More importantly, we show that using the generated correspondences for synthetic pretraining improves recent state-of-the-art semantic correspondence models on SPair-71k after fine-tuning on real data, with gains of up to 5 PCK@0.10 points. Together, these results indicate that approximate semantic-geometric supervision generated from 3D assets can improve real-image correspondence learning and serve as a scalable alternative to costly manual annotation. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/SemGeo-Gen/.

</details>


### [45] [EditJev: System One Routing for Image Editing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32635)
*Zhile Guan,Xu Jiang,Yule Duan,Gongbo Zhang,Shuyu Wang,Xiao Chen,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EditJev,一种"System One"式的图像编辑模型路由方法:在单次前向传播中直接预测各候选编辑模型在样本级上的偏好分数,并结合模型速度分数,在质量与延迟间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式图像编辑模型存在显著的样本级性能波动:同一图像与指令下不同模型结果差异大,最优模型因请求而异;同时各模型推理延迟差异明显,固定单一模型部署效率低。因此需要动态选择编辑器的路由机制。

Method: 构建 EditJev:在视觉-语言骨干上附加轻量偏好头(preference head),单次前向输出固定维度的分数向量,避免自回归生成模型名与解析。训练上,用离线候选模型输出构造成对偏好,优化成对排序目标以学习相对模型适配度。推理时,将预测偏好分数与模型级速度分数结合,权衡编辑质量与生成延迟。

Result: 在 ImgEdit 与 GEdit-En 上,相较最强固定编辑器,聚合质量分别由 4.540 提升至 4.592、由 8.194 提升至 8.255,同时估计端到端延迟分别降低 60.8% 与 31.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明,直接、结构化的模型路由对高效图像编辑是有效的,可在略微提升质量的同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models exhibit pronounced sample-specific performance variation: different models can produce substantially different results for the same source image and editing instruction, and the best-performing model often varies across requests. These models also differ substantially in inference latency, making fixed-model deployment inefficient. We therefore study image editing model routing, which dynamically selects an editor from a candidate pool given an input image and editing instruction. We introduce EditJev, a System One routing model that directly predicts sample-level preference scores over candidate image editing models in a single forward pass. A lightweight preference head on a vision-language backbone produces a fixed-dimensional score vector without autoregressive model-name generation or response parsing. To train EditJev, we construct pairwise preferences from offline candidate outputs and optimize a pairwise ranking objective to learn relative model suitability. At inference time, EditJev combines the predicted preference scores with model-level speed scores to balance editing quality and generation latency. Experiments on ImgEdit and GEdit-En show that EditJev improves aggregate quality over the strongest fixed editor, from 4.540 to 4.592 and from 8.194 to 8.255, respectively, while reducing estimated end-to-end latency by 60.8% and 31.7%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of direct, structured model routing for efficient image editing.

</details>


### [46] [Multimodal Generative AI Assists Comprehensive Echocardiographic Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32880)
*Yi Qin,Marawan Elbatel,Shanshan Song,Taoran Huang,Jiarong Guo,Ningshu Li,Lehan Wang,Chenxu Zhao,Jiexin He,Machteld J. Boonstra,Stephan van der Zwaard,Marielle C. van de Veerdonk,Pere Lopez-Gutierrez,Lydia Dux-Santoy,Andrea Guala,Enrique Almar-Munoz,Sebastian Johannes Reinstadler,Radek Debiec,Yi-Hang Zhu,Huiyu Zhou,Adrian P Cheong,Yike Guo,Agnes Mayr,Karim Lekadir,Yong Jiang,Nilesh J. Samani,Dacheng Tao,Folkert W. Asselbergs,Hongwen Fei,Alex PW Lee,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoChat——一个用于超声心动图综合分析的生成式多模态视觉-语言模型，通过链式推理将结论显式地依据视觉证据进行接地，在六家国际医学中心的多队列上取得SOTA，并支持33种心脏疾病与征象诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图是心血管诊断的基石，但经验丰富的解读医师严重短缺制约其全球可用性。现有AI方案要么是不可解释的"黑箱"，要么局限于狭窄任务，无法复现临床医生依据指南的整体推理过程。

Method: 构建生成式多模态视觉-语言模型EchoChat，模仿心脏病学家的认知工作流，融合大规模医学知识与思维链（chain-of-thought）推理机制，使结论显式关联视觉证据；基于46,358次检查的超声心动图-指令对进行训练。

Result: 在六家国际医学中心的多队列上达到新SOTA；覆盖33种心脏疾病与征象（含15种新增），新增条件在外部评估中平均F1为73.7%；相较现有最佳基础模型，心脏疾病识别平均召回率提升13.54%，量化任务平均R²提升0.275；在来源盲法的超声医师阅片研究中，与EchoChat协同生成的报告在诊断准确性与完整性上获两位资深医师更高评价，F1由72.78%提升至82.75%。

Conclusion: 将临床推理融入多模态AI可推动专科 expertise 的普及化，并有望转变心血管诊疗标准。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the cornerstone of cardiovascular diagnostics, yet its global utility is constrained by a critical shortage of experienced interpreters. Current artificial intelligence (AI) solutions remain limited, typically operating as opaque "black boxes" or focusing on narrow tasks that fail to capture the holistic, guideline-driven reasoning of a clinician. Here we introduce EchoChat, a generative multimodal vision-language model that performs comprehensive echocardiographic analysis to support clinician interpretation. By emulating the cognitive workflow of a cardiologist, EchoChat integrates vast medical knowledge with a chain-of-thought reasoning mechanism to explicitly ground its conclusions in visual evidence. Trained on echocardiography-instruction pairs from 46,358 examinations, our model establishes a new state-of-the-art across diverse cohorts from six international medical centers. It supports the broadest diagnostic scope of 33 cardiac conditions and findings, including 15 newly added conditions for which it achieves an average F1 of 73.7% across available external evaluations. It surpasses existing best-performing foundation models in cardiac condition recognition (improving average recall by 13.54% on shared tasks) and quantification (average R2 score increase of 0.275 on shared tasks). Furthermore, in a source-blinded echocardiographer reader study, echocardiography reports co-generated with EchoChat were rated higher in diagnostic accuracy and completeness by two senior echocardiographers, and improved diagnostic performance from 72.78% to 82.75% in terms of F1 score over routine-reader only reports. These results demonstrate that integrating clinical reasoning into multimodal AI can democratize specialist expertise and transform the standard of cardiovascular care.

</details>


### [47] [SREdit: Scene Response Guided Appearance Editing for Projection Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32792)
*Yuqiao Xia,Yuchen Deng,Ziheng Chang,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SREdit，一种响应引导的投影式外观编辑框架，利用场景响应图约束预训练图像编辑器的采样过程，使编辑结果与固定物理对象保持配准并适配各区域可投影程度，从而提升投影补偿的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于投影映射的外观编辑依赖投影仪补偿，而补偿只能在可用投影范围内近似目标外观、无法修改外观本身。借用预训练LLM图像编辑器可直接由文本或参考图生成目标外观，但这些编辑器缺乏场景几何与场景响应的准确度量，输出常出现几何错位、明暗不一致或超出投影范围的变化，下游补偿无法修正。

Method: SREdit从重建场景中估计场景响应图，反映投影光照对不同表面区域的影响强度；在扩散采样早期用源外观进行约束以保持与固定物理对象的配准，并利用场景响应图在各表面区域自适应调节编辑强度，实现响应引导的编辑。

Result: 在两种预训练图像编辑器上的实验表明，SREdit提升了投影外观质量与复现保真度；每张图像额外耗时不足1秒，显存开销可忽略；用户研究中被试一致偏好SREdit的结果。

Conclusion: 将场景响应信息引入图像编辑采样过程，可有效弥合预训练编辑器与投影补偿之间的鸿沟，在低成本下显著改善投影式外观编辑的可用性与还原度。

Abstract: Projection-based appearance editing changes selected visual attributes of a physical object through projection mapping (PM). To display a desired appearance, PM typically uses projector compensation, which computes a projector input that counteracts the geometric and photometric distortions of the scene. Compensation can only approximate this appearance within the available projection range, and it cannot revise the appearance itself. Pretrained LLM-based image editors provide a direct way to produce the desired appearance from a text prompt or reference image; projector compensation can then attempt to reproduce the edited result through projection. However, these editors do not have an accurate measure of the scene geometry or scene response. Therefore, their outputs may contain geometric misalignment, inconsistent shading, or changes outside the available projection range, which downstream projector compensation cannot correct. To address this problem, we introduce SREdit, an efficient response-guided framework for projection-based appearance editing. SREdit estimates a scene response map from the reconstructed scene that reflects how strongly projected illumination can affect different surface regions. SREdit uses the source appearance to constrain the early sampling process and preserve registration with the fixed physical object, while the scene response map adapts the amount of editing across surface regions. Experiments across two pretrained image editors show that SREdit improves projected appearance quality and reproduction fidelity. It adds less than one second per image with negligible VRAM overhead. Participants also consistently preferred the results produced with SREdit in our user study.

</details>


### [48] [SPOOK: Sparse Pixel Optimization for Off-Manifold Knowledge in Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32835)
*Ankit Gupta,Akshat Pandya,Christoph Adami,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在空白画布上施加稀疏像素编辑（像素级爬山法），可在黑盒条件下使非语义输入进入OpenCLIP与DINOv3的目标语义盆地，揭示现代视觉编码器共享的离流形失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉编码器被广泛用作语义表征，但当输入远离自然图像流形时，嵌入相似度与人类可见语义之间的对应关系可能失效；作者希望探查这一"离流形"区域中表征几何的性质。

Method: 黑盒探查：对空白画布施加稀疏像素编辑，使用简单的像素级爬山优化；在ImageNet与Flickr30k上，分别用面向类别提示（OpenCLIP多模态对比编码器）和面向类别原型（DINOv3自监督视觉编码器）的目标，评估这些编辑后空白画布能否检索到指定目标。

Result: OpenCLIP上，优化输入取得很强的top-1与top-5定向检索性能；DINOv3上，优化输入获得高目标对齐度与接近顶部的原型排名。表明稀疏编辑的、视觉上无语义的空白输入能够进入语言对齐与自监督两类表征空间中的目标语义盆地。

Conclusion: 现代视觉编码器存在共享的离流形失效模式；稀疏空白画布优化可作为探查表征几何的简单探针。

Abstract: Modern vision encoders are widely used as semantic representations, but the relationship between embedding similarity and human-visible semantics can become unclear far from the natural-image manifold. We probe this regime in a black-box setting by applying sparse pixel edits to blank canvases with a simple pixel-level hill-climbing procedure. Across ImageNet and Flickr30k, we evaluate whether these edited blank canvases can retrieve chosen targets in multimodal contrastive encoders and self-supervised visual encoders. On OpenCLIP, inputs optimized toward class prompts achieve strong top-1 and top-5 targeted retrieval performance; on DINOv3, inputs optimized toward class prototypes achieve high target alignment and near-top prototype ranks. These results show that sparse edits on otherwise blank, visually non-semantic inputs can enter target semantic basins in both language-aligned and self-supervised visual representation spaces. Our findings identify a shared off-manifold failure mode of modern vision encoders and suggest sparse blank-canvas optimization as a simple probe of representation geometry.

</details>


### [49] [CityLift: Compositional 3D Urban Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32728)
*Haoran Gong,Jie Feng,Chenxu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityLift 通过耦合场景级空间建模与建筑级生成，从单张城市图像生成可编辑的独立纹理建筑网格，并保持一致的相对尺度与位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张城市图像生成可编辑 3D 场景需完整的建筑几何与一致的空间排布，但遮挡与透视歧义使得同时推断建筑完整几何及其相对尺度与位置十分困难；此外缺乏场景图像与单体 3D 建筑配对的训练数据。

Method: 框架分两阶段：(1) 通过耦合的场景流与实例流联合学习建筑形状与放置，在统一坐标系下生成粗糙稀疏结构作为脚手架；(2) 提出生成式空间继承（generative spatial inheritance），利用同一坐标映射将每个建筑的粗占据与场景条件 3D 特征场映射为局部体素，引导流模型生成更精细结构，并借助该映射将生成网格放回场景，保持与脚手架的空间关系。同时构建 CityLift-5K 数据集（4,997 个合成场景，含实例掩码、建筑网格与放置标注）。

Result: 在 CityLift-5K 及 UrbanScene3D、InstanceBuilding 的场景区块上测试，建筑几何与场景组成均得到改善。

Conclusion: 将场景级空间建模与建筑级生成相耦合、并以统一坐标映射实现空间继承，可有效从单图生成可编辑、空间一致的城市建筑场景。

Abstract: Generating editable 3D urban scenes from a single image requires detailed building assets with a coherent spatial arrangement. Occlusion and perspective ambiguity make it difficult to infer complete building geometry together with consistent relative scales and positions. We present CityLift, a framework that generates individual textured building meshes from an urban image by coupling scene-level spatial modeling with building-level synthesis. CityLift first learns building shape and placement jointly through coupled scene and instance streams, producing coarse sparse structures in a common coordinate system. We then introduce generative spatial inheritance, which maps each building's coarse occupancy and scene-conditioned 3D feature field into a local volume to guide a flow model that generates finer structure. The same coordinate mapping places the generated meshes back into the scene, preserving their spatial relationship to the scaffold. To address the scarcity of urban data pairing scene images with individual 3D buildings, we construct CityLift-5K, comprising 4,997 synthetic scenes with instance masks, building meshes, and placement annotations. Experiments on CityLift-5K and scene blocks from UrbanScene3D and InstanceBuilding demonstrate improved building geometry and scene composition. The project page is available at https://ranhaogong.github.io/CityLift/.

</details>


### [50] [FSVL: Taxonomic Fast-Slow Vision-to-Language Collaborative Framework for Interpretable Confusable Fish Species Identification and Knowledge Generation in the Middle Yangtze River](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31880)
*Meng Liang,Fucheng Wang,Lang Zhao,Jun Chen,Ziying Li,Yuxiang Zhao,Haozhuang Chi,Wenkai Fang,Qiong Zhou,Junlin Yang,Jinming Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FSVL 快慢双路径框架，融合细粒度视觉识别与基于 RAG 的 LLM 知识生成，用于长江中游 51 种易混淆淡水鱼类的准确鉴定与解说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长江十年禁渔提升了水生生物多样性监测与物种级鉴定的重要性，但近缘淡水鱼类形态差异细微，细粒度鉴定困难；现有深度学习方法难以兼顾精度与推理效率，LLM 在专业生物学知识输出上易产生无依据内容。

Method: 采用快慢双路径：快路径用轻量 ResNet50 对 51 种鱼类做初步分类，并将预设的易混淆物种组路由至慢路径；慢路径依据分类学准则，用 YOLOv8n 检测头部、背鳍、尾部等关键形态区域，利用形态测量与轮廓特征精细判别；后端接入检索增强的 Qwen3-14B，结合 soft tokens 与 LoRA 实现参数高效的领域知识生成。

Result: 607 幅图像测试集上快路径准确率 99.67%，级联纠正 2 个错误后整体达 100.00%（仍需独立样本与外部条件验证）；255 个知识生成样本中，学名准确率由 91.37% 升至 100.00%，属名由 90.20% 升至 98.82%，ROUGE-L 由 0.335 升至 0.655。

Conclusion: FSVL 在保证推理效率的同时实现高精度细粒度鱼类识别与可靠知识生成，可支撑智能生物多样性监测与渔业管理，但泛化能力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: The Yangtze River Basin is a critical region for freshwater biodiversity conservation. The implementation of the Yangtze River Ten-Year Fishing Ban has increased the importance of effective aquatic biodiversity monitoring and species-level identification. However, fine-grained identification of closely related freshwater fish species remains challenging due to subtle morphological differences. Existing deep learning methods often struggle to balance classification accuracy and inference efficiency, while LLMs may generate unsupported information when producing specialized biological knowledge. To address these challenges, this study proposes FSVL, a fast-slow dual-pathway framework integrating visual recognition with LLM-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). A lightweight ResNet50 fast path performs preliminary classification across 51 fish species in the Middle Yangtze River Basin and routes predefined groups of easily confused species to a slow path for refined identification. Guided by taxonomic criteria, the slow path uses YOLOv8n to detect key morphological regions, including the head, dorsal fin, and tail, leveraging morphometric and outline traits to improve discrimination among closely related species. The framework further integrates a retrieval-enhanced Qwen3-14B backend, using soft tokens and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient domain knowledge generation. On the evaluated test set of 607 images, the fast classifier achieved 99.67% accuracy, whereas the cascade corrected the two errors, yielding 100.00% overall accuracy; further validation on independent specimens and external conditions remains needed. Across 255 knowledge generation samples, scientific name accuracy rises from 91.37% to 100.00%, genus accuracy from 90.20% to 98.82%, and ROUGE-L from 0.335 to 0.655, supporting intelligent biodiversity monitoring and fisheries management.

</details>


### [51] [IFC-SSL-Bench: Benchmarking Self-Supervised Learning for Annotation-Efficient Multispectral Imaging Flow Cytometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31848)
*Angelo Jovin Yamachui Sitcheu,Roxana-Teodora Fetescu,Thomas Lautenschlager,Nils Friederich,Linus Schilpp,Stephan Allgeier,Luca Deininger,Daniel Hübschmann,Ralf Mikut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IFC-SSL-Bench模块化基准，系统评估6种SSL方法×4种骨干网络在2个异质成像流式细胞术数据集上的表现，量化标注效率并给出部署指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像流式细胞术（IFC）产生大规模多光谱数据，但专家标注需求成为监督深度学习模型开发的瓶颈；自监督学习（SSL）虽有潜力，但其在IFC中的应用零散、缺乏系统性基准评测。

Method: 构建IFC-SSL-Bench模块化框架，在2个异质数据集、4种骨干网络上评测6种SSL方法；通过1%–100%多档训练集标注比例评估下游分类性能，量化标注效率并识别最优配置，同时分析ImageNet初始化与随机初始化的迁移动态。

Result: SSL仅用5%标注即可在形态学分类上匹配100%标注的全监督基线，在低于5%的低数据区间优势明显；对比式MoCo-v2搭配ConvNeXt系列表现最佳（免疫突触分类用ConvNeXtV1-T，形态学分类用ConvNeXtV2-T）；ImageNet初始化利于形态学分类，随机初始化更适域特异的免疫突触分类；1%标注下SSL使结构复杂类别的类级F1提升超35个百分点。

Conclusion: 总结出一套适用于高通量IFC自监督工作流的统一部署指南，并将该基准作为资源开源，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Imaging Flow Cytometry (IFC) generates large multispectral datasets, yet the need for expert annotations bottlenecks the development of supervised deep learning models. While Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) offers a solution, its application to IFC remains fragmented and lacks systematic benchmarking. To address this, we present IFC-SSL-Bench, a modular framework for evaluating SSL in IFC. We benchmark six SSL approaches across four backbones on two heterogeneous datasets. By assessing downstream classification performance across annotation regimes of the training sets (1%-100%), we quantify annotation efficiency and identify optimal configurations for high-dimensional biological imaging. SSL matches the performance of fully supervised baselines trained on 100% of annotated data while using as little as 5% of annotations for morphological classification, with advantages in low-data regimes below 5%. Across both datasets, the contrastive approach MoCo-v2 paired with ConvNeXt backbones emerged as the top-performing configuration, with ConvNeXtV1-T best for immunological synapse classification and ConvNeXtV2-T for morphological classification. We identify a clear pattern in transfer dynamics: ImageNet initialization benefits morphological classification, consistent with its visual similarity to natural images, whereas random initialization better captures the more domain-specific immunological synapse classification. SSL elevates class-specific F1-scores by over 35 percentage points for structurally complex classes at 1% of annotated data. We synthesize these insights into a unified set of guidelines for deploying self-supervised workflows in high-throughput IFC, and release the benchmark as a resource for future work.

</details>


### [52] [Hair Segmentation and Skin Characterization on Macroscopic Dermatological Images for Laser Treatment Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32609)
*Alex Navarro Navarro,Marc Arriaga Hidalgo,Carlos Moreno Rubio,Maxime Talarmain,Noelia Vaquero Gallardo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一套面向宏观皮肤图像的毛发分割与皮肤表征统一流程，融合定制YOLOv9实例分割与经典图像处理，结合分光光度计颜色校准，可客观提取光型、毛发密度、颜色与粗细等参数，在含短毛/剃毛的自建数据集上取得约0.80的mAP@50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激光脱毛、IPL、血管激光及皮肤光疗等光基设备高度依赖主观人工设参或未校准的点式色度计；而已有毛发分割研究多基于受控条件下的皮肤镜数据集，难以迁移到真实临床宏观图像，缺少客观、可复现的参数基础。

Method: 构建单一大流程：以定制训练的YOLOv9变体完成实例分割，并在同一工作流内嵌经典图像处理以提取毛发密度、颜色、粗细及皮肤光型等客观参数；引入基于分光光度计测量的颜色校准环节，以消除光照条件对特征估计的影响；数据为复现真实激光治疗场景的宏观图像，覆盖预处理阶段的短毛、剃毛样本。

Result: 在自建宏观数据集上取得具竞争力的分割性能，mAP@50约为0.80；颜色校准使特征估计在变化光照下保持准确；成功输出光型、毛发密度、颜色与粗细等客观基础特征。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该流程为未来自动化激光参数设定提供客观基础特征的可行性，并具备向更广泛光基医学治疗扩展的潜力；后续需在更多受试者、器械与光照条件下进一步验证泛化性。

Abstract: Current light-based devices, such as laser hair removal, intense pulsed light (IPL), vascular laser therapy, and dermatologic phototherapy, are widely used in clinical dermatology but rely heavily on subjective manual parameterization or uncalibrated point-based colorimeters. To overcome these limitations, this work presents a unified system for hair segmentation and skin characterization in macroscopic dermatological images. Unlike most existing literature that relies on dermoscopic datasets captured under highly controlled conditions, our approach is specifically designed for macroscopic images that replicate real clinical laser treatments. The proposed pipeline integrates a custom-trained variant of the YOLOv9 deep learning architecture for instance segmentation and classical image processing within a single workflow to extract objective skin and hair parameters such as phototype, hair density, color, and thickness. A color calibration stage using spectrophotometric measurements ensures accurate feature estimation regardless of illumination conditions. Experimental results show competitive segmentation metrics (mAP@50 $\approx$ 0.80) on a custom dataset featuring short, shaved hairs, typical of pre-treatment scenarios. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed pipeline in providing the objective, foundational skin and hair features required for future automated laser parameterization, paving the way for its extension to broader light-based medical therapies.

</details>


### [53] [VERA-Hand: A Verified Multidimensional Benchmark for 2D Hand Pose Estimation in Third-Person Dynamic Gesture Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32517)
*Gibran Benitez-Garcia,Hiroki Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VERA-Hand基准，从检测、定位、身份识别、时序稳定性四个维度及联合Pose-F1指标评测手部姿态估计器，发现无估计器全面占优，且暴露了仅定位评测无法发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部姿态估计器普遍采用PCK、MPJPE等逐帧定位指标，仅在匹配的预测上计算，忽视了漏检/误检的手、左右手身份错误及时序不稳定问题，导致评测不全面。

Method: 构建VERA-Hand基准，对10,690只手提供人工校验的帧级2D标注，从检测、定位、身份识别、时序稳定性四个互补维度评测，并提出要求检测正确且定位准确的联合Pose-F1指标；对6个涵盖手部专用、全身、重建式和基础模型范式的估计器进行评测，并做后处理清理案例研究与分层分析。

Result: 无估计器全面占优，各维度排名显著变化；暴露了定位评测与下游评测难以察觉的失败，例如某近期基础模型在超过85%帧中存在左右手重叠；后处理案例验证了基准可用于检验纠正干预，分层分析显示鲁棒性随运动模糊类别与手部区域变化。

Conclusion: VERA-Hand提供了更全面的手部姿态评测协议，揭示现有多指标评测的盲区，公开标注、协议与代码以推动更可靠的评测。

Abstract: Hand landmarks serve as input for gesture recognition, sign language understanding, and touchless human-computer interaction. Yet hand pose estimators are typically evaluated with frame-wise localization metrics such as PCK or MPJPE, which are computed over matched predictions and overlook missed or spurious hands, handedness errors, and temporal instability. To address these limitations, we present VERA-Hand, a benchmark that evaluates estimators using manually verified, frame-level 2D annotations for 10,690 hands across four complementary dimensions (detection, localization, identification, and temporal stability), as well as a joint Pose-F1 metric that requires both correct detection and accurate localization. Evaluating six estimators spanning hand-specific, whole-body, reconstruction-based, and foundation-model paradigms, we find that no estimator dominates: rankings reorder sharply across dimensions. VERA-Hand also exposes failures that localization-only and downstream evaluations may not reveal, including cross-hand overlap in over 85% of frames for a recent foundation model. A post-hoc cleanup case study demonstrates how the benchmark can validate corrective interventions, while stratified analyses show that robustness varies across motion-blur classes and hand regions. We release the verified annotations, protocol, and code at https://gibranbenitez.github.io/VERA_Hand/

</details>


### [54] [Augmentation Strategy for DINO in Glaucoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32511)
*Ashish Kumar Meena,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视网膜眼底图像，作者发现DINO默认的SSL增强策略产生不可靠表征并损害青光眼分类，遂按"裁剪视图信息冗余最小、保留临床信号"原则重设增强策略，使表征偏向诊断关键特征；在七个基准数据集上用冻结特征训练线性分类器即取得强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习（SSL）在医学图像表征学习上有潜力，但在自然图像上验证的增强策略难以迁移到专业医学影像域。作者希望解决DINO默认增强在眼底图像上失效的问题。

Method: 对基于知识蒸馏的DINO框架进行实证分析，证明其默认增强策略在视网膜眼底图像上产生不可靠表征；以"有效裁剪视图应共享最少冗余信息且保留临床相关信号"为准则，重新设计DINO的增强策略，引导表征偏向诊断关键特征而非低层图像统计。

Result: 在冻结表征上训练线性分类器进行青光眼分类，在七个基准数据集上实现强泛化性能。

Conclusion: 针对特定医学模态定制SSL增强策略（面向诊断信号的视图设计）可显著改善DINO表征质量与下游泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a promising approach to medical image representation learning, yet augmentation strategies of SSL frameworks validated on natural images do not reliably transfer to specialized medical imaging domains. We analyze DINO, a knowledge-distillation based SSL framework, and empirically demonstrate that its default augmentation strategy produces unreliable representations for retinal fundus images, degrading downstream glaucoma classification performance. Guided by the principle that effective cropped views should share minimal redundant information while retaining clinically relevant signal, we redesign DINO's augmentation strategy. Our approach biases the learned representations toward diagnostically critical features rather than low-level image statistics. We evaluate our method for glaucoma classification by training a linear classifier on the frozen representations, achieving strong generalization across seven benchmark datasets. URL for code and weights: https://github.com/ashish-256/task_dino

</details>


### [55] [K-CLUE: A Segmentation-to-Clustering Framework for Ki67 Cell Typing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32493)
*Ana Beatriz Vieira,Sara P. Oliveira,Diana Montezuma,João Fraga,Jaime S. Cardoso,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究冻结的预训练视觉表征在无需标注的Ki67细胞分型中的作用，提出基于K-CLUE框架（分割核、预训练编码器、PCA降维、k-means聚类）的方法，发现聚类对编码器选择高度敏感，但通过白化主成分可消除该依赖，并在MIST和BCData上优于DeepLIIF与IHCScoreGAN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌中Ki67是关键的肿瘤增殖标志物，但传统从免疫组化图像中量化Ki67依赖针对每个数据集单独训练的有监督分类器，需要大量人工标注，成本高且泛化性受限。作者希望探索能否利用冻结的预训练视觉表征实现无需标注的Ki67细胞分型。

Method: 采用K-CLUE框架：先分割出单个细胞核；用预训练编码器为每个核生成表征；对得到的嵌入做主成分分析降维；再用k-means聚类将Ki67阳性与阴性核分开，全程无需任务特定训练或人工标注。同时对比七种编码器，分析嵌入空间的方差结构，并考察白化主成分与手工颜色表征的效果。

Result: 直接对原始主成分聚类对编码器选择高度敏感，七个编码器间Ki67阳性F1差异达44分；但Ki67状态在每种表征上都线性可分，有监督探针AUC均超过0.98。分析表明当DAB相关变异占据主导方差方向时聚类才成功。白化保留的主成分后，编码器间F1差异缩小至1.00分。手工颜色表征也具有竞争力。端到端评估中，K-CLUE在MIST上阳性/阴性F1为85.00%和81.91%，在BCData上为79.59%和81.16%，两个数据集上均优于DeepLIIF和IHCScoreGAN。

Conclusion: 预训练表征能够支持无需标注的Ki67分型，前提是其方差结构得到妥善处理（如白化主成分）；这为解决有监督逐数据集训练的标注负担提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ki67 is a key marker of tumor proliferation in breast cancer, but its quantification from immunohistochemistry images normally depends on supervised classifiers trained per dataset. We examine the role of frozen pretrained visual representations in annotation-free Ki67 cell typing. To this end, we use K-CLUE, a framework that segments individual nuclei, represents each nucleus with a pretrained encoder, reduces the resulting embeddings with principal component analysis, and separates Ki67-positive from Ki67-negative nuclei using k-means clustering, without task-specific training or manual annotations. Clustering the raw components is highly sensitive to the choice of encoder: across seven encoders, the Ki67-positive F1-score on matched MIST nuclei varies by 44 points, although Ki67 status remains linearly decodable from every representation, with supervised probe AUCs above 0.98. Analysis of the embedding spaces shows that clustering succeeds when DAB-related variation occupies the leading directions of variance. Whitening the retained principal components removes this dependence, reducing the spread across encoders to 1.00 F1-score point. Handcrafted color representations are also competitive, confirming that Ki67 status is strongly reflected in chromatic information, but they require an explicit choice of color space. In end-to-end evaluation, K-CLUE reaches F1-scores of 85.00% and 81.91% for Ki67-positive and Ki67-negative nuclei on MIST, and 79.59% and 81.16% on BCData, outperforming DeepLIIF and IHCScoreGAN on both datasets. These results show that pretrained representations can support annotation-free Ki67 typing when their variance structure is accounted for.

</details>


### [56] [Learning Fine-Grained Visual Classification with Text as Privileged Information via Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32442)
*Aishwarya Venkataramanan,Christian Ickler,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FG-LUPI框架，在训练阶段利用文本属性描述作为特权信息，通过多级蒸馏将多模态教师知识迁移到纯图像学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉分类需区分高度相似类别，文本属性描述可提供判别性语义监督，但测试时不可用，限制了直接部署。

Method: 基于Learning Using Privileged Information范式：训练一个融合图像块与文本属性的多模态教师（门控交叉注意力，使语义属性调制视觉表示）；再通过预测级、图像块级、注意力级的多级蒸馏，将全局预测、块表示和注意力模式对齐，蒸馏到纯图像学生模型。

Result: 在多个FGVC基准上相较强ViT基线持续提升分类准确率；在全部六个基准上基于ECE评估显示预测校准更优。

Conclusion: 文本属性监督不仅提升细粒度识别性能，还改善模型置信度可靠性，同时保留纯图像推理的效率与实用性。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual classification (FGVC) requires distinguishing between highly similar categories by focusing on subtle, localized visual differences. Recent multimodal methods have demonstrated that textual attribute descriptions can provide valuable semantic supervision by explicitly describing discriminative object characteristics. However, these descriptions are typically unavailable at test time, limiting their direct use in deployment. We propose FG-LUPI, a Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI)-based framework that leverages attribute descriptions during training. We train a multimodal teacher that integrates image patches and textual attributes via gated cross-attention, allowing semantic attributes to condition visual representations. We distill this knowledge into an image-only student using multi-level distillation at the prediction, patch, and attention levels, aligning global predictions, patch representations, and attention patterns with the teacher. This encourages the student to inherit attention structure learned from multimodal supervision while maintaining image-only inference. Experiments on multiple FGVC benchmarks show consistent improvements in classification accuracy over strong ViT-based baselines. Furthermore, evaluation using Expected Calibration Error (ECE) shows that FG-LUPI also produces better-calibrated predictions across all six benchmarks. These results suggest that textual attribute supervision can benefit not only fine-grained recognition performance but also the reliability of model confidence, while preserving the efficiency and practicality of image-only inference.

</details>


### [57] [AlphaAnything: Extending Pretrained RGB Generators to RGBA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32413)
*Qijie Wang,Zhixin Xu,Zhe Li,Haowei Zhu,Honghao Qiao,Bo Peng,Dawei Li,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlphaAnything：在预训练RGB生成器的原始潜空间中直接扩展出RGBA生成，无需RGBA重建预训练，通过LoRA+冻结VAE解码器上的alpha分支与单向特征共享实现，并提出Endpoint-Aware Alpha Loss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBA生成方法通常通过重建学习透明度专属表示，需额外训练阶段，且可能改变继承自预训练模型的RGB解码函数。

Method: 轻量框架：在生成Transformer中插入LoRA模块，在冻结的RGB VAE解码器上附加alpha分支；采用单向RGB→alpha特征共享，使不透明度监督塑造生成潜变量，同时保证固定潜变量下RGB解码与预训练解码器完全一致；提出EA Loss，从合成所用的前景-背景互补权重导出不透明度监督，支持精确端点与连续半透明并对局部alpha误差加权。

Result: 在多种预训练RGB骨干上，文本到RGBA与图像到RGBA任务均展现有竞争力的生成质量；受控消融验证了共享潜空间训练设计与EA Loss的有效性。

Conclusion: AlphaAnything可轻量、无重建预训练地将RGB生成器扩展为RGBA生成，且完整保留原RGB解码行为。

Abstract: Existing RGBA generation methods commonly learn a transparency-specific representation through reconstruction, introducing an additional training stage and potentially altering the RGB decoding function inherited from pretrained models. We present AlphaAnything, a lightweight framework that extends pretrained RGB generators to RGBA directly in their original latent space, without RGBA reconstruction pretraining. AlphaAnything inserts LoRA modules into the generative transformer and attaches an alpha branch to the frozen RGB VAE decoder. Unidirectional RGB-to-alpha feature sharing allows opacity supervision to shape the generative latent while guaranteeing that, for any fixed latent, RGB decoding remains identical to that of the pretrained decoder. We further introduce Endpoint-Aware Alpha Loss (EA Loss), which derives opacity supervision from the complementary foreground--background weights used in compositing. It supports exact opacity endpoints and continuous partial transparency while emphasizing localized alpha errors. Experiments on text-to-RGBA and image-to-RGBA tasks across multiple pretrained RGB backbones demonstrate competitive generation quality, and controlled ablations validate the shared-latent training design and EA Loss.

</details>


### [58] [OpenHome: From Perception to Insight in Smart-Home Camera Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31986)
*Xianglong Wang,Neil Rodrigues,Pei Guo,Yiru Fang,Kevin Beussman,Shuyang Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Liangju Li,Shunyao Xu,Zhongwei Cheng,Wei Li,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenHome，一个纯视觉、四选一的选择题基准，用于评估智能家居摄像头视频理解能力，包含 1,118 段真实视频上的 2,254 道问题，按感知、推理、洞见三个能力层级组织 10 类任务，并对 32 个 VLM 进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 基准主要基于网络精剪视频（通常含字幕、旁白），而智能家居摄像头视频无字幕旁白、视角多变、光照与场景多样、视觉信号稀疏且上下文有限；现有基准无法充分评估模型在该领域的真实能力，因此需要填补这一评测空白。

Method: 构建 OpenHome 基准：采集 1,118 段真实智能家居视频片段，设计 2,254 道四选一纯视觉多选题；将 10 项任务组织为三层能力体系——Perception（可见内容识别）、Reasoning（因果、空间、时间关系）、Insight（主体意图、支撑证据、屋主合理响应）；并给出任务设计、数据流水线与评测协议，对 32 个闭源与开放权重 VLM 进行测试。

Result: 最佳模型 Gemini 4 Argon 总体准确率 81.2%；前沿闭源模型在各层级均优于开放权重模型，最佳闭源模型相对最佳开放权重模型在 Perception 上领先 16.3%、Reasoning 上领先 22.4%、Insight 上领先 37.4%。

Conclusion: 智能家居视频理解仍是 VLM 的薄弱环节，尤其在高阶洞见能力上差距显著；OpenHome 提供了面向该场景的标准化评测工具，揭示闭源与开源模型的能力分层，为后续模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have been increasingly adopted in consumer smart-home camera systems to interpret recorded videos and provide insights. However, video clips captured from smart-home cameras are different from curated web videos dominating existing VLM benchmarks. Smart-home video clips often do not contain subtitles or narration and are captured from different viewing angles, under various lighting conditions, and in diverse scenarios. However, the capabilities of the model in this domain, under sparse visual signals and limited context, are not fully assessed by existing VLM benchmarks. To close this gap, we introduce OpenHome, a visual-only, four-option multiple-choice benchmark for evaluating smart-home video understanding. It contains 2,254 questions on 1,118 real-world video clips and organizes 10 tasks into three capability levels. Perception evaluates visible content, Reasoning evaluates causal, spatial, and temporal relationships, and Insight evaluates subject intent, supporting evidence and appropriate homeowner responses. Based on the dataset, we describe the task design, data pipeline and evaluation protocol, and report results for 32 closed-source and open-weight VLMs. The best model, Gemini 4 Argon, reaches 81.2% overall accuracy. Frontier closed-source models outperform open-weight models on every level. The best closed-source model leads the best open-weight model by 16.3% on Perception, 22.4% on Reasoning and 37.4% on Insight.

</details>


### [59] [Organizing Temporal Evidence in Hyperbolic Space for Video Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32317)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Jiaxuan Lin,Gang Hua,Le Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双曲几何框架HTEO，将查询感知的时间记忆组织到Poincaré球中，通过径向证据组织（REO）与正交角度细化（OAR）建模时间证据结构，再经残差记忆更新注入Transformer解码器，提升视频时刻检索性能并提供可解释的几何组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索方法在欧氏空间做视频-文本对齐，表示嵌入于平坦几何中。而精确定位需在多种grounding状态（目标区域、边界过渡、上下文干扰）上组织查询相关证据，这些状态具有不同的时间结构与相关性模式，欧氏表示缺乏联合刻画这些关系的显式几何结构。

Method: 1) 将时间表示映射到Poincaré球，在双曲空间组织查询感知的时间记忆；2) 径向证据组织（REO）调整时间token的径向位置以编码证据间的结构差异；3) 正交角度细化（OAR）通过正交旋转增强查询条件语义对齐；4) 将组织后的表示投影回欧氏切空间，以轻量残差记忆更新注入解码器，保留原始跨模态记忆；5) 引入径向结构正则与角度相关性监督分别指导证据组织与语义对齐。

Result: 在多个基准上开展大量实验与分析，HTEO在时间定位性能上取得提升，并对时间证据提供了可解释的几何组织。

Conclusion: 从几何视角出发，在双曲空间中显式组织时间证据可有效提升视频时刻检索的定位精度，且该组织方式具备可解释性；通过残差注入可无侵入地兼容标准Transformer解码器。

Abstract: Video moment retrieval aims to localize a query-relevant temporal segment in an untrimmed video. Existing methods typically perform video-text alignment in Euclidean space, where representations are embedded in a flat geometry. However, accurate moment localization requires organizing query-relevant evidence across multiple grounding states, where target regions, boundary transitions, and contextual distractors exhibit distinct temporal structures and relevance patterns. Such Euclidean representations lack an explicit geometry to jointly capture these relationships, motivating us to explore structured temporal evidence organization from a geometric perspective. Specifically, we propose Hyperbolic Temporal Evidence Organization (HTEO), a geometric framework that organizes query-aware temporal memory in hyperbolic space. HTEO maps temporal representations into a Poincaré ball and organizes temporal evidence through Radial Evidence Organization (REO) and Orthogonal Angular Refinement (OAR). REO adjusts temporal tokens' radial positions to encode the structural differences among temporal evidence, while OAR enhances query-conditioned semantic alignment through orthogonal rotation. The organized representation is then projected back to the Euclidean tangent space and injected into the decoder through a lightweight residual memory update, enabling hyperbolic geometric cues to be incorporated into standard Transformer decoders while preserving the original cross-modal memory. Furthermore, we introduce radial structure regularization and angular relevance supervision to explicitly guide temporal evidence organization and query-conditioned semantic alignment, respectively. Extensive experiments and analyses across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HTEO improves temporal localization while providing an interpretable geometric organization of temporal evidence.

</details>


### [60] [GeoLEON-CLIP: Graph-Anchored Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Geographic Region Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32311)
*Radoslaw Malek,Szymon Soltysiak,Aleksandra Kawala-Sterniuk,Piotr Szymanski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeoLEON-CLIP，以 OpenStreetMap 图嵌入为语义锚，对同一 H3 区域的三模态（图、卫星影像、文本）做对称三元对比对齐；并以链式跨模态召回 R@A@B 验证图模态的“锚定”作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理区域本质上是多模态的：卫星影像承载视觉形态，文本承载可解释语义，地图结构刻画城市上下文。现有地理空间对比方法多只对齐图文或图—位置对，结构化的地图数据未被充分利用。

Method: GeoLEON-CLIP 框架：对每个 H3 索引区域取三视图——OSM 派生图嵌入、卫星影像裁剪、文本描述；使用冻结的预训练 LEON 图嵌入作为视觉与语言之间的语义锚；冻结图/图/文编码器，经可训练残差投影头连接，用对称三模态对比目标训练，并以图模态为中间枢纽的链式召回评估。

Result: 图—图、图—文对齐效果强；图—图检索在混合分辨率候选池中取得最佳跨模态表现。R@1@1 下，image→graph→text 与 text→graph→image 分别比直接图文检索高 4.8 与 4.4 个点，仅用一个中间单元。下游探针任务增益依任务而异，Airbnb 与北京房价任务提升最大。

Conclusion: OSM 图结构可作为锚点，统一并强化融合地图、影像与语言的多模态表示；图作为中间模态的“路由”可提升跨模态检索效率与精度。

Abstract: Geographic regions are multimodal: satellite imagery captures visual morphology, text conveys human-interpretable semantics, and map structures describe urban context. Existing geospatial contrastive methods typically align image-text or image-location pairs, underusing structured map data. We introduce GeoLEON-CLIP, a graph-anchored contrastive framework that aligns three views of each H3-indexed region: an OpenStreetMap-derived graph embedding, a satellite image crop, and a textual description. Frozen pretrained LEON graph embeddings provide a semantic anchor between vision and language. Frozen graph, image, and text encoders are connected by trainable residual projection heads and trained with a symmetric tri-modal contrastive objective. Experiments show strong graph-image and graph-text alignment; graph-image retrieval achieves the best cross-modal performance in a mixed-resolution pool. To evaluate the anchoring hypothesis, we propose chained cross-modal recall $\mathrm{R}@A@B$, which routes retrieval through the graph modality and compares it with direct retrieval under a matched candidate budget. At $\mathrm{R}@1@1$, image$\rightarrow$graph$\rightarrow$text and text$\rightarrow$graph$\rightarrow$image outperform direct image-text retrieval by 4.8 and 4.4 points, respectively, despite using a single intermediate cell. Downstream probes show task-dependent gains, with the largest improvements on Airbnb and Beijing housing. These results indicate that OpenStreetMap graph structure can anchor multimodal representations combining maps, imagery, and language.

</details>


### [61] [OlmoEarth v1.3: Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32294)
*Gabriel Tseng,Favyen Bastani,Patrick Alan Johnson,Yawen Zhang,Piper Wolters,Ando Shah,Hadrien Sablon,Joseph Redmon,Heather DeHaven,Rachel Ratner,Sasha Levy,Jordan Steward,Katrina Midgley,Andrew Howe,Hunter Pitelka,Ted Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OlmoEarth v1.3 将多模态、多时相遥感数据压缩为 64/128 维向量，生成 SOTA 嵌入，且训练算力仅为 v1 和 v1.2 的 18% 与 53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要把多模态、多时相遥感数据凝练为紧凑且易用的向量表示，并降低训练成本。

Method: 训练新模型 OlmoEarth v1.3，作为编码器骨干将多模态、多时相遥感输入映射为 64 或 128 维的单一嵌入向量。

Result: 产出 SOTA 级别嵌入；作为编码器骨干与 v1、v1.2 竞争力相当，但训练 GPU 小时数仅为二者的 18% 和 53%；并基于此计算全球、年度化 10m 嵌入并依 OlmoEarth artifact 许可发布。

Conclusion: OlmoEarth v1.3 以更低训练成本获得竞争性骨干性能与领先嵌入质量，并公开全球 10m 年度嵌入产品。

Abstract: We make some OlmoEarth embeddings. To do this, we train a new model: OlmoEarth v1.3. OlmoEarth v1.3 is designed to condense multimodal, multitemporal remote sensing data into a single digestible 64 or 128-dimensional vector. OlmoEarth v1.3 produces state-of-the-art embeddings. In addition, OlmoEarth v1.3 is a strong encoder backbone -- competitive with OlmoEarth v1 and v1.2 -- while requiring 18% and 53% of the GPU hours to train, respectively. We use OlmoEarth v1.3 to compute global, annualized 10m embeddings, which we release under the OlmoEarth artifact license.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Pattern Matching in 3D MLLMs: Part-Level Functional Reasoning for Generalizable Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32262)
*Weihong Li,Hao Jiang,Yaohua Zha,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3D MLLMs rely on pattern matching and fail on geometrically similar objects requiring functional reasoning. PFIR (part-level functional inference for recognition) reverses the "function-as-annotation" information flow, building 507K functional CoT QA pairs on PartNeXt segmentation with a progressive curriculum ending in dual-objective GRPO; it raises zero-shot accuracy 60.5%→66.7% under strict dual OOD, with 2.6× larger gains on ambiguous samples.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D多模态大模型依靠模式匹配识别物体，当物体几何相似、需借助功能语义消歧（如梳妆台 vs. 衣柜）时失效。作者用Geometric Confusion Score (GCS) 量化该问题：ModelNet40最模糊20%样本准确率仅33.7%，最不模糊20%为84.0%。作者进一步指出现有数据中的“function-as-annotation”缺陷——功能语言只是装饰已知身份，而非支持推理，并据此假设：反转该信息流可把增益集中在最模糊样本上。

Method: PFIR以PartNeXt部件分割为基础构建数据管线，生成507K条带功能链式思维（geometry→function→identity）的QA对；采用渐进式课程，最终落到双目标GRPO强化学习：严格功能推理 + 面向Objaverse多样分布的描述性 grounding。两个RL目标产生近似正交的任务向量（cos=0.056），通过Task Arithmetic进行组合。

Result: 在严格双OOD协议（Objaverse→ModelNet40；XYZRGB→纯XYZ）下，零样本准确率由60.5%提升至66.7%，且增益在几何模糊样本上集中程度高2.6倍。

Conclusion: 部件级功能推理是通向更具泛化性3D识别的有效路径；反转“功能作为标注”的信息流能把提升聚焦于歧义样本，双RL目标的低相关性与可加性亦得到验证。

Abstract: 3D MLLMs recognize objects via pattern matching, which fails when geometrically similar objects require functional reasoning to disambiguate (e.g., dresser vs. wardrobe). Our per-sample Geometric Confusion Score (GCS) exposes this failure: baseline accuracy is 33.7% on ModelNet40's most ambiguous 20% versus 84.0% on its least ambiguous 20%. We identify a function-as-annotation pattern, in which functional language decorates a known identity instead of supporting inference, and hypothesize that reversing this information flow should concentrate improvements on the most ambiguous samples. PFIR (Part-level Functional Inference for Recognition) reverses that flow. Built on PartNeXt part segmentation, its data pipeline produces 507K QA pairs with functional CoTs (geometry $\to$ function $\to$ identity), and its progressive curriculum culminates in dual-objective GRPO: strict functional reasoning and descriptive grounding for Objaverse's diverse distribution. The two RL objectives yield nearly orthogonal task vectors ($\cos = 0.056$), enabling composition via Task Arithmetic. Under a strict dual OOD protocol (Objaverse $\to$ ModelNet40; XYZRGB $\to$ XYZ-only), PFIR raises zero-shot accuracy from 60.5% to 66.7%, with gains concentrated $2.6\times$ more on geometrically ambiguous samples---supporting part-level functional reasoning as an effective route to more generalizable 3D recognition. Code: https://github.com/this233/pfir-3d.

</details>


### [63] [DS-BiCA-Net: Pair-Preserving Cross-Temporal Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32200)
*Licheng Zhang,Naveed Akhtar,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DS-BiCA-Net，将图像内处理与跨时相处理拆分为独立模块，并在编码器中保持双流，实现对各模块与各时相方向贡献的可分离度量；在 LEVIR-CC 上 S_m^* 达 82.65，Dubai-CC 达 66.72。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RSICC 方法在重复处理中直接合并图像对，或将图像内关系与跨图像关系混在一次操作中，削弱了源图像身份信息，且使两个时相方向保持隐式。缺乏独立的图像内模块、跨时相模块以及各方向的独立通路，导致无法单独衡量各部分贡献，后续模型设计缺少可依据的证据。

Method: DS-BiCA-Net：编码器全程保持两幅图像为独立流。窗口化状态空间模块（windowed state-space）建模单图内部上下文；双向交叉注意力模块沿两条共享投影的有向路径比较两图。两模块采用相同的前归一化残差架构，仅 mixer 不同；双流仅在聚合头处融合，从而任一模块、任一方向均可单独移除并度量其贡献。

Result: 在 LEVIR-CC 与 Dubai-CC 上进行受控实验，考察算子交互、时相方向与模块顺序：图像内精化单独使用无收益，仅在后续接双向交叉注意力时才产生增益，且该顺序在参数量与 FLOPs 匹配条件下优于相反顺序；双向模块优于任一单向时相方向。LEVIR-CC 上 S_m^* 为 82.65（对比的已发表结果中最高），Dubai-CC 上无几何增强条件下为 66.72（对比结果中第二高）。

Conclusion: 通过模块级与方向级的解耦设计，RSICC 中各组件的作用得以独立量化，并证实"先图像内精化、后双向跨时相比较"的顺序最为有效，为后续模型设计提供了可复用的消融证据。

Abstract: Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) describes in natural language the semantic changes between two images of the same scene captured at different times. Previous methods commonly merge the pair during repeated processing or combine intra-image and cross-image relations in one operation, weakening source identity and leaving the two temporal directions implicit. Without separate modules for intra-image and cross-temporal processing and a separate path for each direction, the contribution of each part cannot be measured on its own, so it remains unclear which part matters most, and the design of later models has no such evidence to build on. To address these issues, we propose DS-BiCA-Net, which assigns the two kinds of processing to separate modules and keeps the two images as separate streams throughout the encoder. A windowed state-space module models context within each image, and a bidirectional cross-attention module compares the two images along two directed paths that share their projections. The two modules use the same pre-normalisation residual architecture and differ only in their mixers, and the two streams are fused only in the aggregation head, so each module and each direction can be removed and its contribution measured on its own. Controlled experiments on LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC examine operator interaction, temporal direction and module order, showing that intra-image refinement carries no benefit alone and becomes beneficial only when followed by bidirectional cross-attention, an order that outperforms the reverse at matched parameter count and FLOPs. The bidirectional module also outperforms either temporal direction alone. Our method reaches an S_m^* of 82.65 on LEVIR-CC, the highest among the published results compared, and 66.72 on Dubai-CC without geometric augmentation, the second-highest among the published results compared.

</details>


### [64] [Separating Angular Alignment from Radial Ordering in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32146)
*Mingyang Gao,Yuwei Wu,Shiyu Luo,Wei Wu,Xiaomeng Fan,Qihao Lv,Haoyu Zhang,Rui Gao,Bingzhen Liu,Zhi Gao,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AURORA，通过角度对齐与径向-角度序锥（RAOC）解耦语义对齐与蕴含建模，解决双曲视觉语言模型中径向坐标同时编码匹配与层级的几何冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲视觉语言预训练目标将语义对齐与蕴含建模纠缠，迫使径向坐标同时编码语义匹配与层级结构，产生冲突的几何约束。

Method: 角度对齐目标捕获语义相似性并保留径向结构表示层级；Radial-Angular Order Cone（RAOC）由父子相对径向差距决定角度容差，替代传统蕴含锥，并嵌入层级蕴含对比训练以捕捉偏序关系；另引入范数正则项防止径向退化以保证训练稳定。

Result: 在数百万图文对上训练，于零样本分类、图文检索、层级推理与组合理解任务上一致优于强欧氏与双曲视觉语言基线。

Conclusion: 解耦语义对齐与层级蕴含建模是有效的，可缓解几何约束冲突并提升双曲多模态表示性能。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models are well suited to modeling the hierarchical structure of multimodal data, because hyperbolic volume grows exponentially with radius, enabling low-distortion representations of tree-like taxonomies from general to specific concepts. However, existing pretraining objectives for hyperbolic vision-language models entangle semantic alignment with entailment modeling, forcing radial coordinates to simultaneously encode semantic matching and hierarchical structure, leading to conflicting geometric constraints. In this paper, we introduce AURORA, a hyperbolic vision-language framework that decouples the objectives through angular alignment and a novel Radial-Angular Order Cone (RAOC) for entailment modeling. Angular alignment captures semantic similarity while preserving the radial structure for representing hierarchies. Unlike conventional entailment cones, RAOC determines angular allowance from relative parent-child radial gaps. It is incorporated into hierarchical entailment contrastive training to capture partial-order relations. We further introduce a norm regularization term that prevents undesirable radial degeneration for stable training. We train models on millions of image-text pairs and evaluate them across zero-shot classification, image-text retrieval, hierarchical reasoning, and compositional understanding. Our model consistently outperforms strong Euclidean and hyperbolic vision-language baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of decoupling semantic alignment from hierarchical entailment modeling.

</details>


### [65] [A Regularity Condition Under Which a CPAB Transformation is a Diffeomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32369)
*Amit Aflalo,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该笔记指出CPAB变换仅保证为双Lipschitz同胚而非必然微分同胚，并给出保证其为微分同胚的充分正则性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原CPAB论文（Freifeld et al., 2015, 2017）遗漏了数学细节：CPAB变换只保证是双Lipschitz同胚，仅几乎处处可微，未必是微分同胚；需澄清并修补该理论空缺。

Method: 分析CPAB变换（对连续分段仿射速度场积分所得流）的可微性失效机制，识别使其退化为非微分同胚的情形，并提出可保证微分同胚的充分正则性条件；在1D、2D中结合常用剖分给出条件的易施加性及在标准采样假设下以概率1成立的论证。

Result: 给出可防止可微性失效的充分条件；证明1D与2D中这些条件易于强制满足，且对常用剖分在标准采样假设下以概率1成立；推测3D具有类似性质。

Conclusion: CPAB变换的微分同胚性需要额外正则性条件保障；所提条件实用且几乎必然成立，从而在不牺牲表达力的前提下完善CPAB的理论基础。

Abstract: Continuous Piecewise-Affine Based (CPAB) transformations, introduced by Freifeld et al. (2015, 2017), are flows obtained by integrating continuous piecewise-affine (CPA) velocity fields. These expressive transformations have proven useful in applications including nonlinear temporal warping and trainable activation functions in 1D, as well as nonrigid spatial deformations in 2D and 3D. While computational costs were not an issue in 1D, they originally limited the practical expressiveness of CPAB transformations in higher dimensions, especially in 3D. Our recent work (Aflalo et al., 2026) removed this bottleneck, enabling efficient optimization with much finer tessellations and substantially greater expressiveness. There is, however, a mathematical subtlety that was overlooked in the original CPAB papers (Freifeld et al., 2015, 2017). CPAB transformations are always bi-Lipschitz homeomorphisms, implying that both the transformations and their inverses are differentiable almost everywhere. They are not, however, necessarily diffeomorphisms. This note remedies the omission by identifying sufficient regularity conditions under which a CPAB transformation is guaranteed to be a diffeomorphism. We explain how differentiability can fail and how the proposed conditions prevent this failure. In 1D and 2D, we show that these conditions can be easily enforced and, for commonly used tessellations, hold with probability one under standard sampling assumptions. We expect analogous behavior in 3D.

</details>


### [66] [StackGen3D: Stable 3D Block Stack Generation from Multiview Silhouettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31803)
*Xiaopeng Zhang,Luzhe Sun,Yiqing Xu,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StackGen3D：以块体库存与三视图剪影为条件生成物理稳定的积木组装结构，两阶段（物理信息图神经网络PI-GNN做稳定性预测与数据筛选 + 条件流匹配生成），推理时用轻量几何选择与精化避免物理仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉规格生成物理稳定的3D结构需同时解决几何歧义与物体间物理交互问题；现有方法缺乏对支撑关系的关系性物理推理，且难以以低成本获得稳定结构数据。

Method: 1) PI-GNN：建模块间支撑关系，作为快速稳定性预测器用于筛选构建多样化稳定结构数据集；2) 在该数据集上训练条件流匹配模型，学习与目标剪影一致的多模态块体构型分布；3) 推理阶段采用轻量几何选择与精化（Best-of-N选优）降低块间穿插，无需物理仿真。

Result: PI-GNN从简单堆叠到复杂结构的泛化显著优于参数量匹配的MLP；结构生成中Best-of-10选择将物理稳定性从45.3%提升至72.4%，剪影IoU达0.970。

Conclusion: 将关系性物理推理、可扩展数据筛选与条件生成建模相结合，可用于创意性3D组装生成，具备良好潜力。

Abstract: Generating physically stable 3D structures from visual specifications requires resolving geometric ambiguity while accounting for physical interactions among objects. We present StackGen3D, a framework for generating stable block assemblies conditioned on a given block inventory and three orthographic silhouettes. Our approach consists of two stages. First, we develop a physics-informed graph neural network (PI-GNN) that models support relations between blocks and serves as a fast stability predictor for curating a diverse dataset of stable structures. Second, we train a conditional flow-matching model on this dataset to capture the multimodal distribution of block configurations consistent with the target silhouettes. At inference time, lightweight geometric selection and refinement reduce inter-block penetration without physics simulation. Preliminary experiments show that the PI-GNN generalizes from simple stacks to complex structures substantially better than a parameter-matched multilayer perceptron. For structure generation, Best-of-10 selection improves physical stability from 45.3% to 72.4% while achieving a silhouette IoU of 0.970. These results highlight the potential of combining relational physical reasoning, scalable data curation, and conditional generative modeling for creative 3D assembly.
  Project website: [https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/](https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/)

</details>


### [67] [LoCA: Enabling Multi-Modality in Geospatial Location Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32894)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Eleanna Vali,Diogenis Antonopoulos,Eleni Saka,Dimitris Christofis,Nikolaos Ioannis Bountos,Konstantin Klemmer,Ioannis Papoutsis,Yannis Panagakis,Mihalis A. Nicolaou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文证明任何Lipschitz位置编码器的输出内在维度至多为2，因而对比损失必然严格高于渐近最优；提出LoCA两阶段框架：先用对比学习联合训练Sentinel-1/2编码器获得共对齐、高秩的多模态目标，再冻结图像编码器，以多视角对比学习训练位置与表格元数据投影适配器，在17个任务上取得最多胜场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码器通过与配对的遥感影像做对比对齐，将地理坐标映射为高维描述子，但其结构本身可能受限；同时扩展到多模态输入时，单一位置需映射到多个潜在不兼容的描述子，单独使用适配器无法对齐独立预训练的编码器，因此需要明确正则化器应满足的条件。

Method: 理论分析：证明Lipschitz位置编码器的输出内在维度至多2，迫使对比损失严格超过渐近最优；并实证发现联合训练会使嵌入协方差塌缩为低秩，而冻结图像编码器可被视为协方差正则化器，提供高秩目标。基于此提出多模态正则化两项要求：跨模态共对齐与高有效秩。方法LoCA分两阶段：阶段1用对比学习联合训练Sentinel-1与Sentinel-2编码器，得到共对齐、高秩的多模态目标；阶段2冻结图像编码器，将位置编码器与表格元数据编码器作为投影适配器，以多视角对比学习训练。

Result: 在覆盖土地覆盖、野火预测、地球物理、社会经济、生物多样性与气候等17项任务上，与五个最先进编码器对比，LoCA获得最多任务胜场，是唯一在多数任务上优于所有其他模型的模型，并在全部逐任务比较中胜出68.8%。

Conclusion: 位置编码的维度受限是结构性问题，可通过共对齐且高秩的多模态目标加以正则化；LoCA的两阶段冻结架构同时满足共对齐与高有效秩要求，在多任务基准上优于现有位置编码器。

Abstract: Location encoders map geographic coordinates to high-dimensional descriptors through contrastive alignment with paired Earth Observation imagery. We prove that this construction is structurally limited: any Lipschitz location encoder produces an output of intrinsic dimension at most 2, regardless of architecture or bandwidth, which forces the contrastive loss to strictly exceed its asymptotic optimum. Empirically, joint training also collapses the encoder's embedding covariance to low rank. Although dimension-limited, current location encoders sidestep this spectral collapse by training against a frozen image encoder, which we reinterpret as a covariance regularizer that supplies a high-rank target for the location encoder to fold onto. However, extending this approach to multimodal inputs is challenging, as location encoders should simultaneously map a single location to multiple, potentially incompatible, descriptors. We identify two requirements a regularizer must satisfy for location encoding in multimodal regimes: co-alignment across modalities, since adapters alone cannot align independently pretrained encoders; and high effective rank, since the location encoder inherits its target's spectrum. We propose LoCA (Location Contrastive Alignment), a two-stage pipeline that satisfies both. Stage 1 jointly trains Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 encoders with contrastive learning, producing a co-aligned, high-rank multimodal target. Stage 2 freezes the image encoders and trains a location and a tabular metadata encoder as projection adapters with multi-view contrastive learning. Against five state-of-the-art encoders on 17 tasks ranging from land cover and wildfire forecasting to geophysical, socio-economic, biodiversity and climate applications, LoCA achieves the most task wins, is the only model to outperform every other on a majority of tasks, and wins 68.8% of all per-task comparisons.

</details>


### [68] [SCDM-HTD: Spectral-Wise Conditional Diffusion Model for Hyperspectral Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32768)
*Dunbin Shen,Wenfeng Kong,Jianjun Liu,Xiaorui Ma,Hongyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出谱向条件扩散模型，将高光谱目标检测重构为检测图生成任务，通过扩散-去噪学习背景与目标的分布差异，并设计轻量MLP去噪网络、确定性反向非马尔可夫链加速采样及复合损失，在四个基准数据集上取得定量与定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱目标检测方法主要关注特征比较，忽视了对背景-目标分离的分布学习，导致实际应用中的检测鲁棒性受限。

Method: 将HTD重构为检测图生成任务：把背景与目标的置信度分数作为训练样本，以对应像素光谱作为引导条件，通过扩散与去噪捕捉二者的分布差异；构建基于多层感知机的轻量条件去噪网络，将含噪置信度映射为理想值；采用确定性反向非马尔可夫链跳过采样步骤以加速生成；引入复合损失，融合约束能量最小化损失（最大化目标响应、最小化平均输出能量以增强目标显著性并抑制背景）与逐像素标签的二元交叉熵损失。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明方法有效，定量指标与定性视觉效果均有提升。

Conclusion: 以生成式分布学习替代单纯特征比较，可为高光谱目标检测提供更鲁棒且实用的新范式。

Abstract: Hyperspectral target detection (HTD) aims to locate and recognize objects at a fine-grained scale, playing a pivotal role in remote sensing. Despite significant progress, current HTD methods primarily focus on feature comparison while neglecting distribution learning for background-target separation, which can limit detection robustness in practical applications. This paper addresses this issue by introducing a spectral-wise conditional diffusion model that reformulates HTD as a task of generating a detection map. Specifically, we treat background and target confidence scores as training samples and use the corresponding pixel spectra as guiding conditions to capture the distributional discrepancy between background and target through diffusion and denoising. To enhance practical applicability, we propose a lightweight conditional denoising network based on multilayer perceptron to map noisy confidence scores to their ideal counterparts and adopt a deterministic reverse non-Markov chain to skip sampling steps, thereby accelerating the generation process. Furthermore, we introduce a composite loss to improve background-target separation. This loss integrates constrained energy minimization loss to enhance target saliency and suppress the background by maximizing the target response while minimizing average output energy, alongside binary cross-entropy loss to classify background and target using pixel-wise labels. Experimental results on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with improvements in both quantitative metrics and qualitative visual effects.

</details>


### [69] [HEALPix-Based Implicit Neural Compression of Omnidirectional Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32561)
*Yulong Cheng,Youneng Bao,Junfeng Zhou,Mu Li,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于HEALPix多尺度表示的全景图像球面过拟合编解码器，利用等面积采样、跨尺度条件熵模型与Multi-Ring球面卷积提升编码效率并降低解码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像实时解码对沉浸式VR至关重要。过拟合编解码器借助隐式神经表示（INR）比传统变换域学习图像压缩（LIC）解码复杂度更低，但现有方法多在等距柱状投影（ERP）上操作，存在球面采样非均匀、空间畸变、两极冗余采样及纬度相关的感受野等问题。

Method: 1）采用多尺度Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization（HEALPix）表示构建球面过拟合编解码器，实现等面积采样与球面信号的层级建模；2）提出Spherical Inter-Scale Conditional熵模型，挖掘HEALPix隐变量间的空间依赖与跨尺度相关性以提升编码效率；3）设计Multi-Ring Spherical Convolution，在跨纬度近均匀球面感受野上聚合邻域像素。

Result: 在公开全景图像数据集上，相较VVC在V-PSNR、V-MS-SSIM、V-LPIPS上分别取得16.3%、26.5%、19.5%的BD-rate降低；在V-PSNR上相较基于ERP的Cool-Chic 5.0降低13.1% BD-rate，同时解码速度比所评估的学习型自编码器快2.6–3.6倍。

Conclusion: 以HEALPix等面积球面表示为基石的过拟合编解码方案有效缓解ERP的非均匀采样与畸变问题，在提升全景图像率失真性能的同时保持低解码复杂度，适合实时VR应用。

Abstract: Real-time decoding of omnidirectional images is essential for immersive virtual reality (VR) experiences. Compared with conventional transform-based learned image compression (LIC), overfitted codecs offer lower decoding complexity by representing each image with a lightweight implicit neural representation (INR). However, existing approaches typically operate on the equirectangular projection (ERP), which introduces nonuniform spherical sampling and spatial distortion, leading to redundant sampling near the poles and latitude-dependent receptive fields. To address these limitations, we propose a spherical overfitted codec based on a multi-scale Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization (HEALPix) representation, which enables equal-area sampling and efficient hierarchical modeling of spherical signals. Building on this representation, we develop a Spherical Inter-Scale Conditional entropy model that exploits spatial dependencies and cross-scale correlations among HEALPix latents to improve coding efficiency. We further introduce a Multi-Ring Spherical Convolution that aggregates neighboring pixels over a nearly uniform spherical receptive field across latitudes. Experiments on a public omnidirectional image dataset demonstrate BD-rate reductions of 16.3%, 26.5%, and 19.5% over VVC in terms of V-PSNR, V-MS-SSIM, and V-LPIPS, respectively. Our method also achieves a 13.1% BD-rate reduction over ERP-based Cool-Chic 5.0 in terms of V-PSNR, while providing 2.6-3.6x faster decoding than the evaluated learned autoencoder codecs.

</details>


### [70] [PathJev: Decision-Native Vision-Language Modelling for Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32478)
*Cristian Tommasino,Antonio M. Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出决策原生模型PathJev-P（MedGemma-1.5-4B+LoRA+决策头），在4万病理patch上训练，输出候选集上的归一化概率；平衡准确率优于同骨干生成模型、CONCH与通用医学决策模型，但校准优势依赖生成模型的读出方式，线性探针校准最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
