Motivation: 病理视觉-语言模型用于决策时，很少能针对用户关心的候选给出校准良好的概率分布。作者希望用"决策原生"方式——将问题与候选集写入输入、单次前向由小型决策头输出归一化分布——直接获得可用的决策概率。

Method: 构建PathJev-P：骨干为MedGemma-1.5-4B，加LoRA适配器与决策头；训练数据为三个公开数据集共40,000个patch状态，专家标签被转换为带器官特异性候选集及"none of these"选项的类型化问题。与同骨干同数据同选择规则的生成模型、零样本CONCH、通用医学决策模型对比，3个随机种子，测试前固定分析方案，测试集与骨干训练数据无文档化重叠。

Result: 平衡准确率0.541 vs 生成模型0.492、CONCH 0.345、通用医学决策模型0.213；对生成模型的优势主要来自训练中未见的两个器官。校准：按以往工作的候选对数似然求和读出，ECE 0.204 vs 0.279；事后考察的长度归一化读出下二者平均校准误差相近（0.201），且生成模型在未见器官上更优，PathJev-P仍过度自信。病理基础模型上的监督线性探针在各设置下校准均更好。全切片定性演示中，PathJev-P可对无监督组织簇标注并支持弃权，在仅用部分tile的情况下区分癌与非癌组织。

Conclusion: 决策原生建模提升了病理决策准确率，尤其对未见器官的泛化，但其概率校准优势并不稳健，取决于生成模型概率的读出方式；在此设定下，监督线性探针校准最优。结果提示需谨慎宣称校准优势，并应以多种读出方式与基线检验。

Abstract: Vision-language models used for decisions in histopathology rarely return calibrated probabilities over the candidates a user cares about. Decision-native models write a question and a candidate set chosen at run time into the input and return a normalised distribution from a small decision head in one forward pass. We build PathJev-P, a decision-native model for histopathology: MedGemma-1.5-4B with LoRA adapters and a decision head, trained on 40,000 patch states from three public collections whose expert labels are turned into typed questions with organ-specific candidate sets and a "none of these" option. In a comparison over 3 seeds with an analysis plan fixed before testing, on test sets without documented overlap with the training data of the backbone, PathJev-P is more accurate than a generative model trained with the same backbone, data and selection rule, the zero-shot pathology model CONCH and a general medical decision model (balanced accuracy 0.541 against 0.492, 0.345 and 0.213); its advantage over the generative model comes from two organs absent from training. The calibration advantage depends on how the generative model's probabilities are read out. With the summed candidate log-likelihood used in previous work, PathJev-P has the lower calibration error (ECE 0.204 against 0.279); with a length-normalised read-out of the same generative model, examined post hoc, the two have similar mean calibration error (0.201) and the generative model is better calibrated on the unseen organs, where PathJev-P remains over-confident. A supervised linear probe on a pathology foundation model is better calibrated in every setting. In a qualitative whole-slide demonstration, PathJev-P labels unsupervised tissue clusters with abstention and separates carcinoma from other tissue while running the model on a fraction of the tiles.

</details>


### [71] [AutoMIL: Automated Multiple Instance Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32452)
*Jonas Waibel,Frank Kramer,Dominik Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoMIL 是一个开源框架，将自动化机器学习（AutoML）思想引入全切片图像（WSI）多示例学习分类，自动完成预处理、训练与评估等流水线步骤；默认设置下在 UBC-OCEAN、PCam 和 TCGA-NSCLC 子集上分别达到 0.94、0.99、0.93–0.96 的 ROC-AUC，性能与专门设计的流水线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建 WSI 多示例学习分类流水线涉及大量相互依赖的设计决策，且同时需要病理学与机器学习两方面的专业知识，门槛高、可复现性差。作者希望降低这一门槛，为无手动调参需求的用户提供可用的基线方案。

Method: 提出开源框架 AutoMIL，基于 AutoML 原则对流水线关键环节进行自动化，包括预处理、训练与评估；并通过基准评测（默认设置）与集成（ensembling）策略考察其表现，分析自动化流水线对不同数据集特性的适应性。

Result: 默认设置下 ROC-AUC 为：UBC-OCEAN 0.94、PCam 0.99、TCGA-NSCLC 子集 0.93–0.96，与相关工作中专用的流水线相当；集成主要在不平衡数据上带来性能提升。

Conclusion: AutoMIL 为无需手动配置流水线的 WSI 分类提供了易获取的基线；同时表明自动化流水线需根据数据集特性（如类别不平衡）自适应调整策略。

Abstract: Developing multiple instance learning pipelines for the classification of whole-slide images involves many interdependent design decisions and expertise in both pathology and machine learning. We present AutoMIL, an open-source framework that applies principles from the field of automated machine learning to tackle this task by automating integral pipeline steps, such as preprocessing, training and evaluation. In a benchmark evaluation with default settings, AutoMIL reached ROC-AUC scores of 0.94 on UBC-OCEAN, 0.99 on PCam and 0.93-0.96 on a TCGA-NSCLC subset, comparable to specialised pipelines in related work. Ensembling mainly improved performance on imbalanced data, showing that an automated pipeline must adapt to dataset characteristics. AutoMIL thus provides an accessible baseline for whole-slide image classification without manual pipeline configuration.

</details>


### [72] [Tempo-Zero: Recursive Self-Play in Executable Worlds for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32433)
*Jingmin Zhu,Yanyan Zou,Xiuyu Li,James Bailey,Dinh Phung,Tao Yu,Yunyun Hou,Jun Liu,Jun Zhou,Sirui Wang,Nan Duan,Jiachen Liu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tempo-Zero：以可执行环境渲染视频并自生成带精确时间标注的问答，实现执行验证式自博弈与难度/规则演化闭环，在12个真实视频基准上超越基线与现有自博弈方法，尤其擅长时间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频时序推理需要理解事件随时间演化的过程，但精确时间标注的视频收集成本高昂。现有视频自博弈方法仅在固定录制视频集上训练，无法改变底层事件动态，且依赖多数投票等来自模型自身输出的噪声伪标签，容易放大模型共享错误。

Method: 提出 Tempo-Zero 自改进框架，构建“可执行环境—执行验证式自博弈—环境演化”闭环：执行程序即渲染出视频，并自带每个问题的真值选项与时间区间。单一策略同时充当提问者与解题者，生成问题并以带时间戳的证据行作答，训练信号为执行验证奖励而非模型共识；解题者表现驱动任务与难度课程，策略再选择规则变更以丰富后续轮次的事件动态。

Result: 仅用简单程序训练、无需监督微调与外部视频数据（而先前方法通常需要）的情况下，Tempo-Zero 在12个真实视频基准上跨不同模型规模平均优于基模型与近期自博弈基线，在时间定位任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 以程序化可执行环境替代固定录像与伪标签，可将视频自博弈的监督从模型共识转为可验证的真值，并通过课程与环境演化持续增强任务丰富度，为低成本、高可靠的时间推理自改进提供可行范式。

Abstract: Video reasoning requires understanding how events unfold over time, yet videos with accurate temporal annotations are expensive to collect. Self-play offers an alternative by letting a model generate and solve its own tasks without manual annotation. However, existing video self-play methods train on fixed sets of recorded videos, so they cannot alter the underlying event dynamics and rely on noisy pseudo-labels derived from the model's own outputs, such as majority voting, that risk reinforcing shared model errors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Tempo-Zero, a self-improvement framework that closes a loop of executable environments, execution-verified self-play, and environment evolution, where executing a program renders a video with the ground-truth option and interval of every question. A single policy plays both Questioner and Solver, proposing questions and answering them with timestamped evidence lines, and is trained on execution-grounded rewards instead of model consensus. The Solver's performance steers a curriculum over tasks and difficulty levels, and the policy selects rule changes that enrich the event dynamics of later rounds. Despite being trained solely on simple programs, without supervised fine-tuning or external video data which prior methods typically require, Tempo-Zero outperforms the base models and recent self-play baselines on average across twelve real-world video benchmarks at different model scales, with particularly strong gains on temporal grounding tasks. Project page: https://alchemist-jo.github.io/tempo-zero/

</details>


### [73] [Complementing DINO Features with Image Structure for Part Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32293)
*Samyak Rawlekar,Nikhil Chowdary Paleti,Amey Gupta,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Part-DINO 是一个无需训练的框架，将低层图像分割得到的边界与 DINO 语义特征结合，实现结构一致、边界精确的部件发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 DINO 特征聚类的部件发现方法产生不精确的部件边界。作者将其归因于 ViT 的刚性 patch 网格：跨越边界的 patch 会混入多个部件的像素，导致特征熵更高、分配置信度更低，从而模糊部件边界；而基于不变性的 DINO 训练并不能清除这种像素级混合。

Method: 不重新训练 DINO，而是从边界检测器直接获取几何信息：利用低层图像分割的边界将图像划分为区域，在每个区域内对 DINO 特征进行池化，再对池化特征聚类以发现部件。整个流程无需任何训练。

Result: 在四个标准基准（CUB、CelebA、PartImageNet-OOD、PartImageNet-Seg）上，Part-DINO 优于所有无需训练的基线，并与弱监督和无监督方法保持竞争力；同时通过对 26K+ 前景 patch 的实验验证了边界重叠 patch 的特征熵更高、置信度更低。

Conclusion: 部件边界不精确的根源在于 patch 网格的像素混合；用边界检测器提供的几何信息弥补 DINO 特征的不足，是无需训练即可获得结构一致部件分配的有效途径。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) like DINO encode rich semantics, enabling training-free segmentation of objects from the background and discovery of their parts via feature clustering. This paper focuses on part discovery: partitioning a foreground object into semantic parts. Existing approaches produce imprecise part boundaries. We trace this to the rigid ViT patch grid used in training DINO, where patches straddling boundaries contain pixels from multiple parts. While DINO's invariance-based training could absorb this pixel-level mixing into clean features, experiments with over 26K foreground patches show it does not: boundary-overlapping patches produce features with higher entropy and lower assignment confidence, blurring predicted part boundaries. Rather than make the massive effort of retraining DINO over geometrically coherent regions, an easier approach is to take boundaries directly from a boundary detector, which supplies the geometric information DINO's features lack. Motivated by this, we introduce Part-DINO, a training-free framework that combines boundaries from low-level image segmentation with DINO's semantic features. We use the boundaries to partition the image into regions, pool DINO features within each region, and group them to discover parts. Without any training, Part-DINO produces structurally coherent part assignments, outperforming training-free baselines across four standard benchmarks (CUB, CelebA, PartImageNet-OOD, PartImageNet-Seg) and remaining competitive with weakly-supervised and unsupervised methods.

</details>


### [74] [Polyp-VSSD: A Pre-trained Transformer with Local-Global Joint VSSD Decoding for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32282)
*Xiaobo Zhu,Shiwei Wu,Yu An,Yuechen Tao,He Sun,Song Zhang,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Polyp-VSSD——基于预训练Transformer骨干的局部-全局联合解码框架，用于高精度结肠息肉分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠息肉早期检测对预防结直肠癌至关重要，但现有内镜分割方法表征能力有限，且全局与局部上下文信息融合不足。

Method: 构建于大规模预训练Transformer骨干之上，利用其强特征表达能力；采用局部-全局联合解码框架，将全局语义建模与局部细粒度重建统一起来以提升分割保真度。

Result: 在多个公开内镜数据集上，Dice与IoU均显著优于SOTA；在EndoScene数据集上取得mIoU 0.903、mDice 0.946，体现鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为智能内镜图像分析提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Early detection of colorectal polyps is essential for preventing colorectal cancer, making accurate polyp segmentation a key component in computer-aided diagnosis systems. However, existing endoscopic segmentation methods often exhibit limited representational capacity and insufficient integration of global and local contextual information. To address these limitations, we propose Polyp-VSSD, a novel local-global joint decoding framework built upon a pre-trained Transformer backbone for high-precision polyp segmentation. Polyp-VSSD leverages the strong feature expressiveness of large-scale visual pre-training and unifies global semantic modeling with local fine-grained reconstruction to enhance segmentation fidelity. Extensive experiments on multiple public endoscopic datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both Dice and IoU. On the EndoScene dataset, Polyp-VSSD achieves mIoU and mDice scores of 0.903 and 0.946, respectively, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization capability. This work presents an effective and scalable solution for intelligent endoscopic image analysis.

</details>


### [75] [RVG-Bench: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Driven Audio-Visual Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32268)
*Yue Feng,Boshen Xu,Zihao Yue,Xiaokun Liu,Miao Yang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RVG-Bench，一个面向"推理驱动"音视频时序定位（TVG）的基准，要求模型先理解查询意图再定位目标片段；含 1,255 个视频、1,890 对查询-区间，覆盖 8 类推理类型；评测 22 个模型，最强模型仅 49.6 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TVG 基准的查询多为对视觉内容的描述性文本，模型只需做语义匹配即可定位；而真实场景中的查询更强调意图，如需先找到视觉证据、判定事件成因或回忆某段时刻，必须先推理再定位。这类查询在现有基准中被严重忽视，导致评测与现实需求脱节。

Method: 构建 RVG-Bench 基准：每条查询都被设计为无法通过表面描述匹配直接定位，必须对查询隐含意图进行推理，并结合音视频内容才能确定目标区间；同时严格设计构建流程并验证数据质量，以确保模型真正理解音视频内容而非利用捷径。基准包含 1,255 个视频与 1,890 对 query-interval，按 8 种推理类型划分。在 22 个模型上进行评测，并开展受控分析，包括 oracle-grounding 探针与 test-time scaling 实验，以定位模型失败环节及原因。

Result: 22 个被测模型中表现最强的 Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash 仅取得 49.6 mIoU，说明该任务对现有模型仍具显著挑战；oracle-grounding 探针与 test-time scaling 实验进一步揭示了性能瓶颈所在（如感知理解不足与推理/定位环节的分离问题），并给出了具体失败模式与改进方向。

Conclusion: 现有模型在推理驱动的音视频时序定位上仍有很大提升空间；RVG-Bench 填补了意图推理型查询评测的空白，并通过系统分析为未来模型设计（强化音视频理解与意图推理、改进推理-定位耦合）提供了方向与诊断依据。

Abstract: Existing temporal video grounding (TVG) benchmarks primarily focus on localizing target video segments according to descriptive text queries of the visual content. However, real-world queries are more diverse and often require the model to first understand the user's intent before localization, such as finding visual evidence, identifying the cause of an event, or recalling a remembered moment, yet such queries remain largely overlooked. To mitigate this gap, we introduce RVG-Bench, a benchmark for reasoning-driven audio-visual temporal grounding, where every query is constructed such that the target segment can only be identified through reasoning about the query's underlying intent. We carefully construct the benchmark and rigorously verify its data quality to ensure models truly comprehend the audio-visual content. RVG-Bench comprises 1,255 videos and 1,890 query-interval pairs across eight reasoning types. We evaluate 22 models and find that the strongest, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, achieves only 49.6 mIoU. Through a series of controlled analyses, including oracle-grounding probes and test-time scaling experiments, we systematically examine where and why current models struggle, providing insights for future model development.

</details>


### [76] [Angular Supervision for Visual Object Grounding in Autoregressive Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32220)
*Parsa Esmaeilkhani,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Angular Relevance ($R_L$) 度量与 Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)，在无需分割头、无需训练的情况下显著提升自回归 VLM 的空间定位（heatmap）锐度与指代分割（RES）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉语言模型难以将生成文本定位到正确图像区域；注意力图和 Logit Lens 等可解释性方法因文本-视觉注意力弱及 sink token 影响，空间解释效果差。

Method: 1) Angular Relevance $R_L$：基于视觉 token 嵌入与 unembedding 矩阵行的余弦相似度构建可视化度量，对 token 范数不变，因此热图更锐利、更忠实，且免训练；2) Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)：辅助损失，直接监督视觉 token 嵌入与其对应物体概念词汇方向的角度对齐，缓解跨模态混合导致的局部信息丢失；与标准下一 token 预测目标联合微调。

Result: 免训练 $R_L$ 在 RES 上优于既有诊断图方法与基于 CLIP 的免训练方法；AOL 微调进一步显著锐化热图目标定位、提升 RES，同时不损害通用 VQA 性能；学到的定位能力可迁移到训练中未见类别的类别级分割。

Conclusion: VLM 自身的视觉 token 可直接用于定位与 RES，无需训练分割头；角度对齐是有效且轻量的定位监督信号。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to ground generated text in the correct image regions, and existing explainability methods, such as attention maps and Logit Lens, often produce poor spatial explanations due to weak text-to-vision attention and sink tokens. We propose \textbf{Angular Relevance} ($R_L$), a simple visualization measure based on cosine similarity between visual token embeddings and unembedding-matrix rows. Because $R_L$ is invariant to token norm, it produces substantially sharper and more faithful heatmaps than attention maps or Logit Lens on out-of-the-box models. Without any training, $R_L$ yields competitive referring-expression segmentation (RES), outperforming prior diagnostic maps and CLIP-based training-free methods. To preserve localized object information during cross-modal mixing, we introduce \textbf{Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)}, an auxiliary loss that directly supervises visual token embeddings to align angularly with the vocabulary directions of their corresponding object concepts. Finetuning with AOL alongside the standard next-token prediction objective significantly sharpens object localization in heatmaps, showing that the VLM's own visual tokens can be used directly for grounding and improving RES, without training any segmentation heads and without degrading general VQA performance. We further demonstrate that the learned grounding also transfers to category-level segmentation of object categories not seen during training.

</details>


### [77] [Data Quality in RVL-CDIP: A Systematic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32126)
*Stefan Larson,Kevin Leach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文审计RVL-CDIP文档分类基准的数据质量问题，发现约10.5%标签错误、测试集35.2%存在重复/近重复，并给出修正语料与重新基准结果（LayoutLMv3达96.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVL-CDIP是文档分类事实上的标准基准且报告准确率已达98.8%，但高饱和成绩可能源于数据缺陷而非模型能力，故需对基准本身进行质量审计。

Method: 系统审计RVL-CDIP的40万文档：(1)核查标签错误率；(2)检测训练/测试集重复与近重复；(3)分析页面识别码对分类的捷径效应（消融实验）；(4)跨源文档的分布外测试及OCR引擎替换实验；(5)识别并去除敏感个人数据；(6)对25个模型重新基准，其中13个重新训练。

Result: 约10.5%文档标签错误；35.2%测试集在训练集中有重复或近重复；仅凭页面识别码即可达69%准确率，SOTA模型去除识别码后下降11个百分点；图像模型跨源泛化下降16–28个百分点；OCR引擎选择带来4.1–8.3个百分点差异；近1.6万文档含敏感个人数据。缺陷修复后重训的13个模型提升0.9–2.9个百分点，最优LayoutLMv3达96.5%。

Conclusion: 现有RVL-CDIP报告的高准确率被数据泄漏、捷径特征与标签噪声高估；作者发布修正语料、标注及分布外数据，为文档分类提供更可靠的评测基准。

Abstract: RVL-CDIP is the de facto standard benchmark for document classification, and reported accuracy on it is now 98.8%. In this paper, we audit RVL-CDIP for data quality issues and find that approximately 10.5% of its 400,000 documents have erroneous labels and that 35.2% of the test set has a duplicate or near-duplicate in the training set. Identification codes on the pages are sufficient to classify at 69% accuracy, and a state-of-the-art model loses 11 points without them. Image-only models trained on RVL-CDIP lose 16 to 28 points on documents from other sources, and the choice of OCR engine changes accuracy by 4.1 to 8.3 points. We find that nearly 16,000 RVL-CDIP documents contain sensitive personal data, and produce de-identified versions of these documents. We re-benchmark 25 models, and with all defects remediated, the 13 that we retrain are 0.9 to 2.9 points more accurate than in the original condition; the most accurate of them, LayoutLMv3, is 96.5% accurate. We release the corrected corpus, the annotations and the out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [78] [Concept Acupuncturist: An Efficient and Precise Concept Erasure Method for Diffusion Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32059)
*Yiran Xiahou,Siliang Qin,Kai Chen,Shengzhi Zhang,Renyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConceptACP框架，将可解释性分析与免推理微调结合，在U-Net交叉注意力块层面实现精准高效的扩散模型概念遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型可生成高质量图像但可能产生有害或违规内容，需要机器遗忘（MU）选择性移除目标概念并保留通用能力。现有MU方法难以应对概念表示的分布性与纠缠性，在全扩散轨迹上大规模微调时优化低效、编辑不精确。

Method: 1）建立语义概念与U-Net去噪器交叉注意力块的映射，定位最相关块进行定向优化，避免全轨迹优化以提升效率；2）基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的特征级纠正机制：利用SAE导出的稀疏语义特征激活构造目标纠正信号，投影回对应块输出空间作为监督，缓解概念纠缠；3）无需外部推理时模块，实现在SDXL-Turbo上的一步生成免推理遗忘。

Result: 在SDXL-Turbo标准一步生成设置下，遗忘成功率平均比最佳基线提升36.34个百分点，同时保持图像质量与提示保真度，并可有效泛化到其他Stable Diffusion骨干。

Conclusion: ConceptACP通过块级定位与SAE特征纠正，实现了高效、细粒度且无需推理开销的概念遗忘，验证了可解释性引导与免推理微调结合的可行性与泛化性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image generation models produce high-quality images but may generate harmful or policy-violating content, motivating machine unlearning (MU) to selectively remove target concepts while preserving general capabilities. However, existing MU approaches often struggle with the distributed and entangled nature of concept representations, leading to inefficient optimization and imprecise edits when applied through large-scale fine-tuning over full diffusion trajectories. To bridge this gap, we propose Concept Acupuncturist (ConceptACP), a novel MU framework that integrates interpretability-guided analysis with inference-free fine-tuning for efficient and precise concept removal. By establishing mappings between semantic concepts and cross-attention blocks in the U-Net denoiser and localizing the most concept-relevant block for targeted optimization, ConceptACP avoids full diffusion trajectory optimization, thereby improving fine-tuning efficiency. To mitigate concept entanglement, we propose a feature-level correction mechanism based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where SAE-derived sparse, semantically meaningful feature activations are manipulated to construct targeted correction signals, which are then projected back to the output space of the corresponding block and used as supervision for precise concept removal. Consequently, ConceptACP eliminates the need for external inference-time modules while achieving efficient and fine-grained unlearning. Instantiated on SDXL-Turbo, ConceptACP improves the forgetting success rate by 36.34 percentage points in the standard one-step generation setting on average over the best-performing baseline, while preserving image quality and prompt fidelity, and generalizes effectively across other Stable Diffusion backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/Eran-Xia/ConceptACP.

</details>


### [79] [LOCI: Localized Continuous Control for Multi-Subject Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31950)
*Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati,Shlok Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOCI 提出一种多主体图像编辑框架，将主体级注意力绑定与 Regional LoRA 结合，用逐 token 缩放向量替代标量系数，实现空间局部化、连续强度、多主体独立同时控制的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑器（如 Flux.1-Kontext）缺乏对编辑位置与强度的细粒度控制：已有方法要么是连续但全局的控制，要么是仅作用于局部但强度固定的编辑；据作者所知，尚无方法同时支持显式空间定位、每区域连续强度以及多主体独立同时控制。

Method: 输入含多主体区域与指令的图像，LOCI 用主体级注意力绑定实现空间隔离，并引入 Regional LoRA 层，把 LoRA 的标量系数替换为与空间布局对齐的逐 token 缩放向量；适配器仅在单一抑制点训练，推理时通过重缩放其区域残差得到连续编辑强度。

Result: 在多主体场景实验中，LOCI 取得高编辑保真度，跨区域干扰显著低于基线方法，同时保持对编辑强度的连续控制。

Conclusion: LOCI 首次同时实现空间局部化、逐区域连续强度与多主体独立编辑（如一人渐变衰老、另一人加胡须且串扰极小），单点训练的适配器即可外推连续强度。

Abstract: Recent instruction-following image editors such as Flux.1-Kontext enable text-driven editing but lack fine-grained control over where and how much an edit is applied. Existing approaches provide either continuous but global control or spatially localized editing at a fixed strength. To our knowledge, none jointly supports explicit spatial localization, continuous per-region strength, and independent simultaneous control of multiple subjects. We present LOCI (LOcalized Continuous control for multi- subject Image editing). Given a multi-subject image with per-subject regions and instructions, LOCI edits each subject at an independently chosen, continuously adjustable strength, for instance gradually aging one person while adding a beard to another with minimal cross-region leakage. LOCI pairs subject-level attention binding for spatial isolation with a Regional LoRA layer that replaces the LoRA's scalar coefficient with a per-token scaling vector aligned to the spatial layout. A single adapter is trained at one suppression point, and continuous edit strengths follow by rescaling its regional residual at inference. Experiments on multi-subject scenes demonstrate that LOCI achieves strong edit fidelity with significantly reduced cross-region interference compared to baselines, while maintaining continuous control over edit strength

</details>


### [80] [SAGE: A hybrid vision-language and geometry framework for work-zone pathway prediction under temporary traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31835)
*Jiaxi Liu,Keshu Wu,Weizhe Tang,Bofeng Cao,Sikai Chen,Xiaopeng Li,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE（Semantic-to-Action Grounded Execution）是一个"读-决-执行-校验"框架，将临时施工区（work zone）中自动驾驶的通行路径分解为候选构造、路径偏好与合规控制三步，并以VLM轨迹作为几何候选的偏好信号，结合MUTCD规则约束实现安全可控的通行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临时交通管制迫使车辆绕行施工区，自动车需把对可见交通装置的语义理解转化为连续可通行路径。几何预测器虽能给出多条可行路径，但在空间候选中选出与临时管制一致的一条仍是难点：候选集常已包含接近人类示范的路径，错误主要来自"选择"而非"生成"（选择遗憾占几何排序器误差的53%）。

Method: 提出共享候选空间下的read-decide-execute-check框架。①角色解耦生成器构造完整几何候选；②以VLM输出的轨迹作为一种偏好，通过rank fusion将候选的几何得分与"候选与VLM轨迹的一致性"融合排序；③按MUTCD（《统一交通控制设施手册》）组织监督，训练SAGE-VLM先输出车道状态与危险侧，再给出通行决策与轨迹；④预测的危险侧转化为图像空间谓词，支撑最终干预，且干预在得分与位移上均有界。

Result: 在2,306个真实ROADWork验证片段上：候选集中87%的片段含有与人类示范距离10 px以内的路径；相对几何排序器，SAGE平均/最终位移误差分别降低8.3%与12.0%（主要改善远场），硬约束违规降低37%；轨迹偏好使全部四组生成器-选择器组合均有提升；精度与SOTA VLM模块相当且VLM响应更快，并在位移误差与安全指标上优于另外四个适配的驾驶VLM。

Conclusion: SAGE表明将语义理解（VLM轨迹偏好）与几何候选分离、再以规范结构化的语义监督与有界干预进行校验，可有效缓解施工区路径选择难题，在精度、远场精度与安全性上取得一致改进。

Abstract: Temporary traffic control redirects vehicles through work zones, requiring automated vehicles to connect an interpretation of the visible devices to a continuous passing path. Geometric predictors can provide several plausible paths, yet selecting the one consistent with the temporary control remains difficult. We propose SAGE (Semantic-to-Action Grounded Execution), a read-decide-execute-check framework that separates path construction, path preference, and control compliance within a shared candidate space. The central idea is to use a vision-language model's (VLM's) trajectory as a preference over intact geometric candidates. A role-decoupled generator constructs the candidates, and rank fusion combines each candidate's geometric score with its agreement with the VLM trajectory. Supervision structured around the Manual on Uniform Traffic Control Devices (MUTCD) trains SAGE-VLM to state the lane status and hazard side before its passage decision and trajectory. The predicted hazard side enters image-space predicates derived from MUTCD provisions, which support a final intervention bounded in score and displacement. On 2,306 real-world ROADWork validation episodes, the candidates include a path within 10 px of the human demonstration in 87% of episodes, yet selection regret accounts for 53% of the geometric ranker's error. SAGE reduces average and final displacement errors by 8.3% and 12.0% relative to the ranker, mostly in the far field, and hard-constraint violations by 37%. The trajectory opinion improves all four tested generator-selector combinations. SAGE is comparable to state-of-the-art VLM modules in accuracy, with a faster-responding VLM, and outperforms four other adapted driving VLMs in displacement error and safety measures. Project page: https://hhjj233.github.io/SAGE-page/.

</details>


### [81] [Search-Based Testing of Modality Sensitivity in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32360)
*Oliver Weißl,Eduardo Breitembach,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出黑盒测试框架MoST，通过多目标搜索比较图像单模态、文本单模态与联合扰动，刻画视觉语言模型的模态敏感性，发现联合扰动可用更小修改达到同等失效效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM鲁棒性评估多对图像与文本分别扰动，或仅评估预设的固定损坏等级，难以判断哪种模态对扰动更敏感，也无法揭示跨模态小扰动联合作用下是否引发失败。

Method: MoST为黑盒测试框架：以多目标搜索同时选择并组合图像与文本扰动、调节扰动强度，目标是在限制图像与提示改动幅度的前提下最大化预测性能下降；对比图像单独、文本单独与联合扰动三类设置。在四个VLM、ImageNet与驾驶图像的目标定位任务上评估，并辅以人类被试研究。

Result: 不同模型敏感性差异明显：部分模型以文本扰动为主，另一些则对两模态均敏感；多组配置下联合扰动在单输入修改更小时即达到或超过单模态扰动的失效效果。人类研究中，跨模型60%–76%的评估认为扰动输入仍可完成任务，且人类定位精度高于对应VLM。

Conclusion: 分别评估单模态会遗漏诱发失败的跨模态组合；模态敏感性分析比单一聚合失效率更能细致刻画VLM鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process images and text jointly, yet robustness evaluations often perturb these inputs separately or assess only predefined corruption levels. Such evaluations provide limited evidence of which modality is more susceptible to perturbations and whether smaller changes across both modalities can jointly expose failures. We present MoST, a black-box testing framework that characterizes modality sensitivity by comparing image-only, text-only, and joint perturbations. MoST uses multi-objective search to select and combine perturbations and adjust their severity, seeking prediction degradation while limiting changes to images and prompts. We evaluate MoST on four VLMs performing object localization on ImageNet and driving images. The models exhibit distinct sensitivities: textual perturbations dominate for some, whereas others are susceptible across both modalities. In several configurations, joint perturbations match or exceed the effectiveness of single-modality perturbations with smaller modifications to each input. In a human study, 60-76% of assessments across models judge the perturbed inputs sufficiently understandable to perform the task, and human localization is more accurate than that of the corresponding VLMs. These results show that evaluating modalities separately can overlook failure-inducing combinations and that modality sensitivity provides a more detailed account of VLM robustness than aggregate failure rates alone.

</details>


### [82] [Representation Learning via Composite Subspace Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32256)
*José Afonso,Mário Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出复合子空间流匹配（CSFM），将表征学习构建为对谱、颜色、光照子空间上确定性退化课程的最小方差逆过程，用硬时序风格截断约束轨迹方差，使容量受限编码器忽略瞬态风格伪影、编码持久内容，从而兼得判别性与生成可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习存在权衡：联合嵌入架构通过激进丢弃类内方差获得有效特征，但空间与重建信息不可逆地丢失；生成模型保留空间细节却难以分离语义，因优化被像素变化主导。需要弥合这一分歧。

Method: CSFM 不用非结构化各向同性噪声破坏数据，而是将表征学习表述为跨谱、颜色、光照子空间的确定性退化课程的最小方差逆变换；通过硬时序风格截断限制轨迹方差，驱动容量受限编码器忽略瞬态风格伪影并编码持久内容。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、STL-10 上，卷积与 ViT 架构均表现出有竞争力的判别迁移性并保持生成合成能力；避免非生成方法的坍缩病理，形成平滑隐空间，在线性探测与少样本分类上优于标准最优传输流，并支持连贯合成与轨迹插值。

Conclusion: 操控生成流的物理动力学，为学习同时具备线性可分性与生成可逆性的表征提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Distilling semantic meaning from raw image data remains a challenge in machine learning. Current self-supervised paradigms face a trade-off: joint-embedding architectures produce relevant features by aggressively discarding intra-class variance, irreversibly spatial and reconstruction. Conversely, generative models preserve spatial details but struggle to isolate semantics, as their optimization is dominated by pixel variations. To bridge this divide, we propose composite subspace flow matching (CSFM). Rather than corrupting data with unstructured isotropic noise, CSFM formulates representation learning as the variance-minimizing inversion of a deterministic degradation curriculum across spectral, color, and illumination subspaces. By bounding trajectory variance with hard temporal style truncation, a capacity-constrained encoder is driven to ignore transient style artifacts and encode persistent content. Empirical evaluations across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and STL-10 show that CSFM yields competitive discriminative transferability while preserving generative synthesis across both convolutional and vision transformer architectures. By avoiding the collapse pathologies of non-generative methods, CSFM forms a smooth latent space that outperforms standard optimal transport flows on linear probing and few-shot classification, while enabling coherent synthesis and trajectory interpolation. These results confirm that steering the physical dynamics of generative flows offers a scalable paradigm for learning representations that are simultaneously linearly separable and generatively invertible.

</details>


### [83] [SP-Guide: Enhancing Aerial Vision-Language Navigation with Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32697)
*Haoxiang Tang,Dong Zhou,Shen Tan,Minxuan Jiang,Xitong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SP-Guide 是一个基于 Transformer 的空中视觉语言导航（AVLN）目标预测框架，通过空间先验跨模态编码器、指令增强解码器和目标收敛滤波器，在 CityNav 基准上提升了成功率，且对未见环境的泛化提升最明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AVLN 方法将视角中心的 RGB 观测与全局锚定的历史地图融合为通用视觉嵌入，把跨模态空间对齐完全交给稀疏的导航监督去学习，导致对齐效果弱、泛化差。

Method: 提出 SP-Guide 框架，包含三部分：(1) Spatial Prior Cross-Modal Encoder，引入物理落地的空间先验作为归纳偏置以强化跨模态空间对齐；(2) Instruction Enhanced Decoder，利用语言指令增强解码；(3) Goal Convergence Filter，用于终端目标的细化收敛。基于 Transformer 进行目标预测。

Result: 在 CityNav 基准上相较代表性基线提升成功率，且在未见（unseen）环境上增益最大。

Conclusion: 物理落地的空间先验为鲁棒的空中视觉语言导航提供了有效的归纳偏置，可显著改善跨模态对齐与未见环境泛化能力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (AVLN) requires a UAV to reach a language-described target from partial egocentric observations while reasoning over large outdoor scenes. Existing approaches typically fuse view-centered RGB observations and globally anchored history maps as generic visual embeddings, leaving cross-modal spatial alignment to be learned from sparse navigation supervision. We present SP-Guide, a transformer- based goal-prediction framework that addresses this weakness of alignment through a Spatial Prior Cross-Modal Encoder, an Instruction Enhanced Decoder, and a Goal Convergence Filter for terminal target refinement. Experiments implemented on the CityNav benchmark show that SP-Guide improves success rate over the representative baselines, with the largest gains on unseen environments. These results indicate that physically grounded spatial priors provide an effective inductive bias for robust AVLN.

</details>


### [84] [ACVA-Bench: Beyond Prompt Following in Abstract Concept Visualization Ability for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32058)
*Mengyan Wang,Jiyuan An,Yining Liu,YaoWen Zhang,Liner Yang ang Erhong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“抽象概念可视化能力”(ACVA)并构建 ACVA-Bench 基准，从语义充分性、表征多样性、概念区分性三维度在低干预设定下评测 T2I 模型对无固定视觉指称的抽象概念（如“忠诚”）的可视化能力；因缺乏参考图，提出受概念语义约束的细粒度视觉-语义问答式评价方法并经人工验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 研究已验证图像质量与指令遵循，但 T2I 模型能否在无明确视觉方案时自主将抽象概念转化为准确、多样且可与近邻概念区分的视觉表征尚不清楚，亦缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 定义 ACVA；基于心理语言学抽象概念规范构建类别均衡的 ACVA-Bench，在不给具体物体、场景或视觉隐喻的低干预提示下，从语义充分性、表征多样性、概念区分性三维度评测；提出受概念语义约束的细粒度视觉-语义评测法，将概念语义分解为可独立验证的视觉-语义问题，并以人工评估验证其效度。

Result: 主流 T2I 模型依赖少量原型化视觉模式，在抽象概念的表征多样性与概念区分性上仍有提升空间；人工评估确认了新评测方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ACVA-Bench 为抽象概念可视化能力提供了可解释、可复现的评测基础，揭示当前模型在多样性、区分性上的不足，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable progress in image quality and instruction following, prompting growing research interest in their capacity for deep understanding that goes beyond literal prompt matching. However, without an explicit visual scheme provided, it remains unclear and has not been systematically evaluated whether T2I models can autonomously translate abstract concepts lacking a fixed visual referent, such as "loyalty," into visual representations that are accurate, diverse, and distinguishable from closely related concepts. We define this capability as Abstract Concept Visualization Ability (ACVA) and introduce ACVA-Bench, a category-balanced benchmark grounded in psycholinguistic norms of abstract concepts, which examines models' ACVA along three dimensions semantic sufficiency, representational diversity, and conceptual distinctiveness under a low-intervention setting that withholds concrete objects, scenes, or visual metaphors. Since abstract concepts lack a unique reference image, conventional evaluation metrics that rely on reference-image matching are difficult to apply. To address this, we propose a fine-grained visual-semantic evaluation method constrained by concept semantics, which decomposes concept semantics into independently verifiable visual-semantic questions to assess the semantic representation quality of generated images, with its validity further confirmed by human evaluation. Benchmarking results on mainstream T2I models show that these models rely on a small set of prototypical visual patterns, leaving room for improvement in representational diversity and conceptual distinctiveness for abstract concepts, thereby providing an interpretable and reproducible evaluation foundation for future research.

</details>


### [85] [Automated Nucleus Segmentation in Histology Images: An Attention U-Net Approach with Comprehensive Preprocessing and Inference Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31902)
*Akshat G,Divyansh Gupta,Tusar Kanti Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在MoNuSeg 2018数据集上实现轻量级Attention U-Net，结合染色去卷积、多次掩码平滑等预处理与高保真重叠块拼接推理策略，在全分辨率未见测试图像上取得IoU 0.8745的细胞核分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理中细胞核分割是定量分析的关键步骤，但组织学图像存在高核密度与染色异质性，给分割带来巨大挑战。

Method: 在MoNuSeg 2018数据集上构建轻量级Attention U-Net；流程包括全面的图像与掩码预处理（染色去卷积、多次掩码平滑），以及高分辨率图像推理时的高保真重叠Patch拼接策略。

Result: 在全分辨率未见测试图像上IoU达0.8745，表明注意力机制有效增强特征定位，并验证了拼接方法可生成无缝且精确的分割图。

Conclusion: Attention U-Net是面向复杂临床场景下自动化、可扩展细胞核勾画的有效且具竞争力的方法。

Abstract: Objectives: In the area of digital pathology, nucleus segmentation is an essential step for quantitative analysis; however, high nuclear density and staining heterogeneity in histology images pose a great challenge.
  Methods: This study implements a lightweight Attention U-Net model on the MoNuSeg 2018 dataset to overcome these challenges. The proposed pipeline integrates comprehensive image and mask preprocessing, including stain deconvolution and multi-pass mask smoothing, with a high-fidelity overlapping patch-stitching strategy during inference on high-resolution images.
  Results: The model achieved an Intersection over Union (IoU) score of 0.8745 on full-resolution, unseen test images. This result demonstrates the effectiveness of attention mechanisms in enhancing feature localization and validates the stitching methodology for generating seamless and accurate segmentation maps.
  Conclusion: Attention U-Net is an effective and competitive method for automated, scalable nucleus delineation in challenging clinical contexts.

</details>


### [86] [SyncVid: World-Equivariant Diffusion for Synchronized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31765)
*Farhad G. Zanjani,Animesh Karnewar,Fatih Porikli,Amirhossein Habibian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SyncVid提出在源自源视频的规范3D空间中采样扩散噪声先验，并光栅化到目标视角，从而实现多视角动态视频的同步生成，无需修改模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单段参考视频生成同一动态场景的多视角视频，需要跨视角同时合成已观测与未观测内容并保持一致。现有相机控制方法依赖相机嵌入、跨视角注意力和几何条件等架构机制，对静态内容有效，但难以保持细粒度动态几何在跨视角与时间上的一致性。作者认为问题根源在于各2D图像平面独立采样噪声先验。

Method: 将单目视频提升为规范4D表示，把噪声与场景几何及未见空间关联，并将结构化噪声光栅化到任意目标视角；由于渲染会引入局部相关性，提出跨视角一致的whitening（白化）过程，在去相关相邻像素的同时保持跨视角对应关系。该结构化噪声作为扩散先验，驱动经LoRA适配的预训练视频扩散模型，无需架构改动。

Result: 在随机性源头即施加时空与跨视角一致性约束，实验表明SyncVid生成同步的多视角视频，并在感知质量与跨视角一致性两方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 噪声采样空间的选择而非架构设计是跨视角一致性的关键；将扩散先验绑定到规范3D几何并以LoRA方式接入现有模型，是一种轻量且有效的多视角动态视频生成方案。

Abstract: Generating multiple videos of a dynamic scene from a single reference video requires synthesizing both observed and unobserved content consistently across viewpoints. Existing camera-control methods for video diffusion typically rely on architectural mechanisms, including camera embeddings, cross-view attention, and geometric conditioning. Although effective for static scene content, these approaches often struggle with the fine-grained, dynamic geometry that must remain coherent across views and over time. We synchronize the diffusion process itself by sampling the Gaussian noise prior in a canonical 3D space derived from the source video and rasterizing it into target cameras, rather than sampling independently in each 2D image plane. Our method, SyncVid, lifts a monocular video into a canonical 4D representation, associates noise with scene geometry and unseen space, and rasterizes the resulting structured noise into arbitrary views. Because rendering can introduce local correlations, we further propose a cross-view-consistent whitening procedure that decorrelates neighboring pixels while preserving correspondence across views. The structured noise serves as the diffusion prior for a LoRA-adapted pretrained video diffusion model, requiring no architectural modifications. By enforcing spatiotemporal and cross-view coherence at the source of stochasticity, SyncVid generates synchronized videos and improves both perceptual quality and cross-view consistency in our experiments. Project page: https://qualcomm-ai-research.github.io/SyncVid-Webpage.

</details>


### [87] [Standing on Open Shoulders: Regional Fine-Tuning of Open-Weight Camera-Trap Models for UK Wildlife with Modest Data and Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32573)
*Paul Fergus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 小团队利用开放权重（DeepFaune + MegaDetector v6）微调出英国区域相机陷阱物种分类器 DeepFaune-UK，严格图像级准确率达 88.8%–90.6%，远超 SpeciesNet 等基线；但最大同口径增益来自他人训练的新基座模型，说明去中心化依赖大机构持续开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱物种分类由资源充足机构的大模型主导，常缺乏区域保护所需的物种级区分（如英国本土红松鼠与入侵灰松鼠）。作者追问：开放权重能否让数据规模有限、仅单卡 GPU 的小团队真正参与 AI 开发。

Method: 在 28 个英国类别上微调开源 DeepFaune 分类器，搭配未改动的 MegaDetector v6 检测器，得到 DeepFaune-UK v1.2；在 20,212 张人工核验的 MammalWeb 公民科学图像上，与 DeepFaune v1.5.0、SpeciesNet 对比，并剔除与训练档案中 1,039 张图像存在共享序列的样本。随后将同一微调配方无缝迁移到 DeepFaune v1.5.0 所基于的 DINOv3 分类器，得到 v1.3；并进行贡献者重采样、同口径（like-for-like）评估、无重叠贡献者子集评估（1,850 张、26 位贡献者），以及 10%（每类约 150 个）训练裁剪的子集实验与显存/耗时测量。

Result: v1.2 严格图像级准确率（含空帧）88.8%，基线 DeepFaune v1.5.0 与 SpeciesNet 分别为 56.5% 与 59.0%；但在 DeepFaune 较粗标签分辨率下，v1.5.0 更准确（91.4% vs 88.8%），贡献者重采样下差异不显著。迁移到 DINOv3 后 v1.3 严格准确率升至 90.6%，同口径差距缩至 0.8 个百分点，将误判为黇鹿的狍减少 69%；在 1,850 张无重叠图像上三者无显著差异（90.5%、90.6%、89.9%）。仅用 10% 训练裁剪即可获得相近的分布外准确率，DINOv2 子集训练约 22 分钟、显存不足 4 GB。

Conclusion: 开放权重使小团队开展区域模型开发可行；但最大同口径增益来自他人训练的新基座模型，因此这种"民主化"依赖大型机构持续以开放权重发布模型，且其许可条款可能变更。

Abstract: Camera-trap species classification is dominated by large models from well-resourced organisations, which often lack the species-level distinctions regional conservation needs, such as native red versus invasive grey squirrels in the UK. We ask whether open weights let a small team with a modest dataset and a single GPU take a real part in AI development. We fine-tuned the open DeepFaune classifier on 28 UK classes, paired it with the unmodified MegaDetector v6, and compared the resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.2 with DeepFaune v1.5.0 and SpeciesNet on 20,212 hand-verified MammalWeb citizen-science images, after removing sequences sharing any of 1,039 images with our training archive. DeepFaune-UK achieved a strict image-level accuracy of 88.8%, including empty frames, against 56.5% and 59.0% for the baselines, but at DeepFaune's coarser label resolution DeepFaune v1.5.0 was more accurate, though not significantly under contributor resampling (91.4% vs. 88.8%). As DeepFaune v1.5.0 uses a newer DINOv3-based classifier, we applied the identical recipe, without new data, to that classifier. The resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.3 reached 90.6% strict accuracy, narrowed the like-for-like gap to 0.8 points and reduced roe deer misclassified as fallow deer by 69%. On 1,850 images from the 26 contributors with no overlapping images, the three DeepFaune-based models did not differ significantly (90.5%, 90.6%, 89.9% like-for-like). Fine-tuning on 10% of the training crops (about 150 per class) gave out-of-distribution accuracy similar to using all of them, and a DINOv2 run on this subset took about 22 minutes with under 4 GB of GPU memory. Open weights thus make regional model development feasible for small teams, but the largest like-for-like gain came from a newer base model trained by others, so this democratisation depends on large actors continuing to release open weights, under licences that may change.

</details>


### [88] [Distortion-Aware Spatiotemporal Learning for Real-Time Wide-Angle Video Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32500)
*Lang Nie,Yihan Yang,Wenbo Nie,Hongguang Zhu,Yang Wei,Chunyu Lin,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FastVC——面向广角全身体视频矫正的高效框架，通过畸变感知薄板样条与双层时空学习实现高保真、稳定的实时矫正（57.0 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广角镜头虽扩大视场，但图像边缘存在强烈非线性畸变，导致人体被非自然拉伸。现有方法多局限于人脸区域、计算成本高、缺乏时序建模，造成全身矫正不完整、难以实时部署且帧间抖动明显。

Method: 基于镜头物理提出畸变感知薄板样条（DA-TPS）模块，将控制点非线性重映射为周边密集布局，在不增加计算量的情况下提升几何拟合精度；并设计双层时空学习方案：模型层面引入邻域时空注意力（NST），以低开销聚合局部上下文并选择性融合历史特征；优化层面采用稀疏时空监督，仅对锚帧施加空间对齐，解耦逐帧精度与时序稳定性。

Result: 构建首个广角全身体视频矫正基准数据集；实验表明FastVC在矫正保真度与视觉稳定性上达到SOTA，运行速度达57.0 FPS。

Conclusion: FastVC以畸变感知的几何建模结合双层时空学习，有效解决了广角全身体视频矫正中的完整性与时序稳定性问题，兼具高精度与实时性，并提供了标准化评测基准。

Abstract: While wide-angle lenses provide an expanded field of view (FoV), they exhibit severe nonlinear distortion near image boundaries, unnaturally stretching people in these regions. Existing methods mostly focus on facial regions, require high computational costs, and lack temporal modeling, resulting in incomplete full-body rectification, impractical real-time deployment, and noticeable inter-frame shakes. To tackle these issues, we propose \textbf{FastVC}, an efficient full-body video rectification framework driven by distortion-aware spatiotemporal learning. Grounded in lens physics, FastVC introduces a distortion-aware thin-plate spline (DA-TPS) module that nonlinearly remaps control points into a peripheral-dense layout, enabling more accurate geometric fitting without additional computational cost. We further develop a dual-level spatiotemporal learning scheme for causal and stable video rectification. At the model level, neighborhood spatiotemporal attention (NST) aggregates local context and selectively fuses historical features with low overhead. At the optimization level, sparse spatiotemporal supervision applies spatial alignment only to anchor frames, decoupling frame-wise accuracy from temporal stability. Besides, we contribute the first benchmark dataset for wide-angle full-body video correction. Extensive experiments show that FastVC achieves state-of-the-art rectification fidelity and visual stability at 57.0 FPS.

</details>


### [89] [L0-Norm-Based CycleGAN for Unpaired Brain CT-to-MR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32491)
*Yunxiao Zhang,Shouqiang Du,Zexian Xu,Chuanlong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将CycleGAN的L1循环一致性损失替换为用平滑有界函数近似的L0范数循环一致性项，以提升非配对CT→MR合成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT与MR对脑部提供互补信息，但同时采集并非总可行。CycleGAN可实现非配对CT-to-MR合成，但标准L1范数循环一致性惩罚随残差幅值线性增长，导致网络过度关注大残差、忽视小残差。

Method: 引入基于L0范数的循环一致性项，并用平滑且有界的函数近似L0范数：既提高对小残差的敏感度，又有效抑制大残差的影响。与结构完全相同的L1基线对比，在102张图像上训练、9张测试图像上评估。

Result: MSE由0.03464降至0.02746（降低20.7%）；平均PSNR由14.77 dB升至15.80 dB；平均SSIM由0.5407升至0.5689。同时最大MSE更低，最小PSNR与最小SSIM均高于基线。

Conclusion: 在该数据集上，修改循环一致性惩罚项可提升重建精度，说明损失函数设计是非配对跨模态合成性能的重要影响因素。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance (MR) imaging provide complementary information for the brain, but acquiring both modalities is not always possible. The cycle-consistent adversarial network (CycleGAN) enables unpaired CT-to-MR synthesis, but because the standard L1-norm cycle?consistency penalty grows linearly with the residual magnitude, the network tends to focus on large residuals. This paper introduces an L0-norm-based cycle?consistency term, in which the L0-norm is approximated by a smooth and bounded function. This approximation not only increases sensitivity to small residuals but also effectively suppresses the influence of large residuals. The proposed model and an otherwise identical L1-norm baseline were trained on 102 images and evaluated on 9 test images. The proposed model achieves a lower mean squared error (0.02746 versus 0.03464, a 20.7% reduction), a higher mean peak signal-to-noise ratio (15.80 versus 14.77 dB), and a higher mean structural similarity index (0.5689 versus 0.5407). It also has a lower maximum MSE and higher minimum PSNR and SSIM than the baseline. These results indicate that modifying the cycle-consistency penalty can improve reconstruction accuracy on this dataset.

</details>


### [90] [PCFBench: How Far Can Large Vision-Language Models Go in Physics-aware Photonic Inverse Design?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32485)
*Shengchao Chen,Ting Shu,Sufen Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个面向光子晶体光纤（PCF）逆向设计全流程的视觉语言模型（VLM）评测基准 PCFBench，覆盖 26 个任务、六大认知层级、18 类光纤结构、超 22 万 FDTD 仿真样本；实验揭示"感知—设计鸿沟"及多智能体流水线中的误差级联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCF 设计长期依赖专家手工完成，需将几何直觉与波导物理深度耦合，自动化程度低。VLM 被视为自动化 PCF 设计的潜在路径，但其能否从显微图像中真正进行光子物理推理，还是仅做视觉模式识别，尚缺乏系统性评估手段。

Method: 构建 PCFBench 基准：按认知需求递增划分六大类别（几何感知、物理理解、文本生成、多模态推理、逆向设计决策、代码生成），共 26 个任务；基于 18 类结构多样的光纤家族、超过 220K 条 FDTD 仿真样本构建数据。评测方式包括单模型在孤立任务上的表现，以及多智能体系统（MAS）在"感知→设计"完整序列流水线上的表现。

Result: 发现显著的"感知—设计鸿沟"：现有模型能以中等成功率识别光纤几何结构，却持续无法将视觉理解转化为物理感知的设计动作；将智能体级联到流水线后，上游感知错误向下游设计环节传播并放大，误差级联明显。

Conclusion: 模型规模扩展与朴素智能体串联均不足以解决该问题，闭环设计需要显式的物理 grounding。PCFBench 为 AI 辅助光子学工程提供了严格、多维的测试平台，用于衡量进展。

Abstract: Photonic crystal fibers (PCFs) underpin critical applications in telecommunications, sensing, and high-power laser delivery, yet their design remains a manual, expert-intensive process that couples geometric intuition with deep waveguide physics. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising path toward automating PCF design, but can they truly reason about photonic physics from microscopy images, or do they merely recognize visual patterns? We introduce PCFBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLMs on the perception, reasoning, and decision steps of the photonic inverse-design loop. PCFBench spans 26 tasks organized into six categories of increasing cognitive demand (Geometry Perception, Physics Understanding, Text Generation, Multi-modal Reasoning, Inverse-Design Decision, and Code Generation), built from over 220K FDTD simulation samples across 18 structurally diverse fiber families. We evaluate both individual VLMs on isolated tasks and multi-agent systems (MAS) on the full sequential pipeline from perception to design. Our results reveal a striking perception-design gap: current models can identify fiber geometry with moderate success, yet consistently fail to translate that visual understanding into physics-aware design actions. Moreover, cascading agents through the pipeline exposes significant error propagation, where upstream perception mistakes compound into downstream design failures. These findings suggest that neither model scaling nor naive agent chaining suffices, and that closing the loop demands explicit physical grounding. PCFBench provides the community with a rigorous, multi-dimensional testbed to measure progress toward AI-assisted photonic engineering. The project is available at https://pcfbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [PROVE: Probabilistic Visual Grounding via Patch-Level Evidence for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32440)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Tianru Zhang,Li Ju,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PROVE，一种后置式证据适配器，将冻结 CLIP 的 patch 特征映射为逐 patch 的 Beta 分布，实现概率化视觉定位，单次前向即可输出后验均值定位图、空间可空（vacuity）图与图像级缺失分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CLIP 类视觉语言模型的视觉定位只给出单一的确定性相关性图，无法指示定位是否可靠、也无法判断指称对象是否存在于图像中。作者希望在不改动主干的前提下，为冻结的视觉语言定位系统引入可验证的概率不确定性。

Method: 将定位建模为逐 patch 的成员判定：每个 patch 累积支持/反对其属于指称对象的正负证据，从而允许匹配零个、一个或多个 patch。用软 patch 覆盖目标上的证据学习目标训练适配器，并设计先验拉回（prior-pull）目标使无指称样本的证据保持接近无信息先验，使 vacuity 本身充当缺失信号，无需专门的在场分类器。推理时闭式解得到后验均值定位图、空间 vacuity 图与图像级缺失分数。

Result: 在指称表达基准上与 SOTA CLIP 定位方法定位性能相当；不确定性能够跟踪定位误差，支持弃权下的选择性预测；图像级分数可检测指称对象完全缺失的情况。

Conclusion: 证据式 patch 成员建模为冻结视觉语言定位系统提供了一种简单、即插即用的概率验证机制。

Abstract: Visual grounding with vision-language models such as CLIP typically produces a single deterministic relevance map for an image-query pair, without indicating whether the localisation is reliable or whether the referent is present at all. We introduce \textbf{PROVE} (\textbf{Pro}babilistic \textbf{V}isual Grounding via Patch-Level \textbf{E}vidence), a post-hoc evidential adapter that maps frozen CLIP patch features to per-patch Beta distributions without modifying the backbone. PROVE casts grounding as per-patch membership: each patch accumulates positive and negative evidence for whether it belongs to the referent, so a query may match zero, one, or many patches. We train PROVE with an evidential objective on soft patch-coverage targets, and define a prior-pull objective that keeps absent-referent examples near an uninformative prior, so that vacuity serves as an absence signal without a dedicated presence classifier. During inference, a single forward pass yields, in closed form, a posterior-mean grounding map, a spatial vacuity map, and an image-level absence score. We evaluate PROVE on referring-expression benchmarks: it is competitive in localisation with state-of-the-art CLIP grounding methods, its uncertainty tracks localisation error and supports selective prediction under abstention, and its image-level score detects when the referent is absent altogether. Our evidential patch-membership modelling offers a simple, drop-in mechanism for probabilistic verification in frozen vision-language grounding systems.

</details>


### [92] [An Error Budget for Shuffled Multimodal Windows in the 3rd WEAR Challenge: Stacking, Count Constraints and Seam Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32398)
*Soheil Kooklan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该系统通过堆叠窗口分类器、图平滑特征与带软计数约束的mean-field式解码器，在WEAR挑战四名未见参与者上取得仿真0.936、提交0.938（私有榜0.9231/0.9247）的macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战要求对四名未见参与者每个1秒窗口给出活动标签，但每窗口仅有15个VideoMAEv2帧嵌入、一条随机肢体的加速度信号，且窗口被打乱；本文旨在对这一设定做实证分析，评估各组件（堆叠、计数约束、后继链接）的实际贡献。

Method: 在参与者图上对视频邻居特征做平均并堆叠窗口分类器，同时对后继候选打分；使用带每类活动软计数约束的mean-field式解码器对单个参与者的全部窗口联合标注（离线、转导设定，依赖每类活动各执行一次）。引入二次接缝距离对约720个候选中的同肢后继排序。解码器参数与混合权重在折内选择，误标片段排除于训练。评估采用参与者级bootstrap区间的参与者内仿真。

Result: 堆叠较stage-1融合提升0.051 macro F1，计数约束提升0.011（95%区间0.006–0.017）；二次接缝距离在83.9%情况下将真实同肢后继排第一，但加入链接模型后macro F1变化为-0.0001（-0.0006至0.0005）。仿真得分0.936（每活动执行一次者0.940），提交配置0.938（0.943），个体得分0.80–0.97。oracle平均后达0.973，剩余错误中79%涉及null类。私有榜：0.9231（公开0.9254），后续提交0.9247（公开0.9253）。

Conclusion: 堆叠与计数约束是主要增益来源，而接缝距离排序虽准确但无端到端收益；剩余误差集中于null类。由于采用非嵌套堆叠且转导使用无标签特征，结果为非完全归纳估计；私有榜分数在未参与任何选择的情况下与仿真基本一致。

Abstract: The 3rd WEAR Dataset Challenge asks for an activity label for every one-second window of four unseen participants. Each window has 15 VideoMAEv2 frame embeddings, the accelerometer signal of one random limb, and the windows are shuffled. This report is an empirical analysis of one system built after the challenge deadline. Window classifiers are stacked with features averaged over per-participant graphs of video neighbours and scored successor candidates, and a mean-field style decoder with a soft count constraint per activity labels all windows of a participant together. The setting is offline and transductive, and the count constraint relies on each activity being performed once. In a participant-wise simulation with participant-level bootstrap intervals, stacking added 0.051 macro F1 over stage-1 fusion and the count constraint 0.011 (95% interval 0.006 to 0.017). A quadratic seam distance ranks the true same-limb successor first among about 720 candidates in 83.9% of cases, but adding it to the link model changed macro F1 by -0.0001 (-0.0006 to 0.0005). With the decoder settings and the mixing weight chosen inside the folds and the mislabelled spans left out of training, the system scores 0.936 in simulation (0.940 for participants who performed each activity once); the submitted configuration scores 0.938 (0.943). Per-participant scores range from 0.80 to 0.97. These results come from a non-nested stacking protocol that uses unlabelled features transductively, so they are not a fully inductive estimate. An oracle that averages the final unary scores over the true segments reaches 0.973, and 79% of the remaining errors involve the null class. On the private leaderboard, which no choice used, the selected Kaggle submission scored 0.9231 (public 0.9254, with bounds partly tuned there), and a later one with bounds chosen in simulation scored 0.9247 (public 0.9253).

</details>


### [93] [UniWorld: A Unified World Model for Real-Time Visual World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32381)
*Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Shuaiwei Wang,Zixuan Duan,Haibin Huang,Feng Xu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniWorld统一框架，在单一视频生成模型中同时耦合深度轨迹与参考外观，实现连续视觉世界仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视觉世界仿真需要将演化中的场景几何与时间上持续的外观耦合起来，而现有方法难以在同一时间线上同时保持视觉与几何历史，并在高效执行下保证历史精确性。

Method: 核心设计是在公共时间线上维护视觉与几何历史：1）噪声对齐的重叠续接（noise-aligned overlapping continuation）把已生成历史放置于规定的采样噪声水平，且仅提交新生成的latent；2）滚动深度latent状态（rolling depth latent state）保存对应几何控制，增量读取新可用几何，并在每个采样窗口内固定条件。由此在精确保留latent历史的同时，将活跃深度状态限制在窗口大小之内。

Result: 在多样场景类别上评估连续生成、成对历史干预与长时程行为；同一生成器支持估计、原生及按需仿真器深度；缓存与增量执行路径输出逐位一致（bitwise-identical）；外观修改与几何控制分支通过同一接口操作而不改变已提交历史；稳态DiT执行路径达到实时生成吞吐。

Conclusion: UniWorld以统一接口实现了几何—外观联合的连续世界仿真，兼顾历史精确性、有界状态、执行一致性与实时效率。

Abstract: Continuous visual world simulation requires coupling evolving scene geometry with temporally persistent appearance. We present UniWorld, a unified framework that integrates depth trajectories and reference appearance within a shared video generation model. The central design maintains visual and geometric histories on a common timeline. Noise-aligned overlapping continuation places generated history at the prescribed sampling noise level and commits only newly generated latents. Rolling depth latent state preserves the corresponding geometric controls, incrementally reads newly available geometry, and keeps the conditioning fixed within each sampling window. Together, these mechanisms ensure exact preservation of latent history while bounding the active depth state by the window size. We evaluate continuous generation, paired history interventions, and long-horizon behavior across diverse scene categories. The same generator supports estimated, native, and on-demand simulator depth, while cached and incremental execution paths produce bitwise-identical outputs. Appearance changes and geometric control branches operate through the same interface without modifying committed history. The measured steady-state DiT execution path achieves real-time generation throughput, demonstrating the efficiency of UniWorld for continuous visual world simulation.

</details>


### [94] [4DMemory: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Memory with Harness-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32332)
*Yanhao Jia,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Weigao Sun,Feida Zhu,Pengkun Jiao,Sihan Yang,Ming Ma,Steven Hoi,Erik Cambria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LongVideo数据集（约900小时视频、5000+人工审核问题）与4DMemoryBench基准，并开发4DMemory Harness（整合四类技能、22+专用工具模型与模型特定RAG系统），用于评估并提升长时域时空推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能高效处理跨越数小时至数年的多模态流，构建持久、细粒度的时空记忆；而现有基准集中于短、中上下文任务，多数基础模型难以在长时域下保持细粒度信息，缺乏对长时域时空推理的系统评估与改进手段。

Method: 构建LongVideo数据集，涵盖视觉、文本与时空推理，跨越多种上下文长度；建立4DMemoryBench基准；提出4DMemory Harness，集成四种互补技能、22+专用工具模型以及针对具体模型的检索增强生成（RAG）系统。

Result: 在13个主流模型上的评测显示：独立基础模型在长时域任务上表现不佳；4DMemory Harness使所有模型性能提升超过50%；相较此前基准，相同模型在4DMemoryBench上性能下降超过10%。

Conclusion: 4DMemory有望推动具备复杂长时域任务处理能力的时空智能体发展。

Abstract: Humans efficiently process multimodal streams spanning hours to years, building persistent, fine-grained spatiotemporal memories that support reasoning about and predicting long-horizon events. In contrast, existing benchmarks focus on short- and medium-context tasks, and most foundation models struggle to preserve fine-grained information over long horizons. To evaluate and improve long-horizon spatiotemporal reasoning, we introduce the LongVideo dataset and the 4DMemoryBench. LongVideo comprises about 900 hours of video and over 5,000 manually reviewed questions covering visual, textual, and spatiotemporal reasoning across diverse context lengths. We further develop the 4DMemory Harness, which integrates four complementary skills, 22+ specialized tool models, and a model-specific retrieval-augmented generation system. Evaluating 13 leading models, we find that standalone foundation models struggle with long-horizon tasks, while the 4DMemory Harness improves all of them by over 50\%. Compared to previous benchmark, the same models exhibit a performance drop of more than 10\% on 4DMemoryBench. We hope 4DMemory will facilitate the development of spatiotemporal agents capable of addressing complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [95] [DeformStruct: Deformable Structure Modeling for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32321)
*Mengnan Liu,Sanping Zhou,Kun Xia,Yonghao Dong,Jie Wang,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DeformStruct：面向点监督时序动作定位（PTAL）的实例级可变形结构建模框架，通过结构锚点挖掘、可变形部件对齐、关系结构组织三阶段显式建模动作实例内部结构，在四个数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTAL 仅对每个实例提供一个标注时间点，监督稀疏，难以指导动作实例结构组织的建模；现有方法主要依赖分类响应推断动作区域，未充分建模单个实例内多个动作相关部件之间的结构组织关系。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 结构锚点挖掘（Structural Anchor Mining）——从标注点与动作响应区域中发现多个锚点，作为当前实例的结构节点；(2) 可变形部件对齐（Deformable Part Alignment）——通过形变感知匹配将潜在结构部件与锚点关联，使各部件的时间支撑可随实例差异自适应调整；(3) 关系结构组织（Relational Structural Organization）——显式建模对齐部件之间的相对时间关系，形成刻画部件组织方式的关系表征。

Result: 在 THUMOS14、ActivityNet-v1.3、GTEA 和 BEOID 四个数据集上进行大量实验，点监督时序定位性能取得一致提升，验证了可变形结构建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 在稀疏点监督下，显式建模实例内部部件级结构及部件间时序关系可有效提升时序动作定位性能，可变形结构建模为 PTAL 提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Point-supervised Temporal Action Localization (PTAL) aims to localize action instances using only a single annotated timestamp for each instance. Such sparse supervision offers limited guidance for modeling the structural organization of an action instance. Existing PTAL methods primarily infer action regions from classification responses, leaving the structural organization of multiple action-related parts within each instance insufficiently modeled. To address this problem, we propose DeformStruct, an instance-level deformable structure modeling framework for PTAL. DeformStruct progressively constructs the structure of each action instance through three stages. First, Structural Anchor Mining discovers multiple anchors from annotated points and action-responsive regions, providing structural nodes grounded in the current instance. Second, Deformable Part Alignment associates latent structural parts with these anchors through deformation-aware matching, allowing their temporal supports to adapt to instance-dependent variations. Third, Relational Structural Organization explicitly models the relative temporal relations among the aligned parts, forming a relational representation that characterizes how different action-related parts are organized together. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14, ActivityNet-v1.3, GTEA, and BEOID demonstrate consistent improvements in point-supervised temporal localization and validate the effectiveness of deformable structural modeling.

</details>


### [96] [Hyperparameter-Free Boundary-Interior t-vMF Dice Loss for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32266)
*Hinako Mitsuoka,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自调边界感知 t-vMF Dice 损失（STB-tvMF Dice），按边界与内部区域分别自动确定形状参数，无需超参数调优，在三个数据集与多种架构上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割中类别不平衡与解剖边界附近的错误是主要难点；自适应 t-vMF Dice 损失虽能在训练中调整相似度函数，但需依赖验证集调优形状自适应参数，代价高且不具原则性。

Method: 将边界与内部区域分开处理：利用上一轮 epoch 中各区预测与目标的平均余弦相似度，确定该区 t-vMF 函数的形状参数，并在当前 epoch 内固定。该规则使相似度函数在观测到的平均相似度处斜率最大，从而在无需验证集选参的情况下提供有原则的适应依据。

Result: 在三个数据集和多种网络架构上验证有效；在 Synapse 数据集上用 U-Net，平均 DSC 达 70.75%，而 Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss 为 63.03%。

Conclusion: STB-tvMF Dice 通过无超参的区域自适应形状参数设定，缓解类别不平衡与边界误差问题，显著优于需调参的自适应基线；代码即将公开。

Abstract: Class imbalance and errors near anatomical boundaries remain challenges in medical image segmentation, motivating loss functions that account for these difficulties. Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss adjusts the similarity function during training but requires hyperparameter tuning. We propose Self-Tuned Boundary-Aware t-vMF Dice Loss (STB-tvMF Dice), which adapts the function separately for boundary and interior regions without tuning shape-adaptation parameters. Each region's shape parameter of t-vMF function is determined from its mean prediction-target cosine similarity in the preceding epoch and remains fixed throughout the current epoch. This rule maximizes the slope of the similarity function at the observed mean similarity, providing a principled basis for adaptation without validation-based parameter selection. Experiments on three datasets and architectures demonstrated its effectiveness. On Synapse with U-Net, STB-tvMF Dice achieved a mean DSC of 70.75%, compared with 63.03% for Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss. Our code will be available soon.

</details>


### [97] [LapaFM: A Laparoscopic Foundation Model via Multi-Stage Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32263)
*Xiaobo Zhu,Shiwei Wu,Yu An,Yuechen Tao,He Sun,Song Zhang,Jianbo Wang,Xiaojing Wang,Junwei Liu,Wei Guo,Yanfei Shao,Xuan Zhao,Jiangwei Xiao,Wenbo Niu,Bo Wei,Yuhan Wang,Gang Hu,Fei Liu,Kunshan He,Chongwei Chi,Jianqiang Tang,Kang Zhang,Jie Tian,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LapaFM是一个基于2万余分钟腹腔镜手术视频、采用多阶段持续学习策略训练的基础模型，学习多模态视觉表征，统一支持去烟、手术阶段识别、器械与息肉分割、病灶识别等多任务，在15个队列上优于任务专用模型与现有基础模型，并可泛化至胃肠道内镜任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的术中腹腔镜视频分析有望提升手术决策与操作精度，但有效的手术辅助要求模型能够在多种不同的术中任务间泛化，现有方法多为任务专用、难以跨任务迁移。

Method: 构建腹腔镜手术基础模型LapaFM，使用超过20,000分钟手术视频，采用多阶段持续学习（continual learning）策略以缓解灾难性遗忘与任务间干扰，学习多模态视觉表征，并统一支持去烟、手术阶段识别、器械与息肉分割、病灶识别等多项任务。

Result: 在来自不同成像设备的13个回顾性队列与2个前瞻性队列上评估，LapaFM在各数据集上持续优于任务专用模型和现有基础模型；可泛化至胃肠道内镜任务（息肉检测与分类）；恢复超过90%的烟雾遮挡帧；在淋巴结转移预测与前瞻性息肉分类上达到临床医生水平或更优。

Conclusion: 多阶段持续学习训练的统一基础模型能够有效实现跨任务、跨设备、跨领域的腹腔镜手术视觉分析，具备临床级辅助潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-based analysis of intraoperative laparoscopic videos has the potential to improve surgical decision-making and precision. However, effective surgical assistance requires models that can generalize across diverse intraoperative tasks. Here we present LapaFM, a foundation model for laparoscopic surgery trained on more than 20,000 minutes of surgical videos using a multi-stage continual learning strategy that mitigates catastrophic forgetting and inter-task interference. LapaFM learns multimodal visual representations and supports multiple tasks, including smoke removal, surgical phase recognition, instrument and polyp segmentation, and lesion identification. The model was evaluated on 13 retrospective and two prospective cohorts collected from different imaging devices. LapaFM consistently outperformed task-specific models and existing foundation models across datasets, and also generalized to gastrointestinal endoscopy tasks, including polyp detection and classification. Notably, it recovered over 90% of smoke-obscured frames and achieved clinician-level or superior performance in lymph node metastasis prediction and prospective polyp classification.

</details>


### [98] [Easy-3Dfy: Object-Level Geometric Composition for Single-Image 3D Scene Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32250)
*Ziang Zhang,Wenyou Yi,Zehan Wang,Jiachen Xu,Xintong Han,Chunchao Guo,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Easy-3Dfy组合式流程，将物体生成与场景布局估计解耦，并用基于RPF的对齐器建立几何对应，在Scene-Maker和3D-FUTURE200上多项几何指标优于SAM-3Dfy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D场景构建是3D视觉的基础问题。SAM-3Dfy类端到端方法在细粒度场景几何与资产品质上不足；而专用3D物体生成器能提供细节几何，3D基础模型具备强泛化空间理解能力，二者优势未被充分利用。

Method: 提出Easy-3Dfy组合式工作流：将物体生成与场景布局估计解耦，使专用模型各自发挥优势；再用基于RPF（相对位姿场）的对齐器，在物体局部网格与部分场景点图之间建立几何对应，恢复物体布局以完成场景组合；无需完整场景级监督。

Result: 在Scene-Maker与3D-FUTURE200数据集上，多项几何指标持续优于SAM-3Dfy。

Conclusion: 显式几何组合能有效结合高质量物体生成与强空间理解，且该流程可分别受益于3D物体生成与基础模型的独立进展。

Abstract: Single-image 3D scene construction is a fundamental problem in 3D vision. Recent methods such as SAM-3Dfy enable end-to-end reconstruction of 3D assets and their scene layouts, but still struggle with fine-grained scene geometry and asset quality. Meanwhile, specialized 3D object generators provide detailed object geometry, while 3D foundation models offer strong and generalizable spatial understanding. We propose Easy-3Dfy, a compositional workflow that decouples object generation from scene layout estimation, allowing specialized models to fully exploit their respective strengths. An RPF-based aligner then bridges these capabilities by establishing geometric correspondence between object-local meshes and partial scene pointmaps, recovering object layout for scene composition. Without requiring complete scene-level supervision, the proposed workflow consistently outperforms SAM-3Dfy across multiple geometric metrics on Scene-Maker and 3D-FUTURE200. Our results demonstrate that explicit geometric composition can effectively combine high-quality object generation with strong spatial understanding, while enabling the workflow to benefit independently from advances in both 3D object generation and foundation models.

</details>


### [99] [SyncPro: Synchronous Reasoning and Responding for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32132)
*Xueyun Tian,Bingbing Xu,Jiaming Liu,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Yu Zhao,Ruihua Huang,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对主动式流式视频理解中"推理进行"与"及时响应"难以协调的问题，提出同步双流框架 SyncPro，将证据追踪与用户响应解耦为可独立激活的两条生成路径，并以 SFT + 轨迹级强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作分别具备"何时响应"的主动能力（proactive video assistants）与"按计划生成中间文本以组织观测"的流式推理能力，但二者如何协同仍缺乏研究。直接拼接存在三点缺陷：固定推理间隔会在无相关变化时误触发更新、或延迟有用更新；共享单一生成路径耦合了"保存证据供后续决策"与"只输出用户所请求内容"两种不同需求；且响应必须等待推理结束后才能生成，即使证据已充分。

Method: 提出 SyncPro 同步双流框架，包含 Inner（内部证据追踪）与 Response（面向用户响应）两条专用生成路径；二者共享因果历史、独立激活，使相关证据变化可触发内部更新，同时响应能与推理并发进行。构建 189K 双流样本，采用监督微调后接轨迹级强化学习的两阶段训练。

Result: 在三个主动式基准上实验显示，响应时机判断更优，对用户请求的答案准确率更高。

Conclusion: 将内部推理与用户响应解耦并允许并发激活，是协调流式视频中持续推理与及时响应的有效设计，配合轨迹级强化学习可显著提升主动响应质量。

Abstract: Proactive streaming video understanding requires assistants to track unfolding events and respond when a user's requested condition is met. To make these decisions, assistants must accumulate relevant evidence and reassess response conditions as new observations arrive. Existing work offers complementary capabilities for this process: proactive video assistants learn when to respond, while streaming video reasoning methods generate scheduled intermediate text to organize observations and support subsequent answers. Together, these capabilities provide a foundation for proactive reasoning, but how to coordinate ongoing reasoning with timely responses remains underexplored. In a direct adaptation, fixed reasoning intervals can trigger updates when nothing relevant has changed or postpone useful updates until the next scheduled step. Sharing one generation path also couples two distinct requirements: preserving evidence for future decisions and producing only the response requested by the user. Moreover, responses generated after reasoning must wait for it to finish, even when sufficient evidence is already available. To address these challenges, we introduce SyncPro, a synchronous dual-stream framework with specialized generation paths for evidence tracking and user-facing responses. Its Inner and Response branches share causal history and activate independently, allowing relevant evidence changes to trigger internal updates and responses to proceed concurrently with ongoing reasoning. We construct 189K dual-stream examples and train SyncPro through supervised fine-tuning followed by trajectory-level reinforcement learning. Experiments across three proactive benchmarks show improved response timing and more accurate answers to user requests.

</details>


### [100] [MAGIC: Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration for VLM Test-time Robustness Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32013)
*Shunshun Gu,Enze Zhang,Ming Pei,Hang Zhou,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MAGIC 方法，通过决策边界（margin）门控选择性激活文本/视觉扰动分支，并结合证据恢复与分支剪枝，高效缓解 LVLMs 推理时的语言偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放（如对比解码）方法存在两点不足：扰动过程中有意移除证据导致信息损失，以及冗余的推理开销。

Method: 先度量关键 token 的预测 margin，用门控函数识别易受语言偏置影响的低置信样本；对选中样本融合多文本/图像扰动分支与原始 logits 进行去偏决策；对模糊样本选择性恢复预测分布尖锐的高信息受损视图以完成证据恢复；并利用校准数据集主动识别、剪枝无效分支以降低成本。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAGIC 相较众多基线方法具有更好的有效性。

Conclusion: MAGIC 在兼顾去偏效果与推理效率的前提下，为 LVLMs 测试时鲁棒性缩放提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of test-time robustness scaling for large vision-language models (LVLMs), which aims to mitigate language bias during inference. Existing approaches typically adopt test-time scaling techniques such as contrastive decoding to improve inference performance. Despite the progress, the performance of these approaches is far from satisfactory due to intentional evidence removal during perturbations and redundant inference cost. Towards this end, we propose a novel approach named Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration (MAGIC). The core of our MAGIC is to evaluate the decision margins of LVLMs to selectively activate textual and visual perturbation branches for efficient debiased inference. In particular, our MAGIC first measures the prediction margins of crucial tokens, and applies a gating function to identify low-confidence responses that are susceptible to language biases. For selected samples, we integrate the token logits from multiple textual and image perturbation branches with the original ones for debiased decision. More importantly, we selectively restore the most informative corrupted views with sharp prediction distributions for ambiguous samples after debiasing, enabling evidence recovery for discriminative decision-making. We further utilize a calibration dataset to actively identify and prune ineffective branches during contrastive debiasing for cost reduction. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed MAGIC in comparison with extensive baselines.

</details>


### [101] [STROKE: Simple Sparse Structural Occlusions for Black-Box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32932)
*Ankit Gupta,Akshat Pandya,Paul Lin,Christoph Adami,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STROKE：基于简单定向矩形遮挡、以贪心增补-精修流程优化的查询高效黑盒对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于patch的黑盒对抗攻击查询成本与计算开销较高，需要寻找更简单、更易搜索的扰动空间。

Method: 以定向矩形遮挡作为扰动原语，用轻量级贪心"添加-精修"（add-and-refine）过程进行优化，仅在约1–2%面积预算下构建扰动。

Result: 在多种图像分类器上以较低查询数取得高攻击成功率，多个配置下优于现有patch类攻击；匹配比较显示实际攻击耗时大幅降低；消融实验验证关键设计选择及不同结构原语的有效性。

Conclusion: 简单结构遮挡构成有效且计算高效的黑盒对抗攻击搜索空间。

Abstract: We introduce STROKE, a query-efficient score-based black-box adversarial attack that constructs perturbations from simple oriented rectangular occlusions. STROKE optimizes these perturbations through a lightweight greedy add-and-refine procedure. Across diverse image classifiers, STROKE achieves high attack success with low query counts at nominal 1--2\% area settings, showing favorable performance relative to existing patch-based attacks in several configurations. Matched comparisons further demonstrate substantial reductions in wall-clock attack time, while ablation studies examine the key design choices and effectiveness across structural primitives. These findings suggest that simple structural occlusions offer an effective and computationally efficient search space for black-box adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [102] [MiD-VLM: Detecting and Grounding Global Semantic Misinformation in Image-Caption Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32967)
*Tarunya Prasad,Bharatesh Chakravarthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SigLIP与DINOv2特征融合、以LoRA适配Qwen2-VL-7B的多模态模型，在DGM4+上实现图文语义失配检测，二分类F1达0.9679。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像配以篡改叙事的字幕会产生语义误导，而图像本身无像素级篡改痕迹，现有基于像素伪影的检测方法难以识别此类图文失配对。

Method: 以DGM4+数据集为评测基准，用投影MLP将SigLIP的图文表示与DINOv2的视觉场景特征融合，并通过LoRA对Qwen2-VL-7B进行参数高效微调；模型联合完成图文对真实性判别、操纵类型识别与误导性字幕token定位三项任务。

Result: 在DGM4+验证集上，二分类真实性判别的F1为0.9679。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的参数高效适配在检测语义不一致方面具有潜力，但token级精确定位仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Semantic misinformation can arise when an authentic image is paired with a caption that changes or distorts its narrative. Because the image itself may contain no visible signs of manipulation, methods designed to detect pixel-level artifacts can miss these misleading image-caption pairs. We study this problem using DGM4+, a dataset designed to evaluate the detection of global scene and semantic inconsistencies. We propose a multimodal model that combines image-text representations from SigLIP with visual scene features from DINOv2 through a projection MLP, and adapts Qwen2-VL-7B using LoRA. The model jointly predicts whether an image-caption pair is authentic, identifies the manipulation type, and grounds the misleading caption tokens. On the DGM4+ validation split, the model achieves an F1 score of $0.9679$ for binary authenticity classification. These results suggest that parameter-efficient adaptation of vision-language models is promising for detecting semantic inconsistencies, while also motivating further work on precise token-level grounding.

</details>


### [103] [SceneBloom: Agentic Indoor Environment Generation with Realistic and Diverse Layouts for Embodied Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32978)
*Yuhan Liu,Lianhui Qin,Shenji Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBloom是一个代理式框架，通过"视觉布局采样+契约引导的3D编译"生成真实、多样且可仿真的室内场景，用于训练可泛化的具身智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练可泛化的具身智能体需要真实、多样且可直接仿真的室内环境。语言引导方法擅长语义规划与物体级推理，但精确空间落地困难；视觉生成模型虽具备合理布局先验，但其输出需几何推理才能成为物理有效的3D场景。因此需要弥合视觉先验与物理有效3D场景之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出SceneBloom：(1) 分层采样器利用扩散先验在功能区域内生成家具布局；(2) 编译器从每个布局提案中抽取空间关系，以契约(contract)形式指导几何拟合与局部位姿修正，从而将2D视觉提案编译为物理合法的3D场景。

Result: 在五类房间上，SceneBloom的视觉质量与物理稳定性均优于对比方法，且是唯一未检测到网格碰撞与越界物体的方法；其布局分布比所有基线更接近真实房间。在这些场景中训练的物体导航策略，在留出的真实扫描上的成功率高于用等量真实训练扫描训练的策略。

Conclusion: 将扩散式视觉布局采样与关系驱动的几何编译相结合，可生成兼具视觉真实性与物理有效性的仿真就绪场景，并能有效替代/补充真实数据用于具身导航策略训练。

Abstract: Training generalizable embodied agents requires realistic, varied, and simulation-ready indoor environments. Language-guided methods support semantic planning and object-level reasoning, but precise spatial grounding remains difficult. Visual generative models offer rich priors over plausible arrangements, yet their outputs require geometric reasoning to become physically valid 3D scenes. We introduce SceneBloom, an agentic framework that combines visual layout sampling with contract-guided 3D compilation. A hierarchical sampler uses diffusion priors to generate furniture arrangements within functional zones. A compiler then uses relations extracted from each proposal to guide geometric fitting and local pose correction. Across five room types, SceneBloom attains the highest visual quality and physical stability among the compared methods and is the only method with no detected mesh collisions and out-of-bounds objects. Its layouts are also closer to the distribution of real rooms than those of all compared baselines. Object navigation policies trained in these realistic scenes achieve a higher success rate in held-out real scans than those trained on an equal number of real training scans. Code is available at https://github.com/Tinaliu0123/scenebloom.

</details>


### [104] [FlexGlue: Sparse-to-Dense Feature Matching and Visual Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32976)
*Philipp Lindenberger,Paul-Edouard Sarlin,Ondrej Miksik,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FlexGlue，一个用于非对称稀疏到稠密匹配与消歧的神经网络，在给定第一幅图像中若干点坐标的条件下估计其在第二幅图像中的对应坐标，同时判定该点对是否匹配，兼顾稀疏匹配的速度与稠密匹配的亚像素精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨图像对应估计方法存在两类局限：稀疏兴趣点匹配高效但受重复性限制；稠密像素网格匹配鲁棒但计算昂贵。此外，这些方法仅用匹配点对训练，难以拒绝具有视觉对称性但实际无关的点对，而该能力在 SfM 等应用中同样关键。

Method: 构建名为 FlexGlue 的神经网络，实现非对称的稀疏到稠密匹配：输入为两幅图像以及第一幅图像中的点坐标，在稠密像素中仅搜索有限数量的候选对应关系，从而同时完成匹配与是否匹配的判别（消歧）。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，FlexGlue 在相对位姿估计任务上推进了帕累托前沿，并在 SfM 与视觉定位任务上显著优于现有方法；同时以高精度拒绝错误点对。

Conclusion: FlexGlue 将稠密匹配的亚像素精度与稀疏匹配的速度相结合，可在保持高效的同时高精度地拒绝错误匹配，适用于 SfM 与视觉定位等场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: To estimate correspondences across images, existing approaches match either sparse interest points, which is highly efficient but limited by repeatability, or dense pixel grids, which is robust but expensive. Furthermore, these approaches are trained with only matching pairs and thus often fail to reject unrelated pairs that exhibit visual symmetries, which is equally important in applications like Structure-from-Motion (SfM). We bridge these gaps by introducing FlexGlue, a neural network for asymmetric sparse-to-dense matching and disambiguation. Given two images and coordinates of points in the first image, FlexGlue estimates the coordinates of the corresponding points in the second image while simultaneously rejecting the pair if non-matching. By searching for a limited set of correspondences among dense pixels, our approach is both fast and effective at both matching and disambiguation: it combines the sub-pixel accuracy of dense matching with the speed of sparse matching, while rejecting incorrect pairs with high precision. Experiments on multiple datasets show that FlexGlue pushes the Pareto frontier in relative pose estimation and significantly outperforms existing approaches for SfM and visual localization. FlexGlue is publicly available at https://github.com/cvg/flexglue .

</details>


### [105] [Flownomaly: Single-Step Conditional-Mean Recovery for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32964)
*Bernardo Perrone Ribeiro,Erkut Akdag,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flownomaly将少样本工业异常检测重构为冻结DINOv2特征空间中的"正常对应物恢复"问题：用条件流匹配学习速度场，以单步欧拉恢复近似后验均值，并用仅依赖正常样本的Jacobian增益-噪声准则选择恢复时间，从而在MVTec-AD、VisA、Real-IAD上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本工业异常检测方法存在三类局限：推理依赖存储的示例（exemplar-based）、强加刚性线性结构、或存在复制异常内容的风险；且缺少异常验证样本用于超参数调优。

Method: 在冻结DINOv2 patch token空间上，以正常支持集为条件，通过条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）学习速度场，将高斯噪声传输至正常特征分布。沿近似直线路径，仅用最优场的一次评估执行单步恢复，直接得到干净正常token的后验均值，无需轨迹积分。恢复时间控制恢复对查询token的修改强度；在无异常验证样本条件下，提出仅用正常样本的Jacobian增益-噪声准则从支持集自动选择该时间。查询token与其恢复对应物之间的平方残差同时用于密集定位与图像级检测。

Result: 在MVTec-AD与VisA的1/2/4-shot基准及Real-IAD的sample-shot评测上，对近期类别专用方法取得SOTA。1-shot下MVTec-AD图像/像素AUROC为97.8%/98.0%，VisA为95.3%/98.7%，VisA图像级AUROC较最强基线提升2.1个百分点。
