Conclusion: 以流匹配实现单步正常性恢复，是一种无需存储示例、无异常样本调参、非刚性线性假设的少样本异常检测范式，兼具精度与推理简洁性。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection requires modeling category-specific normality from only a handful of defect-free images. Common approaches tie inference to stored exemplars, impose rigid linear structure, or risk reproducing anomalous content. Flownomaly instead casts the problem as normal-counterpart recovery in frozen DINOv2 feature space. Given normal supports, a velocity field is learned through conditional flow matching over frozen patch tokens to transport Gaussian noise toward normal features. Along straight paths, a single recovery step using one evaluation of the optimal field yields the posterior mean of the clean normal token without trajectory integration. The operating time governs how strongly recovery modifies a query token. With no anomalous validation samples available for tuning, a normal-only Jacobian gain-to-noise criterion selects this time from the supports alone. Squared residuals between query tokens and their recovered counterparts yield anomaly signals for both dense localization and image-level detection. Across 1-, 2-, and 4-shot benchmarks on MVTec-AD and VisA, together with sample-shot evaluations on Real-IAD, Flownomaly achieves state-of-the-art performance against recent category-specific methods. In the 1-shot setting, image- and pixel-level AUROC reach 97.8% and 98.0% on MVTec-AD and 95.3% and 98.7% on VisA, with a 2.1-point image-level AUROC improvement over the strongest baseline on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/b-rbmp/Flownomaly.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Discrete Tokens: Preserving Numerical Continuity in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32948)
*Dianye Huang,Jiamei Guo,Yue Zhou,Mingde Li,Yi Xiong,Wei Wang,Nassir Navab,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HAD-VLM混合自回归-扩散视觉语言模型，实现提示条件下的定量超声测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续测量目标与离散token数值预测之间存在失配

Method: 共享骨干中结合自回归文本生成与扩散式连续测量预测；视觉提示微调适配超声图像；扩散过程预测测量点坐标与长度的五分量表示；后处理结合坐标-长度一致性机制与集成预测

Result: IVSd/LVIDd/LVIDs/LVPWd的MAE为1.36/2.77/3.05/1.16 mm，较Vicuna自回归基线降低21.4–37.6%；优于微调Qwen3-VL与MLP回归；医学问答性能保持；X射线椎体测量MAE由11.4降至6.7像素

Conclusion: 自回归语言生成与扩散连续预测相结合可提升定量医学图像测量精度并保持提示驱动的任务灵活性

Abstract: Objective: To develop a unified vision-language model for prompt-conditioned quantitative ultrasound measurement that addresses the mismatch between continuous measurement targets and discrete token-based numerical prediction. Methods: This article presents HAD-VLM, a hybrid autoregressive-diffusion framework that combines autoregressive text generation with continuous measurement prediction within a shared backbone. Visual prompt tuning adapts the vision encoder to ultrasound images. A diffusion process predicts a five-component representation comprising caliper endpoint coordinates and measurement length. A post-processing strategy combining the proposed coordinate-length consistency mechanism with ensemble prediction is introduced to refine measurement estimates. We evaluate four echocardiographic measurement tasks on EchoNet-LVH, with additional assessments of medical question answering and X-ray vertebral measurement. Results: HAD-VLM achieves mean absolute errors of 1.36, 2.77, 3.05, and 1.16 mm for IVSd, LVIDd, LVIDs, and LVPWd, respectively, reducing errors by 21.4-37.6\% relative to the Vicuna-based autoregressive baseline. It also outperforms fine-tuned Qwen3-VL and direct MLP regression across all four tasks. Medical question answering performance is preserved on the evaluated dataset. For X-ray vertebral measurement, HAD-VLM reduces mean absolute error from 11.4 to 6.7 pixels. Conclusion: Integrating autoregressive language generation with diffusion-based continuous prediction improves quantitative medical image measurement while retaining prompt-conditioned task flexibility. Significance: HAD-VLM provides a unified approach to combining language interaction with anatomically grounded numerical prediction for quantitative medical image analysis.

</details>


### [107] [UniAction: Unified Action Conditioning for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32977)
*Bowen Xue,Zizhang Li,Hong-Xing Yu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAction 是一个统一动作条件框架，将任意动作表示为带离散语义标识与连续时空定位坐标的稀疏动作元素集合，把坐标映射到预训练视频模型的原生位置空间，使异质动作能在同一时空结构下条件共享世界模型，并提出基数校准注意力以缓解表示粒度差异带来的偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型的动作类型高度异质，现有方法通常针对单一动作类型分别建模，导致本就有限的动作标注数据被割裂到多个独立模型中，无法共享。

Method: 将任意动作分解为稀疏动作元素集合，每个元素包含离散语义身份与连续时空定位坐标；把这些坐标直接映射进预训练视频模型的原生位置空间，实现异质动作在统一时空结构下条件共享世界模型；并引入基数校准注意力（cardinality-calibrated attention）处理不同动作表示粒度造成的偏置。

Result: 在力、相机控制、手部姿态、机器人姿态四种代表性动作形式上评估，UniAction 在动作跟随性上优于其他条件化方案；单一联合训练模型超过各动作专用专家模型。

Conclusion: 异质动作数据可从统一模型与联合训练中获益，统一动作条件化是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: Actions that condition world models are highly heterogeneous, and thus existing action-conditioned world models are typically built for individual action types, fragmenting already limited action-annotated data across separate models. We introduce UniAction, a unified action-conditioning framework that represents any action as a sparse set of action elements, each with a discrete semantic identity and a continuous spatiotemporal grounding coordinate. By mapping these coordinates directly into the pretrained video model's native positional space, UniAction allows heterogeneous actions to condition a shared world model within the same spatiotemporal structure. We further introduce cardinality-calibrated attention to address the bias from differing action representation granularities. We evaluate UniAction on four representative action forms: force, camera control, hand pose, and robot pose. UniAction outperforms conditioning alternatives in action following, and a single jointly trained model surpasses the action-specific experts, showing that heterogeneous action data benefit from unified model and joint training.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [X-Dubbing: Speech-to-Speech Translation under Temporal and Visual Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32042)
*Jeongsoo Choi,Ji-Hoon Kim,Junseok Ahn,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出X-Dubbing，将跨语言配音建模为时间感知语音翻译与视频感知语音合成的联合问题，共享语言无关的时间预算，并用合成语音的配音反馈优化译稿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言配音需在源视频时长内产出忠实且自然可说的译文，但各语言在时长、节奏与发音上差异显著，使同步困难；同时缺乏成对配音数据。

Method: 框架含两部分：Dubbing Translator生成具时间结构的脚本，Voice Actor按源视频同步渲染，二者以语言无关的时间预算（timing budget）为共同约束；并用合成语音的配音感知反馈微调翻译器；两模块分别用语音-文本数据与单语视听数据训练，无需成对配音数据。

Result: 大量实验表明X-Dubbing在等时性（isochrony）与音视频对应性等多类指标上优于近期基线。

Conclusion: 以联合建模+时间预算+配音反馈的方式，可在无配对配音数据条件下实现高质量跨语言视频配音。

Abstract: Cross-lingual dubbing aims to convert source speech into target-language speech that remains synchronized with the original video. The key to this task is to produce a faithful translation that is naturally speakable within the source timing, which is non-trivial because languages differ in duration, rhythm, and articulation. In this paper, we propose X-Dubbing, which formulates the task as a joint problem of time-aware speech-to-text translation and video-aware text-to-speech. Specifically, our method consists of a Dubbing Translator that generates temporally structured scripts and a Voice Actor that renders them in synchrony with the source video, both grounded in a language-agnostic timing budget. We further refine the translator with dubbing-aware feedback from the synthesized speech, encouraging scripts that yield well-synchronized dubbing. This design requires no paired dubbing data, as the two components are trained on speech-text and monolingual audio-visual data respectively. Extensive experiments show that X-Dubbing outperforms recent baselines across diverse metrics, including isochrony and audio-visual correspondence.

</details>


### [109] [SAGE: Sense-Aligned Multimodal Grounding Evaluation with Gloss-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31838)
*Sunjae Kwon,Suhaas Kiran DG,Zonghai Yao,Talha Chafekar,Deepali Singh,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出统一基准SAGE（10候选图像选择，涵盖名词/动词/实体指代的对齐评估）与免训练框架GAR（定义接地+约束引导语义验证），实验表明强VWSD性能不能均匀迁移，二者结合使Hits@1从65.90提升至71.22。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉词义消歧（VWSD）等评测各自为政，缺乏跨名词、动词、实体指代的统一"以词为中心"的接地评测协议，难以判断模型性能是否可迁移，也难以细粒度诊断目标词接地能力。

Method: 构建SAGE基准：将VWSD、VerSe、WikiDiverse重构成共享的10候选图像选择协议，引入易混淆难负例，并设计上下文置换的VerSe诊断探针以检验目标词接地而非整体图-文匹配；提出免训练GAR框架，融合定义接地（definition grounding）与约束引导的语义验证（constraint-guided semantic verification）。

Result: 在Gemma3-12B上，定义增强对三个子集均有提升；约束引导推理提升VWSD并在VerSe上增益尤为显著，但在WikiDiverse上效果较弱；两种信号组合取得最佳宏平均，Hits@1由65.90升至71.22。

Conclusion: SAGE既支持统一评测，又支持跨接地设定的显式语义推理细粒度分析；不同接地任务间性能迁移有限，需针对性设计推理信号。

Abstract: This paper introduces Sense-Aligned Grounding Evaluation (SAGE), a unified 10-candidate image-selection benchmark that reformulates VWSD, VerSe, and WikiDiverse into a shared term-centric protocol covering noun-, verb-, and entity-centered grounding. SAGE includes confusable hard negatives and a context-swapped VerSe diagnostic to probe target-term grounding beyond direct caption--image compatibility. We further propose Gloss-Augmented Reasoning (GAR), a training-free framework combining definition grounding with constraint-guided semantic verification. Experiments show that strong VWSD performance does not transfer uniformly across grounding settings. With Gemma3-12B, definition augmentation improves all three subsets, while constraint-guided reasoning improves VWSD and yields particularly strong gains on VerSe, but is less effective on WikiDiverse. Combining the two signals achieves the best macro-average performance, improving Hits@1 from 65.90 to 71.22. These results show that SAGE supports both unified evaluation and fine-grained analysis of explicit semantic reasoning across grounding settings.

</details>


### [110] [Why Full-Duplex Speech Models Collapse in Long Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32349)
*Yeonju Kim,Bella Godiva,Se Jin Park,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出全双工长时程基准 FD-LHB，揭示现有全双工语音模型在数十分钟对话中必然出现退化或上下文耗尽，并诊断出初始化锚点驱逐与自我重复两个机制，提出免训练的 IAP 与 DRP 两项修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型逐帧生成 token，即使处于聆听状态也在消耗上下文，因而在数分钟内即耗尽上下文窗口；但现有评测仅覆盖单轮或少数几轮对话，无法暴露这一长时程问题，缺少专门的长对话基准与失效机理分析。

Method: 构建 FD-LHB 基准，在长时回放对话与同 LLM 伙伴的实时对话两种设定下评测五个近期全双工模型；对滑动窗口模型进行失效机理诊断，识别出 Initialization-Anchor Eviction（窗口丢弃首个 KV 条目导致乱语与静默）和自我输出强化导致的重复；据此提出两项免训练干预：Initialization-Anchor Pinning（保留首个 KV 条目）与 Duplex Repetition Penalty（抑制回声并防止长时间静默）。

Result: 所有被测模型最终要么性能退化，要么上下文耗尽：保留完整历史的模型常在上下文用尽前即退化，滑动窗口模型则陷入重复、静默与乱语。两项修复验证了上述诊断，并在不重训练的情况下，使最长一小时的回放对话中对话崩溃显著减少，同时保持实时运行。

Conclusion: 长时程对话能力是全双工语音模型被忽视的关键短板，其失效可归因于窗口驱逐与自我重复；简单的推理期干预即可在实时条件下显著缓解崩溃，为长时程全双工对话的评测与改进提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models listen and speak at the same time, which makes them promising for natural, open-ended conversation. But can they sustain a coherent conversation for tens of minutes? Because they generate a token every frame, even while listening, they exhaust their context windows within minutes. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate only single turns or a few conversational rounds, too short to expose the long-horizon problem. We introduce the Full-Duplex Long-Horizon Benchmark (FD-LHB), which tests five recent full-duplex models on long replayed conversations and in live conversation with an LLM partner. Every evaluated model eventually either degrades or exhausts its context. Models that keep the full history often degrade before their context runs out, while sliding-window models still fall into repetition, silence, and gibberish. For sliding-window models, we identify two contributing mechanisms. Gibberish and silence are linked to Initialization-Anchor Eviction, where the sliding window discards the first KV entry. Repetition is also reinforced by the model's own recent outputs. Guided by this diagnosis, we apply two simple fixes: Initialization-Anchor Pinning (IAP) keeps that first entry, and Duplex Repetition Penalty (DRP) discourages echoing while guarding against prolonged silence. Together, they support the diagnosis and, without retraining, substantially reduce conversational collapse in replays of up to an hour while running in real time.

</details>


### [111] [The LLENS Platform for On-Premises Large Language Model Agents in a Japanese University Hospital: Development and Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32151)
*Ken Enda,Jien Saito,Masami Mukai,Feng Han,Emir Turkes,Tatsumi Kusakabe,Reona Umeki,Kazuhiro Sumiyoshi,Mitsuya Numata,Takumi Tanikawa,Akira Endoh,Kenji Hirata,Atsushi Yoshida,Kohsuke Kudo,Shinya Tanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文设计并在封闭院内网络中部署了临床支持AI智能体平台LLENS（8×NVIDIA H200、约1万亿参数开源模型Kimi K2.6、基于Open WebUI重构的Web应用），其10组件工具框架使模型能通过文档工具与沙箱交互读取院内文档，在180题测试中准确率达97.2%，显著优于无文档（10.7%）和固定检索（81.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生大量时间耗费在电子病历与文书工作上，工具调用型LLM智能体有望分担这部分工作；但许多医院的信息系统运行于封闭网络，模型与智能体框架必须同样部署在院内，因此需要一个可落地于断网环境的临床支持智能体平台。

Method: 在北海道大学医院封闭网络中，以单台搭载8块NVIDIA H200的主机部署开源权重模型Kimi K2.6（约1万亿参数）与基于Open WebUI重构的Web应用；智能体框架含10个组件：页面图像读取、全文附件输入、无向量索引的路径寻址文档树、网络隔离沙箱、产物回传、输出自检、子智能体、可保存流程等。模型从3个开源候选者中选出，并设计180道院内文档问题（每种3个随机种子）比较无文档、固定检索（单次词法检索top-10片段）与交互式访问三种条件的准确率。

Result: Kimi K2.6在2026年日本医师资格考试中得分480/500；单用户生成速度119 tokens/s，16并发请求合计908 tokens/s。文档问答平均准确率：无文档10.7%、固定检索81.3%、交互式访问97.2%（较固定检索高15.9个百分点，95% CI 10.9–21.3）；差异主要来自过滤/计数类问题（16.7% vs 87.8%）。另以合成数据跑通5个场景：针刺伤应对、批量不良事件分级、扫描转诊信转录、跌倒数据汇总与绘图、子智能体调研3个主题。

Conclusion: 在完全封闭的院内网络上部署工具调用型临床支持智能体是可行且有效的，交互式文档工具访问显著优于一次性检索式RAG，尤其体现在需要过滤与计数的复杂问题上；平台自2026年9月14日起已在医院网络持续运行。

Abstract: Large language model agents that use tools could take on part of the electronic health record and desk work that occupies physicians' time, but in many hospitals the medical information systems run inside closed networks, so the model and the agent harness must also sit inside. We designed, implemented, and deployed LLENS, a clinical support AI agent platform that runs inside the closed network of Hokkaido University Hospital on one host with 8 NVIDIA H200 GPUs, the open-weight model Kimi K2.6 (~1 trillion parameters), and a web application rebuilt from Open WebUI. The harness has 10 components, including page image reading, full-text attachment input, a path-addressed document tree without a vector index, a network-isolated sandbox, artifact return, self-checking of generated output, subagents, and saved procedures. Kimi K2.6 was chosen from 3 open-weight candidates and scored 480/500 on the 2026 Japanese Medical Licensing Examination; it generated 119 tokens/s for one user and served 16 concurrent requests at 908 tokens/s in total. On 180 questions over hospital documents (3 seeds each), mean accuracy was 10.7% with no documents, 81.3% with fixed retrieval (top 10 chunks of one lexical search), and 97.2% with interactive access, in which the model reads with document tools and the sandbox (15.9 percentage points above fixed retrieval, 95% CI 10.9-21.3); the difference lay in filtering/counting questions (16.7% vs 87.8%). Five scenarios with synthetic data ran on combinations of the components: needlestick injury response, batch adverse event grading, scanned referral letter transcription, falls data aggregation and plotting, and investigation of 3 topics by subagents. The platform has run on the hospital network since September 14, 2026.

</details>


### [112] [Tampered ML: Evaluating LLM Judgment of Machine Learning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32114)
*Soroush Ebadian,Parand A. Alamdari,Joe Benton,Hailey Joren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Tampered ML基准（1,858个条目对），用于评估模型识别研究方法学缺陷的能力；前沿模型准确率高于随机但远未可靠（Opus 5仅77.9%），且受呈现顺序影响，偏好看似更具体、透明或常规的有缺陷设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速AI对齐研究的一条路径是自动化部分对齐研究工作，而这要求系统能在难以监督的研究任务上可靠表现，例如识别研究提案中的缺陷、判断实验能否支持其预期主张。现有缺乏对这类能力的评测手段。

Method: 构建Tampered ML基准：1,858个条目对，每个条目对包含一个从已发表机器学习论文重建的实验提案，以及一个引入针对性方法学缺陷的最小编辑反事实版本。模型需在两种呈现顺序下均指出方法学更严谨的提案。另对分层样本开展人工验证研究。

Result: 人工验证估计86%的条目有效（95% CI: 81-91%）。前沿模型表现高于随机水平但远未可靠，Opus 5达到77.9%准确率。模型对呈现顺序高度敏感，并倾向于偏好外观更具体、更透明或更常规的有缺陷设计。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型尚不具备可靠的研究方法学评判能力，顺序敏感性与对表面特征的偏好揭示了系统性偏差，自动化对齐研究仍需进一步提升模型在难监督任务上的可靠性。

Abstract: One approach to accelerating progress on AI alignment is to automate parts of alignment research itself. Doing so requires systems that can perform reliably on hard-to-supervise research tasks, including identifying flaws in research proposals and evaluating whether a proposed experiment supports its intended claim. To evaluate this capability, we introduce Tampered ML, a benchmark of 1,858 item-pairs, each consisting of an experiment proposal reconstructed from a published machine learning paper and a minimally edited counterfactual that introduces a targeted methodological flaw. Models must identify the more methodologically sound proposal in both presentation orders. A human validation study on a stratified sample estimates that 86% of items are valid (95% CI: 81-91%). Frontier models perform above chance but remain far from reliable (with Opus 5 achieving 77.9% accuracy). They are highly sensitive to the order of presentation and favor flawed designs that appear more specific, transparent, or conventional.

</details>


### [113] [Kolibri: A Sovereign European Model on the Pareto Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31945)
*Aleph Alpha,:,Moritz Althaus,Artur Baranowski,Michael Barlow,Fabien C. Y. Benureau,Felix Berkenkamp,Amrita Bhattacherjee,Lukas Blübaum,Duncan Blythe,Augustijn de Boer,Bastian Boll,Thomas F. Burns,Björn Deiseroth,Niko Dürr,Béni Balázs Egressy,Niklas Finken,Niklas Freymuth,Fabian Gebhart,Max Hammer,Bastian Harren,Andreas Hartel,Johann Higl,Steffen Hirschmann,Pablo Iyu Guerrero,Yasser Jadidi,Andreas Jagusch,Mitja Kleider,Markus Klein,Jonas Knupp,Murali Kondragunta,Garwin Lechner,Dominik Lê-Kellner,Jurek Leonhardt,Jiaze Li,Sohir Maskey,Omar Mehio,Pit Neitemeier,Letitia Parcalabescu,Alejandro Petry Pacheco,Pranav Ragupathy,Martin Reinhardt,Peter Rodenkirch Nfissi,Dylan Rodriquez,Leonard Salewski,Raziye Sari,Jordan Sassoon,Ivo Schaper,Ilhan Scheer,Frank Schneider,Philipp Scholl,Lukas Schott,Eric Schreiber,Pablo Schumacher,Alessio Serra,Philipp Siedler,Prabhu Teja Sivaprasad,Mohamed Soliman,Till Speicher,Volker Stampa,Simon Thel,Serge Thilges,Irina Vidal Migallón,Denis Waßmann,Samuel Weinbach,Sascha Wirges,Michael Wüthrich,Alexei Zacharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Kolibri 是一个英德双语混合专家（MoE）Transformer，总参数 78.1B、每 token 激活 3.46B，以 Apache 2.0 开放权重发布，面向主权与专用部署（德国及公共管理、工业、航空航天等受监管领域），在质量-服务成本上处于开源模型帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向主权与专用化部署需求，尤其是德语场景与受监管领域（公共管理、工业、航空航天），需要可完全自主控制全流程的开源模型。

Method: 自建 Model Factory 打通数据、实验、训练、评估与部署的版本化流程；架构为 MoE Transformer；训练共 24T token，覆盖预训练、中期训练与长上下文扩展，德语按字节占比超 20%（自建网页清洗管线加合成生成补充超 2T 德语 token）；后训练为德语/英语精选数据监督微调 + 在 120 万余条内部任务上的强化学习（推理、工具调用、指令遵循、代码、检索）。

Result: 总参数 78.1B、每 token 激活 3.46B（激活率 4.4%）；在本报告评估的开源模型中，英德双语的质量-服务成本权衡位于帕累托前沿；Kolibri Origin 作为前代迭代已验证全流程可规模化。

Conclusion: Kolibri 通过全流程自主可控的模型工厂与德语数据强化，实现了低成本服务下的双语高性能开源 MoE 模型，可作为主权与受监管场景的部署基础。

Abstract: This report introduces Kolibri, an English-German mixture-of-experts transformer with 78.1B total parameters and 3.46B active parameters per token, released as open weights under the Apache 2.0 licence. We built Kolibri for sovereign and specialised deployment, with a particular focus on German and on regulated domains such as public administration, industry, and aerospace. To support these settings, we control the full model-development process, including data, architecture, training infrastructure, post-training, and evaluation.
  We train Kolibri on 24T tokens across pre-training, mid-training, and long-context extension, with German accounting for more than 20% of the data mix by bytes. To reach this share, we complement German datasets with more than 2T German tokens that we curate from the web with our own pipeline and generate synthetically. Post-training combines supervised fine-tuning on curated German and English data with reinforcement learning on more than 1.2M internally curated tasks, which cover reasoning, tool use, instruction following, code, and retrieval. The model activates 4.4% of its parameters per token, and among the open models evaluated in this report, it lies on the Pareto frontier of quality and serving cost in English and German.
  Kolibri builds on Kolibri Origin, an earlier iteration that validated our full pipeline at scale. Both models come from our Model Factory, which connects data, experimentation, training, evaluation, and deployment through version-controlled workflows.

</details>


### [114] [Yalda-Embedding: Open Development of a Competitive Persian Retrieval Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32933)
*Yalda Taheri,Mohammad Hassan Heydari,Armon Rasooli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Yalda Embedding，一个 595.8M 参数的波斯语稠密检索模型，由 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 经百万级查询-文档对微调与知识蒸馏得到，在 FaMTEB 八项波斯语检索任务上取得 0.6B 模型中最优宏平均 0.5488，并开源权重、数据与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语稠密检索受限于语言特定训练资源稀缺与紧凑嵌入模型不足，多语言文本表示的整体进展未能有效转化为波斯语检索性能。

Method: 以 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 为基座，使用超过一百万查询-文档对进行单轮（one-epoch）训练；开展增量式消融，依次考察对比学习批大小、优化稳定性策略，以及从 F2LLM-v2-8B 进行的关系型知识蒸馏；训练中未显式挖掘或提供难负样本。

Result: 在 FaMTEB 八项波斯语检索任务上宏平均 0.5488，为所评估 0.6B 模型最高，逐任务均优于 Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B 与 F2LLM-v2-0.6B；接近 Qwen3-Embedding-4B（4 胜 1 平，宏平均落后 0.0075）。仅对比微调相比初始化提升有限且在两项任务上退化；最终蒸馏配置在全部八项任务上提升，均值由 0.4813 升至 0.5488，且不增加推理时模型规模，增益亦覆盖难负样本评测任务。

Conclusion: 关系型知识蒸馏是紧凑波斯语检索模型性能提升的关键环节，可在不增大推理模型规模的前提下全面改善检索表现；作者开源模型权重、训练与评测代码、成对数据集及其教师嵌入版本，便于数据构成与训练流程审查、独立评测及免重复教师推理的复用。

Abstract: Despite advances in multilingual text representations, effective dense retrieval for Persian remains constrained by the limited availability of language-specific training resources and compact embedding models. We present Yalda Embedding, a 595.8-million-parameter Persian retrieval model adapted from Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B using more than one million query-document pairs. On eight Persian retrieval tasks from FaMTEB, the model achieves the highest macro-average among the evaluated 0.6B models, scoring 0.5488 and outperforming Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and F2LLM-v2-0.6B on every task. It also approaches Qwen3-Embedding-4B, winning on four tasks, tying on one, and trailing by 0.0075 in macro-average. An incremental ablation study examines contrastive batch size, optimization stabilization, and relational knowledge distillation from F2LLM-v2-8B. Under the one-epoch training setup, contrastive fine-tuning alone yields a modest mean improvement over initialization and regressions on two tasks. The final distilled configuration improves all eight tasks over the non-distilled model, raising the mean from 0.4813 to 0.5488 without increasing inference-time model size. These gains include hard-negative evaluation tasks despite training without explicitly mined or supplied hard negatives. We release model weights, training and evaluation code, the pairwise dataset, and its teacher-embedded counterpart, enabling inspection of data composition and training procedures, independent evaluation, and reuse without repeating expensive teacher inference.

</details>


### [115] [What the Query Remembers: Query Abstraction Governs Lexical and Dense Retrieval of Classical Arabic Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31821)
*Omar Farouk Zouak,Selma Mani,Larbi Said Chikh,Raid Abderrezak Ouahioune*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 诗歌档案检索性能由"查询中保留多少原文措辞"决定：仅凭记忆片段的查询BM25优于所有稠密编码器（含8B模型），抽象主题查询则稠密编码器大幅领先；两种评测协议在同一系统上结论不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索效果不仅取决于模型理解能力，还取决于用户记得多少原文。作者希望检验检索器优劣是否随查询中残留的诗句措辞量而系统性地翻转，并揭示单一评测协议或单一查询语域可能给出错误结论。

Method: 以古典阿拉伯诗歌为对象，构建两个语料库，对比BM25与九个稠密编码器（含8B先进模型），采用三种索引模式和两种评测协议；通过改写同一批查询的措辞（增加一个记住的半句、手工改写引用词）进行控制实验；用词汇包含度与嵌入邻近度分别解释两类系统的行为机制；并由两名盲评人类标注员与两名开源权重评判模型（其一使用不含字面匹配标准的评分细则）复现协议差异。

Result: 舌尖现象式（只记得片段）查询：BM25在Recall@5上以0.746超过所有稠密编码器（最佳稠密为0.717）；抽象查询：BM25降至十中之第八（0.392 vs. 0.748）。加入一个记住的半句使BM25提升38点；人工改写引用词后BM25与8B模型持平；BM25对记忆措辞的敏感度显著高于最强稠密编码器。同一轴线决定解释增强的效果，其影响从+37点摆动到-39点。机制上，词汇包含度预测BM25行为，嵌入邻近度预测稠密编码器行为。已知条目检索与主题相关性两种标准协议在同一系统上出现分离，该结果被两名盲评人类标注员与两名开源评判模型复现。

Conclusion: 检索器的排名由查询记忆的粒度与语域决定，而非单一"模型能力"排序；只报告一种评测协议或一种查询语域的基准可能给出实质性错误的图景。评测设计需同时覆盖查询措辞丰富度与多协议，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: How well a poetry archive can be searched depends on what the user still remembers, not only on what the model understands. We show this for Classical Arabic poetry, benchmarking nine dense encoders and BM25 over two corpora, three indexing modes and two evaluation protocols. Which retriever wins is governed by how much of a poem's wording the query still carries. On tip-of-the-tongue queries BM25 leads every dense encoder, including an 8B state-of-the-art model (0.746 vs. 0.717 Recall@5); on abstract queries it falls to eighth out of ten (0.392 vs. 0.748). Editing the wording of the same queries shows that this ranking largely reflects query composition: adding one remembered half-verse raises BM25 by 38 points, rewriting the quoted words by hand leaves BM25 level with the 8B encoder, and BM25's sensitivity to remembered wording is significantly steeper than that of the strongest dense encoder. The same axis controls explanation augmentation, whose effect swings from +37 to -39 points. The mechanism splits by system type: lexical containment predicts BM25's behaviour, while embedding proximity predicts dense encoders. Finally, the field's two standard protocols, known-item retrieval and thematic relevance, dissociate on the same systems, a result reproduced by two blind human annotators and by two open-weights judges, one of them under a rubric with no literal-match criterion. A benchmark that reports one protocol, or one query register, can give a materially wrong picture. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ellzo/what-the-query-remembers.

</details>


### [116] [An Agentic Approach with Evidence-Grounded Verification for Multimodal Emotion Reasoning in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31685)
*Elyas Rashno,Farhana Zulkernine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出可追踪的智能体式多模态情感推理框架，通过证据核验定位错误组件并做提示空间自适应，在MER2025上使四种MLLM均获提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多模态情感推理在标准基准上表现良好，但指令微调成本高；使用冻结模型构建的智能体流水线虽避免训练成本，却带来新难题：预测出错时难以定位是哪个智能体或阶段导致了错误。

Method: 模态专属感知智能体分别处理视觉、声学、词汇输入并在共享板上发布引用证据的观察；整合智能体结合检索到的多模态样例生成情感描述、标签集与引用，MLLM批评者可要求一次修订。对错误预测，独立的证据接地验证阶段用确定性检查核验引用与参考文献有效性，并用蕴含检查判断最终断言是否被证据支持；将检测到的失败按责任组件与失败类型分组，转化为定向提示补丁，仅在留出集验证通过后采纳，从而在不更新权重的情况下适配智能体提示。

Result: 在MER2025数据集上，定向提示自适应在2B至38B的四个MLLM上均优于冻结静态流水线，并达到或超过无差别反思基线：VideoLLaMA3-2B、Phi-3.5-vision-instruct、Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct、InternVL2.5-38B；F-score提升0.6–2.2分，Qwen2.5-VL-7B由52.3提升至54.5。

Conclusion: 基于失败归因的组件级提示补丁可在冻结权重条件下有效提升多模态情感推理性能，且相比无差别反思更具针对性与可追踪性。

Abstract: LLM-based multimodal emotion reasoning systems perform well on standard benchmarks, but improving them through instruction tuning can be computationally expensive. Agentic pipelines built from frozen models avoid this cost, but introduce a different challenge: when a prediction is wrong, the system must identify which agent or stage caused the error. We address this problem with an agentic framework for traceable multimodal emotion reasoning and prompt-space adaptation. Modality-specific perception agents analyze visual, acoustic, and lexical inputs and post evidence-cited observations to a shared board. An integration agent combines these observations with retrieved multimodal examples to produce an emotion description, label set, and supporting citations, while an MLLM critic can request one revision before the output is finalized. For incorrect predictions, a separate evidence-grounded verification stage applies deterministic checks to verify that cited evidence and references are valid, and an entailment check to determine whether the final claims are supported by that evidence. Detected failures are then grouped by the responsible component and failure type, converted into targeted prompt patches, and accepted only after validation on a held-out set. This adapts the agent prompts without updating model weights. On the MER2025 dataset, targeted prompt adaptation outperforms the frozen static pipeline on all four MLLMs, ranging from 2B to 38B parameters, and matches or outperforms undifferentiated reflection baselines on each of them: VideoLLaMA3-2B, Phi-3.5-vision-instruct, Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, and InternVL2.5-38B. It improves F-score by 0.6--2.2 points depending on the backbone, including an increase from 52.3 to 54.5 on Qwen2.5-VL-7B.

</details>


### [117] ["Not" is Not Lost in Translation: A Causal Analysis of Negation Representations in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32281)
*Giang Son Nguyen,Wray Buntine,Dung D. Le,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究多语言大语言模型是否在不同语言间共享否定的底层表示。作者在六个开源模型上，覆盖英、法、越、中四种语言，通过因果干预（对比激活方向与稀疏自编码器特征）证明否定表示可被因果控制且跨语言基本共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定存在于所有语言中，但其表层实现差异巨大。然而多语言大语言模型却能稳健地翻译否定句，这一现象引出一个核心问题：模型是否在跨语言间共享否定的底层表示？作者希望系统性地探究并回答该问题。

Method: 作者选取六个开源多语言模型，覆盖英语、法语、越南语、中文。构建两类数据：合成对比数据集，以及基于FLORES-200衍生的否定翻译数据集。采用因果干预方法：(1) 提取对比激活方向，检验其能否从一种语言迁移到其他语言以诱导或移除否定，并测量不同语言方向之间的对齐程度；(2) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE），检验单一稀疏特征是否足以跨语言控制极性且对否定输入具有选择性；(3) 在四种额外语言及FLORES衍生句子上测试稠密与稀疏干预的泛化性。

Result: 发现否定表示具有强因果可控性，且跨语言基本共享：从某一语言提取的对比激活方向可在其他语言中诱导或移除否定，不同语言提取的方向高度对齐。否定以稀疏特征形式表示，每个模型仅需一个SAE特征即可跨语言控制极性，同时对否定输入有选择性响应。稠密与稀疏干预均能泛化到四种额外语言及FLORES衍生句子。

Conclusion: 多语言大语言模型在跨语言间共享一个可因果控制的否定底层表示，且该表示在稠密（激活方向）与稀疏（SAE特征）两个层面均可被定位与操控。作者公开数据与代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Negation occurs in every language, yet its surface realization differs drastically. Despite this, multilingual large language models (LLMs) translate negation robustly, raising the question of whether they share an underlying representation of negation across languages. We study this question in six open models across English, French, Vietnamese, and Chinese, using causal interventions on a synthetic contrastive dataset and a negation translation dataset derived from FLORES-200. Across models, we find strong evidence that negation representations are causally controllable and largely shared across languages. In particular, we show that a contrastive activation direction extracted from one language can induce or remove negation in the others, and directions extracted from different languages are substantially aligned. We further find that negation is represented in sparse features: a single sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature per model suffices to control polarity across languages, while responding selectively to negated inputs. Finally, both dense and sparse interventions generalize to four additional languages and FLORES-derived sentences. We release data and code to support future work: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/negation-translation/.

</details>


### [118] [Analysing Austrian Parliamentary Speeches by Predicting Party Affiliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32515)
*Sarah Sulollari,Vanja Karan,Benjamin Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用微调BERT模型为奥地利国民议会每次演讲分配政党概率分布，并将其聚合到议员与政党层面，借助"预测错分"而非单纯纠错来分析政党话语特征与政治演变，并公开1996–2018年政党归属预测数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有议会话语研究缺乏基于细粒度、可量化的政党话语相似度度量；作者希望建立一种以议员（CM）为单位的语音/文本特征刻画方法，并说明模型误判本身也能提供关于议员与政治动态的信息，而非仅是性能缺陷。

Method: 构建基于微调BERT的分类模型，对每篇演讲输出奥地利各政党的概率分布，再在演讲者层面与政党层面聚合；在开展分析前进行广泛评估以保证分布质量；并将预测的"错分"作为信息源加以利用。

Result: 联合执政的政党在话语特征上趋于相似，而在野党与执政党的话语距离进一步拉大；更换党派的议员通常仅在转党发生之后才调整其话语特征；作者还公开发布了1996–2018年奥地利国民议会演讲的政党归属预测数据集。

Conclusion: 将BERT概率分布聚合用于议会话语分析可揭示政党结盟/对立与议员转党的话语适应规律；误分类可作为分析信号；公开数据集支持后续可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper examines speech characteristics of the Austrian National Council members (CMs). We introduce analysis methodology based on a fine-tuned BERT-Model which assigns to every speech a probability distribution over Austrian parties. These are then aggregated at the speaker and party level. We perform an extensive evaluation to ensure the quality of the generated distributions prior to further analysis. Instead of considering mis-assignments of predicted parties as a performance issue, we leverage them to derive insights about the CMs and political developments. Our findings show that parties that govern together become more similar in their speech characteristics, while, analogously, parties that are in opposition move further away from governing parties. Moreover, CMs who switch party usually adapt their speech characteristics only after the switch occurs. Finally, we publicly release a data set containing party affiliation predictions for the speeches of the Austrian National Council from 1996 to 2018.

</details>


### [119] [Ontology-Guided Knowledge Graph Construction from Structured Metadata and Climate Science Publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32725)
*Aayush Acharya,Qi Zhang,Eduard Dragut,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种智能体驱动、本体引导的知识图谱构建框架，用于从科学文献中抽取可溯源的结构化知识，并在气候科学领域构建了大规模本体对齐知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献快速增长远超人工整理速度，从科学文本中抽取结构化知识成为瓶颈，亟需自动化且语义一致、可验证的知识图谱构建方法。

Method: 采用多个专用智能体分别负责文档理解、信息抽取、本体一致性与实体链接；LLM先抽取带显式文本证据的三元组，再经多阶段验证使其与本体对齐，通过模式约束与证据片段要求保证语义一致性与可追溯性。

Result: 在1000+三元组的一致性研究中，Gemini-2.5-flash对三位人工标注者的Cohen's κ为0.640–0.700，与人工间一致性0.616–0.689相当；应用于2000篇论文构建气候科学知识图谱，含133,990个节点、755,308条关系，验证后23,327条三元组中98.96%被判定正确。

Conclusion: 本体引导的智能体框架可实现接近人工水平的高质量知识抽取，并支撑大规模、可溯源领域知识图谱的自动化构建。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of scientific literature far outpaces the speed of manual curation, creating a fundamental bottleneck in extracting structured knowledge from scientific texts. To address this, we introduce an agentic, ontology-guided framework for knowledge graph (KG) construction. Our framework utilizes specialized agents for document understanding, information extraction, ontology conformance, and entity linking. First, an LLM extracts factual knowledge in the form of triples with explicit textual evidence from research papers. Then the triplets undergo a multistage verification process to ground the triplets in the ontology. By enforcing schema constraints and requiring evidence in the form of text fragments, we ensure the data is both semantically consistent and traceable. In an agreement study over 1,000 extracted triplets, Gemini-2.5-flash achieves Cohen's $κ$ scores of 0.640--0.700 against three human annotators, matching the human--human agreement range of 0.616--0.689. We applied this framework to 2,000 scientific publications and construct a novel, ontology-grounded KG for climate science with 133,990 nodes and 755,308 relations; of the 23,327 triplets that the publications contribute after validation, 98.96\% are judged correct. The pipeline code and KG is publicly available https://github.com/aayushacharya/climatekg.

</details>


### [120] [Machine-Readable Operator Manuals over the ISO 11783 Network for Offline, Multilingual LLM Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31863)
*Timo Oksanen,Julius Gun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文分析在ISO 11783 CAN总线上同步操作手册（语义同步）的可行性：实测完整手册在物理总线上传输耗时31–89秒，与操作员已习惯的机具首次挂接对象池上传相当；Markdown文件最小，三种格式问答性能相近；本地运行LLM配合语义检索可跨语言作答，仅需新增一个参数组，具备标准化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农机智能离线助手需要动态访问技术文档，因此需让拖拉机端助手的本地知识库与所连接机具的操作手册保持一致，即实现基于ISO 11783总线的语义同步，并评估其传输性能、文档格式与跨语言问答能力。

Method: 在物理ISO 11783网络上传输完整手册，对比Markdown、JSON、XML三种格式及不同传输设置；使用多个开放权重LLM在跨语言查询任务上评估，采用检索增强生成（RAG），并对自动评估结果与四名人工评分者进行对照验证。

Result: 完整手册传输耗时31–89秒（随格式与传输设置变化），与机具首次挂接时的对象池上传等待时间相当；Markdown文件最小，多数模型下三种格式问答性能相近；RAG下本地模型对英文手册大部分问题答对，最大模型相对7B/8B模型无优势，但在其他五种语言版本上表现较差；关键词检索跨语言失败，语义检索提供了跨语言能力。

Conclusion: 该手册交换仅需新增一个参数组、复用ISO 11783现有请求与传输机制，因此具备标准化候选资格；本研究为概念验证（PC级硬件推理），嵌入式农机硬件上的部署为未来工作。

Abstract: If agricultural machinery is to support intelligent, offline assistance, then the system must access technical documentation dynamically. In this paper, the Semantic Synchronization of operator manuals over ISO 11783-based CAN buses is analyzed, defined as the process that brings the local knowledge base of a tractor-side assistant into agreement with the operator manual of the connected implement. Data transfer performance was benchmarked using Markdown, JSON, and XML; various open-weight Large Language Models (LLMs) were also evaluated in cross-lingual query tasks. The automatic evaluation of the answers was validated against four human raters. A complete manual was transferred over a physical ISO 11783 network in 31 to 89 s, depending on format and transport settings, which is comparable to the object pool upload that operators already wait for at the first coupling of an implement. Markdown produced the smallest files, while the question-answering performance was comparable across the three formats for most models. With retrieval-augmented generation, locally run models answered most questions on the English manual correctly, with no advantage of the largest model over 7B and 8B models, but performed worse on the five other language editions. Keyword retrieval failed across languages, whereas semantic retrieval provided the cross-lingual capability. The exchange requires only a new parameter group that uses the existing request and transport mechanisms of ISO 11783, which makes it a candidate for standardization. The study is a proof of concept with inference on PC-grade hardware; deployment on embedded agricultural hardware remains future work.

</details>


### [121] [Validating the Comparison, Not Just the Scorer: Why Overall Accuracy Can Mislead in Interactive LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31678)
*Ancil Crayton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 高总体F1（.916）掩盖了提示词特异性评分误差，导致所报告的提示词效应可能被夸大、缩小或方向反转；作者建议验证"计划报告的差值"而非评分器整体性能，并在匹配案例上对两种提示词同时审计、纳入评分者与审计不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM研究常用不同后续提示词（中立追问 vs. 坚持虚构实体存在）对比模型响应，以测量"对话压力"效应，并用自动评分器标注响应是否含无依据的事实主张。现有做法仅用单一总体准确率或F1验证评分器，但高总体性能可能掩盖提示词特异性错误：若评分器在某一提示词下错误更多，测得的差值会偏大、偏小甚至方向错误；审计样本若以困难响应为主，也会误代表全研究性能。

Method: 推导评分误差如何改变提示词比较的偏差机制；实证使用6个模型、180个虚构实体问题、6种提示词条件。审计一：288条平衡响应，检查各提示词下自动评分器与主LLM评分者不一致率及其不确定性。审计二：评分器与两名独立LLM评分者在每种提示词下评估同一批300个模型-问题案例，比较提示词效应估计。

Result: 审计一：总体F1=.916，但按提示词不同，评分器将2.6%–33.3%被主评分者判为"无无依据主张"的响应误标为有问题，且这些提示词特异性估计不确定性较大。审计二：从"中立请求重新考虑"到"断言确定性"的表面9.3个百分点提升，在两名评分者下均降至2.3个百分点；主评分者区间含零，故未确立提升。另两个提示词比较在两名评分者下方向保持一致；尝试的提示词特异性校正仍不确定性过高，无法得出确定结论。

Conclusion: 研究者应验证其计划报告的"具体差值"而非评分器整体指标：在两个提示词的匹配案例上进行审计，并在最终结果中同时纳入评分器与评分者（含审计抽样）的不确定性；否则提示词效应的推断可能失真。

Abstract: Many LLM studies compare how models respond to different follow-up prompts. For example, one prompt may neutrally ask a model to reconsider, while another insists that an invented film really exists. The difference is used to measure the effect of conversational pressure. An automatic scorer then labels whether each response makes an unsupported factual claim. Researchers commonly validate the scorer with one overall accuracy or $F_1$ score. However, high overall performance can hide prompt-specific mistakes: if the scorer makes more errors after one prompt, the measured difference can be too large, too small, or point in the wrong direction. An audit containing mostly difficult responses can also misrepresent performance in the full study. We derive how such errors alter a prompt comparison and examine the problem using six models, 180 invented-entity questions, and six prompt conditions. In a balanced audit of 288 responses, overall $F_1$ was $.916$. Yet, depending on the prompt, the scorer flagged 2.6%-33.3% of responses that the primary LLM rater judged not to contain an unsupported claim; these prompt-specific estimates were uncertain. In a second audit, the scorer and two separate LLM raters evaluated the same 300 model-question cases under each prompt. An apparent 9.3-point increase from asserting certainty rather than neutrally asking the model to reconsider fell to 2.3 points under both raters. The primary-rater interval included zero, so it did not establish an increase. Two other prompt comparisons kept their direction under both raters, while an attempted prompt-specific correction remained too uncertain for a firm conclusion. Researchers should therefore validate the exact difference they plan to report, not just the scorer as a whole: audit both prompts on matching cases and include scorer and rater uncertainty in the final result.

</details>


### [122] [Do Halting Heads Really Save Compute in Looped Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32246)
*Tsung-Han Wu,Minsuk Heo,Kenny Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究指出循环语言模型的halting head存在训练-推理失配问题，平均迭代数指标会高估节省，建议按实际运行迭代数报告并发批大小与阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型对每个token多次经过同一组层以节省算力，常加入halting head在每次迭代后预测停止概率，使简单token提前退出。但现有训练方式与评估指标存在缺陷，需澄清并给出改进建议。

Method: 从零预训练最多1.3B参数的模型，分析halting head在混合所有迭代上训练与逐迭代打分训练的差异；同时考察批生成时序列以最慢token为终止条件带来的实际成本，并设计一个在训练head之前运行的测试来界定其收益上界。

Result: 混合迭代训练下，head预测token平均只需不到一半迭代，但标准停止规则下几乎每个token都跑满全部迭代，因为训练从未对token实际停止的那一次迭代打分；逐迭代打分可修复该问题，但仅在有强烈偏向早停的惩罚时有效。此外，平均每token迭代数在批生成（以最慢token为准）情形下会高估节省，无head模型在该成本下几乎同样好。

Conclusion: 建议报告token实际运行的迭代数，并同时给出停止阈值与批大小，且在训练head前先运行一个界定其收益上限的测试。

Abstract: Looped language models run each token through the same layers several times, and each run is one iteration. To save compute, an extra layer, the halting head, gives after each iteration the probability that the token stops there, so that easy tokens can stop early. Pretraining models from scratch, up to 1.3B parameters, we find two problems. First, heads trained on a mix of all iterations predict that tokens need under half the iterations, yet under the standard stopping rule tokens run almost every iteration, because this training never scores the single iteration where a token stops. Scoring every iteration instead fixes this, but in our runs it works only with a strong penalty favoring early stops. Second, savings are commonly reported as an average number of iterations per token, but when sequences are generated in batches that run until their slowest token stops, a model without a head is almost as good at that cost. The reported average can therefore overstate the saving. We recommend reporting the iterations tokens actually run, with the stopping threshold and batch size, and give a test, run before training a head, that bounds its gain.

</details>


### [123] [Context or Knowledge: How LLMs Attribute Their Own Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32109)
*Kirill Afendulev,Alexey Dontsov,Alla Chepurova,Elena Tutubalina,Anton Korznikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过干预答完题后、被问"答案来自何处"之前的KV缓存，发现源报告取决于答题阶段产生的Q+A缓存状态，而非文档缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型既能依据给定文档作答，也能依据参数知识作答；当随后被问及答案来源时，究竟是什么决定了模型的"来源报告"尚不明确。作者希望从因果层面定位该报告的来源机制。

Method: 在受控事实性任务上，对四个模型（7B–32B）在"答案已生成、来源问题未提出"的时点干预KV缓存：分别替换/移除文档缓存、在问题与答案token完全相同的两次运行间移植Q+A缓存；并拟合Q+A值的"方向"进行引导（steering），与范数匹配的随机方向作对照；进一步拆解key与value沿拟合方向的分量，并在答题阶段施加同样的值方向。

Result: 改换或删除文档缓存从不改变报告；移植Q+A缓存则可完整转移报告（所有符合条件的报告随供体变化，而受体文档不变）。在两个较小模型上，沿预先拟合方向引导Q+A值可在固定强度下逆转98.9–100%的报告（四种文档条件），随机方向至多翻转1.1%。复制键值沿拟合方向的分量几乎可复现报告变化，固定这些分量则几乎阻止该变化。在答题阶段施加同样的值方向也能改变答案由哪个来源提供，但效应较弱且依赖提示词。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，源报告因果性地依赖于答题过程中产生的缓存状态；但作者明确指出未确立普遍的来源报告准确性，也未给出该状态的完整解释。

Abstract: Language models can answer a question from a supplied document or from their parametric knowledge. When later asked where the answer came from, what determines their source report? We study this by intervening on the model's key-value (KV) cache after the answer is generated and before the source question is asked. On a controlled factual task with four models (7B-32B), changing or removing the document's cache never changes the report. In contrast, transplanting the question-and-answer (Q+A) cache between runs with identical question and answer tokens transfers it: every eligible report follows the donor, while the recipient's document is unchanged. On the two smaller models, steering Q+A values along directions fitted before any source question reverses 98.9-100% of reports across four document conditions at a fixed strength, while norm-matched random directions flip at most 1.1%. The components of keys and values along fitted directions carry the transplant effect: copying them alone nearly reproduces the report change; holding them fixed nearly prevents it. Applied during answer generation, the same value directions also change which source supplies the answer, though more weakly and depending on the prompt. Source reporting in this setting thus depends causally on cached state produced while answering. We do not establish general reporting accuracy or a complete interpretation of this state.

</details>


### [124] [LazyFlash: Forward-Dense, Backward-Sparse Attention for Accelerated Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31911)
*Keqi Deng,Ruchao Fan,Jinyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LazyFlash提出"前向稠密、反向稀疏"的注意力训练方法：前向保持完全稠密以保证模型为标准稠密模型可直接部署，反向基于注意力输出梯度范数做梯度感知的块稀疏化并跳过贡献可忽略的查询块，实现最高5.7倍注意力反向加速与1.7倍端到端训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列下注意力成为LLM计算瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力多面向推理，许多方法仍需稠密训练或无法支持稀疏训练，且额外复杂度难以转化为实际墙钟加速，导致实践中稠密注意力仍被广泛使用。作者观察到注意力训练开销主要由反向传播主导，因此稀疏化应聚焦于反向。

Method: 1) 前向保持精确稠密，使训练得到的仍是标准稠密模型，可被vLLM等现有系统零改动服务；2) 反向采用梯度感知块稀疏化：按注意力输出梯度范数对查询块排序，跳过对参数更新贡献可忽略的块，将稠密反向转为块稀疏反向；3) 引入梯度质量预算，按每个头和每个训练步自适应调整跳过比例；4) 采用重要性采样，使梯度在期望意义上保持稠密等价；5) 在分块FlashAttention反向核内实现，被跳过的块不加载、不计算，从而将梯度稀疏转化为真实墙钟加速。

Result: 在长上下文文本与语音LLM实验中，LazyFlash在保持下游性能的同时，相对FlashAttention取得最高5.7倍注意力反向加速，端到端训练加速最高1.7倍。

Conclusion: 将稀疏性定位于训练中占主导的反向传播，并保持前向稠密，可在不改变模型结构与部署路径的前提下获得显著训练加速；梯度感知块稀疏化、自适应质量预算与重要性采样的组合使稀疏真正落地为系统级墙钟收益。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) process increasingly long sequences, attention becomes a major computational bottleneck. Sparse attention mostly targets inference, yet many methods still train densely or cannot support sparse training, and their added complexity makes sparsity hard to turn into wall-clock speedups, leaving dense attention widely used in practice. This paper introduces LazyFlash, a forward-dense, backward-sparse attention method built on the observation that attention training is dominated by the backward pass. LazyFlash keeps the forward pass exactly dense, so the trained model remains a standard dense model that existing systems such as vLLM can serve without any change. For the backward pass, LazyFlash proposes a gradient-aware block sparsification design that ranks query blocks by their attention-output gradient norms and skips those contributing negligibly to parameter updates, turning the dense backward into a block-sparse one. A gradient-mass budget adapts the amount of skipped computation to each head and training step, while importance sampling preserves the dense gradient in expectation. These designs are implemented inside the tiled FlashAttention backward kernel, where skipped blocks are never loaded or computed, translating gradient sparsity into real wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on long-context text and speech LLMs show that LazyFlash maintains downstream performance while achieving up to 5.7x attention-backward speedup over FlashAttention and 1.7x end-to-end training speedup.

</details>


### [125] [Reading Between the Layers: Cross-Modal Alignment and Representational Hierarchy in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32463)
*Sugam Karki,Raúl Vázquez,Yova Kementchedjhieva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了基于Transformer的视觉编码器如何处理文本及其语义，发现跨模态对齐仅在最深层的视觉层中涌现，并伴随从表层到语义表征的层级演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型在OCR任务上表现优异，但其底层视觉编码器如何表征文本及其含义、是否具备类似阅读理解的能力仍不清楚。作者希望以受控方式揭示这些更广泛的能力。

Method: 利用渲染文本与视觉概念之间的跨模态对齐，结合探针（probing）与检索（retrieval）分析，对Transformer视觉编码器进行逐层分析，考察其在正字法、语法和语义层面的组织方式。

Result: 强大的跨模态映射仅在最深层的视觉层中出现，且发生在单模态物体与文本识别能力形成之后，同时伴随从表层表征到语义表征的转变；该层级结构在对比训练模型中最为显著，而更大规模的自监督模型与文本丰富的训练数据在无显式语言监督的情况下也能部分支持类似行为。

Conclusion: 模型规模、训练数据规模以及文本丰富的训练数据，可以在一定程度上弥补语言监督的缺失；视觉编码器具备在深层涌现的、接近语义层面的文本理解能力。

Abstract: While large vision-language models excel at optical character recognition, it remains unclear how their underlying vision encoders process text and its meaning, and whether they support anything resembling reading comprehension. We systematically study transformer-based vision encoders, using cross-modal alignment between rendered text and visual concepts together with probing and retrieval-based analyses as a controlled window into these broader capabilities. Our layer-wise analyses span orthographic, grammatical, and semantic organization, and reveal that strong cross-modal mapping emerges only in the deepest layers, after unimodal object and text recognition have already developed, coinciding with a progression from surface-level to semantic representations. Although this hierarchy is strongest in contrastively trained models, larger self-supervised models and text-rich training data partially support similar behaviors without explicit language supervision. Together, our results suggest that increasing model and training-data scale, as well as text-rich training data, can partially compensate for the absence of language supervision.

</details>


### [126] [Acoustic and Lexical Signals for Native Language Identification of Language Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32382)
*Ahmet Yavuz Uluslu,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill,Mark Gales*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了基于WavLM的音频母语识别分类器与基于转录文本的零样本GPT-4o分类器，在CEFR均衡的英语学习者语料上分析二者在熟练度与输入长度变化下的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 母语（L1）识别传统上依赖语音的声学特征，而对语音转录文本中L1痕迹的分析关注甚少；作者希望考察声学与文本两种表征在L1识别中的相对价值及其随学习者熟练度和可用语言材料量的变化。

Method: 使用带语音及对应转录文本的口语学习者英语语料，构建基于WavLM的音频L1分类器，并与在转录文本上运行的零样本GPT-4o分类器进行对比；在CEFR均衡的评估集上按熟练度水平分析预测结果，并考察限制转录长度的影响。

Result: 在完整转录可用时，音频分类器整体优于文本方法，但相对优势随熟练度变化：A1水平音频更优，A2至C1水平完整转录的文本更优。文本准确率总体随熟练度提升而上升，声学性能则相对稳定。限制转录长度会显著降低文本准确率，而音频对输入长度较不敏感，在有限输入下表现仍较好。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了学习者语音的声学与文本表征在L1识别中的互补性，并表明识别性能依赖于学习者熟练度与可用语言材料的多少。

Abstract: Spoken native language (L1) identification for second language learners has traditionally relied on acoustic attributes of speech. Comparatively little attention has been paid to analysing speech transcripts for L1 traces. In this paper, we present a study of L1 identification using both speech and corresponding transcripts from a spoken learner English corpus. We compare a WavLM-based audio L1 classifier with a zero-shot GPT-4o classifier operating on transcripts and analyse their predictions across proficiency levels using a CEFR-balanced evaluation set. Our experiments show that the audio classifier outperforms the transcript-based approach overall when full transcripts are available, although their relative strengths vary with learner proficiency. Audio performs better at A1, whereas text performs better from A2 to C1 with full transcripts. Transcript-based accuracy generally increases with proficiency, while acoustic performance remains comparatively stable. Restricting transcript length substantially reduces text-based accuracy, whereas audio is less sensitive to input length and performs relatively well with limited input. These findings provide insights into L1 identification from acoustic and textual representations of learner speech and how performance varies with proficiency and the amount of available language material.

</details>


### [127] [Mining Frames: Association Rule Mining as an Unsupervised Framework for News Framing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31874)
*Hager Hesham Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以关联规则挖掘（ARM）作为无监督、免标注的新闻框架检测框架：将文档/句子视为词汇项事务，把高提升度（lift）共现规则读作候选框架信号（Gamson与Modigliani的"浓缩符号"），并由记者-研究者在语境中确认。主要验证基于25,512篇英语文章，14/16家媒体出现闭合规则{israeli, strike}→{kill}，并能无事件标注地复原事件驱动的框架转向、识别标题-正文框架分歧；在1,575篇阿拉伯语文章上验证FP-Growth可实现低内存的句级分析，成果获两家媒体调查报道采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算框架分析以监督分类器为主导，依赖大规模标注语料和预设框架分类体系，因而难以适用于非英语语言、新兴事件与低资源场景。作者希望提供一种无需标注、无需预设类别的可解释框架检测路径。

Method: 以ARM为无监督框架：文档/句子作为词汇项事务，用Apriori（mlxtend）、平台加权支持度、POS过滤的n-gram与lift评分提取高提升度共现规则，作为候选"浓缩符号"由记者-研究者结合语境确认；主验证语料为16家美、英、法、德媒体的25,512篇英语文章（2023年10月—2024年10月），并进行逐月分析与标题-正文对比；在Seven Days项目（1,575篇阿拉伯语文章）上以FP-Growth进行句级方法学验证。

Result: ARM可归纳性地呈现可解释模式而无需预设类别：{israeli, strike}→{kill}在16家媒体中14家为闭合规则；逐月分析在无事件先验标注下复原事件驱动的框架转向（如2024年4月World Central Kitchen事件）；发现系统性的标题-正文分歧——标题承载集中、高lift的编辑性框架，正文再现机构化、程序性语言；FP-Growth在阿拉伯语语料上实现更低内存开销的句级分析；结论经两家媒体（Daraj Media、Saheeh Masr）的调查报道采纳而获得验证。

Conclusion: ARM可作为监督式框架分析的低成本、可解释替代方案，适用于多语言、新兴事件与低资源情境，并能提供句级粒度的框架信号。预处理代码公开，完整流程虽被记录但为保留可操作性部署而暂不公开，限制了可复现性。

Abstract: Computational framing analysis is dominated by supervised classifiers that require large annotated corpora and predefined frame taxonomies, limiting their use for non-English languages, novel events, and low-resource settings. This paper presents Association Rule Mining (ARM) as an unsupervised, annotation-free framework for news framing detection. Each document or sentence is treated as a transaction of lexical items, and high-lift co-occurrence rules are read as candidate framing signals (Gamson and Modigliani's condensing symbols), confirmed in context by journalist-researchers. The primary validation is Framing Gaza: 25,512 English-language articles from 16 US, UK, French, and German outlets covering the first year of the Gaza war (October 2023 to October 2024), analyzed with Apriori (mlxtend), platform-weighted support, POS-filtered n-grams, and lift-based scoring. ARM inductively surfaces interpretable patterns without predefined categories: a cross-platform military frame, {israeli, strike} to {kill}, appears as a closed rule in 14 of 16 outlets, and a month-by-month analysis recovers event-driven framing pivots (e.g., the April 2024 World Central Kitchen strike) with no prior event labeling. A systematic headline-body divergence is identified, with headlines carrying concentrated, high-lift editorial framing and body text reproducing institutional, procedural language. A methodological validation on the Seven Days project (1,575 Arabic articles on the 2026 Iran war) shows that FP-Growth enables sentence-level analysis with far lower memory overhead. Findings were validated through editorial uptake: two published investigative stories (Daraj Media; Saheeh Masr). Preprocessing code is released openly; the full pipeline is documented but withheld to preserve operational deployability.

</details>


### [128] [SPECMEM: Accelerating Agentic Tool Calling via Live Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31840)
*Zihe Song,Wenting Zhao,Ye Liu,Yuanjie Zhu,Ke Xu,Shanghao Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecMem 提出面向工具调用智能体的在线、按用户、可更新的推测解码草稿存储器，验证通过的调用实时回写并做 LRU 淘汰，配合副作用门控，使多会话流下的接受长度显著优于冻结存储，最高 1.74x 墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用智能体通常只优化准确率，但部署时每次工具调用都需自回归解码，延迟成本持续上升。推测解码可无损加速，且用户行为具有重复性，使历史调用成为优质草稿来源；然而现有系统要么没有跨会话记忆，要么一次性构建草稿库后不再更新，导致草稿与现实使用分布脱节、接受率衰减。

Method: 提出 SpecMem：部署期持续维护的工具调用记忆。1）将验证通过的调用在线回写至有界、按用户划分的存储，采用 LRU 淘汰；2）设置副作用门控，对不可逆调用延迟执行直至验证确认；3）按用户本地作用域隔离存储以阻断跨用户泄漏。

Result: 在三个开源权重模型处理的多会话、多用户查询流上，冻结存储在平均接受 token 数上持续下降，而 SpecMem 保持稳定，两者存在 27% 差距（各基准 +11% 至 +34%，与存储构建后新增工作量的比例相关），墙钟加速最高 1.74x。消融显示收益几乎全部来自保持存储在线更新；按用户作用域不提升接受率，但可关闭实测的跨用户泄漏通道；基于声明副作用而非检索置信度的门控是消除不可逆错误的决定性因素。

Conclusion: 推测解码草稿存储的价值关键在于部署期的持续维护而非一次性构建；按用户作用域与基于副作用声明的门控分别承担隐私隔离与安全保证的职责，二者不可由检索置信度替代。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents are mostly optimized for accuracy; in deployment, however, their growing cost is latency: every tool call is decoded autoregressively. Speculative decoding accelerates this without changing the output, and retrieved past calls make strong drafts under real use, where each user's behavior tends to repeat. Yet existing systems keep no cross-session memory, or build the draft store once and never update it. We propose SpecMem, a tool-call memory kept alive and managed throughout deployment: verified calls are written back online into bounded per-user stores with LRU eviction, and a side-effect gate defers irreversible calls until verification confirms. On multi-session, multi-user query streams served by three open-weight models, a frozen store steadily loses acceptance while SpecMem holds it: a 27% gap in mean accepted tokens (+11% to +34% across benchmarks, tracking how much novel work arrives after the store is built) and up to 1.74x wall-clock speedup. Ablations attribute the gain almost entirely to keeping the store live; local per-user scoping adds no acceptance but closes a measured cross-user leakage channel, and gating on declared side effects, rather than on retrieval confidence, is what eliminates irreversible mistakes.

</details>


### [129] [Multi modal perceived conversation success prediction for spontaneous autistic and non-autistic dyadic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31682)
*Thanushi Withanage,Desi Jones,Noah Sasson,Elizabeth Redcay,Carol Espy-Wilson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究跨三个含自闭症与非自闭症个体的自发二元对话数据集，用发音、面部、声学韵律与话轮转换四类互补特征预测参与者主观感知的对话成功度，表明对话成功是多维构念、需多模态框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话成功对沟通、社会联结与幸福感至关重要，而自闭症—神经典型者对话中常被削弱。既有研究或用声学/词汇特征（儿童富集样本）、或用非语言行为（非自闭症群体），或聚焦韵律、面部、动作与话轮同步但仅用于诊断分类与组间差异，缺乏对主观感知对话成功的多模态预测。

Method: 以三个自发二元互动数据集（含自闭症与非自闭症个体）为对象，提取发音、面部、声学韵律与话轮转换四类互补特征，构建多模态预测模型，预测参与者自我报告的感知对话成功度。

Result: 多模态特征组合可提升对感知对话成功的预测，捕获任何单一模态无法提供的信息；结果支持对话成功本质上是多维的。

Conclusion: 感知对话成功需多层面分析框架，融合互补模态是有效路径；为自闭症沟通研究从诊断分类转向互动质量预测提供新方向。

Abstract: Conversation success is important for communication, social bonds, and well-being and is often reduced in autistic-neurotypical conversations. Previous studies have predicted perceived conversation success using acoustic and lexical features in pediatric autism-enriched samples, or using nonverbal behavior in non-autistic group interactions. Other work has characterized prosodic, facial, motor, and turn-taking synchrony in autistic interactions, but has primarily targeted diagnostic classification or group differences. The present study instead predicts participant perceived conversation success from complementary articulatory, facial, acoustic-prosodic, and turn-taking measures across three spontaneous dyadic-interaction datasets including autistic and non autistic individuals. By leveraging multimodal features, we demonstrate that conversational success is inherently multidimensional and requires a multi-faceted analytical framework. Combining these complementary modalities offers the potential to improve prediction of perceived conversation success by capturing information unavailable from any single modality.

</details>


### [130] [KrishokTech: A Bengali Agricultural Advisory System with Explicit Authorization Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32817)
*Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sanjana Aktar Maria,Shakil Ahmed,Faiyad Hossain,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KRISHOKTECH 是一个孟加拉语农业咨询系统，在生成前后设置显式授权边界，实现 unsafe 请求控制、跨作物检索约束与剂量核验，并支持离线运行与免重训更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业咨询不仅需要生成答案，还需控制不安全请求、适应有限网络连接，并支持指导内容的动态更新。现有系统缺乏从请求到交付建议全流程的显式管控机制。

Method: 在生成前进行归一化与规则准入检查，将政策覆盖的高风险请求转介（REFER），对治疗意图不明确时追问作物（ASK）；检索绑定单一作物，文本与图像作物不一致时要求确认（CONFIRM）；生成后用窄域验证器剔除无 BARI/BRRI 证据支撑的剂量声明（DROP）。指导内容存于外部证据索引，可免重训更新；浏览器端视觉与本地检索降低连通性要求。

Result: 在 200 条农户查询与 400 条压力查询上，控制机制拦截了欠明确请求；在测试别名过滤下跨作物检索违规由 36.5% 降至 0%；文本-图像冲突检测捕获 453/454；剂量错误验证器捕获 114/118。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了如何以显式授权边界与可解释的 ASK/CONFIRM/REFER/DROP 决策，管控从请求到交付建议的完整路径，兼顾安全性、可更新性与低连通性场景。

Abstract: For agricultural advisory, generating an answer is only part of the problem: the system must also control unsafe requests, work with limited connectivity, and accommodate changing guidance. KRISHOKTECH is a Bengali agricultural advisory system built around an explicit authorization boundary around generation. Before retrieval, normalization and rule-based admission checks refer policy-covered high-risk requests and ask for a crop when treatment intent is underspecified. The system then binds retrieval to a single crop and asks for confirmation when the text and image indicate different crops. After generation, a narrow verifier removes dosage claims not supported by retrieved BARI/BRRI evidence. Agricultural guidance is kept in an external evidence index that can be updated without retraining the generator, while browser-side vision and local retrieval reduce connectivity requirements. The interface makes these decisions visible as ASK, CONFIRM, REFER, and DROP, together with their evidence. On 200 farmer queries and 400 stress queries, the controls halted underspecified requests and reduced cross-crop retrieval violations from 36.5% to 0% under the tested alias filter. Mismatch detection caught 453 of 454 text-image conflicts, while the verifier caught 114 of 118 inserted dosage errors. The demonstration shows how these controls govern the path from request to delivered advice.

</details>


### [131] [ParaTaxe: a Suite of Tools for Processing and Publishing Multi-parallel Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32796)
*Olivier Kraif,Cyrille François,Elnaz Jalilian,Natacha Rimasson-Fertin,Pascale Roux*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套开源工具（AIlign对齐器、Python处理管线、ParaTaxe网页界面及基于TEI-Publisher的语料探索界面），用于构建、管理与长期维护平行语料库，以应对学术平行语料易失访的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十五年数字人文领域文本并行化与对齐工具发展显著，已有多个项目与软件用于比较不同译本或文本版本；但学术场景产出的平行语料常因缺乏维护与资金而在网上迅速不可访问，形成资源浪费与可持续性缺口。

Method: 在ACR Grimm Tradalign与ParaTaxe项目框架下开发生成一套开源工具：基于多语种嵌入的AIlign对齐器、用于语料转换与对齐的Python处理管线、名为ParaTaxe的平行语料集合管理网页界面，以及基于TEI-Publisher构建的对齐语料探索网页界面。

Result: 构建出一套完整的开源工具链，覆盖语料转换、对齐、集合管理与可视化探索等环节，可用于学术平行语料的持续托管与再利用。

Conclusion: 开源、可维护的工具套件是解决学术平行语料可访问性与可持续性问题的可行路径，该套件为数字人文中的多译本比较研究提供了集成化基础设施。

Abstract: Over the past fifteen years, digital tools for parallelizing and aligning textual corpora have developed significantly within the field of digital humanities. Several projects and software tools have been created to compare different translations or versions of texts. Despite these technical advances, many parallel corpora produced in academic contexts quickly become inaccessible online due to the lack of maintenance or funding. To address this gap, we present a suite of open-source tools developed within the ACR Grimm Tradalign and ParaTaxe projects. This suite includes the AIlign aligner, based on multilingual embeddings, a Python processing pipeline for converting and aligning corpora, a web interface called ParaTaxe for managing collections of parallel corpora, and a web interface for exploring aligned corpora built with TEI-Publisher.

</details>


### [132] [When is more search useful? An Empirical Study of Search Utility in Quality-Aware Decoding for Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32679)
*Javier Garcia Gilabert,Rico Sennrich,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究质量感知解码（QAD）中候选预算按句自适应分配的问题，提出用总缩放增益、边际缩放增益与采样饱和点三个量刻画搜索效用，并分析可用的预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAD对所有源句固定使用相同候选预算N，隐含假设各句所需搜索量相同；作者指出该假设不成立，额外候选带来的质量增益在句间差异显著，因此需要按句分配搜索预算。

Method: 将搜索效用分解为三个量：总缩放增益（句子从远大于当前规模的候选池中可获得的增益）、边际缩放增益（再生成一批候选的期望增益）、采样饱和点（实现大部分总增益所需预算）；并比较两类预测信号——候选集扩展前的句子级信号，以及搜索过程中已累积候选池的统计特征。

Result: 句子级信号与总缩放增益相关，但对采样饱和点的预测信息量较弱；当前候选池的性质（如池内质量分布/一致性统计）对边际缩放增益具有较好的指示性。基于这些信号可识别出在额外搜索下获益更大的句子。

Conclusion: QAD的固定预算假设不成立；通过组合扩展前信号与候选池动态统计，可实现选择性的额外搜索分配，从而提升搜索效率与质量增益。

Abstract: Quality-Aware Decoding (QAD) improves machine translation through test-time scaling, generating multiple candidate translations and selecting the final output with a quality-aware decoder. Usually the same candidate budget N is spent on every source sentence, assuming that all inputs require the same amount of search. We show that this assumption does not hold and that the quality gain obtained from additional candidates varies across sentences. We study search utility through three quantities: total scaling gain, how much quality a sentence can gain from a much larger candidate pool; marginal scaling gain, the expected gain from generating one additional batch of candidates; and the sampling saturation point, the candidate budget required to realize most of the total scaling gain. We investigate two sources of information for predicting these quantities: signals available before candidate-set expansion, and statistics computed from the candidate pool accumulated during search. We find that sentence-level signals correlate with total scaling gain but are less informative about the sampling saturation point. In contrast, properties of the current candidate pool provide useful signals of marginal scaling gain. Finally, we show that these signals can identify sentences that obtain larger gains when additional search is selectively applied.

</details>


### [133] [Residual Expert Routing for Text-Only Domain Adaptation of Factorized Transducer ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32637)
*Shashi Kumar,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Kadri Hacioglu,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"保守残差路由"：将目标域自适应预测器作为基座预测器的残差修正，用16k参数的门控在每个前缀位置决定接纳多少，闭门即等价于基座模型。在40小时对话语料上，路由几乎保留全部纯文本自适应增益（两个留出域WER 19.89→16.74、20.91→16.21），源域WER不变（12.90→12.93），而单独使用自适应预测器会损失1.08 WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对因子化transducer的非空白预测器进行目标域文本微调，可提升目标域识别，却损害源域性能。基座与自适应预测器之间单一的插值权重无法解决该冲突：自适应预测器在某些前缀上有益、在另一些上有害，甚至同一句内亦如此。

Method: 保守残差路由：把自适应预测器改写为对基座预测器的残差修正，并引入仅16k参数的门控（gate），在每个前缀处决定残差修正的接纳程度；门控关闭时精确复现基座输出。门控基于两个预测器间的不一致程度进行调制。

Result: 40小时对话语料：路由在两个留出域上保留几乎全部纯文本增益（19.89→16.74、20.91→16.21 WER），源域WER基本不变（12.90→12.93）；单独自适应预测器则带来1.08 WER的源域损失。路由无需在目标域音频上做逐域调参。门控并非简单的域级开关，而是通过两预测器间的不一致度调节干预强度：域内抑制、域外放大。

Conclusion: 前缀级、细粒度残差门控可在不牺牲源域性能的前提下获取目标域文本自适应收益，且无需目标域音频调参；其机制为基于预测器间分歧动态缩放干预，而非域级切换。

Abstract: Fine-tuning the non-blank predictor of a factorized transducer on target-domain text improves recognition on that domain but degrades performance on the source domain. A single interpolation weight between base and adapted predictors cannot resolve this: the adapted predictor helps at some prefixes and hurts at others, often within one utterance. We propose conservative residual routing: the adapted predictor becomes a residual correction to the base, and a 16k-parameter gate decides at each prefix how much of it to admit. Closing the gate reproduces the base exactly. On a 40-hour conversational corpus, routing retains essentially all of the text-only gain on two held-out domains (19.89 to 16.74, 20.91 to 16.21 WER) while leaving source-domain WER unchanged (12.90 to 12.93); the adapted predictor alone costs 1.08 WER. Routing needs no per-domain tuning on target-domain audio. Analysis reveals the gate does not behave as a simple domain-level switch: it modulates intervention through the disagreement between the two predictors, suppressing it in-domain and amplifying it out-of-domain.

</details>


### [134] [Knowledge Graphs Quality Assessment in the Era of LLMs: A Conceptual Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31684)
*Elwin Huaman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述数据、信息、关联数据及知识图谱（KG）的质量评估文献，并结合KG与LLM交叉领域，将现有框架扩展为包含24个质量维度（QDs）与多种质量指标（QMs）的目录，并提出一个通用、可定制、实用的KG质量评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KG被LLM广泛构建与消费，各类应用依赖KG提供简洁、完整、准确、忠实、最新且可追溯的答案；但KG本身的质量如何、何时可用、能否可信地为LLM提供依据、如何评估，仍缺乏系统回答。

Method: 文献综述法：系统梳理数据质量、信息质量、关联数据质量、KG质量评估以及KG与LLM交叉领域的文献；在此基础上扩展现有最先进框架，归纳出24个质量维度及多种质量指标。

Result: 形成包含24个质量维度和相应质量指标的KG质量评估目录，并提出一个通用、可按领域或任务定制、具备实用性的KG质量评估框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为判断KG在何种场景下可用、可否信赖地为LLM提供依据，以及如何评估KG质量，提供了系统化的维度体系与可定制评估框架。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) have been popularized during the last few decades and are increasingly constructed and consumed by Large Language Models (LLMs). Various applications therefore rely on KGs to provide concise, complete, accurate, faithful, fresh, and traceable answers. However, what is the quality of those KGs? In which cases should a KG be used, and can it be trusted to ground an LLM? How might it be evaluated? We reviewed the literature on quality assessment (QA) of data, information, linked data, KGs, and the intersection of KGs and LLMs. We extended the current state-of-the-art frameworks to a catalog of 24 quality dimensions (QDs) and various quality metrics (QMs). Furthermore, we propose a general-purpose, customizable to a domain or task, and practical QA framework for assessing the quality of KGs.

</details>


### [135] [The Schema Coherence Inventory: Measuring Whether an Assigned Belief Organizes a Language Model's Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31683)
*Yuqi Wu,Deyang Li,Katharine Hibbard,Ron Oswald,David Sinclair,Adegboyega Sapara,Kalutota Kuruwe Arachchige Sajana Indula Samarasinghe,Abhinav Pillai,Reham Shalaby,Riffat Alam,Xin-Min Li,Yanbo Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Schema Coherence Inventory（SCI），一种基于长度归一化对数似然的96题迫选测量工具，用于区分语言模型是被赋予了组织行为的"信念/图式"还是仅采用了某种"情绪"，并以目标特异性作为判别标准，经六项验证研究支持，并发现现有患者模拟器均未携带其设定的核心信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人格条件化语言模型被用作模拟患者和情感代理，但缺乏有效方法判断模型中设定的信念是否真正组织其行为，还是模型仅仅采纳了一种情绪状态；现有评估无法将构念（信念）与伴随的情感区分开。

Method: 构建SCI量表：96道迫选题，覆盖Beck认知模型的八个层级，每题提供六个选项，分别对应三种负性自我图式及其正性对应项；采用长度归一化对数似然评分，无需生成或解析文本。判别标准为目标特异性：为某一信念构建的条件应比同极性其他信念条件更频繁地选择该信念。开展六项验证研究，覆盖5个主干模型（1.7B–14B参数）上的107种条件。

Result: 9名临床受训评分者对关键信念的识别率为79.5%（随机水平16.7%）；信念适配器在所有主干模型上均表现出目标特异性（+17.7个百分点，30/30信念单元为正）；条件自身的SCI剖面可识别其自由生成结果，24/30正确（随机期望5）；48题简版与另外48题相关r=0.73；应用于6个已发表患者模拟器，均未比一句话陈述更好地携带其表述的核心信念。

Conclusion: SCI提供了一条将构念与伴随情感分离、并以临床评分者校验模型评判者的验证路径，可作为语言模型情感测量的模板，并可扩展至不同任务类型、评分者、站点和语言；作者公开了题目、评分代码与简版。

Abstract: Persona-conditioned language models are used as simulated patients and affective agents, but there is no validated way to ask whether a belief assigned to such a model organizes its behaviour or whether the model has merely adopted a mood. We present the Schema Coherence Inventory (SCI): 96 forced-choice items at eight levels of Beck's cognitive model, each offering six replies keyed to three negative self-schemas and their positive counterparts, scored by length-normalized log-likelihood so that nothing is generated or parsed. Affect alone can move a model onto a default belief, so the discriminant criterion is target specificity: a condition built for one belief must select that belief more often than conditions built for the other beliefs of its pole. Six validation studies over 107 conditions on five backbones (1.7B-14B parameters) support this reading. Nine clinically trained raters recover the keyed belief at 79.5% (chance 16.7%). Belief adapters are target-specific on every backbone (+17.7 points; 30/30 belief cells positive), and a condition's SCI profile identifies its own free generations in 24/30 cases against 5 expected by chance. A 48-item short form correlates r = 0.73 with the other 48 items. Applied to six published patient simulators, none carries its formulation's core belief better than a one-sentence statement of it. The validation route, which separates a construct from the affect that accompanies it and checks model judges against clinicians, offers a template for affective measurement in language models; we outline its extension across task types, raters, sites and languages. We release the items, scoring code and short form.

</details>


### [136] [MisconceptDialogue: Evaluation Criteria Shape Measured Success in LLM Math Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31679)
*Woojin Lee,Yoonkyu Woo,Jinah Kim,Hyeok-Min Gwon,Jin-Xia Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出MISCONCEPTDIALOGUE全自动框架，用5个7B-14B开源LLM导师与LLM模拟学生进行5000+轮多轮对话，检验其错误检测、误概念诊断与补救能力；同时揭示标准精确匹配评分（42.1%）与人工校准LLM裁判（81.3%）间存在39.2个百分点鸿沟，且导师对诊断信息几乎不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用作数学导师，但两个问题未解：单一LLM能否在检测错误、诊断误概念、并通过多轮对话补救这一完整链路上胜任；以及现有汇报其表现的指标数字是否可信。作者认为测量问题与能力问题同等重要，需并重审视。

Method: 构建全自动模拟协议框架：5个7B-14B开源权重LLM导师 × LLM模拟学生，5000+对话，采用配对条件设计与"错误诊断"对照（对真实学习者不安全），强调为刻意模拟、不具真实学生学习效度。测量上对比基准式精确匹配（下界压力测试）与人工校准LLM-as-judge多准则评分，并以4个独立LLM裁判+200条人工标注做收敛验证（kappa最高0.91）。

Result: 同一批对话上，精确匹配测得补救成功率42.1%，人工校准LLM裁判为81.3%，差距39.2个百分点，大于导师模型、教学策略与所提供诊断信息三者效应之和。四名独立LLM裁判与200条人工标注收敛至数点以内。能力方面：告知真实误概念或故意替换的误概念，得分几乎不变，说明在该诊断呈现格式下导师缺乏可靠利用诊断信息的证据。

Conclusion: LLM辅导评估需成熟化：应将多准则评分、多裁判收敛与人工锚定作为标准实践；测量问题应获得与其所声称回答的能力问题同等程度的审视。模拟协议结论不可外推至真实学生学习的结论。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as math tutors, raising two questions: can a single LLM detect a student's error, diagnose the underlying misconception, and remediate it through multi-turn dialogue - and can we trust the numbers that report how well it does? We address both with MISCONCEPTDIALOGUE, a fully automated framework that runs the full pipeline on five open-weight LLM tutors (7B-14B) interacting with LLM-simulated students over 5,000+ dialogues - a deliberately simulated protocol (paired conditions, a wrong-diagnosis control unsafe with real learners, scale) whose results do not establish real-student learning. On measurement, our headline finding is uncomfortable for the field: benchmark-style exact-match scoring common in standard math NLP - a lower-bound stress test - reports 42.1% measured Remediation Success Rate on identical dialogues where a human-calibrated LLM-as-judge reports 81.3%, a 39.2 percentage-point spread larger than the combined effects of tutor model, teaching strategy, and supplied diagnostic information. This is not a single-judge artifact: four independent LLM judges and 200 human annotations converge to within a few points (kappa up to 0.91). On capability, the verdict is equally striking: the score barely changes whether we tell the tutor the student's actual misconception or a deliberately swapped one - under the tested diagnosis-presentation format, these tutors show little evidence of reliably conditioning on the diagnostic information they are handed. For LLM tutoring evaluation to mature, multi-criterion scoring with multi-judge convergence and human anchoring should become standard practice - and the measurement question deserves as much scrutiny as the capability question it claims to answer.

</details>


### [137] [From Noisy Retrieval to Decision Evidence: Silver Evidence Demonstrations for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32128)
*Masoud Monajatipoor,Fazlolah Mohaghegh,Hamid Reza Hassanzadeh,Akash Uttamrao Bhoite,Robert E. Tillman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"银证据示范"(silver-evidence demonstrations)，用LLM生成的、目标条件化的证据摘要替换ICL示例中的原始检索段落，在长而嘈杂的上下文中提升标签准确率与证据F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG与检索增强的few-shot ICL在决策相关证据被埋于长而嘈杂的上下文时表现不佳：检索段落与示范含冗余或干扰信息；专家标注的证据通常仅作为答案侧的rationale，而非用于监督过滤噪声检索。

Method: 对每个有标签的开发示例，让LLM在无法访问专家标签与金证据的条件下生成"银证据"，再与专家标签（及可用时的金证据）配对构成ICL示范；测试时用一次结构化调用先从检索段落生成银证据，再预测最终标签与决策证据。

Result: 在EI 2.0、SciFact、合成政策检索、HICRIC/IMR上，完整方法在4个数据集中3个上优于同等规模的gold-only ICL（政策数据为label-only），并在所有有标注数据集上提升证据F1；平均标签准确率在4个数据集上均高于InstructRAG式ICL。

Conclusion: 以紧凑、目标条件化的银证据替换示范侧检索段落，对基于长嘈杂文本的决策尤为有效：既减少干扰上下文，又保留决策相关信号与专家监督。

Abstract: RAG and retrieval-augmented few-shot ICL can struggle when decision-relevant evidence is buried in long, noisy context. Retrieved passages and demonstrations may contain redundant or distracting information. Although expert-labeled development examples may identify the text supporting each decision, these annotations are typically used only as answer-side rationales rather than as supervision for filtering noisy retrieval. We propose silver-evidence demonstrations, which replace raw retrieved passages in ICL examples with generated, target-conditioned evidence summaries. For each labeled development example, an LLM generates silver evidence without access to the expert label or gold evidence; this evidence is then paired with the expert label and gold evidence where available. At test time, a single structured call generates silver evidence from retrieved passages and then predicts the final label and decision evidence. Across EI 2.0, SciFact, synthetic policy retrieval, and HICRIC/IMR data, our full method improves over equally sized gold-only ICL (label-only for policy) on three of four datasets and improves evidence F1 on all annotated datasets. It also achieves higher mean label accuracy than InstructRAG-style ICL on all four datasets. These results suggest that replacing demonstration-side retrieved passages with compact, target-conditioned silver evidence is particularly useful for decisions based on long and noisy text, where it reduces distracting context while preserving decision-relevant signals and expert supervision.

</details>


### [138] [EDWARD-NMT: Elastic Dimensions With Adaptive Routing and Dual-consistency for Neural Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32115)
*Edward Nguyen,Thai Tran,Kiet Anh Hoang,Duy Hoang,Duy Vo,Khanh Le,Vu Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EDWARD-NMT，一种可在训练一次后按需抽取子网络的稀疏MoE编码器-解码器架构，通过弹性维度、自适应路由与双一致性目标实现内存与算力的动态伸缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE虽利于扩展NMT规模并应对语言异质性，但在资源受限设备上部署受阻：需常驻全部专家池，且训练时的top-k路由使每token计算预算静态固定。

Method: 三项创新：(i) Matryoshka式弹性维度，灵活压缩内存占用；(ii) 带可学习、任务条件阈值的自适应sigmoid路由器，使每个token动态决定激活专家数；(iii) 双一致性目标，最大化所抽取子网络性能。

Result: 在PhoMT(En-Vi)、WMT-14(En-De)、多语OPUS-16及多领域De-En基准上验证：全容量架构以更优参数效率持平或超越强MoE基线；单一检查点抽取的子网络无需重训即稳定优于同规模稠密Transformer。

Conclusion: EDWARD-NMT以"训练一次、任意部署"范式，兼顾MoE表达力与部署灵活性，为资源受限场景提供可行的高效NMT方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a powerful paradigm for scaling Neural Machine Translation (NMT) and managing language heterogeneity. However, practical limitations hinder its deployment on resource-constrained devices: the entire expert pool must remain in memory, and the per-token compute budget is statically fixed during training by rigid top-k routing. We introduce EDWARD-NMT (Elastic Dimensions With Adaptive Routing and Dual-consistency for NMT), a novel MoE encoder-decoder architecture designed for a train once, deploy anywhere paradigm that exposes a family of deployable sub-networks at inference time. EDWARD-NMT integrates three key innovations: (i) Matryoshka-style elastic dimensions to flexibly reduce the memory footprint; (ii) an adaptive sigmoid router with a learned, task-conditioned threshold, allowing each token to dynamically determine its active expert count; and (iii) a dual-consistency objective to maximize the performance of the extracted sub-networks. Extensive experiments on PhoMT (En-Vi), WMT-14 (En-De), multilingual OPUS-16, and a multi-domain De-En benchmark validate our approach. The full-capacity architecture matches or exceeds strong MoE baselines with improved parameter efficiency. Furthermore, the sub-networks seamlessly extracted from a single checkpoint consistently outperform comparably sized dense Transformers without any retraining.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond What to Say: Proactive Utterance Timing with Inner Thoughts for Werewolf Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32107)
*Yoshiki Tanaka,Shuya Uchiyama,Daichi Hamaguchi,Michimasa Inaba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向狼人杀"随时发言"赛道的Adversarial Inner Thoughts方法，在内心思考框架基础上改造发言时机决策，并通过自博弈日志分析与人工评测验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中不仅要决定说什么，还要决定是否发言；在利益冲突、信息不完全的狼人杀环境中，发言与沉默都会被他人解读和评估，因此"何时说"与"说什么"同等关键。现有内心思考方法面向一般对话，未适配这种对抗性与不完全信息场景。

Method: 基于Inner Thoughts框架（通过生成内部想法并评估每个想法在当前时刻的表达价值来确定发言时机），针对狼人杀的竞争性与不完全信息特性重新设计系统，命名为Adversarial Inner Thoughts，用于AIWolfDial共享任务的Anytime Utterance Track。

Result: 通过自博弈日志分析与人工评测两类实验，对方法有效性进行了分析与讨论（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 将内心思考机制改造为对抗性版本可支持狼人杀LLM智能体的发言时机决策，实验分析表明该思路具备有效性，为对抗性不完全信息对话中的发言时机控制提供了参考。

Abstract: Participating in a conversation involves not only deciding what to say but also whether to speak at all. This judgment carries even greater significance in environments like the Werewolf game, where participants' interests conflict, because both utterances and silence are subject to interpretation and assessment by others. Therefore, determining when to speak, as well as what to say, is crucial for an LLM agent playing the Werewolf game. In this study, we introduce a Werewolf AI agent developed for the Anytime Utterance Track of the AIWolfDial shared task. Our system builds on the existing Inner Thoughts framework, which determines utterance timing during conversations by generating internal thoughts and evaluating the value of each thought for expression at that moment. Since existing methods were designed for general dialogue, we have redesigned the system specifically for the competitive environment and incomplete information setting of Werewolf games.
