We call our adapted method Adversarial Inner Thoughts. Through experiments involving analysis of self-play logs and human evaluations, we analyze and discuss the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [140] [POS-Signature Demonstration Retrieval for In-Context Dependency Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31966)
*Muhammad Azam Afridi,Rajab Ali,Marco Passarotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究UD依存句法分析中的示例选择问题，提出以词性签名（加权POS统计）代替语义相似度检索三条长度兼容的标注示例，在英文EWT上取得优于语义检索和固定示例的LAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）的示例通常按语义相似度检索，但在结构化预测任务中，语义相似度可能并非相关性的良好代理，因此需要探索更适合句法分析任务的示例检索策略。

Method: 提出POS-signature检索策略：用加权词性统计表示句子，从标注训练集检索三条长度兼容的gold-parsed示例。在两个开源权重语言模型上不进行微调，于UD English Web Treebank测试集全部2,077句上评测；对比等示例数且等格式的固定三示例与语义三示例基线，以及zero-shot、chain-of-thought、critique-refinement系统。

Result: POS-signature检索在两个模型上均取得所测提示系统中最高LAS（72.4与73.2），较语义检索分别提升1.9与2.7个点，成对bootstrap 95%置信区间均不含零；链式思维与精炼比较的结果随模型而异。

Conclusion: 探索性结果表明，在英文EWT上POS-signature检索策略更优；作者公开提示词、存档输出与评测代码。

Abstract: Demonstrations for in-context learning are often retrieved by semantic similarity, which may be a poor proxy for relevance in structured prediction. We study demonstration selection for Universal Dependencies parsing with a POS-signature policy that represents sentences with weighted part-of-speech statistics and retrieves three length-compatible, gold-parsed examples from the labeled training split. We evaluate two open-weight language models without fine-tuning on all 2,077 sentences of the UD English Web Treebank test set, comparing the policy with shot- and format-matched fixed and semantic three-shot baselines, as well as zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and critique-refinement systems. POS-signature retrieval yields the highest labeled attachment score among the tested prompting systems for both models (72.4 and 73.2), outperforming semantic retrieval by 1.9 and 2.7 points; paired-bootstrap 95% confidence intervals exclude zero for both contrasts. Chain-of-thought and refinement comparisons vary across models. These exploratory results favor the tested POS-signature retrieval approach on English-EWT. We release the prompts, archived outputs, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Final Outcomes: Quantifying Claim-Level Information Dynamics in Multi-Agent LLM Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32333)
*Haocheng Jiang,Herun Wan,Yongyuan Peng,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RIVER框架，将多智能体交互视为命题级信息动态，从收敛、探索、保留三个维度评估信息流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体评估多聚焦最终任务结果，忽视了交互过程中信息被反复共享、扩展与遗忘的动态特性。

Method: 将智能体输出分解为原子命题，构建三维指标：收敛（跨智能体命题对应与立场一致性）、探索（语义广度及对源信息的扩展）、保留（源信息保持及新命题在阶段与谱系中的持续性）；在40个受控新闻生成案例上比较五种交互策略。

Result: 反复池化式交互提升收敛但压缩信息空间；分布式接力产生更广但共享度更低的信息；方法特定约束提升探索却削弱收敛与保留。

Conclusion: 集体信息动态更宜由底层信息流机制解释，而非仅凭下游任务表现。

Abstract: When LLM-powered agents interact, information is repeatedly shared, expanded, and forgotten, yet multi-agent evaluation typically focuses on final task outcomes. We study such interaction as claim-level information dynamics and propose RIVER, which decomposes agent outputs into atomic claims and evaluates three complementary dimensions: convergence, exploration, and retention. Convergence captures cross-agent claim correspondence and stance alignment; exploration captures semantic breadth and extension beyond the source; and retention tracks source preservation and the persistence of new claims across stages and lineages. We evaluate five interaction strategies on 40 controlled news-generation cases. Repeatedly pooled interaction promotes convergence but narrows the information space, whereas distributed relay yields broader, less shared information. Further comparisons show that method-specific constraints increase exploration while weakening convergence and retention. Our findings suggest that collective information dynamics are better explained by underlying information-flow mechanisms than by simple downstream performance.

</details>


### [142] [System One models Are Not Better Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32318)
*Michael Dinzinger,Kanishka Ghosh Dastidar,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文检验"决策微调模型（System One）是否比同一模型的普通重排序微调更适合做重排器"，结论是否定的：决策微调从未带来更好重排效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System One/决策模型以校准概率而非生成文本回答输入问题，发布数周内即被用作重排器。作者要验证其背后的决策微调是否真的比同一模型的普通重排器微调更优。

Method: 使用 6 个 Qwen 主干（0.6B–27B）和 10 个 TREC DL 与 BEIR 数据集，比较三类模型：零样本未微调模型、决策微调版本、重排器微调版本（其中两个由作者自行训练），以 nDCG@10 为主要指标，并分析校准性与阈值下的排序质量。

Result: 0.6B–9B 上重排器微调在每个主干上以 5–29 点 nDCG@10 胜出，零样本数据集上亦然；27B 时二者持平。9B 及以下，决策微调相较"直接询问未微调模型"无增益。校准优势取决于提问方式：问"段落是否回答查询"时，4B 起校准优于公开的重排器微调模型；换成先前工作中的略有不同的问题则丧失该优势。且校准无法弥补更差的排序：9B 及以下，重排器微调在固定阈值下保留的优质段落更多。

Conclusion: 需要重排器就微调重排器；决策微调不构成更好的重排方案，其校准优势依赖问题表述且不能抵消排序质量劣势。

Abstract: System One models, or decision models, answer a question about an input with a calibrated probability instead of generated text. Within weeks of the first release, in September 2026, they were used as rerankers. We test whether the decision fine-tuning behind them makes a better reranker than plain reranker fine-tuning of the same model. For six Qwen backbones from 0.6B to 27B parameters and ten TREC DL and BEIR collections, we compare the untuned model, asked zero-shot, with its decision fine-tunes and its reranker fine-tunes, two of which we train ourselves. In our work, we draw the conclusion that decision fine-tuning never makes the better reranker. From 0.6B to 9B, reranker fine-tuning wins on every backbone by 5 to 29 points nDCG@10, also on zero-shot collections, and at 27B the two tie. Up to 9B, decision fine-tuning adds nothing over simply asking the untuned model. Moreover, we find that the calibration advantage of decision models depends on the question. Asked whether a passage answers the query, they are better calibrated than the published reranker fine-tunes from 4B on. Asked a slightly different question from prior work, they lose this advantage. Lastly, calibration does not make up for a worse ranking: up to 9B, a reranker fine-tune keeps the better passages at a fixed threshold. If you need a reranker, fine-tune a reranker.

</details>


### [143] [Collection-Date Assignment Redirects Answers in Temporal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32191)
*Minsu Oh,Seunghui Jwa,Soo Kyun Kim,Yeo-Chan Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文利用“采集日期”元数据交换实验证明：即使证据内容不变，时间问答仍会被元数据重定向，产生37.6–51.4个百分点的EM下降，并据此提出一种针对时间问答输入的审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出“采集日期”记录的是源版本被获取的时间，而非其内容发生变更的时间，因此需回答：在证据不变的前提下，仅仅改派这一元数据是否会改变时间问答的输出？现有研究尚缺乏将元数据效应与证据变化、版本缺失等因素分离的诊断手段。

Method: 在595个维基百科页面上构造两版本之间的“问题日与其后一日”交换，并在128个不相交页面上复现；另做前一日交换（497页）、七种提示变体（含显式忽略采集日期指令与档案版本出处置信）、虚构答案与英文月份名问句日期、匹配思考（thinking）设置、两个API模型、远距离日期交换，以及补充含单个观测采集日期的字段等对照实验。

Result: 交换问题日与次日使后续引用精确匹配（EM）在五个开源权重LLM上下降37.6–51.4个百分点，前一日交换仍损失29.0–39.6个百分点；七种提示变体均保留该效应；虚构答案与相同日期字符串表明记忆答案、相同日期串并非必要条件；匹配思考使Qwen3.5惩罚由30.1降至11.1个百分点，原生推理模型表现不一；两个API模型效应很小，且在虚构记录上很少转向更早答案；交换致损集中于问题日期附近，远距离交换效应明显更小；加入含一个观测采集日期的字段在五模型中有三个在校正后EM低于省略该字段。

Conclusion: 该诊断将元数据效应与证据改变、遗漏相关版本等因素隔离开，表明采集日期元数据能够在不改变证据的情况下重定向时间问答，并为时间问答输入提供了一种实用的审计工具。

Abstract: A collection date records when a source revision was acquired, not when its contents changed. Can assigning this metadata redirect temporal question answering despite unchanged evidence? Swapping the question day and following day between two revisions lowers later-reference exact match (EM) by 37.6-51.4 percentage points across five open-weight LLMs on 595 Wikipedia pages, replicated on 128 disjoint pages. Previous-day swaps retain 29.0-39.6-point losses on 497 pages. Seven prompt variants, including explicit ignore-collection instructions and archived-revision provenance, retain the effect. Fictional answers and English month-name question dates show that remembered answers and identical date strings are unnecessary. Matched thinking reduces Qwen3.5's penalty from 30.1 to 11.1 points, while native reasoners vary. Two API LLMs show small Wikipedia swap effects and rarely shift to earlier answers on fictional records. Large swap-induced losses concentrate around the question date; swaps between distant dates have much smaller effects. Adding fields containing one observed acquisition date lowers EM relative to omission in three of five LLMs after correction. The diagnostic isolates metadata effects from changing evidence or missing the relevant revision, and provides a practical audit for temporal QA inputs.

</details>


### [144] [CounselPlex: A Stressor-Aware Full-Duplex Counseling Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32702)
*Yeonju Kim,Bella Godiva,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出CounselPlex——一个压力源感知的全双工语音框架，通过"求助者沉默期更新压力源状态"并将状态注入监听中的<PAD>位置，缓解全双工语音模型在咨询场景中的"压力源盲视"问题；并提出RPSA位置偏移增强以扩大位置覆盖、提升长对话轮次性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型可同时听与说，实现自然低延迟交互；但在心理咨询场景中，模型往往只关注最新话语而丢失求助者的深层问题（作者称之为Stressor Blindness，压力源盲视），导致咨询质量与问题锚定能力不足。

Method: CounselPlex采用两项操作：1）在求助者沉默（silence）期间更新一个简短的压力源状态（stressor state）；2）在监听时将该状态注入<PAD>位置。此外提出RoPE Position Shift Augmentation（RPSA），在训练中扩大位置覆盖范围。

Result: LLM-judge评测显示，相比强基线，咨询质量与压力源锚定（stressor grounding）均有提升；RPSA在评测中改善了更长对话轮次深度下的表现。

Conclusion: 将压力源状态显式建模并注入全双工监听流，可有效缓解压力源盲视；配合RPSA可增强长轮次鲁棒性，为面向咨询场景的全双工语音模型提供可行方案。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models can listen and speak at the same time, enabling natural, low-latency interaction. In counseling, however, these models often focus on the latest utterance and lose track of the seeker's underlying problem. We call this failure Stressor Blindness. We propose CounselPlex, a stressor-aware full-duplex framework that uses two operations. CounselPlex updates a short stressor state during seeker silence and injects this state into <PAD> positions while listening. We also introduce RoPE Position Shift Augmentation (RPSA), which broadens positional coverage during training and improves performance at longer turn depths in our evaluation. LLM-judge evaluations show improved counseling quality and stressor grounding over strong baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Natural Language Autoencoders Identify Without Explaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32691)
*Kristina Šekrst,Barbara Geld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，自然语言自编码器（NLA）以"激活—解释—重构"往返成功作为评估标准，该标准只能检验信息充分性，并不能保证解释的描述忠实性；因此NLA作为可解释性与对齐工具存在认识论漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作将NLA作为对齐与可解释性工具，但默认"重构成功即可信"。作者意在揭示这一默认假设的问题：往返重构成功与解释是否真实描述激活功能之间可能存在系统性脱节。

Method: 概念性/论证性分析。给出NLA的组件形式（verbalizer 将激活转为英文解释，reconstructor 将解释还原为激活），并以重构成功（round-trip）为判据，对判据的含义进行逻辑辨析：区分"信息充分性（sufficiency/identifiability）"与"描述忠实性（descriptive faithfulness）"，并与思维链忠实性问题及心理学中内省报告与实际认知过程分离的现象作结构性类比。

Result: 论证出：即便一句解释包含足以唯一识别某激活的信息，它也可能错误地说明该激活所做的事情；重构任务的首要目标是准确识别激活而非描述激活，因此重构成功并不能作为描述忠实性的证据。该问题与思维链忠实性问题同构，并对应人类心理学中内省报告与真实支配过程的分离。

Conclusion: NLA虽被提出作为服务于对齐的可解释性工具，却通过未经核验的解释重新引入了它本欲解决的认识论问题；评估NLA需在重构判据之外另行建立描述忠实性的检验标准。

Abstract: Natural Language Autoencoders (NLAs) express a model's internal activations in the form of readable text. The verbalizer turns an activation into an English explanation; the reconstructor then converts that explanation back into an activation, and the explanation is considered successful if the round trip brings back the original activation. We claim that this criterion evaluates whether the information is sufficient and does not address descriptive faithfulness. A sentence can contain sufficient information to make it possible to identify an activation even if it wrongly explains what the activation does: in the case of reconstruction, the primary aim is to accurately identify the activations and not to describe them. This issue has the same structure as the chain-of-thought faithfulness problem, in which verbal output that has been trained to go with a process is taken as a report on that process, and it parallels the dissociation between an individual's introspective report and the actual governing process as documented in human psychology. Although proposed as interpretability tools for the purpose of alignment, NLAs reintroduce, through unverified explanations, the epistemic problem that they were intended to resolve.

</details>


### [146] [Measure Before You Train: Typed Decision Models vs. a Classical Baseline vs. a Generative LLM for Routing a Personal Multilingual Inbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32955)
*Federico Lucca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自托管个人收件箱智能体 Foyer 及一套训练前的测量协议，用 739 封真实邮件（主人人工标注）对九类系统做分组交叉验证。发现生产规则在邮件类型上仅 28%（多数类 58%），约 200 标注下逻辑回归各字段最优但对稀有类失效；发件人关系需从联系人图获取（98.6%）而非单封邮件；准确率不等于产品指标，按成本设阈值可兼顾成本与漏处理率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人邮件智能体需对每封邮件判断类型、紧急度及是否草拟回复/建任务/忽略，但其错误率几乎不被测量——存储标签由系统自身生成，形成自证循环。作者希望在任何训练开始前就获得可信的真实标签，并检验实际部署系统的真实表现。

Method: 发布自托管个人收件箱智能体 Foyer；设计测量协议：分层抽样、冻结快照、按线程分组切分、预注册假设、人工标签嵌入智能体复核回路。在 739 封真实意大利语/英语邮件（231 封无偏、500 封对抗采样）上，按线程分组交叉验证比较九个系统：生产关键词规则、TF-IDF+逻辑回归基线、本地类型化决策模型（Tev1、Nimble）、托管决策模型（Nimble v4-max）及托管生成式 LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma 4 26B/31B）。

Result: 1）生产规则在邮件类型上仅 28%，低于多数类基线 58%，最醒目字段不含信息。2）约 200 标注下逻辑回归在各字段准确率最高（类型 0.79、紧急度 0.74、需回复 0.83、噪声 0.90），但从不预测两个最稀有类别，且在类型上与 Gemma 4 统计持平；少样本提示未能弥合差距。3）发件人关系（客户/供应商/服务）在联系人已出现时从同联系人其他邮件中以 98.6% 准确率恢复，单封邮件无法推断。4）组合为动作后，最准确系统最常忽略需处理邮件（18% vs 最佳 LLM 4%）；按其噪声概率做成本阈值可将该比率减半并成为最便宜系统。

Conclusion: 个人邮件智能体的评测应先建立真实标签再训练；简单线性模型在少量标注下表现强于少样本 LLM，但不能覆盖稀有类；发件人关系应存入联系人图而非提示词；准确率不能替代产品指标，需将字段概率组合为动作并按成本优化阈值，才能在漏处理率与成本间取得更优折中。

Abstract: Personal email agents decide, for every message, what it is, whether it is urgent, whether to draft a reply, create a task, or ignore it. Their error rate is rarely measured: the stored labels were written by the system itself. We present Foyer, a self-hosted personal inbox agent in daily use, and a measurement protocol (stratified sampling, frozen snapshots, thread-grouped splits, pre-registered hypotheses, human labels in the agent's review loop) that yields ground truth before any training. On 739 real Italian/English emails labelled by the owner (231 unbiased, 500 adversarially sampled), we compare nine systems under thread-grouped cross-validation: the production keyword rules, a TF-IDF + logistic-regression baseline, local typed decision models (Tev1, Nimble), a hosted decision model (Nimble v4-max), and hosted generative LLMs (Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma 4 26B/31B). Four results. The production rules score 28% on message type against a 58% majority class: the field shown most prominently carried no information. With ~200 labels, logistic regression has the highest accuracy on every field (type 0.79, urgency 0.74, needs-reply 0.83, is-noise 0.90), though it never predicts the two rarest classes and is statistically tied with Gemma 4 on type; few-shot prompting does not close the gap. The sender's relationship (client, supplier, service) is recovered from other emails of the same contact with 98.6% accuracy where the contact has been seen, and is not inferable from a single message by any model: it belongs in the contact graph, not the prompt. Finally, accuracy is not the product metric: composed into actions, the most accurate system most often ignores emails needing action (18% vs 4% for the best LLM); a cost-chosen threshold on its noise probability halves that rate and makes it the cheapest system.

</details>


### [147] [J-Comm: Jacobian-Guided Latent Communication between LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32871)
*Xiangwei Wang,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Ken Chen,Yu Xia,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: J-Comm提出免训练的GEAR潜在推理机制与H+J隐状态通信接口，使不同宽度模型间无需共享KV缓存即可传递中间推理信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在通信需要有效的连续推理过程，以及能让他模型利用其中间计算的接口。

Method: 两部分：1) GEAR（训练无关的几何一致性潜在推理），在每步对候选token按嵌入空间中的几何共识进行重加权，单调优化候选token间的几何一致性；2) H+J接口，H通道提供由反馈分布导出的连续输入，J通道以词表索引的Jacobian透镜坐标传递中间状态线索，Receiver用自身字典重构并注入prefill中间层，无需共享KV缓存或对齐模型宽度。

Result: Qwen3-8B→Qwen3-4B通信中，七个基准平均分最高：MATH500 94.80%、GPQA-Diamond 54.55%、AIME2024+2025 73.33%、ALFWorld 62.93%；较TextMAS提升1.29–8.59个百分点，生成文本token减少19.2–74.8%。

Conclusion: 通过几何一致性潜在推理与双视图隐状态接口，J-Comm在异构模型间实现高效、低文本开销的潜在通信并取得领先性能。

Abstract: Latent communication requires both an effective continuous reasoning process and an interface through which an external model can leverage its intermediate computation. We present J-Comm, a framework comprising two core components. First, a training-free Geometric Agreement for Latent Reasoning (GEAR) reweights token candidates over existing model embeddings, which monotonically optimizes geometric consensus among candidate tokens in embedding space at each step. Second, we introduce an H+J (Hidden-and-Jacobian) communication interface that gives the Receiver two complementary views of the Sender's reasoning. The H channel supplies continuous inputs derived from feedback distributions, while the J channel conveys intermediate-state cues in vocabulary-indexed Jacobian-lens coordinates. Intuitively, H provides inputs for the Receiver to reason from, while J offers additional guidance within its computation. The Receiver reconstructs these cues using its own dictionary and injects them into intermediate layers during prefill, enabling communication without sharing key-value caches or requiring identical model widths. In Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-4B communication, J-Comm achieves the highest mean score across seven benchmarks, including 94.80% on MATH500, 54.55% on GPQA-Diamond, 73.33% on AIME2024+2025, and 62.93% success on ALFWorld. Compared with TextMAS, it improves performance by 1.29-8.59 percentage points and reduces generated text tokens by 19.2-74.8%.

</details>


### [148] [Latent Coherence: Do Frontier Language Models' Probability Judgments Obey the Law of Total Probability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31833)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文零样本地检验前沿语言模型是否满足全概率公式等概率一致性：通过单轮分别查询P(X)、P(z)、P(X|z)（及因果情形下的P(y|do(x))与后门调整），发现模型间一致性差异明显（最优0.08对数几率绝对残差），且残差可无需真值地标记不可靠回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LawZero的Scientist AI以惩罚潜变量解释间概率不一致为目标训练Predictor以逼近贝叶斯后验；作者追问未经过此类训练的前沿语言模型是否已天然具备这种一致性，即其概率判断在潜变量需从上下文记录推断时是否满足全概率公式。

Method: 在独立单轮调用中分别询问P(X)、潜变量各状态上的P(z)与P(X|z)，检验P(X)=Σ_z P(z)P(X|z)；因果扩展中用同样方式检验P(y|do(x))与后门调整。所有查询均有精确真值；部分题目以三种措辞重复提问，以区分持续性不一致与措辞噪声；比较七个模型，并分析残差与真值误差的相关性、推理长度的影响。

Result: GPT-6.1 Sol与Claude Opus 5.5最一致（平均绝对残差0.08与0.12对数几率，约2个百分点）；Claude Haiku 5.5、Gemini 3.8 Flash与DeepSeek V4-Pro存在持续不一致（0.27–0.35对数几率），且多为题目特异性而非共同偏移。各模型内残差越大、对真值误差越大（Spearman 0.40–0.62）。推理长度不能预测一致性。简单一致性检查几乎全部通过，失败集中于涉及潜变量的推理。

Conclusion: 现有前沿模型在潜变量推理上的一致性参差不齐，尚未零样本达到接近贝叶斯后验的一致性水平；但无需真值的概率残差可作为回答可靠性的实用信号，且不一致源于特定潜变量推理环节而非普遍缺陷。

Abstract: LawZero's Scientist AI trains a Predictor toward the Bayesian posterior, with an objective that penalizes inconsistent probabilities among latent explanations. We ask whether current frontier language models, with no such training, are already consistent in this sense zero-shot: do their probability judgments satisfy the law of total probability, $P(X)=\sum_z P(z)P(X|z)$, when the latent $z$ must be inferred from records in the context? In separate single-turn calls, models are asked for $P(X)$, for $P(z)$ over the latent's states, and for $P(X|z)$; a causal family applies the same test to $P(y|do(x))$ and the back-door adjustment. Every query has an exact ground-truth probability, and a subset of items is asked in three wordings to separate persistent incoherence from rewording noise. Across seven models, GPT-6.1 Sol and Claude Opus 5.5 are the most coherent (mean absolute residual 0.08 and 0.12 log-odds, about 2 percentage points). Claude Haiku 5.5, Gemini 3.8 Flash and DeepSeek V4-Pro show persistent incoherence (0.27-0.35 log-odds) that is mostly item-specific rather than a shared offset. Within every model, larger residuals go with larger errors against the truth (Spearman 0.40-0.62), so the residual, which needs no ground truth, flags unreliable answers. Reasoning length does not predict coherence, across models or within a model across rewordings of the same question. Simple consistency checks pass almost everywhere; the failures are specific to reasoning through a latent variable. Code and data: https://github.com/ppotash/latent-coherence

</details>


### [149] [Free Lunch for LLMs Confidence: The RL Critic Already Knows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31820)
*Shujin Wu,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LLM置信度训练与强化学习价值函数学习的等价性：在基于正确性的结果下，置信度估计与critic训练预测的是同一目标，因此基于价值RL训练的模型可直接复用已有critic作为免训练、免额外数据的置信度估计器，且不损害任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的置信度估计对LLM可信部署至关重要，但专门的置信度训练阶段需要额外数据和算力。作者追问一个根本问题：置信度训练究竟在解决什么预测问题？

Method: 借鉴外部置信度估计的前期工作，在相同的（问题, 回答, 正确性）三元组上训练容量相当的自估计器与外部估计器进行对比。外部模型仅依赖策略的行为轨迹、不访问内部表示即可达到与策略自身相当的置信度估计精度。进而从理论上证明：在正确性结果下，置信度估计与critic训练等价，二者都在从轨迹预测回答正确的似然。

Result: 对经价值型RL训练的LLM，现有critic可作为即插即用的置信度估计器，无需额外训练或数据，策略保持不变因而任务性能不受影响，且critic能在整个生成过程（含中间推理步骤）提供置信度。在涵盖数学、代码、工具调用和通用推理的十余个领域上，复用critic在每一个同分布划分上都是最佳置信度估计器，在16个设定中的14个上校准最佳。

Conclusion: 置信度训练与critic训练在正确性目标下等价；价值型RL训练的critic可零成本复用为高质量、全过程、良好校准的置信度估计器，为LLM可信部署提供高效方案。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimation is essential for trustworthy deployment of large language models (LLMs), but dedicated confidence training stage requires additional data and compute. We ask a fundamental question: what prediction problem does confidence training actually solve? Inspired by prior work on external confidence estimation, we compare self- and external estimators of comparable capacity trained on the same (question, response, correctness) triples. We show that an external model trained solely on a policy's behavioral traces can estimate the policy's confidence as accurately as the policy itself, without accessing the policy's internal representations. This points to a deeper connection with value function learning in reinforcement learning (RL): under a correctness-based outcome, we prove that confidence estimation and critic training are equivalent, both predicting the likelihood that a policy's response will be correct from its trajectory. A simple consequence follows: for LLMs trained with value-based RL, the existing critic can be reused as an off-the-shelf confidence estimator with no additional training or data. The policy remains unchanged, so its task performance is preserved, and the critic provides confidence throughout generation, including intermediate reasoning steps. Across math, code, tool calling, and general reasoning spanning a dozen domains, the reused critic is the best confidence estimator on every in-distribution split and the best-calibrated on 14 of 16, without hurting the task performance.

</details>


### [150] [How Much Do You Trust A Dog? Quantifying Hint-Following in Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32055)
*Anxin Guo,Chirag Pabbaraju,Sara Ahmadian,Kamesh Munagala,Aravindan Vijayaraghavan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过MMLU上的提示诱导答案偏移实验，发现LLM的谄媚性影响可由指数倾斜（log-odds上的常数均值平移）很好地刻画，且该偏移在提示来源明显不可靠时仍持续存在，揭示模型对对抗性引导的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM普遍存在谄媚性（sycophancy），即使用户建议与客观事实/推理任务相悖也会改变答案，但其定量形式及是否对来源可靠性进行校准仍缺乏理解。

Method: 在MMLU问题上使用"[Source] thinks the answer is (X)"形式的提示，系统改变来源（教授、朋友、狗），覆盖Qwen、Gemma、SmolLM三个开源模型家族；用指数倾斜模型刻画诱导偏移，提出提示响应的形式化分解，并在受控合成实验中分析KL正则化奖励训练如何重塑提示与未提示答案分布；最后检验偏移是否随来源可靠性校准。

Result: 提示诱导的变化呈现可预测结构，由指数倾斜良好刻画：提示在被提示答案的log-odds上产生近似恒定的均值平移，且问题—目标易感性在不同来源间基本共享。该log-odds偏移在提示者被声明不准确或附带明确不可信警告时依然存在。

Conclusion: LLM的提示响应具有统一的指数倾斜结构，但未对来源可靠性进行校准，凸显其在对抗性引导下的脆弱性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit sycophancy, altering their answers to align with user suggestions even on objective factual and reasoning tasks. Yet the quantitative form of this influence--- and whether it is calibrated to source reliability--- remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this behavior through hint-induced answer shifts on MMLU questions, evaluating prompts of the form ``[Source] thinks the answer is (X)''. We systematically vary the source to which the hint is attributed (a professor, a friend, or a dog) across three open-source model families, Qwen, Gemma, and SmolLM. We show that the change induced by the hint follows a predictable structure well-characterized by an exponential tilt: the hint induces an approximately constant mean shift in the hinted answer's log-odds, with question--target susceptibility largely shared across sources. To explore how this pattern can arise, we introduce a formal decomposition of the hint response and illustrate in controlled synthetic experiments how KL-regularized reward-based training reshapes both hinted and unhinted answer distributions. Finally, we evaluate whether this shift is calibrated to source reliability, finding that the log-odds shift persists even when the hint-giver is declared inaccurate or accompanied by explicit warnings not to trust the source, highlighting the vulnerability of LLMs to adversarial steering.

</details>


### [151] [From Promises to Accountability: Comparative AI Modeling and Visualization of Electoral Commitments in Nepali Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32150)
*Prakash Poudyal,Nischal Karki,Amrit Dahal,Rajani Chulyadyo,Sameer Tamrakar,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建首个尼泊尔语政治文本金标准数据集，并系统评测机器学习、深度学习与大语言模型在选举承诺自动抽取与分析任务上的表现，最终将最优模型集成到移动应用中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南亚发展中国家候选人及政党常未兑现竞选承诺，且竞选宣言、海报、传单、视频等材料缺乏系统透明归档，导致承诺随时间消逝、难以问责。因此需要构建能结构化选举承诺的系统。

Method: 构建首个尼泊尔语政治文本金标准数据集（Cohen's kappa = 0.777），并对比评测机器学习、深度学习和大语言模型三类方法进行选举承诺的自动抽取与分析；结果集成到移动应用，借助生成式AI实现承诺可视化。

Result: 机器学习中 LaBSE-LR 最佳（准确率0.897，宏F1 0.896，AUROC 0.947）；深度学习中 fastText-LSTM 最佳（准确率0.914，宏F1 0.913，AUROC 0.961）；三个大语言模型中 Qwen3-8B 在少样本设置下宏F1最高（0.848）。

Conclusion: 该工作为尼泊尔语选举承诺挖掘提供了数据资源与模型基准，并通过移动应用与GAI可视化提升透明度、公民参与和选举问责。

Abstract: Election Commitment Mining refers to the analysis of election commitments made by electoral candidates or political parties during election campaigns. However, many elected candidates and political parties, especially in developing South Asian countries, do not fully deliver on the promises they make before elections. Even after the elections, commitments presented through election manifestos, posters, pamphlets, videos, and other campaign materials are often not systematically or transparently archived. As a result, these promises tend to fade away over time, making it difficult to hold the elected candidates accountable for their commitments. To address this persistent issue, this research aims to develop a system capable of structuring the commitments made by candidates during election periods. In this work, we created the first gold-standard dataset of Nepali political texts (Cohen's kappa = 0.777) and evaluated machine learning, deep learning and large language models to automatically extract and analyze election commitments. LaBSE-LR achieved the best results among machine learning models (0.897 accuracy, 0.896 macro F1-score, 0.947 AUROC), while fastText-LSTM achieved the highest performance among deep learning models (0.914 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1-score, 0.961 AUROC). Among the three evaluated large language models, Qwen3-8B performed best in the few-shot setting (0.848 macro F1-score). The outcome of this work is integrated into a mobile application, where commitments can be visualized with the support of GAI, enhancing transparency, civic engagement, and electoral accountability.

</details>


### [152] [Frontier-OPD: Discovering the Edge of Agent Capability and Teaching Agents to Go Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32802)
*Zheng Jiang,Yiming Chen,Houde Qian,Ling Li,Chaoyang Li,Lifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Frontier-OPD 提出以“学生学习前沿”为核心的 on-policy 蒸馏框架：通过共享状态轨迹图定位师生分歧，并用短教师桥接的可续性评估筛选监督信号，从而为多轮智能体提供有效蒸馏数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy 蒸馏能使教师反馈对齐学生当前行为，但对多轮智能体而言，关键在于同时回答两个问题：何处需要指导，以及学生接受指导后能否自主延续交互。现有方法缺乏对这种“可承接性”的刻画。

Method: 将 on-policy 经验组织为共享状态轨迹图，通过轨迹平衡的师生分歧聚合识别干预候选，并保留回放所需历史；随后用短教师桥接，对比桥接段及其后学生续写与原始轨迹等长片段的一致性，作为“学生能否承接指导”的行为代理指标；被接受的教师响应加入常规蒸馏数据，并随策略更新用新 rollout 刷新前沿估计。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 与 ScienceWorld 上，跨所评估的学生规模与教师配置，平均自主性能均优于对比的蒸馏基线；消融显示候选选择与指导过滤具有互补作用；在某 ALFWorld 计时设置下，额外采集开销被更短的目标成功率达成时间所抵消。

Conclusion: 以“可承接的学习前沿”为准则筛选教师指导，可显著提升多轮智能体蒸馏的自主任务表现，并在部分场景下具备时间成本优势。

Abstract: On-policy distillation aligns teacher feedback with a student's current behavior, yet acquiring useful supervision for multi-turn agents requires identifying both where guidance is needed and whether the student can sustain the interaction afterward. We introduce Frontier-OPD, which addresses this problem through the student's learning frontier: decision situations that remain challenging but admit useful autonomous continuation after brief teacher guidance. To operationalize this frontier, Frontier-OPD organizes on-policy experience into a shared-state trajectory graph, where trajectory-balanced aggregation of teacher-student disagreement identifies distinct intervention candidates while preserving the histories needed for replay. Short teacher bridges are then assessed by comparing agreement over the bridge and the ensuing student continuation with an equally long segment of the original trajectory, providing a behavioral proxy for guidance that the current student can build on. Accepted teacher responses augment ordinary distillation data, and fresh rollouts renew the frontier estimate as the policy changes. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld report higher mean autonomous performance than the compared distillation baselines across the evaluated student scales and teacher configurations, with ablations indicating complementary effects of candidate selection and guidance filtering. In one ALFWorld timing setting, the additional collection cost is offset by a shorter time to a target success rate.

</details>


### [153] [PHYRD: A Living Benchmark for Reproducing and Extending Theoretical Physics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32575)
*Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出理论物理推理基准 PHYRD（63 道 Reproduce + 43 道 Discover），配套 FormulaForest 构建方法提供参考解与子题级评分细则；评测显示模型在闭卷 Discover 上表现波动大（18.60%–74.42%），工具可提升完成量但概念性错误与论证缺失仍存，FormulaForest 作为合成数据可将 Qwen-35B-A3B 在 FrontierScience 上从 61% 提升至 72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理推理评测多聚焦解题正确性，缺乏对"复现已发表推导"与"在修改理论条件下推演新结论"两类能力的区分性考察；且缺少细粒度、可诊断的评分与可规模化构建流程。

Method: 构建 PHYRD 动态基准，含 Reproduce（复现已发表结果的推导）与 Discover（在修改后的理论条件下推导后果）两类任务；提出 FormulaForest 流水线，为每题生成参考解与子题级 rubric 评分标准；在五种模型上进行整题准确率评测，并测试工具调用设置；另将 FormulaForest 产物作为合成数据用于训练验证。

Result: 整题准确率：Reproduce 为 49.21%–68.25%；闭卷 Discover 为 18.60%–74.42%。引入工具后四个模型可完成更多 Discover 题，但仍存在概念性错误与论证缺失；FormulaForest 合成数据使 Qwen-35B-A3B 在 OpenAI 的 FrontierScience 基准上从 61% 提升至 72%。

Conclusion: 区分"复现"与"发现"能更细粒度揭示模型的理论物理推理短板；子题级 rubric 提供可诊断信号；FormulaForest 兼具基准构建与高质量合成数据双重价值，可迁移提升模型在外部科学基准上的表现。

Abstract: Large language models and agents have shown significant power in advanced physical research. In this paper, We introduce PHYRD, a living benchmark for theoretical-physics reasoning with 63 Reproduce and 43 Discover problems. Reproduce tests derivation of published results; Discover tests consequences of modified theoretical conditions. FormulaForest constructs the tasks with reference solutions and subquestion-level rubrics. Across five models, whole-problem accuracy ranges from 49.21% to 68.25% on Reproduce and from 18.60% to 74.42% on closed-book Discover. With tools, four models complete more Discover problems, but conceptual errors and missing arguments persist. Besides, our experimental results demonstrate the value of FormulaForest as synthetic data. On FrontierScience, a benchmark developed by OpenAI, it raises the accuracy of a Qwen-35B-A3B model from 61% to 72%.

</details>


### [154] [Pseudo-Labels as Rewards, Not Targets: Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Semi-Supervised ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32572)
*Shashi Kumar,Yanis Labrak,Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Petr Motlicek,Kadri Hacioglu,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在仅有未转写音频的领域自适应ASR中，将伪标签作为奖励参考用于GRPO比监督微调（SFT）对标签噪声更鲁棒：银行领域相对WER改进29.3%–56.2%，SFT几乎100%复现教师错误而GRPO仅27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅有未转写音频时的领域自适应ASR依赖伪标签（强教师转写、学生在其上微调）。但SFT把每个伪标签token都当作目标，学生因而继承教师错误，导致噪声标签被逐字拟合。

Method: 不把伪标签作为SFT的目标，而将其作为GRPO的奖励参考信号，利用组内相对优势（group-relative advantage）优化策略；并通过on-policy rollout统计（错误伪标签词在采样中的出现频率、优势对齐情况、奖励方差）分析其抗噪机制。

Result: 银行领域：在Whisper生成标签上，GRPO相对SFT取得29.3%–56.2%的相对WER改进；该优势在4种Whisper规模、4类教师模型、3个领域、4种语言上均成立。错误传递率：SFT约100%，GRPO仅27%。

Conclusion: 错误的伪标签词很少出现在on-policy rollout中，组内相对优势仍与干净转写对齐；而SFT强行将其纳入策略支撑集，反而摧毁了GRPO所依赖的奖励方差。因此以伪标签作奖励参考比作训练目标更抗噪。

Abstract: Adapting ASR to a domain with only untranscribed audio typically relies on pseudo-labeling, a strong teacher transcribes the audio and a student is fine-tuned on it. Because supervised fine-tuning (SFT) treats every pseudo-label token as a target, the student inherits the teacher's errors. We show that using the same pseudo-labels as a reward reference for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is more robust to label noise. In the banking domain, GRPO yields 29.3% to 56.2% relative WER improvements over SFT on Whisper-generated labels, and the advantage holds across four Whisper sizes, four teacher families, three domains and four languages. While SFT reproduces almost 100% of teacher errors verbatim, GRPO passes through only 27%. Rollout statistics explain why, erroneous pseudo-label words rarely appear in on-policy rollouts, so group-relative advantages stay aligned with the clean transcript, whereas SFT forces them into the policy's support and collapses the reward variance GRPO relies on.

</details>


### [155] [The Punjabi Geet Toxicity Dataset (PGT-FG): Fine-Grained Multimodal Annotations for Toxic Content in Punjabi Songs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32513)
*Lakhjeet Kaur,Jaspreet Kaur,Komal Rani,Yougal Attri,Ramakant KumarJaydeep Kishore,Pravin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布旁遮普语歌曲毒性检测数据集PGT-FG：从YouTube采集620首歌，最终获得392首歌的2,396个片段，四名母语标注者标注二元毒性、八类细粒度类别、严重度、情感、调式及不确定标记，总体毒性标签Fleiss kappa=0.6521，29.42%片段被标为有毒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旁遮普语毒性检测资源稀缺，尤其是融合语言与多模态信息的音乐内容缺乏可用数据支撑研究。

Method: 从YouTube公开旁遮普语歌曲构建片段级数据集：初始620首→成功下载465首→450首产生3,402个初步片段；经质量控制与标注后保留392首的2,396个唯一片段。四名旁遮普语母语者产出2,870条标注记录，含158个片段的全员共享子集；标注维度包括二元毒性、8个细粒度毒性类别、严重度、情感、调式与不确定标志；在共享子集上用Fleiss' kappa评估一致性，并以多数投票生成最终片段级标签。

Result: 2,396个片段中705个（29.42%）为有毒、1,691个（70.58%）为无毒；暴力类最常见（539个有毒片段，76.45%），其次为帮派指涉（227，32.20%）与药物指涉（133，18.87%）；总体毒性标签达到较高一致性（kappa=0.6521）。数据集经Zenodo公开，原始歌词、歌词片段及受版权保护的音视频不予再分发。

Conclusion: PGT-FG为旁遮普语毒性检测与多模态内容分析提供了细粒度资源，但受版权限制仅发布标注结果而非原始内容，后续研究需依赖可获取的源媒体或既有特征。

Abstract: Resources for toxicity detection in Punjabi remain limited, particularly for musical content that combines linguistic and multimodal information. We present the Punjabi Geet Toxicity Dataset (PGT-FG), a segment-level resource developed to support research on toxicity detection in Punjabi musical content. The dataset was constructed from publicly available Punjabi songs collected from YouTube. An initial collection of 620 songs resulted in 465 successfully downloaded songs and 3,402 preliminary segments from 450 songs. After quality control and annotation, the final dataset contains 2,396 unique segments from 392 songs. Four native Punjabi annotators produced 2,870 annotation records, including a shared subset of 158 segments annotated by all four. Each annotation includes binary toxicity, eight fine-grained toxicity categories, severity, sentiment, mode, and an uncertainty flag. Inter-annotator agreement was measured with Fleiss' kappa on the shared subset, and majority-based resolution produced the final segment-level labels. Of the 2,396 segments, 705 (29.42%) were labelled toxic and 1,691 (70.58%) non-toxic. Violence was the most frequent category, occurring in 539 toxic segments (76.45%), followed by gang references (227, 32.20%) and drug references (133, 18.87%). The overall toxicity label achieved substantial agreement (kappa = 0.6521). PGT-FG provides a fine-grained resource for Punjabi toxicity detection and multimodal content analysis. The resolved dataset, with one final record per segment, is publicly available on Zenodo (https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23040156). Original lyrics, lyric excerpts, and copyrighted audio or video content are not redistributed.

</details>


### [156] [SCI-AGENT-EXAM: Benchmarking Agents on Scientific Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32441)
*Tianyun Zhong,Wei Wang,Jialin Li,Jianhong Tu,Liyao Li,Chuqiao Kuang,Tianshi Zheng,Songchi Zhou,Weiqi Zhai,Qile Jin,Jiajia Li,Jiasheng Zheng,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shiwei Ye,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Xianpei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出工作流导向的基准 SCI-AGENT-EXAM，含 11 个科学领域、7 类研究任务共 120 个专家 curated 任务，要求同时产出可执行与文本产物，并采用确定性程序验证 + 校准的 LLM 裁判混合评测；实验显示 10 个前沿 LLM agent 存在显著性能差距（最佳 GPT-6 Astra 总分 80.9，但仅 26.0% 运行超过 0.95 成功率阈值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在科研工作流中的能力日益增强，但现有基准多聚焦于特定科学能力或任务类型，难以系统刻画 agent 在真实科研流程中的整体强弱，缺少综合、工作流层面的评测手段。

Method: 构建 SCI-AGENT-EXAM：基于科学工作流任务分类法（SWTT）定义 7 类研究任务，覆盖 11 个科学领域，共 120 个专家设计任务；每个任务同时要求可执行产物（代码、脚本）与文本产物（报告、解释）。评测采用混合协议：可执行产物用确定性程序化验证，文本产物用经校准、专家验证的 LLM 裁判。此外沿执行轨迹分析进展性与解稳定性。

Result: 10 个前沿 LLM agent 存在明显性能差距：GPT-6 Astra 总分最高（80.9），但仅 26.0% 的运行超过 0.95 成功率阈值；不同科学任务类型呈现差异化能力画像，执行效率与稳定性差异显著。

Conclusion: SCI-AGENT-EXAM 提供了工作流导向、兼顾产物正确性与过程稳定性的科学 agent 评测框架，揭示当前前沿模型在端到端科研任务上仍远未达到可靠成功，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of supporting scientific workflows, but their capabilities across diverse research activities remain difficult to assess systematically. Existing benchmarks often focus on selected scientific capabilities or task types, limiting a comprehensive characterization of scientific agents' strengths and weaknesses in realistic research workflows. We introduce SCI-AGENT-EXAM, a workflow-oriented benchmark comprising 120 expert-curated tasks across 11 scientific fields and 7 research task types defined by the Scientific Workflow Task Taxonomy (SWTT). Each task requires both executable artifacts (e.g., code and scripts) and textual artifacts (e.g., reports and explanations). To evaluate the heterogeneous task outputs, we develop a hybrid evaluation protocol combining deterministic programmatic verification for executable artifacts with a calibrated, expert-validated LLM-based judge for textual artifacts. Beyond outcome assessment, we analyze progress and solution stability along execution trajectories to characterize how agents reach their final results. Experiments with 10 frontier LLM agents reveal substantial performance gaps: GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest overall score of 80.9, yet only 26.0% of its runs exceed the 0.95 success threshold. Further analyses uncover distinct capability profiles across scientific task types and substantial variation in execution efficiency and stability. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/SKYLENAGE-AI/Sci-Agent-Exam.

</details>


### [157] [A Broader Look into MLLM's Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32411)
*Jia Wang,Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Zheng Lin,Mo Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文验证了检索头（retrieval heads）机制在多模态扩展下的稳健性，并提出以排序目标训练检索头注意力来提升长文档检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究将多模态大模型在长输入中定位证据的能力归因于稀疏的"检索头"，但相关分析局限于有限模态与模型，且如何利用检索头提升模型性能尚待探索。作者追问：这一机制是否随模型新增输入模态而扩展。

Method: 以音视频为新增模态，构建"同一答案分别以语音或屏幕呈现"的配对输入，保持证据内容一致；在18个语言、视觉语言与全模态checkpoint上检验检索头的稀疏性、跨模态共享性与对骨干模型的继承性，并进行消融实验；随后将检索头作为优化目标，对其注意力施加排序目标训练。

Result: 证据内容固定时，新模态下检索头依然稀疏且跨模态高度共享；全模态模型大体保留其骨干的检索头；消融这些头几乎完全消除音视频检索；跨模态共享头占比不足2%，但消融后也会损害文档理解、空间感知与视觉数学能力。以排序目标训练检索头注意力使Qwen3-VL-8B在MMDocIR上的page Recall@1提升6.03点，并在三个模型族上改善文档检索。

Conclusion: 功能性注意力头既可作为理解多模态模型的透镜，也可作为提升模型性能的优化目标。

Abstract: Locating relevant evidence in long inputs is a core capability of Multimodal Large Language Models. Prior studies attribute this capability to retrieval heads, a sparse set of attention heads that transfer information from the context to the output. However, these analyses are confined to a limited set of modalities and the corresponding models. Moreover, how retrieval heads can be leveraged to enhance the model calls for further exploration. In this paper, we ask whether this mechanism expands together with the input modalities of the model. Taking audio-visual input as a newly added modality, we construct paired inputs in which the same answer is either spoken or shown on screen. With the evidence content fixed, retrieval heads remain sparse in the new modality and are shared to a large extent across modalities. Across 18 language, vision-language, and omni-modal checkpoints, omni-modal models largely preserve the retrieval heads of their backbones. Ablating these heads eliminates nearly all audio-visual retrieval. The heads shared across modalities make up fewer than 2\% of all heads, yet ablating them also degrades document understanding, spatial perception, and visual mathematics. Building on these findings, we turn retrieval heads into a target of optimization by training their attention with a ranking objective. This raises the page Recall@1 of Qwen3-VL-8B on MMDocIR by 6.03 points and improves document retrieval across three model families. Functional attention heads can thus serve both as a lens on multimodal models and as a target for improving them.

</details>


### [158] [Many Perturbations, Shared Failure Dynamics: Tracing Direction-Sensitive Perturbation Influence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32309)
*Zhibo Hu,Chen Wang,Yanfeng Shu,Liming Dong,Hye-young Paik,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“方向敏感、以读出为参照”的语义脆弱性框架，将语义保持改写导致的失败分解为决策储备、表征变换与响应对齐三类互补描述，并证明方向（orientation）在标量几何之外携带因果信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释一个反直觉现象：语义保持的改写能让正确答案变为失败，而更大的内部表征变化却不影响答案。作者认为现有基于标量传输的几何解释不足，需要引入方向性与读出关系的视角来刻画“异质语言变化可以共享几何/读出关系，却不共享与输入无关的失败方向”。

Method: （1）构造精确的纵向—横向（longitudinal-transverse）分解，识别被标量传输丢弃的方向信息；（2）建立紧凑的“储备—传输”（reserve-and-transmission）模型，在 Qwen3-32B、Gemma 4 31B 的留出问题族与样本上检验预测，并迁移至 ReClor；（3）匹配干预实验，检验方向相对标量几何的附加因果效应；（4）在自然失败改写上做注意力组件检验（使用不相交的原始样本）与答案顺序控制，对比自然配对方向与随机方向、同答案字母供体方向；（5）选项顺序置换检验失败稳健性；（6）数值路径积分评估有限补丁近似误差；（7）固定 CoT 线索研究，检验读出关联的跨域迁移与有符号效应预测。

Result: 储备—传输模型能跨留出族与样本预测失败，向 ReClor 的迁移有效但依赖参数化；匹配干预显示方向携带标量几何之外的因果信息；注意力组件与顺序控制支持自然配对方向优于随机及同字母供体方向；但失败很少跨选项顺序持续存在，且当前顺序的纠错依赖重算的干净参照；数值路径积分降低但不消除固定上下文下的有限补丁近似误差，与自然改写效应仍有较大差距；固定 CoT 研究中读出关联可跨域迁移，但正均值因果准则不成立，有符号效应预测仅为事后分析。

Conclusion: 结果支持储备、表征变换与响应之间的共享关系，并将局部因果敏感性同对自然失败的完整解释区分开来；语义脆弱性应被理解为方向敏感且依赖读出与上下文的现象，而非单一几何量所能刻画。

Abstract: Why can a task-preserving rewrite turn a correct answer into a failure while a larger internal change leaves the answer intact? We develop a direction-sensitive, readout-relative account of semantic fragility: heterogeneous language changes can share geometric and readout relationships without sharing an input-independent failure direction. Clean decision reserve, representation transformation, and response alignment provide complementary descriptions of this process. An exact longitudinal-transverse decomposition identifies directional information discarded by scalar transmission. A compact reserve-and-transmission model predicts failures across held-out families and items in Qwen3-32B and Gemma 4 31B, with useful but parameterization-dependent transfer to ReClor. Matched interventions show that orientation carries causal information beyond scalar geometry. Among naturally failed rewrites, attention-component tests on disjoint original items and subsequent answer-order controls favor the natural paired direction over tested random and same-answer-letter donor directions. Yet failures rarely persist across option orders, and current-order corrections use recomputed clean references. Shared predictive regularity thus coexists with context-dependent directional effects. Numerical path integration reduces, but does not eliminate, finite-patch approximation error within a fixed context; a larger discrepancy from the natural rewrite effect remains. A complementary fixed-CoT cue study extends this account to influence unacknowledged in recorded reasoning: readout associations transfer across domains, although the positive-mean causal criterion fails and signed-effect prediction remains post hoc. Together, these results support shared relations among reserve, transformation, and response while distinguishing local causal sensitivity from a complete explanation of natural failure.

</details>


### [159] [DraMaze: A Multi-Agent Pipeline with Future-Aware Memory for Diverse Microdrama Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32002)
*Siyang He,Qiqi Wang,Fan Liu,Yuncheng Huang,Pengfei Xu,Xi Chen,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多智能体流程 DraMaze，用桥段记忆引导的演化树搜索与变异探索多样因果路径，并构建反向叙事依赖树组织历史记忆，配合新基准 MiDramaBench，显著降低长剧本的因果模式重复并提升逻辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇微短剧剧本生成需兼顾情节多样性与长程叙事连贯性；观察发现随生成推进，候选续写越来越集中于少数叙事机制，而常规检索会加剧套路复用、忽略对未来情节至关重要的历史信息。

Method: DraMaze 为多智能体流水线：通过桥段（trope）记忆引导的演化式树搜索与变异探索多样因果路径；构建 Backward Narrative Dependency Tree，依据未来叙事目标组织、保留与检索历史记忆；并提出中英双语基准 MiDramaBench 及衡量叙事重复与多样性的因果模式指标。

Result: 在三个骨干 LLM 上均取得一致提升；对最长 60 集、约 6 万词的剧本，因果模式重复降低 32.1%，Logic 得分 82.5，较最佳基线高 8.5 分。

Conclusion: 以未来目标为导向的历史记忆组织与桥段引导的多样化搜索能有效缓解长剧本生成中的套路收敛问题，兼顾逻辑性与创造性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of microdramas has increased demand for high-quality script production. Long-form microdrama script generation with LLMs requires both diverse plot progression and long-range narrative coherence. Our observations reveal that candidate continuations increasingly concentrate on fewer narrative mechanisms as generation progresses, while conventional retrieval may reinforce trope reuse and overlook historical information essential to future plots. To address these challenges, we propose DraMaze, a multi-agent pipeline that explores diverse causal paths through trope memory-guided evolutionary tree search and mutation. It further constructs a Backward Narrative Dependency Tree to organize, retain, and retrieve historical memories based on future narrative goals. We also introduce MiDramaBench, a bilingual benchmark for evaluating script logic and creative quality across different script lengths, together with causal-pattern metrics for narrative repetition and diversity. Experiments show consistent improvements across three backbone LLMs. For 60-episode scripts of up to 60k words, DraMaze reduces causal-pattern repetition by 32.1% and achieves a Logic score of 82.5, outperforming the best-performing baseline by 8.5 points.

</details>


### [160] [Which Answer Did the Seventeen-Year-Old Write? A Behavioral Case Study of Author-Age Misattribution, Miscalibrated Confidence, and Non-Corrective Self-Explanation in Six Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31946)
*Brianne Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对六款前沿语言模型进行结构化案例研究，发现它们在"根据文本推断作者年龄与性别"任务上系统性失败：三个模型准确率降至随机水平却报告73–88%的置信度，置信度与正确性无相关甚至负相关，并表现出刻板化归因与事后自省脱节的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者观察到，三名年龄分别为17、28、45岁的学生手写回答同一道"什么是幸福？你幸福吗？"的题目时，17岁答句措辞谨慎、句式正式，而多数前沿模型却自信地将其判为成年人。作者希望以受控的小样本案例研究，隔离出"语言风格刻板归因"这一具体机制，而非估计总体偏见比例。

Method: 采用结构化案例研究（three-item、单一主题、单一语言），覆盖 Claude Fable 5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.5、GPT-4o (2024)、DeepSeek V4 六个模型；共运行1,081次API会话，在六个操纵"提问框架、信息披露格式、受众意识指令"的实验条件下对3,225条回答打分；任务包括年龄—性别标签匹配（三选一）以及开放式年龄估计。

Result: 六个模型中三个在匹配任务上处于或接近随机水平（1/3），却报告73–88%的平均置信度；最年幼作者的回答最高有90%的试验被误判为成年人。移除多选支架后，开放式估计下三个模型从未落在真实年龄±3岁内。机构化框架（"社区学院生物学期末"）显著降低最佳模型的准确率，中性标签则无此影响。单一受众意识指令使一个模型提升21个百分点、另一个达到上限，但对三个最弱模型无可检测影响。六个模型中五个的自信程度与正确性不相关或负相关。揭示真实答案后，某模型在114条解释中有68条点明自身的刻板化，但它在盲测中准确率最低、置信度最高。

Conclusion: 模型对作者年龄/性别的推断存在稳定的刻板化机制，且其口头置信度不能作为判断质量的可靠信号；事后"自省式诊断"与实际判断质量脱节，说明可解释性的表述不等于校准。由于样本量小、单话题单语言，结论定位于机制隔离而非偏见率估计；数据、代码与原始输出已公开。

Abstract: Three students answered the same exam prompt by hand: "What is happiness? Are you happy?" Their ages, recorded at collection, are 17, 28, and 45. The 17-year-old's answer is hedged and formally phrased. Asked to match each answer to an age and gender label, most frontier language models confidently assign it to an adult. This paper reports a structured case study of that failure across six models: Claude Fable 5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.5, GPT-4o (2024), and DeepSeek V4. We ran 1,081 API sessions and scored 3,225 responses across six experimental conditions that manipulate framing, disclosure format, and audience-awareness instruction. Three of six models perform at or near chance (1/3) on the matching task while reporting 73-88% mean confidence. The youngest writer's answer is misattributed to an adult in up to 90% of trials. Removing the multiple-choice scaffold collapses accuracy further: three models never place within three years of the true age once open-ended estimation is required. Institutional framing ("a community-college biology final") significantly degrades the best-performing model's accuracy; a neutral label does not. A single audience-awareness instruction significantly improves one model (+21 points) and moves another to ceiling, with no detectable effect on the three weakest models. Stated confidence is uncorrelated or inversely correlated with correctness for five of six models. After ground truth is revealed, one model names its own stereotyping in 68 of 114 explanations, yet shows the lowest accuracy and highest confidence of any model under blind presentation: articulate retrospective diagnosis that does not track the quality of its judgments. This is a three-item, single-topic, single-language case study meant to isolate a mechanism, not to estimate a population bias rate. Data, code, and raw model outputs are publicly released.

</details>


### [161] [LAMB: A Modern Contextual Language Model for Latin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31723)
*Aidan McCarthy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出拉丁语编码器 LAMB（基于 ModernBERT，两阶段预训练，74B+ 拉丁词元，形态学感知掩码），并发布 Acervus（24B 词元，含 760K+ 经 ByT5 修复的严重损坏 OCR 文本）与 Corpus Trinum（首个拉丁语语义三元组数据集，1 亿+ 强弱三元组）；在 5 任务 17 子任务基准上，LAMB 在 16/17 子任务上优于所有对比模型，第 17 项仅差 SOTA 0.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁语缺乏专用高质量编码器、大规模清洗语料与语义相似度监督资源；作者希望构建面向拉丁语的 ModernBERT 风格编码器，并系统刻画预训练与微调各因素对下游性能的影响。

Method: (1) 基于 ModernBERT 架构、以形态学信息引导的掩码策略两阶段预训练 LAMB（>74B 拉丁词元）；(2) 构建 Acervus（24B WordPiece 词元，含 760K+ 由 ByT5 修复的严重损坏 OCR 样本）与 Corpus Trinum（1 亿+ 拉丁语语义三元组）；(3) 构建 5 任务、17 子任务评测矩阵，对 15 个预训练编码器、6 个 LAMB 检查点及一个掩码消融模型，采用多种微调配置与子词聚合方式做系统评测与排序。

Result: LAMB 在 17 个子任务中的 16 个上超过所有被评测模型，第 17 个子任务与 SOTA 差距在 0.8 个百分点以内；分析揭示了拉丁语开类词在训练末期衰减阶段的独特习得动态，并表明对某些任务类别而言，微调配置选择对下游分数的影响可大于增加预训练词元。

Conclusion: LAMB 确立了拉丁语编码器的新基准，配套语料与三元组数据集为拉丁语语义相似度任务提供了开放基础；同时说明分词器行为、句法结构、微调配置、预训练阶段与子词聚合均显著影响性能，实践中需将预训练规模与微调配置协同优化。

Abstract: This article introduces Latin Modern BERT (LAMB), an encoder for the Latin language based on the ModernBERT architecture, pretrained in two phases on more than 74B Latin tokens using a novel morphologically informed masking regimen. We also present two corpora for the Latin language. Acervus contains 24B BERT WordPiece tokens, including over 760K samples from heavily damaged OCR transcriptions, repaired by a ByT5 model trained for Latin text repair. The second dataset, Corpus Trinum, contains a mixture of over 100M strong and weak Latin language semantic triplets. Corpus Trinum is the first Latin semantic triplet dataset, opening the door for Latin language models to be adapted to general sentence semantic similarity tasks. We construct an experimental grid using five tasks and seventeen subtasks, applying a variety of finetuning configurations and subword aggregation techniques for 15 pretrained encoders, 6 LAMB training checkpoints, and an ablation model designed to quantify the impact of different masking regimens. Using this benchmark, we rank Latin encoders and find that LAMB outperforms all other evaluated models on 16 of 17 subtasks, coming within 0.8 percentage points of state of the art on the seventeenth. We provide additional analyses relating Latin encoder performance to tokenizer behavior, syntactic structure, finetuning configuration, pretraining state, subword aggregation, and variation in source texts and domains. In these, we identify unique acquisition dynamics for Latin open class words during terminal decay phases, and establish that for some task categories, finetuning configuration selection can have a greater effect on downstream scores than additional pretraining tokens.

</details>


### [162] [Scaling cross-situational word learning on children's naturalistic experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31842)
*Michael C. Frank,Alvin W. M. Tan,Khai Loong Aw,Robert Z. Sparks,Victor Moreno,Grace Keene-Lee,Meesha R. Ryan,Tarun Sepuri,Jane Yang,Virginia A. Marchman,Daniel L. K. Yamins,Bria Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在BabyView语料（48名儿童、2441小时纵向自我中心视频）上训练跨情境学习模型，检验早期词汇习得中统计学习的作用及其对视觉表征质量的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨情境学习被提出作为儿童学习具体词汇的机制，但此前仅在小型数据集上得到概念验证；同时视觉模型在自我中心视频上难以学到稳健表征，因此需要明确：跨情境学习效果在多大程度上依赖视觉表征质量，以及该方法能否扩展到更大规模、更多样的发展性数据。

Method: 使用BabyView语料（2441小时、48名儿童的纵向自我中心视频）训练跨情境词—物映射模型，并对比两种视觉表征来源：仅从发展性视频中学到的表征 vs. 互联网预训练表征；同时分析学习信号在话语中的分布以及模型习得的词汇语义结构。

Result: 模型能够学到物体—标签映射，但使用互联网预训练视觉表征时性能远高于仅用发展性视频学习的表征；学习信号主要由少数描述可见指称物的话语承载；模型学到的词汇表征呈现可识别的人类式语义结构，但其随训练经验的性能轨迹与儿童显著不同。

Conclusion: 跨情境学习是早期名词词汇习得的可行策略，但构建发展上合理的模型需要解决如何从自然情境数据中学习高质量视觉表征的问题。

Abstract: How do children learn the meanings of the simple, concrete words that form a majority of their early vocabulary? Statistical information about many word meanings can be recovered from the gradual accumulation of information about the co-occurrence of words with their referents, a proposal known as ``cross-situational learning''. Contrastive vision--language models allow this learning strategy to be tested on egocentric video data capturing children's early real-life experiences with visual and linguistic inputs. Initial successes of this method on small datasets provide a proof-of-concept, but vision models struggle to learn robust visual representations when trained on these same egocentric videos. How dependent is cross-situational word learning performance on the quality of the model's visual representations? And can cross-situational models scale to larger and more diverse datasets? To address these questions, we trained models on the BabyView corpus, including 2,441 hours of longitudinal egocentric video from 48 children. Models trained on this dataset learned object--label mappings, but performance was far higher when they used internet-pretrained visual representations as opposed to when visual representations were learned solely from the developmental video data. Further, the majority of the learning signal was carried by a small proportion of utterances that described a visible referent. Models learned lexical representations that showed recognizable human-like semantic structure, but model performance showed substantially different trajectories over training experiences than those observed in children. Cross-situational learning is a viable word learning strategy for early noun vocabulary, but constructing developmentally plausible models will require understanding how to learn high-quality visual representations from naturalistic data.

</details>


### [163] [Shorter Is Not Cheaper: A Controlled Comparison of Declarative Animation Specifications and Free-Form Code for LLM-Generated Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32129)
*Jiantang Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过预注册的对照实验检验"声明式动画格式比自由形式HTML/JavaScript更便宜、更快、更可靠"这一假设，发现该假设在推理模型上并不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天助手越来越多地用交互动画解释概念性内容，生成方式分为自由形式HTML/JavaScript与声明式规范（由固定运行时播放）两类。业界普遍预期声明式路线因输出更短、受限更强而更便宜、更快、更可靠，但这一预期缺乏受控实证检验。

Method: 提出时间索引的声明式动画格式ExplainSpec及使生成解释可编程检查的"观察契约"；使用经校验、与节奏无关的检查器对五个领域20个解释请求评分。假设与分析方案在运行前冻结。三个Claude模型共360个任务，追加实验用Qwen3-14B（思考开/关）共240个任务，每个请求均以两种格式作答。

Result: 各模型的规范格式答案token（不含推理）缩短25-48%，但开启推理时仅最大Claude模型的总输出显著下降，且无模型显著更快。原因各异：Claude模型写规范前推理大增（两个较大模型从15-20%升至85-88%的生成占比）；Qwen3-14B两格式推理量相当（预注册的偏移检验不成立），短答案被两倍多长的推理链抵消。可靠性亦无优势：自由形式页面经一轮修复后95-100%有效；Qwen3-14B仅33%生成有效规范，全部语义测试通过率17%（HTML/JavaScript为62%）。

Conclusion: 对推理模型而言，仅靠更短的输出格式并不能使生成更便宜、更快或更可靠，且上下文内习得的格式反而可能损害可靠性。

Abstract: Chat assistants increasingly answer conceptual questions with interactive, animated explanations, generated either as free-form HTML/JavaScript or as declarative specifications that a fixed runtime plays. The declarative route is widely expected to be cheaper, faster and more reliable, because its outputs are shorter and constrained. We test this expectation in controlled comparisons whose hypotheses and analysis plans were frozen before the runs. We designed ExplainSpec, a time-indexed declarative animation format, and an observation contract that makes every generated explanation programmatically inspectable; validated, pacing-agnostic checkers score 20 explanation requests from five domains. Three Claude models (360 jobs) and, in a follow-up, the open-weight Qwen3-14B with thinking enabled and disabled (240 jobs) answered every request in both formats. For every model the specifications were 25-48% shorter in answer tokens (output excluding reasoning), yet with reasoning enabled total output fell significantly only for the largest Claude model and no model generated significantly faster. The reasons differed. The Claude models reasoned far more before writing specifications (exploratory; for the two larger models, from 15-20% to 85-88% of generations). Qwen3-14B reasoned about as much in both formats (a pre-registered test of this shift was not supported), and its shorter answers saved little against a reasoning trace more than twice as long. Nor were specifications more reliable: free-form pages were valid after one repair round in 95-100% of jobs for every model, whereas Qwen3-14B produced valid specifications in only 33% of jobs and passed all semantic tests in 17% (HTML/JavaScript: 62%). For reasoning models, a shorter format alone therefore does not make generation cheaper, faster or more reliable, and a format learned in context can cost reliability.

</details>


### [164] [Semantically Rich, Metrically Affect-Blind: A Geometric Blind Spot in Modern Text Embedders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32122)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现代文本嵌入器存在“系统性能解离”：情感可从嵌入中高精度线性解码，但在余弦相似度度量中几乎不可见；作者提出属性对齐差距诊断，并用小型监督对比头(SISE)在冻结编码器上快速注入情感，显著提升探针精度、检索MAP与极性间隔，但牺牲STS-B性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索、聚类、去重与RAG均依赖嵌入度量而非训练好的探针。若嵌入中可解码的属性在度量中缺失，系统将对编码器实际包含的信息“视而不见”。作者希望系统刻画这一解离并给出可行的低成本修复方案。

Method: 对11个2021–2026年的冻结编码器（含4B的Qwen3-Embedding-4B）做线性探针与余弦相似度极性间隔对比；用Jina-v5的任务条件化控制验证原因（预训练目标 vs. 提示伪影）；提出SISE：在冻结编码器上训练0.13M–0.69M参数、128维的监督对比头，CPU秒级完成；并评估128-d头与拼接融合变体对STS-B的影响。

Result: 探针极性准确率最高91.3%，而余弦相似度的极性间隔仅0.003–0.064；Qwen3-Embedding-4B探针91.3%但检索MAP仅64.3。任务条件化仅恢复约一半注入所得MAP增益（63.2→73.8 vs. 85.1），对齐差距基本不变。SISE使2022年后各编码器探针精度最多+5.8点、MAP最多+26.0、极性间隔提升7–61倍；代价为STS-B下降38–41点（128-d头）或2.4–12.0点（融合变体）。带头的0.6B/128维编码器MAP 91.8，优于带头4B的90.3。

Conclusion: 嵌入的信息含量与其度量几何之间存在系统性错配，根源更可能是预训练目标而非提示方式；SISE以极小参数代价有效对齐属性与度量，属于任务特化而非免费增益。作者发布CPU可复现流程与“属性对齐差距”诊断工具。

Abstract: We report a systematic dissociation in modern text embedders: sentiment is almost perfectly decodable from their embeddings, yet almost entirely absent from their metric. Across eleven frozen encoders spanning 2021 to 2026, including 2026-generation models and the 4B-parameter LLM embedder Qwen3-Embedding-4B, a linear probe recovers sentence polarity at up to 91.3% accuracy while cosine similarity separates polarity by a margin of only 0.003-0.064. Qwen3-Embedding-4B, the strongest probe in the panel, reaches 91.3% probe accuracy but only 64.3 retrieval MAP. Because retrieval, clustering, deduplication, and retrieval-augmented generation operate on the metric rather than on a trained probe, these systems are effectively blind to information their encoder demonstrably contains. A task-conditioning control suggests this reflects the pretraining objective rather than a prompting artifact: conditioning Jina-v5 on its classification task rather than its retrieval task recovers only about half of the MAP gain that injection achieves on the same weights (63.2 to 73.8, versus 85.1), and leaves the alignment gap essentially unchanged. Sentiment-Injected Sentence Embeddings (SISE) train a small (0.13M-0.69M-parameter) supervised-contrastive head on a frozen encoder in seconds on a CPU, using the labelled training set. The head improves every encoder released since 2022 on full-data probe accuracy (up to +5.8 points), retrieval MAP (up to +26.0) and polarity margin (7-61x). This is a specialization, not a free gain: the compact 128-d head loses 38-41 Spearman points on STS-B, while a fused variant that concatenates it with the base embedding loses 2.4-12.0. With the head, a 0.6B encoder at 128 dimensions matches a 4B encoder given the same head (91.8 vs. 90.3 MAP). We release a CPU-reproducible pipeline and a reusable diagnostic, the Attribute Alignment Gap.

</details>


### [165] [TaxoSAE: Rethinking Sparse Autoencoders through Taxonomic Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31894)
*Xinyu Guo,Minglai Yang,Clayton T. Morrison,Eduardo Blanco,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TaxoSAE将is-a层级信息显式编码进稀疏自编码器特征空间，实现按分类学的读出与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE将每个特征视为独立单元，而现实知识常按分类学组织；导致读出缺乏分类一致性、控制粒度无法限定编辑范围。

Method: 用SAE堆栈对应分类学各层级，并引入分类对齐损失，使某概念在其任一上位/下位关系触发时保持激活（概念在其任一下位词激活时即激活）。

Result: 在两个事实QA分类学（传记关系、动物）上：未见抽象概念上位词读出+31.3分；swap成功率+25.5分；抑制某概念仅影响其自身答案（0-2%）而其他概念保持91-100%；通过上位词读出未见概念最高+37.9分。

Conclusion: 显式建模is-a分类结构可提升SAE读出的准确性与控制精度，并支持借助上位词推断未见概念。

Abstract: Recent Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) decompose the representations of large language models (LLMs) into interpretable features, enabling precise readout and control of encoded knowledge. However, existing SAEs treat each feature as a standalone unit, whereas real-world knowledge is often taxonomically organized. As a result, readout carries no guarantee of taxonomic consistency, and control lacks the granularity to specify the scope of an edit. We propose TaxoSAE, which explicitly encodes taxonomical information, built on is-a (hypernym-hyponym) relations, into the SAE feature space. TaxoSAE mirrors a taxonomy with a stack of SAEs, one per taxonomy level, and introduces a taxonomic alignment loss that trains each concept to be active whenever one of its hyponyms is. On two factual QA taxonomies, one over biographical relations and one over animals, TaxoSAE enables (1) more accurate readout through the taxonomy with concentrated activations, e.g., +31.3 points in reading out the hypernyms of abstract concepts never queried in training; (2) more precise control, with +25.5 points in swap success, while suppressing a concept removes only its own answers (0-2% accuracy vs. 91-100% on other concepts); and, more importantly, (3) readout of unseen concepts through their hypernyms, with up to +37.9 points in hypernym readout accuracy.

</details>


### [166] [Profile-Driven User Simulation via Reinforcement Learning with Hierarchical Rewards for Task-Oriented Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32435)
*Juan Li,Fangshu Chang,Xinyi Jiang,Ling Huang,Yunzhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProfileSim，一个面向金融营销外呼场景的画像驱动用户模拟框架，联合建模用户画像（人口统计+大五人格）与业务画像（从真实通话日志反推意图），并通过GRPO层次化奖励微调，生成高口语自然度、高画像遵循度的对话数据，用于意图识别训练、坐席评估与冷启动部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融营销等高价值场景构建任务型对话（TOD）系统需大量领域对话数据，但隐私法规、标注成本与专家人力使其难以获取。现有用户模拟方法面向通用领域，存在两点不足：一是缺乏对领域特定业务流程与合规约束的建模，二是未能捕捉语音交互的口语化特征。

Method: ProfileSim采用画像驱动框架：联合建模用户画像（人口统计特征与大五人格）和业务画像（意图由真实通话日志逆向工程得到），从而对模拟行为实现细粒度控制；基座模型先在真实语音对话数据上微调，再用GRPO结合对话级与话语级的层次化奖励进行优化。配套客服坐席智能体集成SOP约束与RAG。

Result: 在所有评测的用户生成器中，ProfileSim取得最高的口语自然度、最强的用户画像遵循度，并在长尾意图上使坐席得分最低；在业务合规性相当的情况下，其生成的用户话语显著更短。

Conclusion: ProfileSim生成的对话数据可支撑意图识别训练、坐席评估与快速冷启动部署，为高价值领域TOD系统缓解数据稀缺问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Building Task-Oriented Dialogue (TOD) systems for high-value scenarios such as financial marketing requires large volumes of domain-specific dialogue data, which privacy regulations, annotation costs, and the need for expert labor make hard to obtain. User simulation offers a remedy, but existing methods target general-domain dialogue and exhibit two key limitations: insufficient modeling of domain-specific business processes and compliance constraints, and inadequate capture of the colloquial, spoken-language character of voice interactions. We propose ProfileSim, a profile-driven user simulation framework for financial marketing outbound calls. It jointly models user profiles---demographics and Big Five personality traits---and business profiles---intents reverse-engineered from real call logs---enabling fine-grained control over simulated behavior. The base model is fine-tuned on real voice dialogue data and refined by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with hierarchical rewards at the dialogue and utterance levels. Paired with a customer service agent integrating Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) constraints and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), ProfileSim achieves the highest colloquial naturalness, the strongest user profile adherence and the lowest agent score on long-tail intents among all evaluated user generators, while producing substantially shorter user utterances at comparable business compliance. The generated dialogue data is designed to support intent recognition training, agent evaluation, and rapid cold-start deployment.

</details>


### [167] [Can LLM Annotators Match Human Consistency? Benchmarking Human Agreement and Optimizing Multi-Agent with Contrastive Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32415)
*Xiaopeng Ke,Zhixin Wang,Xuebo Liu,Xinyu Ma,Xing Zheng,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向真实标注场景的多标注者基准ANT-A及多智能体标注框架ANT-OR，通过对比反馈蒸馏决策规则优化提示，显著提升与人类标注分布的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准与真实标注实践脱节：真实标注依赖冗长且任务特定的标注指南，且不同标注者（尤其在边界案例上）会产生分歧但合理的判断，单标签数据无法反映这种分歧；现有单智能体与工作流式标注系统也难以适应复杂、持续演化的指南。

Method: 构建ANT-A基准，涵盖7个跨领域跨模态数据集，配备任务特定指南，每个样本有5个独立人工标签，并采用Fleiss kappa进行分布感知评测；提出ANT-OR多智能体标注框架，从正负反馈中提炼对比性决策规则以迭代精炼提示。

Result: ANT-OR在agreement@>=2准确率上较最强基线提升4.5个百分点，并在Fleiss kappa指标上取得与人类标注分布最高的一致性（差距在2个点以内）。

Conclusion: 面向真实标注实践、以标注分布一致性为评测目标，可有效提升LLM标注系统的适应性与可靠性；代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: LLM-based annotation has advanced rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain misaligned with real-world practice. Actual annotation often involves lengthy, task-specific guidelines, and different annotators may produce divergent but reasonable judgments, especially on boundary cases, where single-labeled dataset fail to reflect this disagreement. This gap also limits existing single-agent and workflow-based annotation systems, which often lack adaptability to complex, evolving guidelines. To bridge this gap, we introduce ANT-A Benchmark, a real-world multi-annotator benchmark spanning 7 datasets across domains and modalities, with task-specific guidelines and 5 independent human labels per instance. ANT-A enables a distribution-aware evaluation, using Fleiss kappa to assess how closely model annotations align with human labeling distributions. We further propose ANT-OR, a multi-agent annotation framework that refines prompts by distilling contrastive decision rules from positive and negative feedback. Experiments show that ANT-OR improves agreement@>=2 accuracy by 4.5 points over the strongest baseline and achieves the highest alignment with human annotator distributions as measured by Fleiss kappa within 2 points. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Krueske/ANT-OR.

</details>


### [168] [DIAL-S2ST: Learning Latency Control for Simultaneous Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32214)
*Jeongsoo Choi,Junseok Ahn,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DIAL-S2ST，一种面向同声语音翻译（SimulS2ST）的推理时延迟控制训练方法，通过自蒸馏复用预训练翻译能力并结合LoRA实现可调延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同声语音翻译模型在推理时的延迟（lookahead）难以灵活调节；传统方法需要配对目标数据或奖励计算，成本高且受"模仿差距"（imitation gap）影响。

Method: 基于lookahead预测做自蒸馏，复用预训练翻译能力；抑制大文本蒸馏损失的影响以缓解模仿差距，从而无需配对目标数据或奖励即可为更早发射提供监督；跨多个lookahead尺度联合训练单个LoRA适配器，并配以匹配的引导，使学习到的时序在推理时可调。

Result: 在Hibiki上法→英翻译实验中，仅需10小时源语音即可完成适配，实现灵活的延迟控制并获得良好的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低数据成本为同声语音翻译模型赋予推理时可调的延迟控制能力，兼具数据高效性与实用性。

Abstract: We propose DIAL-S2ST, a post-training method for learning inference-time latency control in simultaneous speech-to-speech translation models. Our approach reuses pretrained translation capabilities through self-distillation from lookahead predictions, while limiting the influence of large text distillation losses to account for the imitation gap. This provides supervision for earlier emission without paired target data or reward computation. To make the learned timing adjustable at inference, we jointly train a single LoRA adapter across scales with matched lookahead guidance. Experiments on French-to-English translation with Hibiki demonstrate that only 10 hours of source speech enable this adaptation, providing flexible latency control with a favorable quality-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [169] [Disentangling Question, Response, and Verifier Languages in Multilingual P(True) Self-Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32144)
*Jewon Yeom,Jaewon Sok,Seonghyeon Park,Jeongjae Park,Hwiyeong Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在多语言置信度评估中需同时报告问题语言、回答语言与验证指令语言，并验证评分器，否则结果不可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型自评答案正确性时涉及三种语言（问题语言、被要求的回答语言、验证指令语言），但现有多语言置信度评估很少将三者分离，导致结果的可比性与结论稳健性存疑。

Method: 采用六语言因子设计，独立变化三种语言，在 LSQA、MMMLU、TriviaQA 上测试十个 3B–14B 开源模型；固定每个答案跨验证语言不变，用首 token 的 P(True) 探针测量置信度，并考察评分器（judge）的影响。

Result: 三种语言影响评估的不同环节：决定准确率的语言随基准而异；将回答语言与问题或验证语言对齐可提升区分度但不改善校准；仅验证语言一项即可使校准误差变化达 0.04，而准确率不变。评分器亦有影响：未经验证的判别器会将 MMMLU 英–印准确率差距从 0.198 压缩到 0.038。

Conclusion: 多语言置信度结果（包括模型排名）只有在三种语言全部报告且评分器经过验证时才具有可比性。

Abstract: When a language model is asked whether its own answer is correct, three languages are involved: that of the question, that requested for the answer, and that of the verifier instruction asking for the judgment. Multilingual confidence evaluations seldom separate the three. We vary them independently in a six-language factorial design with ten open-weight models (3B-14B) on LSQA, MMMLU and TriviaQA, holding each answer fixed across verifier languages and measuring confidence with a first-token P(True) probe. The three languages move different parts of the evaluation: which of them governs accuracy depends on the benchmark, matching the response language to the question or verifier language sharpens discrimination but not calibration, and the verifier language alone shifts calibration error by up to 0.04 without changing accuracy. The scorer matters as well: an unvalidated judge would have shrunk the MMMLU English-Hindi accuracy gap from 0.198 to 0.038. Multilingual confidence results, model rankings included, are therefore comparable only when all three languages are reported and the scorer is validated.

</details>


### [170] [The Language of Instruction: Genre and Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32982)
*James E. Dobson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文主张将指令微调语言模型理解为一种体裁——"指令-对话体裁"，它由生成模型与人类既往对话中的话语语言递归式地汇合而成，并通过指令微调、系统提示词以及特殊标记与角色轮次结构等标记语言形式加以实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以技术或认知视角讨论指令微调模型，缺乏从话语/体裁角度理解其语言组织方式的理论框架，本文旨在填补这一概念空白。

Method: 概念性理论论证：提出"指令-对话体裁"概念，将其界定为既往人机对话话语的递归性混合，并从指令微调、系统提示词（pretext）、以及含特殊标记与角色轮转结构的标记语言输入形式化三个层面分析该体裁的实例化机制。

Result: 论证表明，指令微调模型的语言行为可被系统地刻画为一种由对话话语递归沉淀而成、经系统提示词预设与角色化标记结构规约的体裁实践。

Conclusion: 应把指令微调语言模型视为体裁的实现而非单纯技术产物，这一视角为理解其话语特征与交互规范提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: This article argues that instruction-fine-tuned language models should be understood as implementing a genre, what is proposed as the instructional-conversational genre. This genre is an amalgamation of discursive language from prior conversations between generative models and humans recursively run through these massive neural networks. It is instantiated in applications through instruction fine-tuning, the inclusion of a pretext known as a system prompt, and the formalization of inputs in mark-up languages characterized by the use of special tokens and role assignment in turntaking structures.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive Frequency Threshold Byte Pair Encoding for Mixed Natural Language and Structured Symbolic Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32921)
*Pietro Di Nicco,Lucio La Cava,Andrea Tagarelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AdaBPE：通过基于长度与类型（通用/形式语言）的双重自适应频率阈值来改进超词级分词，使 LaTeX 等域内记号获得足够词表容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词（如 BPE）将空白视为固定语义边界，无法覆盖跨空格的多词表达与 LaTeX 结构化片段；近期超词分词虽放宽空白约束，但采用贪心频率驱动合并，忽视 token 长度与低资源领域语言的需求。

Method: 提出 Adaptive Frequency Threshold BPE（AdaBPE），基于自适应频繁模式挖掘：最小频率阈值随候选 token 的长度动态调整以防止偏袒高频短模式，并随 token 类型调整以区分通用语言与形式语言（以 LaTeX 为案例）模式，从而将词表容量合理分配给域内记号。

Result: 在覆盖 LaTeX 与自然语言的五个数据集上评估，AdaBPE 在编码效率与域内内容的下游语言模型性能上均取得一致提升，且不损害通用语言性能。

Conclusion: 长度与类型双重的自适应频率阈值可有效缓解超词分词中的贪心合并缺陷，在保持通用语言能力的同时提升领域特定语言的分词与建模效果。

Abstract: Modern language models rely on subwording tokenization to define the discrete units of input processing, typically using methods like Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) that treat whitespace as a fixed semantic boundary. This design is inherently limited, as many meaningful concepts---including multiword expressions and the structured environments of formal languages like LaTeX---frequently span across whitespaces. While recent superword tokenization methods attempt to address this by relaxing whitespace constraints, they often rely on greedy frequency-driven merging that fails to account for token length or the specific needs of underrepresented domain-specific languages. In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Frequency Threshold Byte Pair Encoding (AdaBPE), a new tokenization method that leverages an adaptive frequent-pattern mining approach. AdaBPE features a twofold dynamicity: it adapts minimum frequency thresholds based on both the length of a candidate token, to prevent favoring frequent short patterns, and its type, to distinguish between general and formal language patterns, using LaTeX as case in point. This allows the model to efficiently allocate vocabulary capacity to domain-specific notations, such as LaTeX, even when they represent a small fraction of the training data. We evaluate AdaBPE across five datasets spanning LaTeX and natural language, showing consistent gains in encoding efficiency and downstream language-model performance on domain-specific content without compromising general-language performance.

</details>


### [172] [Communicating with the Entire Population: Machine Translation of Emergency Alerts and Warnings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32842)
*Jen Wilson,Vipasha Bansal,William D. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过全国性调查了解应急管理者的工作流程、观点与挑战，并据此评估商用翻译工具在应急响应中的实用性与安全使用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机期间需与全部受影响人群沟通，包括不讲当地主流语言者；商用翻译工具虽可作为易得方案、在无其他资源时提供生命线，但应急管理者对其输出质量与充分性缺乏了解。

Method: 对应急管理者开展全国性问卷/调查，刻画其现有工作流程、视角与操作挑战；随后以这些需求为基准，对商用翻译工具进行评测。

Result: 揭示了商用翻译工具相对于应急管理者实际需求的效用状况，并给出关于如何在应急响应中安全使用这些工具的实践性指导。

Conclusion: 商用翻译工具可作为应急沟通的辅助手段，但需在明确其质量与适用边界的前提下审慎使用；研究为应急管理者提供了何时、如何安全使用的实践建议。

Abstract: It is essential that we communicate with the entirety of an affected population during a crisis, including those who do not speak a region's dominant language(s). Commercial Translation tools offer a tempting solution to broaden access and can provide a lifeline when no other resources are available. However, Emergency response managers often operate in the dark regarding the quality and adequacy of these automated outputs. In this paper, we conduct a nationwide survey of emergency managers to understand their current workflows, perspectives, and operational challenges. We then evaluate commercial translation tools against these needs, shedding light on their utility and offering practical guidance on how and when these tools can be safely used in emergency response.

</details>


### [173] [Decoupling Patient Personas and Clinical Workflows: A Dual-Agent Approach to Realistic Medical Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32443)
*Qiyuan Li,Xiaomei Guo,Chao Gao,Haijiang Liu,Deyu Chen,Caicai Guo,Jinguang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CoRPS-Dial 双智能体可控框架，结合患者人设与指南对齐的临床流程规则生成预诊医疗对话，并通过事后质量控制提升合成数据的临床相关性与真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预诊医疗对话对采集患者信息至关重要，但受隐私与伦理限制难以大规模获取；现有基于 LLM 的合成方法依赖提示工程或刚性规则，难以兼顾临床相关性与真实患者多样性。

Method: CoRPS-Dial 为双智能体框架：人设驱动的患者智能体 + 流程引导的医生智能体交互生成对话，并引入事后质量控制（角色一致性、流程完整性、文本一致性）。

Result: 在冠心病场景下，合成对话与真实对话在信息覆盖度、临床合规性和对话自然度上的对齐程度提升；下游微调实验验证了合成数据对医疗对话任务的实用价值。

Conclusion: CoRPS-Dial 能有效实现临床有据且可控的医疗对话合成。

Abstract: Pre-consultation medical dialogues are essential for collecting comprehensive patient information, but are costly to obtain at scale due to privacy and ethical constraints. Existing LLM-based synthesis methods often rely on prompt engineering or rigid rules, limiting their ability to balance clinical relevance with realistic patient diversity. We propose CoRPS-Dial, a controllable dual-agent framework for pre-consultation medical dialogue synthesis that integrates patient personas with guideline-aligned clinical workflow rules. CoRPS-Dial generates dialogues through interactions between a persona-driven patient agent and a process-guided doctor agent, followed by post-hoc quality control for role consistency, workflow completeness, and textual consistency. Experiments on coronary heart disease demonstrate that CoRPS-Dial improves alignment between synthetic and real dialogues in information coverage, clinical compliance, and dialogue naturalness. Downstream fine-tuning experiments further confirm the utility of the generated synthetic data for medical dialogue tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of CoRPS-Dial for clinically grounded and controllable medical dialogue synthesis.

</details>


### [174] [Distributional Alignment of LLMs and Its Generalization Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31687)
*Nelson Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究LLM的分布对齐及其泛化性：LLM可在同领域未见问题上实现分布对齐与泛化，跨主题泛化依赖主题相关性，但泛化性质不可预测且不稳定，可能伴随能力退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于模拟和探测人类行为（如问卷调查），关键要求是输出分布与参考（人类）分布对齐，而从小训练数据泛化是实现分布对齐的障碍。

Method: 基于两个大规模基准，研究(1)如何实现LLM的分布对齐；(2)对齐后的LLM能否泛化到未见样本及不相关主题与任务。

Result: LLM确实能实现分布对齐并泛化到同领域的未见问题；跨主题泛化取决于主题间关系（如政治相关问题是机构信任相关）；对齐后LLM可能以出人意料的方式退化（如丧失创造力）。

Conclusion: 对齐LLM的泛化性质本质上不可预测且不稳定，需警惕分布对齐带来的意外能力损失。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate and probe human behavior, e.g., for surveys. For these applications, a key property is that outputs satisfy distributional alignment: to match its output distribution with some reference (in this case, human) distribution. An obstacle in achieving distributional alignment is generalizing from small training data. In this work, we study (1) how to distributionally align LLMs; and (2) how aligned LLMs might generalize to unseen samples and unrelated topics and tasks. Using two large-scale benchmarks, we find that LLMs can indeed achieve distributional alignment and generalize to unseen questions of the same domain. Cross-topic generalization depends on the relationship between topics (e.g., questions about politics are related to trust in institutions). However, aligned LLMs can degrade in surprising ways (e.g., lose their creative ability), suggesting that its generalization properties are inherently unpredictable and unstable.

</details>


### [175] [Rethinking Structured Generation: Can Graph-Based Reasoning Resolve Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31681)
*Ratun Rahman,Atit Pokharel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将结构化生成重新表述为歧义感知推理问题，用多有效学习与图表示候选选择替代单一确定性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化生成把输入到结构化输出视为确定性映射，并以单参考监督训练；但自然语言常存在多个结构不同却语义合理的解释，该假设导致歧义被压缩为单一预测，在欠定输入下输出不稳定、不一致。
