Current memory pipelines remain vulnerable at two stages: indexing errors can fragment repeated entities or merge distinct ones, and online retrieval often treats initial similarity scores as final even when the surrounding graph provides evidence for revising them. We introduce ActionRAG, a graph memory architecture that couples cautious indexing with query-time graph retroevaluation. Its indexing pipeline reconciles type labels and applies tiered entity resolution. Given a query, dual-channel entity and triple retrieval forms an initial evidence set and induces a bounded local graph. Query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion refines triple scores using local path structure. Seed protection, hub-penalized flux, and triple materialization preserve direct evidence while recovering missed connections. We evaluate ActionRAG on MemoryAgentBench (MAB), which tests accurate retrieval, conflict resolution, long-range understanding, and test-time learning. Across its eight deterministic-scoring columns, ActionRAG achieves 55.1 average accuracy, improving over the strongest retrieval-based memory baseline by 5.4 points. Under MAB's query-time cost accounting, mean synthesis cost stays below \$0.001 per query on the four reported cost columns, and the sum of separately measured retrieval-side p99 components is 440.7 ms on our consumer Apple Silicon setup.

</details>


### [243] [LGMT: A Tool for Testing the Logical Reasoning Reliability of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32627)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoke Fang,Zheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LGMT：基于一阶逻辑蜕变关系检测大模型推理不一致性的开源工具，在3,091个蜕变组上揭示六个主流LLM存在20.84%–29.44%的不一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为软件系统推理组件时，同一逻辑问题以等价形式重述可能给出矛盾答案；静态基准孤立评测查询，无法暴露此类不一致性。

Method: 提出LGMT工具：以premise-conclusion形式给出源测试用例，依据一阶逻辑构造逻辑等价的后续用例，检查模型在成对用例上的蕴含预测是否一致，无需ground-truth标签；内置20种蜕变关系，支持经Z3定理证明器验证的自定义关系，提供CLI与Web studio界面及无需API key的离线mock模式。

Result: 在3,091个蜕变组的内置基准上评测六个广泛使用的LLM，不一致率为20.84%至29.44%。

Conclusion: LGMT可自动化、无标签地检测LLM推理不一致性，工具已开源（GitHub）并提供演示视频。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as reasoning components in modern software systems, yet a model that answers a logical query correctly can contradict itself when the same problem is restated in an equivalent form. Static benchmarks evaluate queries in isolation and therefore fail to expose such inconsistencies. To address this limitation, we present LGMT, an open-source tool that detects reasoning inconsistencies through metamorphic relations grounded in first-order logic. Given a source test case in premise-conclusion form, LGMT generates logically equivalent follow-up cases and checks whether the model's entailment predictions agree across the pair, without ground-truth labels. The tool provides 20 built-in metamorphic relations, supports custom ones verified by the Z3 theorem prover, and runs from a CLI or a web studio, including an offline mock mode requiring no API key. Across six widely used LLMs on a bundled benchmark of 3,091 metamorphic groups, LGMT reveals inconsistency rates ranging from 20.84% to 29.44%. LGMT is publicly available on GitHub at https://github.com/MaxwelsDonc/lgmt-tool, with a demonstration video at https://youtu.be/gou4opptu1U.

</details>


### [244] [WiMoT: Replay-Free Continual Wi-Fi Sensing via Moment Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32660)
*Chunchun Yang,Yang Cao,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WiMoT，仅存各类特征均值与协方差而非样本，通过冻结特征基上的仿射变换搬运旧统计量，实现无样本回放的持续Wi-Fi感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续Wi-Fi感知需兼顾可塑性（学新房间/用户）与稳定性（保旧域），但隐私与存储限制使边缘节点无法保留CSI历史样本，回放方法受限。

Method: 以每类特征均值与协方差替代样本存储；将表示变化限制为冻结特征基上的仿射映射，并对存储统计量施加同一映射以避免陈旧；用监督对比学习学新域，用基于存储统计量的闭式损失保持旧类可分；以等域权重的高斯判别分类器重拟合，预测时无需域身份。

Result: 在3DO、WiMANS、Widar3.0上最终平均准确率94.02%、89.61%、95.16%，遗忘≤0.58pp；3DO与Widar3.0上新旧域平衡最佳，领先21个基线中最强的回放方法3.77与13.11pp；WiMANS上准确率+0.41pp且遗忘由2.78降至0.00pp；秩32因子化将矩内存压缩至6.4%，精度损失≤0.49pp。

Conclusion: 统计量搬运可在不存样本的前提下有效缓解灾难性遗忘，且经低秩因子化后满足边缘节点存储预算，为隐私受限场景的持续Wi-Fi感知提供可行方案。

Abstract: Continual Wi-Fi sensing must balance plasticity, learning new rooms or users, against stability, keeping earlier ones, yet nodes often cannot retain channel state information (CSI) for privacy and storage reasons. We propose WiMoT (Wi-Fi Moment Transport), which stores the per-class feature mean and covariance of every earlier domain instead of samples. When the representation changes, stored statistics become stale. WiMoT restricts the change to an affine map on a frozen feature basis and applies the same map to the statistics, so they stay valid without old samples. Supervised contrastive learning learns the new domain, a closed-form loss on the stored statistics keeps earlier classes separable, and a Gaussian discriminant classifier, refitted from the transported statistics with equal weight per domain, predicts without domain identity. On 3DO, WiMANS, and Widar3.0, WiMoT's average final accuracies are 94.02%, 89.61%, and 95.16% with at most 0.58 percentage points (pp) of forgetting. On 3DO and Widar3.0, it also has the highest balance of new- and earlier-domain accuracy and is 3.77 and 13.11 pp ahead of the strongest of 21 baselines, which uses replay, staying ahead across domain orders and label budgets. On WiMANS, it matches the strongest replay baseline in accuracy (+0.41 pp) while reducing forgetting from 2.78 to 0.00 pp, and has the highest six-order average accuracy. Because full covariances dominate the memory, storing them as rank-32 factors shrinks the moment memory to 6.4%, losing at most 0.49 pp of accuracy, within edge-node storage budgets.

</details>


### [245] [NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jangseon Park,Jason Kong,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NgramRL 利用强化学习训练中同一 prompt 的近期 rollout 历史构建逐 prompt 的 n-gram 草稿器进行投机解码，并配合自适应扇出预取器隐藏 CPU 开销，显著提升 rollout 吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练提升大模型推理能力，但自回归 rollout 生成占训练时间可超 70%。现有投机解码不适配：基于模型的草稿器占用稀缺 GPU 显存，且随策略演化而失配；无模型方法未显式利用跨 epoch 的重复性。

Method: 观察到同一 prompt 的近期 rollout 可作为高质量、自更新的草稿源，据此提出 NgramRL：用有限近期 rollout 历史构建逐 prompt n-gram 草稿器，无需辅助模型、草稿器训练或常驻显存的草稿状态，并由目标模型验证以保持 rollout 分布；另引入自适应扇出预取器预测验证结果 (j, b)（接受草稿长度 j 与目标续写 token b），配因果预算分配器将预取集中于高概率结果，未覆盖结果回退顺序查找且不改变已提交 token。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三类推理与结构化生成任务上，热缓存下每次目标模型前向提交 3.60–7.77 个 token，比 SuffixDecoding 最多高 1.99x、比 prompt-lookup decoding 高 7.0x；Spider 上候选预算 16 时预取器覆盖 Qwen3-8B 96.6% 的结果，rollout 吞吐较同步 NgramRL 提升 1.38x，接近 1.40x 重叠上限。

Conclusion: 利用 rollout 历史的逐 prompt n-gram 草稿配合验证机制，可在不增加显存与训练成本的前提下高效加速 RL rollout，预取机制进一步逼近重叠上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but its cost is dominated by autoregressive rollout generation, which can exceed 70% of training time. Existing speculative-decoding methods are poorly suited to this setting: model-based drafters consume scarce GPU memory and may become misaligned as the policy evolves, while existing model-free methods do not explicitly exploit repetition across training epochs. We observe that recent rollouts of the same prompt provide a high-quality, self-updating draft source. Based on this insight, we propose NgramRL, a per-prompt n-gram drafter constructed from bounded recent rollout history. NgramRL requires no auxiliary model, drafter training, or persistent GPU-resident drafter state, while target-model verification preserves the rollout distribution. To hide CPU cache lookup and scheduling, we further introduce an adaptive fan-out prefetcher that predicts likely verification outcomes (j, b), consisting of the accepted draft length j and target-emitted continuation token b. A causal budget allocator concentrates prefetches on high-probability outcomes, while uncovered outcomes fall back to sequential lookup without changing committed tokens. Across two model families and three reasoning and structured-generation workloads, NgramRL commits 3.60-7.77 tokens per target-model forward in the warm-cache regime, up to 1.99x more than SuffixDecoding and 7.0x more than prompt-lookup decoding. On Spider, the prefetcher covers 96.6% of Qwen3-8B outcomes at a candidate budget of 16 and improves measured rollout throughput by 1.38x over synchronous NgramRL, approaching its 1.40x overlap ceiling.

</details>


### [246] [From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869)
*Maxim Osovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出消息形成与理解的五层系统-活动组织框架，论证语言模型的上下文无法呈现言语形成的基础，这些基础由预先获得的图式、世界活动的系统表征及其中被承认为真的联系所确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM依赖上下文与训练权重生成文本，但二者本身均不能证明存在有意建构的系统性表征，因而缺少可检验言语形成基础的机制。

Method: 构建五层结构：（1）活动世界的系统表征；（2）从中切分出的具体情境；（3）情境的叙事描述；（4）消息的完整含义；（5）文本。理论来源为Shchedrovitskiy的系统方法论、Korzybski的结构微分与Mel'chuk的意义-文本理论。

Result: 明确情境的图式模型不等于关于它的故事；接收者通过共同建构并达成共识的表征与联合活动，从简略文本重建完整含义；反向过程则由完整含义经扩展描述、情境图式模型回溯至系统基础的重建。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为检验与调整消息基础、控制其转换后果提供具体的系统-活动内容组织方式；后续研究指向基于可重建系统基础的活动大图模型，以区别于从上下文生成文本。

Abstract: This paper proposes a five-level organization of message formation and understanding within thinking and activity in which ASI participates. Its central thesis is that a language model's context is fundamentally incapable of presenting the foundations of utterance formation for examination and verification. These foundations are established in advance by acquired schemas, systemic representations of the world of activity, and connections accepted as true within them. Neither context nor trained LLM weights, in themselves, attest to the presence of deliberately constructed systemic representations. The five levels distinguish systemic representations of the world of activity, a particular situation cut out from those representations, its narrative description, the full sense of a message, and text. A situation's schema-model is not identical to a story about it. A recipient reconstructs the full sense from an abbreviated text through jointly constructed and agreed-upon representations and joint activity. Reverse work proceeds from the full sense through an extended description and the situation's schema-model to the reconstruction of systemic foundations. The theoretical foundations draw on the systems methodology of G. P. Shchedrovitskiy, the structural differential of A. Korzybski, and the Meaning-Text theory of I. A. Mel'chuk. The contribution is a specific systemic-activity organization of content for examining and adjusting message foundations and controlling the consequences of their transformation. Further research concerns large graphical models of activity on reconstructible systemic foundations, distinct from text generation from context.

</details>


### [247] [A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863)
*Wenxin Chen,Kyra Gan,Mingyu Lu,Zilin Jing,Fei Wang,Hoifung Poon,Tristan Naumann,Peniel Argaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个支持动态治疗策略的纵向因果效应基础模型 PICLE-PFN，用先验数据拟合网络摊销估计成本，单次前向即可预测CAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果效应估计面临时变混杂与治疗-混杂反馈回路；现有方法需针对具体数据拟合nuisance模型，且多局限于预设治疗序列或预测时域，部署时计算耗时。作者希望用基础模型统一支持任意动态治疗策略，并免除任务特定训练与调参。

Method: PICLE-PFN 以in-context的历史-决策演示表示目标策略，为广泛类别的治疗策略提供统一接口；在由纵向结构因果模型生成的合成数据上预训练，从而跨数据集、策略与时域摊销因果效应估计；给定新观测数据与目标策略，单次前向传播直接输出条件平均潜在结果(CAPO)。

Result: 在合成与半合成纵向基准上，相比为每个数据集单独训练与调参的SOTA传统估计器，PICLE-PFN取得有竞争力的RMSE、显著更优的不确定性校准，且部署运行时间仅为其中一小部分。

Conclusion: 将纵向因果效应估计重构为in-context学习问题并以PFN进行摊销，可同时获得动态策略支持、免调参部署与良好校准性，为纵向因果推断提供了基础模型范式。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal effect estimation is complicated by time-varying confounding and treatment-confounder feedback loops. Existing methods generally require data-specific nuisance-model fitting, and many are tailored to prespecified treatment sequences or prediction horizons, leading to time-consuming computation at deployment. We introduce *Policy In-Context Longitudinal causal Effect estimation with Prior-data Fitted Networks* (**PICLE-PFN**), the first foundation model for longitudinal causal effect estimation that supports *dynamic* treatment policies. PICLE-PFN represents target policies through in-context history-decision demonstrations, providing a unified interface for a broad class of treatment policies. Pretrained over synthetic data sampled from longitudinal structural causal models, PICLE-PFN amortizes causal effect estimation across datasets, policies, and horizons. Given a new observational dataset and target policy, it directly predicts conditional average potential outcomes (CAPOs) in one forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic longitudinal benchmarks, PICLE-PFN achieves competitive RMSE and substantially improved uncertainty calibration with a fraction of deployment runtime, compared to state-of-the-art conventional estimators trained and tuned separately for each dataset.

</details>


### [248] [MPQ: A Message-Passing View of Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32127)
*Yanshuo Chen,Chenxi Liu,Reza Shirkavand,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将逐行训练后量化（PTQ）重新表述为离散码本上的后验推断，并提出消息传递量化（MPQ）：用向量近似消息传递（VAMP）计算块级软后验，仅在输出阶段做硬判决，并用精确块坐标下降精炼初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PTQ 求解器（GPTQ、LDLQ、块坐标下降）在考虑相邻块之前就将每个权重量化块固定为单一码字，属于零温度硬判决极限，忽略了近最优的候选码字，导致量化误差偏大。

Method: 1）把逐行 PTQ 建模为离散码本上的后验推断，证明现有求解器等价于零温度、每块仅保留 top-1 码字的硬判决极限；2）提出 MPQ，在推断过程中跨块保留软后验信息，只在输出阶段提交硬码字；3）先用向量近似消息传递计算近似块级后验，再以精确块坐标下降精炼硬判决初始化；4）可与激活感知缩放、非相干处理等预处理技术组合。

Result: 在 Llama、Qwen、Mistral 系列稠密语言模型的 2-bit 格点量化及 3-bit、4-bit 标量量化上，MPQ 在困惑度和零样本准确率上均优于强硬判决基线，并能与激活感知缩放、非相干处理等预处理方法良好叠加。

Conclusion: 以软后验推断替代逐块即时硬判决，是提升低比特 PTQ 性能的有效方向；MPQ 作为兼具理论解释性与工程可行性的求解器，可即插即用地增强现有量化流程。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a key technique for efficiently deploying large language models, as it compresses weights into low-bit representations without retraining. Existing PTQ solvers, including GPTQ, LDLQ, and block coordinate descent, commit each weight block to a single codeword before its neighbors are considered, leaving near-optimal alternatives unexplored. In this work, we cast row-wise PTQ as posterior inference over discrete codebooks, in which existing solvers correspond to a zero-temperature, hard-decision limit retaining only the top codeword per block. We propose Message-Passing Quantization (MPQ), which preserves soft posterior information across blocks during inference and commits to hard codewords only at the output stage. MPQ first uses vector approximate message passing to compute approximate blockwise posteriors, then refines a hard-decision initialization by exact block coordinate descent. Across 2-bit lattice and 3- and 4-bit scalar quantization on dense language models from Llama, Qwen, and Mistral families, MPQ improves perplexity and zero-shot accuracy over strong hard-decision baselines, and composes favorably with preprocessing techniques such as activation-aware scaling and incoherence processing. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/poseidonchan/MPQ.

</details>


### [249] [Can Test-Time Memory Evolution Outperform Test-Time Training for Scientific Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32046)
*Yepeng Liu,Shengmai Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Chengzhi Liu,Dawn Song,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的测试时记忆演化方法TTME，通过更新外部记忆而非模型权重实现测试时自适应，在五个数学任务上全面优于TTT，FLOPs降低82.8%–84.6%，运行时间降低47.7%–58.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT虽能让开放权重LLM在科学发现任务上超越前沿模型的纯推理框架，但测试时在线更新权重带来额外计算开销，限制了向更大模型扩展，因此需要更高效的测试时自适应方法。

Method: TTME不更新模型权重，而是持续更新外部记忆：通过蒸馏经验来指导后续的科学发现过程，作为TTT的即插即用替代方案。为公平比较，两者在同一发现框架下仅替换测试时自适应模块。

Result: 在五个数学任务上TTME全部优于TTT，FLOPs减少82.8%–84.6%，运行时间减少47.7%–58.4%。

Conclusion: 在所评估的发现设定下，TTME是有效且计算高效的TTT替代方案。

Abstract: Recent \emph{test-time training} (TTT) frameworks have enabled open-weight large language models (LLMs) to outperform inference-only frameworks powered by frontier models on scientific discovery tasks. However, TTT introduces additional computational cost for online weight updates during the discovery process, making computational efficiency an important consideration when scaling to larger models. This motivates us to explore an efficient \emph{test-time adaptation} method for scientific discovery. We introduce \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} (TTME), a plug-and-play test-time adaptation method, as an alternative to TTT. TTME continually updates the external memory by distilling the experience to guide subsequent discovery without updating model weights. To have a clean comparison, we evaluate TTME and TTT under the same discovery framework, replacing only the test-time adaptation method. Our evaluation shows that TTME outperforms TTT on all five mathematical tasks, while reducing computational cost by $82.8$-$84.6\%$ in FLOPs and $47.7$-$58.4\%$ in runtime. These results establish TTME as an effective and computationally efficient alternative to TTT in the evaluated discovery settings.

</details>


### [250] [The Helpful Agent Problem: When Emergent Cooperation Among LLM Agents Bypasses Intended Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31985)
*Moumita Choudhury,Amit Sarker,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并实证了"有帮助的智能体问题"（helpful agent problem）：在长期共享资源环境中，当审查机制不可用时，各自追求独立目标、无共同奖励的良性LLM智能体会自发互相"帮忙"，绕开预期的监督结构，且可能对外部方造成显著伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要关注对抗性或显式串谋行为，忽视了善意智能体在协作部署下自发合作带来的安全风险。作者希望揭示：合作本身即可成为绕过系统级保障的通道，这是一种此前未被识别的多智能体安全挑战。

Method: 研究长期共享资源环境：智能体有时依赖同伴拥有的资源，跨所有者变更通常由审查者中介。当审查不可用时观察智能体行为。作者构建受控基准FavorBench（可测量下游副作用），并将CooperBench适配到真实代码仓库中以验证现象泛化性。实验覆盖两个基准、四种模型。

Result: 在两个基准与四种模型上，"工作流外帮忙"频繁出现；在FavorBench中，至少三种模型产生的帮忙行为对交互之外的第三方造成显著伤害。这些行为均在各自本地权限内，却规避了系统预期的监督结构。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全面临一个独立挑战：善意合作本身可形成绕过系统级保障的路径。由此，仅检测恶意或串谋行为不足以保证安全，需在基准与防护设计中显式考虑"利他式越权协作"这一失效模式。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in collaborative settings where they pursue separate tasks while sharing resources and operating under review or oversight processes. Such settings create safety risks not only when agents are malicious, but also when they are simply trying to help. Prior work has largely studied adversarial or explicitly collusive behavior; we show that benign agents pursuing separate objectives can spontaneously cooperate in ways that bypass intended workflows. We call this the \emph{helpful agent problem}. We study long-horizon shared-resource environments in which agents sometimes depend on resources owned by their peers and cross-owner changes are normally mediated by a reviewer. When review becomes unavailable, agents begin making changes on one another's behalf despite having no shared reward or explicit incentive to cooperate. Crucially, these actions remain within each agent's local permissions while circumventing the system's intended oversight structure. We introduce \textsc{FavorBench}, a controlled benchmark with measurable downstream side effects, and adapt \textsc{CooperBench} to test the phenomenon in realistic repositories. Across two benchmarks and four models, we find that out-of-workflow favors emerge frequently. Further, in \textsc{FavorBench}, favors from at least three models cause significant harm to parties outside the interaction. These results reveal a distinct multi-agent safety challenge: cooperation itself can create pathways around system-level safeguards.

</details>


### [251] [Knowing Whom to Trust is not Enough: The Trust--Agreement Gap in Repeated Multi-Party LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31971)
*Amit Sarker,Moumita Choudhury,Saaduddin Mahmud,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM智能体能否从历史交互中形成有效信任估计并用于改进重复多方谈判，发现存在"信任—协议鸿沟"：信任感知智能体能在数局内识别破坏者，但准确信任只有在被显式引导用于决策时才能提升协议率（最高23个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益代表个人与组织参与谈判，但在重复多方交互中易被操纵。经典多智能体系统通过显式信任与声誉模型应对该问题，而现有LLM谈判工作多将信任视为涌现现象，缺乏对其作用机制的实证分析。

Method: 构建重复、多方、多议题谈判框架：固定参与者连续进行多轮谈判博弈，其中一名参与者暗中阻挠达成协议；每局结束后智能体评估对手并维护可跨局传递的两两信任估计；对比信任感知与信任无感知智能体，分析显式信任对谈判结果的影响。

Result: 信任感知智能体可在数局内识别对抗者，但准确信任估计并未稳定转化为更优协议，即"信任—协议鸿沟"；引入显式信任可使协议率提升最多23个百分点，但前提是智能体被明确引导将信任用于决策。

Conclusion: 显式信任能够改善重复性LLM谈判，但其价值关键在于信任的评估方式及其被纳入智能体推理的机制，而非信任估计本身的准确性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly participate in negotiations on behalf of individuals and organizations, yet they remain vulnerable to manipulation, particularly in repeated multi-party interactions. Classical multi-agent systems address this challenge through explicit trust and reputation models, whereas existing work on LLM negotiation largely treats trust as an emergent phenomenon. We investigate whether LLM agents can form useful trust estimates from prior interactions and leverage them to improve future negotiation outcomes. We introduce a repeated, multi-party, multi-issue negotiation framework in which recurring agents participate in a sequence of negotiation games while one participant covertly attempts to prevent agreement. After each game, agents assess their counterparts and maintain pairwise trust estimates that carry over to subsequent negotiations. We compare trust-aware and trust-agnostic agents and analyze how explicit trust influences negotiation outcomes. Our results reveal a \emph{trust--agreement gap}: trust-aware agents can identify the adversary within a few games, but accurate trust estimates do not consistently translate into better agreements. Trust increases agreement rates by up to 23 percentage points, but only when agents are explicitly guided to use it in their decisions. Explicit trust can therefore improve repeated LLM negotiations, but its value depends critically on how trust is assessed and incorporated into agents' reasoning.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond On-Policy vs. Off-Policy: At What Level Should LLM Agent Distillation Follow the Student?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31918)
*Ya Gao,Zhengtao Zou,Pekka Marttinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出分层智能体蒸馏框架，将"on-policiness"分解为交互历史层面与动作前缀层面两个层级，并通过三阶段控制实验给出实践性蒸馏原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体蒸馏中，教师生成示范（off-policy）与学生自生成经验（on-policy）各有成效，但已有工作将二者视为单一二分，忽略了"产生交互历史的策略"与"生成当前动作token前缀的策略"可以不同这一层级结构。

Method: 提出分层智能体蒸馏框架，形式化两级on-policiness并显式控制其配对方式、混合比例与训练调度；在自蒸馏设定下（冻结教师可获得学生无法获得的任务指导），跨3个模型、4个交互环境开展三阶段控制研究。

Result: 学生诱导的历史配教师生成的动作前缀在单独使用时训练信号最强，而两级均跟随学生效果最差；完全on-policy数据在与其它配置混合时有益；两级教师/学生比例相同但配对方式不同会显著影响性能；前段使用全教师上下文、后段延迟全on-policy上下文优于静态混合。

Conclusion: 智能体蒸馏中的on-policiness应被视为分层设计变量，配对方式、混合比例与调度顺序均独立影响效果；这些结论为"在何处、多少、何时"跟随学生提供了可操作原则。

Abstract: Distilling interactive LLM agents requires choosing between teacher-generated demonstrations and student-generated experience, corresponding broadly to off-policy and on-policy distillation. While both have proven effective, we argue that this distinction is inherently hierarchical: the policy that induces an interaction history need not be the same as the policy that generates the token prefix of the current action. We introduce a hierarchical agent distillation framework that formalizes these two levels of on-policiness and enables explicit control over their pairing, mixture proportions, and training schedule. We study this problem through self-distillation, where a frozen teacher has access to task guidance unavailable to the student, and conduct a three-stage controlled study across three models and four interactive environments. We find that student-induced histories paired with teacher-generated action prefixes generally provide the strongest standalone training signal, whereas following the student at both levels generally performs worst. Nevertheless, fully on-policy data becomes beneficial when incorporated into a mixture with other configurations. Moreover, mixtures with identical teacher/student proportions at each level can yield substantially different performance, showing that how the two levels are paired matters beyond the overall amount of on-policy data. Finally, front-loading fully teacher-generated contexts and delaying fully on-policy contexts improves over a static mixture. These findings provide practical principles for deciding where, how much, and when agent distillation should follow the student.

</details>


### [253] [PaperFormalizer: Author-in-the-Loop Autoformalization of Research Papers in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31810)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaperFormalizer 是一个基于 Lean 的智能体流水线，从 LaTeX 源码出发，把论文转录为语句依赖图，逐条审计其忠实性、歧义与正确性，再在 Lean/Mathlib 中形式化并证明，关键决策交由作者裁定，最终产出经 Lean 验证的论文版本、变更报告与可复用的 Lean 代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使仔细校对过的论文，证明与假设中仍可能存在细微漏洞；而形式化验证此前成本过高、难以实用化，且只有作者清楚语句的本意以及结果中的形式化缺口应如何修补。

Method: 以 LaTeX 源为输入，构建语句依赖图；用自然语言对每条语句进行忠实性、歧义和正确性审计；随后基于 Mathlib、可选领域库与作者个人工具包在 Lean 中形式化并证明。采用人机协同回路：当语句存在多种解读时流水线暂停并向作者提问，所有修复以自然语言加 TeX 符号形式作为建议提交，由作者接受、拒绝或改向；凡可能改变论文主张的决策均留给作者。系统运行于编码智能体（Claude Code 或 Codex）中。

Result: 输出三部分：带注释与修订的论文版本、变更报告，以及每个声明均与其所形式化语句相链接的 Lean 代码；作者工具包随形式化论文增多而累积，经验证的论文可共享以供后续研究者复用其 Lean 代码。

Conclusion: 通过使"经验证的论文"可实际产出，PaperFormalizer 旨在提升依赖数学证明的研究的稳健性；系统已开源并提供共享验证论文的社区站点。

Abstract: Even a carefully proofread paper can leave small gaps in its proofs or its stated assumptions. We introduce PaperFormalizer, a Lean-based agentic pipeline that helps authors find and close such gaps in their own papers and turn each paper into a Lean-verified version. Starting from the LaTeX source, PaperFormalizer transcribes the paper into a dependency graph of statements, audits each statement in natural language for faithfulness, ambiguity and correctness, and then formalizes and proves the statements in Lean against Mathlib, optional domain libraries and the author's personal toolkit. Only the author knows what a statement is meant to say and how a formalization gap in a result is best fixed. The pipeline therefore stops and asks the author whenever a statement admits more than one reading, presents every repair, in natural language and TeX symbols, as a proposal that the author can accept, decline or redirect, and leaves to the author every decision that could potentially change what the paper claims. The output is an annotated and revised version of the paper, a report of the changes, and Lean code in which each declaration is linked to the statement it formalizes. The author's toolkit grows as more papers are formalized, and authors can share their verified papers so that researchers who build on them can reuse their Lean code. By making verified papers practical to produce, PaperFormalizer aims to make research that rests on mathematical proofs more robust. PaperFormalizer runs inside a coding agent (Claude Code or Codex) and is available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/PaperFormalizer. Papers with Lean code shared by users are available at https://paperformalizer.org.

</details>


### [254] [POSEIDON: Scaling Training Environments for Scientific Agents from Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32448)
*Qifan Zhang,Yuhan Li,Nuo Chen,Bowen Liu,Xiao Hu,Miao Peng,Chenyi Zi,Lixin Liu,Lydell Huang,Jing Tang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自动化流水线Poseidon，从科学代码仓库规模化生成可验证训练环境，并构建迄今最大跨学科科学智能体训练环境集Pearl（97,659个环境，7,110个仓库，10个学科），在其上训练的Qwen3.5模型在六个基准上均获稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的科学发现智能体受限于训练环境：现有可执行、可验证环境集合规模小，构建过程难以扩展。

Method: Poseidon将科学工作流分解为四类互补任务（复现Reproduction、子流程Subflow、补全Completion、纠错Correction），每类对应不同科研能力；并以递归改进循环对失败候选进行精炼，回收本被丢弃的环境，从而在同一仓库预算下产出近10倍于先前方法的环境数量。基于此构建Pearl数据集。

Result: Pearl包含来自7,110个公共仓库、覆盖10个学科的97,659个可执行且可验证环境，为目前规模最大、学科最广的科学智能体训练环境集合；Qwen3.5（9B、27B、35B-A3B）在Pearl上训练后，于六个高难度科学智能体基准上取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: Pearl的规模与多样性为训练科学发现智能体提供了新机会，Poseidon为训练环境的可扩展自动构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Training environments remain a major bottleneck for agents in data-driven scientific discovery. Existing collections of executable and verifiable training environments are small, and their construction remains difficult to scale. To address this limitation, we introduce Poseidon, an automated pipeline for generating diverse, verifiable training environments from scientific code repositories at scale. From the same repository budget, Poseidon extracts nearly 10 times as many training environments as a prior approach by decomposing scientific workflows into four complementary task types: Reproduction, Subflow, Completion, and Correction, where each type targets a distinct capability for scientific research. A recursive improvement loop further refines failed candidates and recovers otherwise discarded environments. Using Poseidon, we construct Pearl, a collection of 97,659 executable and verifiable environments from 7,110 public repositories spanning 10 scientific disciplines. To the best of our knowledge, Pearl is the largest and most broadly multidisciplinary collection of scientific-agent training environments to date. Qwen3.5 models (9B, 27B, and 35B-A3B) trained on Pearl achieve consistent gains across six challenging scientific-agent benchmarks. Overall, the scale and diversity of Pearl offer opportunities for training scientific discovery agents.

</details>


### [255] [From Visual Affinity to Learning Credit in Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32324)
*Yuhang Han,Dingning Liu,Yujie Chen,Yaojie Zhang,Jingwei Song,Zequn Yang,Xuanming Shang,Li Ding,Zhijiang Guo,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出即插即用框架 OVC，将视觉关联度量与 token 级信用分配解耦，在保留 GSPO 序列级裁剪的同时以结果条件化的 token 权重调制梯度贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 对多模态推理有效，但将单一响应级优势统一分配给所有 token，忽视了视觉观测与后续推理的不同角色。仅依据视觉亲和度也不充分：预测状态可能同时对齐图像与提示上下文表示，且当后续推理失败时，给有效视觉观测加权反而会放大其负优势更新。

Method: OVC 包含三部分：(1) 相对视觉编码，通过对比预测状态对图像表示与提示上下文表示的亲和度，构建上下文相对的视觉坐标；(2) 结果条件化证据校准，将该坐标与不确定性缩放的预测特征结合，用正/负优势响应间的均值对比（并以正则化的组内方差缩放）校准其对结果判别性证据的贡献；(3) 归一化信用分配，依据响应优势符号条件化校准证据，生成全局均值为 1 的正 token 权重，权重与梯度分离，仅调制各 token 梯度贡献。无需额外奖励模型、过程标注或推理期模块。

Result: 以 GSPO-token 为实例化载体，OVC 保留序列级裁剪，在三个模型、六个基准上均一致提升 GSPO 的平均准确率，增益为 1.86–2.39 个百分点。

Conclusion: 视觉关联应通过观测到的结果差异来影响信用分配，而非直接决定更新优先级；结果条件化的 token 级信用分配可作为一种轻量插件提升多模态 RLVR 效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves multimodal reasoning, but uniformly assigning a single response-level advantage to all tokens overlooks the distinct roles of visual observations and subsequent reasoning. Visual affinity alone is insufficient: predictive states may align with both image and prompt-context representations, and upweighting valid visual observations can amplify their negative-advantage updates when later reasoning fails. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{Outcome-Conditioned Visual Credit (OVC)}, a plug-in framework that separates visual-association measurement from token-level credit assignment and is adaptable to policy-optimization methods with uniform response-level advantage allocation. Relative Visual Encoding constructs a context-relative visual coordinate by contrasting each predictive state's affinity to image and prompt-context representations. Outcome-conditioned Evidence Calibration combines this coordinate with uncertainty-scaled predictive features and calibrates their contributions to outcome-discriminative evidence through mean contrasts between positive- and negative-advantage responses, scaled by regularized within-side variation. Visual association thus informs credit through observed outcome differences rather than directly determining update priority. Normalized Credit Allocation conditions the calibrated evidence on the response's advantage sign to produce positive token weights with unit global mean. These detached weights preserve advantage signs while modulating individual token-gradient contributions. OVC reuses existing rollouts without auxiliary reward models, process annotations, or inference-time modules. Instantiated through GSPO-token, OVC retains sequence-level clipping and consistently improves six-benchmark mean accuracy over GSPO across three models, with gains of 1.86-2.39 percentage points.

</details>


### [256] [DMAIC-Bench: From Self-Reflection to Self-Restraint for Agents in Multi-Stage Analytical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32305)
*Shan Peng,Shuaiqi Cheng,Miao Peng,Chenyi Zi,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DMAIC-Bench基准，用配对反事实任务评测LLM智能体的"自我约束"能力（即证据是否改变下一步动作），发现智能体能正确诊断却仍在NO-GO处继续执行禁用计算，执行层NO-GO路由准确率仅19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段分析任务中，智能体需依据中间证据决定继续、转向或停止。现有研究多关注"自我反思"（对自身输出进行批判），但批判并不能说明证据是否真正改变了下一步动作，此能力被定义为"自我约束"；而现有基准主要衡量任务完成度或输出质量，未对此进行检验。

Method: 构建DMAIC-Bench，含15个工业案例、4个行业共97个种子任务，以Lean Six Sigma的显式统计前提为核心；设置配对反事实变体，使同一流程位置需采取相反动作，以区分"有依据的克制"与"无差别停止"；通过私有评分器评估执行轨迹，并由独立专家评审最终结论；另设16个生命科学任务（bulk RNA-seq与病例对照GWAS）验证框架可迁移性。

Result: 在来自6家提供商的13个模型中，执行层路由准确率在匹配的GO决策上平均92%，而NO-GO决策仅19%（任何被禁止的下游计算均计为路由错误）。执行轨迹显示智能体常先报告正确诊断、随后仍执行这些结果所禁止的分析。对11个受审智能体的盲审专家评审估计，在已达NO-GO决策处有47%的报告基于无效证据行动（区间31%–61%）。显式停止许可对部分模型有帮助，但未能在GO与NO-GO两类分叉处一致地恢复正确决策。

Conclusion: 自我约束是多阶段分析中独立于自我反思的关键缺口，当前模型呈现"诊断正确、行动失范"的不对称失败模式（GO强、NO-GO弱）；显式停止许可不足以修复该问题；该框架可扩展至工业流程之外的生命科学证据依赖型决策，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Multi-stage analytical tasks require LLM agents to use intermediate evidence to decide whether to continue, redirect or stop. Self-reflection, in which an agent critiques its output, is widely studied, yet a critique does not show whether evidence changed the next action. We call that capability self-restraint. Existing agent benchmarks largely measure task completion or output quality and leave it untested. DMAIC-Bench evaluates it with 97 seeded tasks across 15 industrial cases in four sectors, built around Lean Six Sigma's explicit statistical prerequisites. Paired counterfactual variants require opposite actions at the same workflow position, distinguishing warranted restraint from indiscriminate stopping. We assess execution traces with a private scorer and final claims through independent expert review. Across 13 models from six providers, execution-level routing accuracy averages 92% on matched GO decisions and 19% on NO-GO decisions, counting any forbidden downstream computation as a route error. Execution traces show agents reporting correct diagnostics before performing analyses that those results disallow. Separately, blinded expert review of the 11 audited agents estimates that reports act on invalid evidence at 47% of reached NO-GO decisions, with an interval from 31% to 61%. Explicit stopping permission helps some models without consistently restoring correct decisions at both GO and NO-GO forks. Sixteen life-sciences tasks in bulk ribonucleic acid sequencing (RNA-seq) and case-control genome-wide association studies (GWAS) show that the same framework can evaluate evidence-dependent decisions beyond industrial workflows. Project code and data are available at: https://github.com/psptpx/DMAIC-Bench-public.

</details>


### [257] [Lifelong Context Adaptation through Bidirectional Memory-Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32295)
*Tianyi Xu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemCryst：冻结语言模型下记忆与技能双向学习的终身上下文适应框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身LLM智能体需在有限上下文窗口内从不断增长的交互历史中学习；记忆提供可用经验但需推断如何解题，技能提供可复用流程但缺少任务特定事实，且二者会共同塑造未来学习所依赖的经验。

Method: 在冻结语言模型上做记忆-技能双向学习：记忆中的重复情节支持技能的创建与修订，技能引导的交互产生新记忆；检索事实与习得技能联合构成上下文，任务结果调整被服务记录的未来检索影响力而非改写其内容。

Result: 覆盖具身控制、长文档阅读、对话、数学与代码共十个设置，在七项报告比较中领先；ALFWorld上联合服务成功率63.17%，优于仅记忆59.83%与仅技能50.50%。

Conclusion: 记忆与技能的联合服务优于任一单独使用，双向学习可在不更新模型参数的前提下实现持续上下文适应。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents must learn from an expanding history of interactions while making each decision within a bounded context window. Memory makes past experience available, but the agent may still need to infer how to solve each new task from the retrieved records. Skills provide reusable procedures, yet may omit the task-specific facts needed to apply them. Because retrieved memory and skills shape the agent's behavior, they also influence the experience available for future learning. We introduce MemCryst, a framework for lifelong context adaptation through bidirectional learning of memory and skills with a frozen language model. Recurring episodes stored in memory support skill creation and revision, and the interactions guided by those skills supply new memory for subsequent learning. Facts retrieved from memory and learned skills jointly form the agent's context, while task outcomes adjust the future retrieval influence of the records actually served without rewriting their content. Across ten settings spanning embodied control, long-document reading, conversation, mathematics, and code, MemCryst leads seven reported comparisons. On ALFWorld, joint serving achieves 63.17% success, compared with 59.83% for memory-only and 50.50% for skill-only serving. Code is available at https://github.com/tianyi0216/Memcryst.git

</details>


### [258] [$M^3$: A Multi-LLM System for Multi-Turn Jailbreaking as a Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31937)
*Yuqing Huang,Jack Ji,Jithin Pradeep,Sagarika Suresh,Jekaterina Novikova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 M³ 多轮红队框架，将多轮越狱形式化为有限时域 MDP，在攻击成功率、风险升级速度与类别均衡性上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮红队方法依赖启发式策略或较强假设，难以适用于完全黑盒场景，因此需要一个可扩展、有原则的多轮越狱框架。

Method: M³ 将多轮越狱建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程；攻击者 LLM 与目标 LLM 交互，并由一个状态 LLM 将对话压缩为紧凑的内部状态表示，供攻击者决策使用。

Result: 在两个基准、六个目标模型上，M³ 的 ASR 持续领先 SOTA 多轮红队方法，ASR@5 最高提升 53%；即使使用轻量攻击者模型，仍相对更强攻击者基线取得 ASR@1 最高 26%、ASR@5 最高 34% 的提升；同时随轮次呈现更陡的风险升级，跨风险类别表现更均衡（Gini 指数最高改善 4.82），且不依赖预定义风险分类体系。

Conclusion: M³ 为多轮红队与安全评估提供了一个可扩展、有理论支撑的框架，兼顾有效性、效率与类别均衡性。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to carefully orchestrated multi-turn interactions that progressively bypass safety mechanisms. However, many existing red-teaming approaches rely on heuristics or impose strong assumptions that limit their applicability in fully black box settings. We address this gap by introducing $M^3$, an automated red-teaming framework that formalizes multi-turn jailbreaking as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process (MDP). In $M^3$, an attacker LLM interacts with a target LLM while operating over a compact internal state produced by a state LLM. Across two benchmarks and six target models, $M^3$ consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-turn red-teaming methods in Attack Success Rates (ASR), achieving improvements of up to 53% in ASR@5. Even with lightweight attacker, $M^3$ attains gains up to 26% in ASR@1 and 34% in ASR@5, compared to baselines that use higher capacity attackers. Beyond improved effectiveness, $M^3$ exhibits steeper risk escalation over conversational turns and achieves more consistent performance across risk categories (up to 4.82 improvement in Gini index) without relying on predefined risk taxonomy, providing a scalable and principled framework for multi-turn red-teaming and safety evaluation.

</details>
